238:# Wrapper for ModelRunnerOutput to support overlapped execution.
239:class AsyncGPUModelRunnerOutput(AsyncModelRunnerOutput):
240:    def __init__(
241:        self,
242:        model_runner_output: ModelRunnerOutput,
243:        sampled_token_ids: torch.Tensor,
244:        logprobs_tensors: LogprobsTensors | None,
245:        invalid_req_indices: list[int],
246:        async_output_copy_stream: torch.cuda.Stream,
247:        vocab_size: int,
248:        routed_experts: RoutedExpertsTensors | None = None,
249:    ):
250:        self._model_runner_output = model_runner_output
251:        self._invalid_req_indices = invalid_req_indices
252:
253:        # Event on the copy stream so we can synchronize the non-blocking copy.
254:        self.async_copy_ready_event = torch.Event()
255:
256:        # Keep a reference to the device tensor to avoid it being
257:        # deallocated until we finish copying it to the host.
258:        self._sampled_token_ids = sampled_token_ids
259:        self.vocab_size = vocab_size
260:        self._logprobs_tensors = logprobs_tensors
261:        self._routed_experts = routed_experts
262:
263:        # Initiate the copy on a separate stream, but do not synchronize it.
264:        default_stream = torch.cuda.current_stream()
265:        with torch.cuda.stream(async_output_copy_stream):
266:            async_output_copy_stream.wait_stream(default_stream)
267:            self.sampled_token_ids_cpu = self._sampled_token_ids.to(
268:                "cpu", non_blocking=True
269:            )
270:            self._logprobs_tensors_cpu = (
271:                self._logprobs_tensors.to_cpu_nonblocking()
272:                if self._logprobs_tensors
273:                else None
274:            )
275:            self._routed_experts_cpu = (
276:                self._routed_experts.to_cpu_nonblocking()
277:                if self._routed_experts is not None
278:                else None
279:            )
280:            self.async_copy_ready_event.record()
281:
282:    def get_output(self) -> ModelRunnerOutput:
283:        """Copy the device tensors to the host and return a ModelRunnerOutput.
284:
285:        This function blocks until the copy is finished.
286:        """
287:        max_gen_len = self.sampled_token_ids_cpu.shape[-1]
288:        self.async_copy_ready_event.synchronize()
289:
290:        # Release the device tensors once the copy has completed.
291:        del self._logprobs_tensors
292:        del self._sampled_token_ids
293:        if max_gen_len == 1:
294:            valid_sampled_token_ids = self.sampled_token_ids_cpu.tolist()
295:            for i in self._invalid_req_indices:
296:                valid_sampled_token_ids[i].clear()
297:            logprobs_lists = None
298:            if self._logprobs_tensors_cpu is not None:
299:                logprobs_lists = self._logprobs_tensors_cpu.tolists()
300:        else:
301:            valid_sampled_token_ids, logprobs_lists = RejectionSampler.parse_output(
302:                self.sampled_token_ids_cpu,
303:                self.vocab_size,
304:                self._invalid_req_indices,
305:                logprobs_tensors=self._logprobs_tensors_cpu,
306:            )
307:
308:        output = self._model_runner_output
309:        output.sampled_token_ids = valid_sampled_token_ids
310:        output.logprobs = logprobs_lists
311:
312:        if self._routed_experts_cpu is not None:
313:            output.routed_experts = self._routed_experts_cpu.tolists()
314:        del self._routed_experts
315:
316:        return output
317:
318:
319:def _copy_pooler_output_to_cpu(
320:    raw_pooler_output: PoolerOutput, finished_mask: list[bool]
321:) -> list[torch.Tensor | None]:
322:    num_reqs = len(finished_mask)
323:
324:    if isinstance(raw_pooler_output, torch.Tensor):
325:        if raw_pooler_output.shape[0] != num_reqs:
326:            raise ValueError(
327:                "Pooler output batch size does not match finished mask size: "
328:                f"{raw_pooler_output.shape[0]} != {num_reqs}."
329:            )
330:
331:        num_finished = sum(finished_mask)
332:        if num_finished == 0:
333:            return [None] * num_reqs
334:        if num_finished == num_reqs:
335:            return list(raw_pooler_output.to("cpu", non_blocking=True))
336:
337:        # partial finished
338:        finished_indices = [i for i, include in enumerate(finished_mask) if include]
339:        index_tensor = torch.tensor(
340:            finished_indices, device=raw_pooler_output.device, dtype=torch.long
341:        )
342:        finished_outputs = raw_pooler_output.index_select(0, index_tensor).to(
343:            "cpu", non_blocking=True
344:        )
345:        partial_pooler_output: list[torch.Tensor | None] = [None] * num_reqs
346:        for i, out in zip(finished_indices, finished_outputs):
347:            partial_pooler_output[i] = out
348:        return partial_pooler_output
349:
350:    assert isinstance(raw_pooler_output, list)
351:    if len(raw_pooler_output) != num_reqs:
352:        raise ValueError(
353:            "Pooler output batch size does not match finished mask size: "
354:            f"{len(raw_pooler_output)} != {num_reqs}."
355:        )
356:
357:    pooler_output: list[torch.Tensor | None] = [None] * num_reqs
358:    for i, (out, include) in enumerate(zip(raw_pooler_output, finished_mask)):
359:        if include and out is not None:
360:            pooler_output[i] = out.to("cpu", non_blocking=True)
361:    return pooler_output
362:
363:
364:class AsyncGPUPoolingModelRunnerOutput(AsyncModelRunnerOutput):
365:    def __init__(
366:        self,
367:        model_runner_output: ModelRunnerOutput,
368:        raw_pooler_output: PoolerOutput,
369:        finished_mask: list[bool],
370:        async_output_copy_stream: torch.cuda.Stream,
371:    ):
372:        self._model_runner_output = model_runner_output
373:
374:        # Event on the copy stream so we can synchronize the non-blocking copy.
375:        self.async_copy_ready_event = torch.Event()
376:
377:        # Keep a reference to the device tensors to avoid them being
378:        # deallocated until we finish copying it to the host.
379:        self._raw_pooler_output = raw_pooler_output
380:
381:        # Initiate the copy on a separate stream, but do not synchronize it.
382:        default_stream = torch.cuda.current_stream()
383:        with torch.cuda.stream(async_output_copy_stream):
384:            async_output_copy_stream.wait_stream(default_stream)
385:            self._model_runner_output.pooler_output = _copy_pooler_output_to_cpu(
386:                raw_pooler_output=self._raw_pooler_output,
387:                finished_mask=finished_mask,
388:            )
389:            self.async_copy_ready_event.record()
390:
391:    def get_output(self) -> ModelRunnerOutput:
392:        """Copy the device tensors to the host and return a ModelRunnerOutput.
393:        This function blocks until the copy is finished.
394:        """
395:        self.async_copy_ready_event.synchronize()
396:
397:        # Release the device tensors once the copy has completed.
398:        del self._raw_pooler_output
399:        return self._model_runner_output

402:class ExecuteModelState(NamedTuple):
403:    """Ephemeral cached state transferred between execute_model() and
404:    sample_tokens(), after execute_model() returns None."""
405:
406:    scheduler_output: "SchedulerOutput"
407:    logits: torch.Tensor
408:    spec_decode_metadata: SpecDecodeMetadata | None
409:    spec_decode_common_attn_metadata: CommonAttentionMetadata | None
410:    hidden_states: torch.Tensor
411:    sample_hidden_states: torch.Tensor
412:    aux_hidden_states: list[torch.Tensor] | None
413:    ec_connector_output: ECConnectorOutput | None
414:    cudagraph_stats: CUDAGraphStat | None
415:    slot_mappings: dict[str, torch.Tensor] | list[dict[str, torch.Tensor]] | None

418:class GPUModelRunner(
419:    LoRAModelRunnerMixin, KVConnectorModelRunnerMixin, ECConnectorModelRunnerMixin
420:):
421:    def __init__(
422:        self,
423:        vllm_config: VllmConfig,
424:        device: torch.device,
425:    ):
426:        self.vllm_config = vllm_config
427:        self.model_config = vllm_config.model_config
428:        self.cache_config = vllm_config.cache_config
429:        self.offload_config = vllm_config.offload_config
430:        self.compilation_config = vllm_config.compilation_config
431:        self.lora_config = vllm_config.lora_config
432:        self.load_config = vllm_config.load_config
433:        self.parallel_config = vllm_config.parallel_config
434:        self.scheduler_config = vllm_config.scheduler_config
435:        self.speculative_config = vllm_config.speculative_config
436:        self.observability_config = vllm_config.observability_config
437:
438:        model_config = self.model_config
439:        cache_config = self.cache_config
440:        scheduler_config = self.scheduler_config
441:        parallel_config = self.parallel_config
442:        self.device = device
443:        self.pin_memory = is_pin_memory_available()
444:        self.dtype = self.model_config.dtype
445:
446:        self.kv_cache_dtype = kv_cache_dtype_str_to_dtype(
447:            cache_config.cache_dtype, self.model_config
448:        )
449:
450:        self.is_pooling_model = model_config.runner_type == "pooling"
451:        self.enable_prompt_embeds = model_config.enable_prompt_embeds
452:        self.is_multimodal_raw_input_only_model = (
453:            model_config.is_multimodal_raw_input_only_model
454:        )
455:        # These will be overridden in load_model()
456:        self.is_multimodal_pruning_enabled = False
457:        self.requires_sequential_video_encoding = False
458:        # Set to True after init_routed_experts_capturer() completes.
459:        # Prevents routed experts code from running during profiling/dummy run.
460:        self.routed_experts_initialized = False
461:        self.max_model_len = model_config.max_model_len
462:
463:        # Always set to false after the first forward pass
464:        self.calculate_kv_scales = self.cache_config.calculate_kv_scales
465:        self.dcp_world_size = self.parallel_config.decode_context_parallel_size
466:        self.dcp_rank = 0 if self.dcp_world_size <= 1 else get_dcp_group().rank_in_group
467:        self.max_num_tokens = scheduler_config.max_num_batched_tokens
468:        self.max_num_reqs = scheduler_config.max_num_seqs
469:
470:        # Broadcast PP output for external_launcher (torchrun)
471:        # to make sure we are synced across pp ranks
472:        # TODO: Support overlapping micro-batches
473:        # https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/18019
474:        self.broadcast_pp_output = (
475:            self.parallel_config.distributed_executor_backend == "external_launcher"
476:            and len(get_pp_group().ranks) > 1
477:        )
478:
479:        # Model-related.
480:        self.num_query_heads = model_config.get_num_attention_heads(parallel_config)
481:        self.inputs_embeds_size = model_config.get_inputs_embeds_size()
482:        # Only relevant for models using ALiBi (e.g, MPT)
483:        self.use_alibi = model_config.uses_alibi
484:
485:        self.cascade_attn_enabled = not self.model_config.disable_cascade_attn
486:        self.is_mm_prefix_lm = self.model_config.is_mm_prefix_lm
487:
488:        # Multi-modal data support
489:        self.mm_registry = MULTIMODAL_REGISTRY
490:        self.uses_mrope = model_config.uses_mrope
491:        self.uses_xdrope_dim = model_config.uses_xdrope_dim
492:        self.supports_mm_inputs = self.mm_registry.supports_multimodal_inputs(
493:            model_config
494:        )
495:
496:        if self.model_config.is_encoder_decoder:
497:            # Maximum length of the encoder input, only for encoder-decoder
498:            # models.
499:            self.max_encoder_len = scheduler_config.max_num_encoder_input_tokens
500:        else:
501:            self.max_encoder_len = 0
502:
503:        # Async scheduling
504:        self.use_async_scheduling = self.scheduler_config.async_scheduling
505:
506:        # Sampler
507:        self.sampler = Sampler(
508:            logprobs_mode=self.model_config.logprobs_mode,
509:            use_fp64_gumbel=self.model_config.use_fp64_gumbel,
510:        )
511:
512:        self.eplb_state: EplbState | None = None
513:        self._moe_model: MixtureOfExperts | None = None
514:        # NOTE(yongji): flag to temporarily disable EPLB during scaling up/down
515:        self.eep_eplb_suppressed = False
516:        """
517:        State of the expert parallelism load balancer.
518:
519:        Will be lazily initialized when the model is loaded.
520:        """
521:
522:        # Lazy initializations
523:        # self.model: nn.Module  # Set after load_model
524:        # Initialize in initialize_kv_cache
525:        self.kv_caches: list[torch.Tensor] = []
526:        # Initialize in initialize_kv_cache_tensors
527:        self.cross_layers_kv_cache: torch.Tensor | None = None
528:        self.cross_layers_attn_backend: type[AttentionBackend] | None = None
529:        # indexes: [kv_cache_group_id][attn_group]
530:        self.attn_groups: list[list[AttentionGroup]] = []
531:        # self.kv_cache_config: KVCacheConfig
532:
533:        # mm_hash ->  encoder_output
534:        self.encoder_cache: dict[str, torch.Tensor] = {}
535:        self.late_interaction_runner = LateInteractionRunner()
536:
537:        # Encoder CUDA graph manager (initialized after model load if enabled)
538:        self.encoder_cudagraph_manager: EncoderCudaGraphManager | None = None
539:
540:        self.use_aux_hidden_state_outputs = False
541:        # Set up speculative decoding.
542:        # NOTE(Jiayi): currently we put the entire draft model on
543:        # the last PP rank. This is not ideal if there are many
544:        # layers in the draft model.
545:        if self.speculative_config and get_pp_group().is_last_rank:
546:            self.drafter: (
547:                NgramProposer  # noqa: F823
548:                | NgramProposerGPU
549:                | SuffixDecodingProposer
550:                | EagleProposer
551:                | DFlashProposer
552:                | DraftModelProposer
553:                | MedusaProposer
554:                | ExtractHiddenStatesProposer
555:                | Gemma4Proposer
556:                | Step3p5MTPProposer
557:            )
558:            if self.speculative_config.method == "custom_class":
559:                self.drafter = create_custom_proposer(  # type: ignore[assignment]
560:                    self.vllm_config
561:                )
562:            elif self.speculative_config.method == "ngram":
563:                from vllm.v1.spec_decode.ngram_proposer import NgramProposer
564:
565:                self.drafter = NgramProposer(self.vllm_config)
566:            elif self.speculative_config.uses_draft_model():
567:                self.drafter = DraftModelProposer(
568:                    vllm_config=self.vllm_config,
569:                    device=self.device,
570:                    runner=self,
571:                )
572:            elif self.speculative_config.use_ngram_gpu():
573:                self.drafter = NgramProposerGPU(self.vllm_config, self.device, self)
574:                self.num_tokens_no_spec_gpu = torch.zeros(
575:                    self.max_num_reqs, dtype=torch.int32, device=device
576:                )
577:                self.token_ids_gpu_tensor = torch.zeros(
578:                    self.max_num_reqs,
579:                    self.max_model_len,
580:                    dtype=torch.int32,
581:                    device=device,
582:                )
583:                self._ngram_pinned_idx_buf = torch.zeros(
584:                    self.max_num_reqs, dtype=torch.long, pin_memory=True
585:                )
586:                self._ngram_pinned_val_buf = torch.zeros(
587:                    self.max_num_reqs, dtype=torch.int32, pin_memory=True
588:                )
589:            elif self.speculative_config.use_gemma4_mtp():
590:                self.drafter = Gemma4Proposer(self.vllm_config, self.device, self)
591:            elif self.speculative_config.use_step3p5_mtp():
592:                self.drafter = Step3p5MTPProposer(self.vllm_config, self.device, self)
593:            elif self.speculative_config.use_dflash():
594:                self.drafter = DFlashProposer(self.vllm_config, self.device, self)
595:                self.use_aux_hidden_state_outputs = True
596:            elif self.speculative_config.method == "suffix":
597:                self.drafter = SuffixDecodingProposer(self.vllm_config)
598:            elif self.speculative_config.use_eagle():
599:                self.drafter = EagleProposer(self.vllm_config, self.device, self)
600:                if self.speculative_config.method == "eagle3":
601:                    self.use_aux_hidden_state_outputs = (
602:                        self.drafter.eagle3_use_aux_hidden_state
603:                    )
604:            elif self.speculative_config.method == "medusa":
605:                self.drafter = MedusaProposer(
606:                    vllm_config=self.vllm_config, device=self.device
607:                )
608:            elif self.speculative_config.method == "extract_hidden_states":
609:                self.drafter = ExtractHiddenStatesProposer(
610:                    vllm_config=self.vllm_config, device=self.device
611:                )
612:                self.use_aux_hidden_state_outputs = True
613:            else:
614:                raise ValueError(
615:                    "Unknown speculative decoding method: "
616:                    f"{self.speculative_config.method}"
617:                )
618:            self.rejection_sampler = RejectionSampler(
619:                self.sampler, self.speculative_config, self.device
620:            )
621:
622:        self.num_spec_tokens = 0
623:        self.valid_sampled_token_count_gpu: torch.Tensor | None = None
624:        if self.speculative_config:
625:            self.num_spec_tokens = self.speculative_config.num_speculative_tokens
626:            draft_config = self.speculative_config.draft_model_config
627:            if draft_config is not None and draft_config.max_model_len is not None:
628:                self.effective_drafter_max_model_len = draft_config.max_model_len
629:            else:
630:                self.effective_drafter_max_model_len = self.max_model_len
631:        self.use_async_spec_decode = (
632:            self.use_async_scheduling and self.num_spec_tokens > 0
633:        )
634:
635:        # Request states.
636:        self.requests: dict[str, CachedRequestState] = {}
637:        # NOTE(rob): num_prompt_logprobs only includes reqs
638:        # that are currently in the prefill phase.
639:        self.num_prompt_logprobs: dict[str, int] = {}
640:
641:        # Input Batch
642:        # NOTE(Chen): Ideally, we should initialize the input batch inside
643:        # `initialize_kv_cache` based on the kv cache config. However, as in
644:        # https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/18298, due to some unknown
645:        # reasons, we have to initialize the input batch before `load_model`,
646:        # quantization + weight offloading will fail otherwise. As a temporary
647:        # solution, we initialize the input batch here, and re-initialize it
648:        # in `initialize_kv_cache` if the block_sizes here is different from
649:        # the block_sizes in the kv cache config.
650:        logits_processors = model_config.logits_processors
651:        custom_logitsprocs: Sequence[str | type[LogitsProcessor]] = (
652:            tuple(logits_processors) if logits_processors is not None else ()
653:        )
654:        placeholder_block_size = (
655:            self.cache_config.block_size or CacheConfig.DEFAULT_BLOCK_SIZE
656:        )
657:        self._init_block_sizes = [placeholder_block_size]
658:        self._init_kernel_block_sizes = [placeholder_block_size]
659:        self.input_batch = InputBatch(
660:            max_num_reqs=self.max_num_reqs,
661:            # We need to use the encoder length for encoder-decoder
662:            # because of KV cache for cross-attention.
663:            max_model_len=max(self.max_model_len, self.max_encoder_len),
664:            max_num_batched_tokens=self.max_num_tokens,
665:            device=self.device,
666:            pin_memory=self.pin_memory,
667:            vocab_size=self.model_config.get_vocab_size(),
668:            block_sizes=[placeholder_block_size],
669:            kernel_block_sizes=[placeholder_block_size],
670:            num_spec_tokens=self.num_spec_tokens,
671:            logitsprocs=build_logitsprocs(
672:                self.vllm_config,
673:                self.device,
674:                self.pin_memory,
675:                self.is_pooling_model,
676:                custom_logitsprocs,
677:            ),
678:            # We currently don't know whether a particular custom logits processor
679:            # uses output token ids so we set this conservatively.
680:            # ThinkingTokenBudgetLogitsProcessor also needs output token ids to
681:            # correctly track think start/end token sequences in async scheduling.
682:            logitsprocs_need_output_token_ids=bool(custom_logitsprocs)
683:            or self.vllm_config.reasoning_config is not None,
684:            is_pooling_model=self.is_pooling_model,
685:            cp_kv_cache_interleave_size=self.parallel_config.cp_kv_cache_interleave_size,
686:            reasoning_config=self.vllm_config.reasoning_config,
687:        )
688:
689:        # Separate cuda stream for overlapping transfer of sampled token ids from
690:        # GPU to CPU when async scheduling is enabled.
691:        self.async_output_copy_stream: torch.cuda.Stream | None = None
692:        # cuda event to synchronize use of reused CPU tensors between steps
693:        # when async scheduling is enabled.
694:        self.prepare_inputs_event: torch.Event | None = None
695:        if self.use_async_scheduling:
696:            self.async_output_copy_stream = torch.cuda.Stream()
697:            self.prepare_inputs_event = torch.Event()
698:
699:        # self.cudagraph_batch_sizes sorts in ascending order.
700:        if (
701:            self.compilation_config.cudagraph_capture_sizes
702:            and self.compilation_config.cudagraph_mode != CUDAGraphMode.NONE
703:        ):
704:            self.cudagraph_batch_sizes = sorted(
705:                self.compilation_config.cudagraph_capture_sizes
706:            )
707:        else:
708:            self.cudagraph_batch_sizes = []
709:
710:        # Cache the device properties.
711:        self._init_device_properties()
712:
713:        # Encoder timing registry for observability
714:        self.encoder_timing_registry: dict[str, EncoderTimingStats] = {}
715:        self._encoder_timing_lock = threading.Lock()
716:
717:        # Persistent buffers for CUDA graphs.
718:        self.input_ids = self._make_buffer(self.max_num_tokens, dtype=torch.int32)
719:        self.positions = torch.zeros(
720:            self.max_num_tokens, dtype=torch.int64, device=self.device
721:        )
722:        self.query_start_loc = self._make_buffer(
723:            self.max_num_reqs + 1, dtype=torch.int32
724:        )
725:        self.seq_lens = torch.zeros(
726:            self.max_num_reqs, dtype=torch.int32, device=self.device
727:        )
728:        self.optimistic_seq_lens_cpu = torch.zeros(
729:            self.max_num_reqs, dtype=torch.int32, pin_memory=self.pin_memory
730:        )
731:        self.num_computed_tokens = torch.zeros(
732:            self.max_num_reqs, dtype=torch.int32, device=self.device
733:        )
734:        self.prev_num_draft_tokens = self._make_buffer(
735:            self.max_num_reqs, dtype=torch.int32
736:        )
737:        self.req_indices = self._make_buffer(self.max_num_tokens, dtype=torch.int64)
738:        # Maps current batch position -> previous batch position (-1 for new reqs)
739:        self.prev_positions = self._make_buffer(self.max_num_reqs, dtype=torch.int64)
740:        self.num_scheduled_tokens = self._make_buffer(
741:            self.max_num_reqs, dtype=torch.int32
742:        )
743:
744:        self.encoder_seq_lens = self._make_buffer(self.max_num_reqs, dtype=torch.int32)
745:        if self.dcp_world_size > 1:
746:            self.dcp_local_seq_lens = self._make_buffer(
747:                self.max_num_reqs, dtype=torch.int32
748:            )
749:        # Because inputs_embeds may be bfloat16 and we don't need a numpy
750:        # version of this tensor, avoid a RuntimeError by not creating a
751:        # numpy buffer.
752:        self.inputs_embeds = self._make_buffer(
753:            self.max_num_tokens, self.inputs_embeds_size, dtype=self.dtype, numpy=False
754:        )
755:        self.is_token_ids = self._make_buffer(self.max_num_tokens, dtype=torch.bool)
756:        self.discard_request_mask = self._make_buffer(
757:            self.max_num_reqs, dtype=torch.bool
758:        )
759:        self.num_decode_draft_tokens = self._make_buffer(
760:            self.max_num_reqs, dtype=torch.int32
761:        )
762:        self.num_accepted_tokens = self._make_buffer(
763:            self.max_num_reqs, dtype=torch.int32
764:        )
765:
766:        # Only relevant for models using M-RoPE (e.g, Qwen2-VL)
767:        if self.uses_mrope:
768:            # NOTE: `mrope_positions` is implemented with one additional dummy
769:            # position on purpose to make it non-contiguous so that it can work
770:            # with torch compile.
771:            # See detailed explanation in https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/12128#discussion_r1926431923
772:
773:            # NOTE: When M-RoPE is enabled, position ids are 3D regardless of
774:            # the modality of inputs. For text-only inputs, each dimension has
775:            # identical position IDs, making M-RoPE functionally equivalent to
776:            # 1D-RoPE.
777:            # See page 5 of https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.12191
778:            self.mrope_positions = self._make_buffer(
779:                (3, self.max_num_tokens + 1), dtype=torch.int64
780:            )
781:
782:        # Only relevant for models using XD-RoPE (e.g, HunYuan-VL)
783:        if self.uses_xdrope_dim > 0:
784:            # Similar to mrope but use assigned dimension number for RoPE, 4 as default.
785:            self.xdrope_positions = self._make_buffer(
786:                (self.uses_xdrope_dim, self.max_num_tokens + 1), dtype=torch.int64
787:            )
788:
789:        # None in the first PP rank. The rest are set after load_model.
790:        self.intermediate_tensors: IntermediateTensors | None = None
791:
792:        # OPTIMIZATION: Cache the arange tensors rather than creating them
793:        # every step. Keep in int64 to avoid overflow with long context.
794:        # - arange_np: immutable [0, 1, 2, ...] used as source for batched computation
795:        # - query_pos: CpuGpuBuffer for the computed batched arange result
796:        arange_size = max(self.max_num_reqs + 1, self.max_num_tokens)
797:        self.arange_np = np.arange(arange_size, dtype=np.int64)
798:        self.query_pos = self._make_buffer(arange_size, dtype=torch.int64)
799:        self._arange_scratch = np.empty(arange_size, dtype=np.int64)
800:
801:        # Layer pairings for cross-layer KV sharing.
802:        # If an Attention layer `layer_name` is in the keys of this dict, it
803:        # means this layer will perform attention using the keys and values
804:        # from the KV cache of `shared_kv_cache_layers[layer_name]`.
805:        self.shared_kv_cache_layers: dict[str, str] = {}
806:        self.kv_sharing_fast_prefill_eligible_layers: set[str] = set()
807:
808:        self.kv_sharing_fast_prefill_logits_indices = None
809:        if self.cache_config.kv_sharing_fast_prefill:
810:            self.kv_sharing_fast_prefill_logits_indices = torch.zeros(
811:                self.max_num_tokens, dtype=torch.int32, device=self.device
812:            )
813:
814:        self.uniform_decode_query_len = 1 + self.num_spec_tokens
815:
816:        # Cudagraph dispatcher for runtime cudagraph dispatching.
817:        self.cudagraph_dispatcher = CudagraphDispatcher(self.vllm_config)
818:
819:        self.mm_budget = (
820:            MultiModalBudget(self.vllm_config, self.mm_registry)
821:            if self.supports_mm_inputs
822:            else None
823:        )
824:
825:        self.reorder_batch_threshold: int | None = None
826:
827:        # Attention layers that are only in the KVCacheConfig of the runner
828:        # (e.g., KV sharing, encoder-only attention), but not in the
829:        # KVCacheConfig of the scheduler.
830:        self.runner_only_attn_layers: set[str] = set()
831:
832:        # Cached outputs.
833:        self._draft_token_ids: list[list[int]] | torch.Tensor | None = None
834:        self._draft_probs: torch.Tensor | None = None
835:        self._draft_prob_req_ids: list[str] | None = None
836:        # N-gram GPU path: async D2H buffer/event for per-request valid draft counts.
837:        self._num_valid_draft_tokens: torch.Tensor | None = None
838:        self._num_valid_draft_tokens_cpu: torch.Tensor | None = None
839:        self._num_valid_draft_tokens_event: torch.cuda.Event | None = None
840:        self._num_valid_draft_tokens_copy_stream: torch.cuda.Stream | None = None
841:        if (
842:            self.speculative_config is not None
843:            and self.speculative_config.use_ngram_gpu()
844:        ):
845:            self._num_valid_draft_tokens_cpu = torch.empty(
846:                self.max_num_reqs, dtype=torch.int32, pin_memory=self.pin_memory
847:            )
848:            self._num_valid_draft_tokens_event = torch.cuda.Event()
849:            self._num_valid_draft_tokens_copy_stream = torch.cuda.Stream()
850:
851:        self._draft_token_req_ids: list[str] | None = None
852:        self.transfer_event = torch.Event()
853:        self.sampled_token_ids_pinned_cpu = torch.empty(
854:            (self.max_num_reqs, 1),
855:            dtype=torch.int64,
856:            device="cpu",
857:            pin_memory=self.pin_memory,
858:        )
859:
860:        # Pre-allocated tensor for copying valid sampled token counts to CPU,
861:        # with dedicated stream for overlapping and event for coordination.
862:        self.valid_sampled_token_count_event: torch.Event | None = None
863:        self.valid_sampled_token_count_copy_stream: torch.cuda.Stream | None = None
864:        # We also copy the drafted tokens to the CPU asynchronously,
865:        # in case we need them for structured outputs.
866:        self.draft_token_ids_event: torch.Event | None = None
867:        self.draft_token_ids_copy_stream: torch.cuda.Stream | None = None
868:        self.valid_sampled_token_count_cpu: torch.Tensor | None = None
869:        self.draft_token_ids_cpu: torch.Tensor | None = None
870:        self.num_accepted_tokens_event: torch.Event | None = None
871:        if self.num_spec_tokens:
872:            self.draft_token_ids_event = torch.Event()
873:            self.num_accepted_tokens_event = torch.Event()
874:            self.draft_token_ids_copy_stream = torch.cuda.Stream()
875:            self.draft_token_ids_cpu = torch.empty(
876:                (self.max_num_reqs, self.num_spec_tokens),
877:                dtype=torch.int64,
878:                device="cpu",
879:                pin_memory=self.pin_memory,
880:            )
881:            if self.use_async_scheduling:
882:                self.valid_sampled_token_count_event = torch.Event()
883:                self.valid_sampled_token_count_copy_stream = torch.cuda.Stream()
884:                self.valid_sampled_token_count_cpu = torch.empty(
885:                    self.max_num_reqs,
886:                    dtype=torch.int32,
887:                    device="cpu",
888:                    pin_memory=self.pin_memory,
889:                )
890:
891:        # Model weight offloader
892:        # Make sure this is called before any get_offloader call
893:        set_offloader(create_offloader(self.offload_config))
894:
895:        # Ephemeral state transferred between execute_model() and sample_tokens().
896:        self.execute_model_state: ExecuteModelState | None = None
897:        self.kv_connector_output: KVConnectorOutput | None = None
898:        self.mamba_state_idx: dict[str, int] = {}
899:        self._mamba_bufs: mamba_utils.MambaBuffers | None = None
900:        self.mamba_prev_last_scheduled_idx: CpuGpuBuffer | None = None
901:        if self.cache_config.mamba_cache_mode == "all" and self.num_spec_tokens > 0:
902:            self.mamba_prev_last_scheduled_idx = self._make_buffer(
903:                self.max_num_reqs, dtype=torch.int32
904:            )
905:        self.layerwise_nvtx_hooks_registered = False
906:
907:    def update_max_model_len(self, max_model_len: int) -> None:
908:        self.max_model_len = max_model_len
909:        if self.speculative_config:
910:            draft_config = self.speculative_config.draft_model_config
911:            if draft_config is None or draft_config.max_model_len is None:
912:                self.effective_drafter_max_model_len = self.max_model_len
913:
914:    def reset_mm_cache(self) -> None:
915:        """
916:        Clear the multi-modal cache that was used during profiling,
917:        but no longer needed during inference.
918:        """
919:        if self.mm_budget:
920:            self.mm_budget.reset_cache()
921:        self.late_interaction_runner.clear()
922:
923:    def reset_encoder_cache(self) -> None:
924:        """Clear the GPU-side encoder cache storing vision embeddings.
925:
926:        This should be called when model weights are updated to ensure
927:        stale embeddings computed with old weights are not reused.
928:        """
929:        self.encoder_cache.clear()
930:        self.late_interaction_runner.clear()
931:
932:    def post_kv_cache_wake_up(self) -> None:
933:        self.init_fp8_kv_scales()
934:
935:    @torch.inference_mode()
936:    def init_fp8_kv_scales(self) -> None:
937:        """
938:        Re-initialize the KV cache and FP8 scales after waking from sleep.
939:        1. Zero out the KV cache tensors to remove garbage data from re-allocation.
940:        2. Reset Attention layer scaling factors (_k_scale, _v_scale) to 1.0.
941:          If these are left at 0.0 (default after wake_up), all KV cache values
942:          become effectively zero, causing gibberish output.
943:        """
944:        if not is_quantized_kv_cache(self.cache_config.cache_dtype):
945:            return
946:
947:        kv_caches = getattr(self, "kv_caches", [])
948:        for cache_tensor in kv_caches:
949:            if cache_tensor is not None:
950:                cache_tensor.zero_()
951:
952:        k_attr_names = ("_k_scale", "k_scale")
953:        v_attr_names = ("_v_scale", "v_scale")
954:
955:        attn_layers = self.compilation_config.static_forward_context
956:        for name, module in attn_layers.items():
957:            if isinstance(module, (Attention, MLAAttention)):
958:                # TODO: Generally, scale is 1.0 if user uses on-the-fly fp8
959:                # kvcache quant. However, to get better accuracy, compression
960:                # frameworks like llm-compressors allow users to tune the
961:                # scale. We may need to restore the specific calibrated scales
962:                # here in the future.
963:                k_scale_val, v_scale_val = 1.0, 1.0
964:
965:                # Processing K Scale
966:                for attr in k_attr_names:
967:                    if hasattr(module, attr):
968:                        param = getattr(module, attr)
969:                        if isinstance(param, torch.Tensor):
970:                            param.fill_(k_scale_val)
971:
972:                # Processing V Scale
973:                for attr in v_attr_names:
974:                    if hasattr(module, attr):
975:                        param = getattr(module, attr)
976:                        if isinstance(param, torch.Tensor):
977:                            param.fill_(v_scale_val)
978:
979:    def _get_positions(self, num_tokens: Any):
980:        if isinstance(num_tokens, int):
981:            if self.uses_mrope:
982:                return self.mrope_positions.gpu[:, :num_tokens]
983:            if self.uses_xdrope_dim > 0:
984:                return self.xdrope_positions.gpu[:, :num_tokens]
985:            return self.positions[:num_tokens]
986:        else:
987:            if self.uses_mrope:
988:                return self.mrope_positions.gpu[:, num_tokens]
989:            if self.uses_xdrope_dim > 0:
990:                return self.xdrope_positions.gpu[:, num_tokens]
991:            return self.positions[num_tokens]
992:
993:    def _make_buffer(
994:        self, *size: int | torch.SymInt, dtype: torch.dtype, numpy: bool = True
995:    ) -> CpuGpuBuffer:
996:        return CpuGpuBuffer(
997:            *size,
998:            dtype=dtype,
999:            device=self.device,
1000:            pin_memory=self.pin_memory,
1001:            with_numpy=numpy,
1002:        )
1003:
1004:    def _get_mamba_bufs(self) -> mamba_utils.MambaBuffers:
1005:        # Only reachable on the ``mamba_cache_mode == "align"`` path.
1006:        # The postprocess sub-object is additionally gated on spec
1007:        # decode + hybrid model.
1008:        assert self.cache_config.mamba_cache_mode == "align"
1009:        if self._mamba_bufs is None:
1010:            self._mamba_bufs = mamba_utils.MambaBuffers.create(
1011:                max_num_reqs=self.max_num_reqs,
1012:                kv_cache_config=self.kv_cache_config,
1013:                copy_funcs=self.model.get_mamba_state_copy_func(),
1014:                make_buffer=self._make_buffer,
1015:                device=self.device,
1016:                with_postprocess_align=(
1017:                    self.speculative_config is not None and self.model_config.is_hybrid
1018:                ),
1019:            )
1020:        return self._mamba_bufs
1021:
1022:    def _init_model_kwargs(self):
1023:        model_kwargs = dict[str, Any]()
1024:
1025:        if not self.is_pooling_model:
1026:            return model_kwargs
1027:
1028:        num_reqs = self.input_batch.num_reqs
1029:        pooling_params = self.input_batch.get_pooling_params()
1030:
1031:        token_type_id_requests = dict[int, Any]()
1032:        for i, param in enumerate(pooling_params):
1033:            if (
1034:                param.extra_kwargs is not None
1035:                and (token_types := param.extra_kwargs.get("compressed_token_type_ids"))
1036:                is not None
1037:            ):
1038:                token_type_id_requests[i] = token_types
1039:
1040:        if len(token_type_id_requests) == 0:
1041:            return model_kwargs
1042:
1043:        # Build ids on CPU using the CPU-resident upper bound for seq_lens;
1044:        # `torch.arange(seq_lens[i])` with a GPU scalar would force a sync.
1045:        seq_lens_cpu = self.optimistic_seq_lens_cpu[:num_reqs].tolist()
1046:        token_type_ids = []
1047:
1048:        for i in range(num_reqs):
1049:            seq_len_i = seq_lens_cpu[i]
1050:            pos = token_type_id_requests.get(i, seq_len_i)
1051:            ids = (torch.arange(seq_len_i) >= pos).int()
1052:            token_type_ids.append(ids)
1053:
1054:        token_type_ids_cpu = torch.empty(
1055:            sum(seq_lens_cpu), dtype=torch.int32, pin_memory=self.pin_memory
1056:        )
1057:        torch.cat(token_type_ids, out=token_type_ids_cpu)
1058:        model_kwargs["token_type_ids"] = token_type_ids_cpu.to(
1059:            device=self.device, non_blocking=True
1060:        )
1061:        return model_kwargs
1062:
1063:    def _may_reorder_batch(self, scheduler_output: "SchedulerOutput") -> None:
1064:        """
1065:        Update the order of requests in the batch based on the attention
1066:        backend's needs. For example, some attention backends (namely MLA) may
1067:        want to separate requests based on if the attention computation will be
1068:        compute-bound or memory-bound.
1069:
1070:        Args:
1071:            scheduler_output: The scheduler output.
1072:        """
1073:        # Attention free models have zero kv_cache_groups, however models
1074:        # like Mamba are also attention free but use the kv_cache for
1075:        # keeping its internal state. This is why we check the number
1076:        # of kv_cache groups instead of solely checking
1077:        # for self.model_config.is_attention_free.
1078:        if len(self.kv_cache_config.kv_cache_groups) == 0:
1079:            return
1080:
1081:        if self.reorder_batch_threshold is not None:
1082:            reorder_batch_to_split_decodes_and_prefills(
1083:                self.input_batch,
1084:                scheduler_output,
1085:                decode_threshold=self.reorder_batch_threshold,
1086:            )
1087:
1088:    def _init_kv_zero_meta(self) -> None:
1089:        """One-time precomputation for _zero_block_ids.
1090:
1091:        Called from gpu_worker.py outside the CuMem pool context.
1092:        """
1093:        self._kv_block_zeroer = KVBlockZeroer(
1094:            self.device,
1095:            self.pin_memory,
1096:            attn_groups_iter=self._kv_cache_spec_attn_group_iterator(),
1097:            kernel_block_sizes=self._kernel_block_sizes,
1098:            cache_dtype=self.cache_config.cache_dtype,
1099:            runner_only_attn_layers=self.runner_only_attn_layers,
1100:            static_forward_context=(self.compilation_config.static_forward_context),
1101:        )
1102:
1103:    def _zero_block_ids(self, block_ids: list[int]) -> None:
1104:        """Zero the KV cache memory for the given block IDs."""
1105:        if hasattr(self, "_kv_block_zeroer"):
1106:            self._kv_block_zeroer.zero_block_ids(block_ids)
1107:
1108:    # Note: used for model runner override.
1109:    def _init_device_properties(self) -> None:
1110:        """Initialize attributes from torch.cuda.get_device_properties"""
1111:
1112:        self.num_sms = num_compute_units(self.device.index)
1113:
1114:    # Note: used for model runner override.
1115:    def _sync_device(self) -> None:
1116:        torch.accelerator.synchronize()
1117:
1118:    def _get_or_create_async_output_copy_stream(self) -> torch.cuda.Stream:
1119:        stream = self.async_output_copy_stream
1120:        if stream is None:
1121:            stream = torch.cuda.Stream()
1122:            self.async_output_copy_stream = stream
1123:        return stream
1124:
1125:    def _update_states(self, scheduler_output: "SchedulerOutput") -> Callable | None:
1126:        """Update the cached states and the persistent batch with the scheduler
1127:        output.
1128:
1129:        The updated states are used by the `_prepare_inputs` function to create
1130:        the input GPU tensors for the model.
1131:
1132:        The SamplingMetadata is updated and copied to the GPU if there is a
1133:        new/resumed/paused/finished request in the batch.
1134:        """
1135:        # Remove finished requests from the cached states.
1136:        for req_id in scheduler_output.finished_req_ids:
1137:            self.requests.pop(req_id, None)
1138:            self.num_prompt_logprobs.pop(req_id, None)
1139:        self.late_interaction_runner.on_requests_finished(
1140:            scheduler_output.finished_req_ids
1141:        )
1142:        # Remove the finished requests from the persistent batch.
1143:        # NOTE(woosuk): There could be an edge case where finished_req_ids and
1144:        # scheduled_req_ids overlap. This happens when a request is aborted and
1145:        # then resubmitted with the same ID. In this case, we treat them as two
1146:        # distinct requests - clearing the cached states for the first request
1147:        # and handling the second as a new request.
1148:        for req_id in scheduler_output.finished_req_ids:
1149:            self.input_batch.remove_request(req_id)
1150:
1151:        # Zero GPU memory for freshly allocated cache blocks to prevent
1152:        # stale NaN/data from corrupting attention or SSM computation.
1153:        if scheduler_output.new_block_ids_to_zero:
1154:            self._zero_block_ids(scheduler_output.new_block_ids_to_zero)
1155:
1156:        # Free the cached encoder outputs.
1157:        for mm_hash in scheduler_output.free_encoder_mm_hashes:
1158:            self.encoder_cache.pop(mm_hash, None)
1159:
1160:        # Remove the unscheduled requests from the persistent batch.
1161:        # NOTE(woosuk): The unscheduled requests are either preempted requests
1162:        # or running requests that are not scheduled in this step. We remove
1163:        # them from the persistent batch but keep their cached states since
1164:        # they will be scheduled again sometime in the future.
1165:        scheduled_req_ids = scheduler_output.num_scheduled_tokens.keys()
1166:        cached_req_ids = self.input_batch.req_id_to_index.keys()
1167:        resumed_req_ids = scheduler_output.scheduled_cached_reqs.resumed_req_ids
1168:        # NOTE(zhuohan): cached_req_ids and resumed_req_ids are usually disjoint,
1169:        # so `(scheduled_req_ids - resumed_req_ids) == scheduled_req_ids` holds
1170:        # apart from the forced-preemption case in reset_prefix_cache. And in
1171:        # that case we include the resumed_req_ids in the unscheduled set so
1172:        # that they get cleared from the persistent batch before being re-scheduled
1173:        # in the normal resumed request path.
1174:        unscheduled_req_ids = cached_req_ids - (scheduled_req_ids - resumed_req_ids)
1175:        # NOTE(woosuk): The persistent batch optimization assumes that
1176:        # consecutive batches contain mostly the same requests. If batches
1177:        # have low request overlap (e.g., alternating between two distinct
1178:        # sets of requests), this optimization becomes very inefficient.
1179:        for req_id in unscheduled_req_ids:
1180:            self.input_batch.remove_request(req_id)
1181:
1182:        is_ngram_gpu = (
1183:            self.speculative_config is not None
1184:            and self.speculative_config.use_ngram_gpu()
1185:        )
1186:        if is_ngram_gpu:
1187:            ngram_gpu_new_reqs: list[CachedRequestState] = []
1188:
1189:        reqs_to_add: list[CachedRequestState] = []
1190:        deferred_spec_decode_corrections = []
1191:
1192:        # Add new requests to the cached states.
1193:        for new_req_data in scheduler_output.scheduled_new_reqs:
1194:            req_id = new_req_data.req_id
1195:            if req_id in self.requests:
1196:                # For streaming case only.
1197:                req_state = self._update_streaming_request(req_id, new_req_data)
1198:                reqs_to_add.append(req_state)
1199:                continue
1200:
1201:            sampling_params = new_req_data.sampling_params
1202:            pooling_params = new_req_data.pooling_params
1203:
1204:            if (
1205:                sampling_params
1206:                and sampling_params.sampling_type == SamplingType.RANDOM_SEED
1207:            ):
1208:                generator = torch.Generator(device=self.device)
1209:                generator.manual_seed(sampling_params.seed)
1210:            else:
1211:                generator = None
1212:
1213:            if self.is_pooling_model:
1214:                assert pooling_params is not None
1215:                task = pooling_params.task
1216:                assert task is not None, "You did not set `task` in the API"
1217:
1218:                model = cast(VllmModelForPooling, self.get_model())
1219:                to_update = model.pooler.get_pooling_updates(task)
1220:                to_update.apply(pooling_params)
1221:
1222:            req_state = CachedRequestState(
1223:                req_id=req_id,
1224:                prompt_token_ids=new_req_data.prompt_token_ids,
1225:                prompt_embeds=new_req_data.prompt_embeds,
1226:                prompt_is_token_ids=new_req_data.prompt_is_token_ids,
1227:                mm_features=new_req_data.mm_features,
1228:                sampling_params=sampling_params,
1229:                pooling_params=pooling_params,
1230:                generator=generator,
1231:                block_ids=new_req_data.block_ids,
1232:                num_computed_tokens=new_req_data.num_computed_tokens,
1233:                output_token_ids=[],
1234:                lora_request=new_req_data.lora_request,
1235:            )
1236:            self.requests[req_id] = req_state
1237:            self.late_interaction_runner.register_request(req_id, pooling_params)
1238:
1239:            if sampling_params and sampling_params.prompt_logprobs is not None:
1240:                self.num_prompt_logprobs[req_id] = (
1241:                    self.input_batch.vocab_size
1242:                    if sampling_params.prompt_logprobs == -1
1243:                    else sampling_params.prompt_logprobs
1244:                )
1245:
1246:            # Only relevant for models using M-RoPE (e.g, Qwen2-VL)
1247:            if self.uses_mrope:
1248:                self._init_mrope_positions(req_state)
1249:
1250:            # Only relevant for models using XD-RoPE (e.g, HunYuan-VL)
1251:            if self.uses_xdrope_dim > 0:
1252:                self._init_xdrope_positions(req_state)
1253:
1254:            reqs_to_add.append(req_state)
1255:            # Track new requests for ngram_gpu full tensor copy
1256:            if is_ngram_gpu:
1257:                ngram_gpu_new_reqs.append(req_state)
1258:
1259:        # Update the states of the running/resumed requests.
1260:        is_last_rank = get_pp_group().is_last_rank
1261:        req_data = scheduler_output.scheduled_cached_reqs
1262:        scheduled_spec_tokens = scheduler_output.scheduled_spec_decode_tokens
1263:
1264:        # Save scheduler-allocated spec lengths before trimming so
1265:        # prev_num_draft_len keeps the optimistic count for rejection correction.
1266:        original_num_spec_per_req: dict[str, int] = {}
1267:        if (
1268:            self.speculative_config is not None
1269:            and self.speculative_config.use_ngram_gpu()
1270:        ):
1271:            for req_id, toks in scheduled_spec_tokens.items():
1272:                original_num_spec_per_req[req_id] = len(toks)
1273:            update_scheduler_for_invalid_drafts(
1274:                self._num_valid_draft_tokens_event,
1275:                self._num_valid_draft_tokens_cpu,
1276:                scheduler_output,
1277:                self.input_batch.req_id_to_index,
1278:            )
1279:        if self.use_async_spec_decode:
1280:            self.prev_num_draft_tokens.np.fill(0)
1281:
1282:        for i, req_id in enumerate(req_data.req_ids):
1283:            req_state = self.requests[req_id]
1284:            num_computed_tokens = req_data.num_computed_tokens[i]
1285:            new_block_ids = req_data.new_block_ids[i]
1286:            resumed_from_preemption = req_id in req_data.resumed_req_ids
1287:            num_output_tokens = req_data.num_output_tokens[i]
1288:            req_index = self.input_batch.req_id_to_index.get(req_id)
1289:
1290:            if req_state.prev_num_draft_len and self.use_async_scheduling:
1291:                # prev_num_draft_len is used in async scheduling mode with
1292:                # spec decode. it indicates if need to update num_computed_tokens
1293:                # of the request. for example:
1294:                # first step: num_computed_tokens = 0, spec_tokens = [],
1295:                # prev_num_draft_len = 0.
1296:                # second step: num_computed_tokens = 100(prompt length),
1297:                # spec_tokens = [a,b], prev_num_draft_len = 0.
1298:                # third step: num_computed_tokens = 100 + 2, spec_tokens = [c,d],
1299:                # prev_num_draft_len = 2.
1300:                # num_computed_tokens in first step and second step doesn't contain
1301:                # the spec tokens length, but in third step it contains the
1302:                # spec tokens length. we only need to update num_computed_tokens
1303:                # when prev_num_draft_len > 0.
1304:                if req_index is None:
1305:                    req_state.prev_num_draft_len = 0
1306:                else:
1307:                    # Optimistically assume all accepted; queue up a correction
1308:                    # to be called after the model forward to preserve async
1309:                    # scheduling. Corrected on GPU in _prepare_inputs.
1310:                    optimistic_num_accepted = req_state.prev_num_draft_len
1311:                    req_state.output_token_ids.extend([-1] * optimistic_num_accepted)
1312:
1313:                    deferred_spec_decode_corrections.append(
1314:                        (req_id, optimistic_num_accepted, req_state)
1315:                    )
1316:
1317:                    prev_req_index = (
1318:                        self.input_batch.prev_req_id_to_index.get(req_id)
1319:                        if self.input_batch.prev_req_id_to_index
1320:                        else None
1321:                    )
1322:                    if prev_req_index is not None:
1323:                        self.prev_num_draft_tokens.np[prev_req_index] = (
1324:                            optimistic_num_accepted
1325:                        )
1326:
1327:                    if is_ngram_gpu and optimistic_num_accepted > 0:
1328:                        self.input_batch.num_tokens_no_spec[req_index] += (
1329:                            optimistic_num_accepted
1330:                        )
1331:
1332:            # Update the cached states.
1333:            req_state.num_computed_tokens = num_computed_tokens
1334:
1335:            if not is_last_rank:
1336:                if not req_data.new_token_ids:
1337:                    # Async scheduled PP: Sampled tokens propagated via GPU broadcast.
1338:                    new_token_ids: list[int] = []
1339:                else:
1340:                    # Non-async scheduling with PP: The scheduler sends
1341:                    # sampled token ids back because there's no direct communication
1342:                    # between the first-stage worker and the last-stage worker.
1343:                    new_token_ids = req_data.new_token_ids[i]
1344:                    # Add the sampled token(s) from the previous step (if any).
1345:                    # This doesn't include "unverified" tokens like spec tokens.
1346:                    num_new_tokens = (
1347:                        num_computed_tokens + len(new_token_ids) - req_state.num_tokens
1348:                    )
1349:                    if num_new_tokens == 1:
1350:                        # Avoid slicing list in most common case.
1351:                        req_state.output_token_ids.append(new_token_ids[-1])
1352:                    elif num_new_tokens > 0:
1353:                        req_state.output_token_ids.extend(
1354:                            new_token_ids[-num_new_tokens:]
1355:                        )
1356:            elif num_output_tokens < len(req_state.output_token_ids):
1357:                # Some output tokens were discarded due to a sync-KV-load
1358:                # failure, or output_token_ids was inflated by the optimistic
1359:                # extend above (async spec decode). Align the cached state.
1360:                del req_state.output_token_ids[num_output_tokens:]
1361:                if req_index is not None:
1362:                    end_idx = (
1363:                        self.input_batch.num_prompt_tokens[req_index]
1364:                        + num_output_tokens
1365:                    )
1366:                    self.input_batch.num_tokens_no_spec[req_index] = end_idx
1367:
1368:            # Update the block IDs.
1369:            if not resumed_from_preemption:
1370:                if new_block_ids is not None:
1371:                    # Append the new blocks to the existing block IDs.
1372:                    for block_ids, new_ids in zip(req_state.block_ids, new_block_ids):
1373:                        block_ids.extend(new_ids)
1374:            else:
1375:                assert req_index is None
1376:                assert new_block_ids is not None
1377:                # The request is resumed from preemption.
1378:                # Replace the existing block IDs with the new ones.
1379:                req_state.block_ids = new_block_ids
1380:
1381:            if req_index is None:
1382:                # The request is not in the persistent batch.
1383:                # The request was either preempted and resumed later, or was not
1384:                # scheduled in the previous step and needs to be added again.
1385:
1386:                if self.use_async_scheduling and num_output_tokens > 0:
1387:                    # We must recover the output token ids for resumed requests in the
1388:                    # async scheduling case, so that correct input_ids are obtained.
1389:                    resumed_token_ids = req_data.all_token_ids[req_id]
1390:                    req_state.output_token_ids = resumed_token_ids[-num_output_tokens:]
1391:
1392:                reqs_to_add.append(req_state)
1393:                # Track resumed requests for ngram_gpu full tensor copy
1394:                if is_ngram_gpu:
1395:                    ngram_gpu_new_reqs.append(req_state)
1396:                continue
1397:
1398:            # Update the persistent batch.
1399:            self.input_batch.num_computed_tokens_cpu[req_index] = num_computed_tokens
1400:            if new_block_ids is not None:
1401:                self.input_batch.block_table.append_row(new_block_ids, req_index)
1402:
1403:            # For the last rank, we don't need to update the token_ids_cpu
1404:            # because the sampled tokens are already cached.
1405:            if not is_last_rank:
1406:                start_token_index = self.input_batch.num_tokens_no_spec[req_index]
1407:                # For chunked prefill, num_computed_tokens may less
1408:                # than num_tokens_no_spec.
1409:                # Async scheduled PP: no new_token_ids, advance num_tokens_no_spec
1410:                # according to num_computed_tokens.
1411:                end_token_index = max(
1412:                    start_token_index,
1413:                    num_computed_tokens + len(new_token_ids),
1414:                )
1415:                if end_token_index > start_token_index:
1416:                    if new_token_ids:
1417:                        # Add new_token_ids to token_ids_cpu.
1418:                        num_new_tokens = end_token_index - start_token_index
1419:                        tokens_to_append = new_token_ids[-num_new_tokens:]
1420:                        self.input_batch.token_ids_cpu[
1421:                            req_index, start_token_index:end_token_index
1422:                        ] = tokens_to_append
1423:                    self.input_batch.is_token_ids[
1424:                        req_index, start_token_index:end_token_index
1425:                    ] = True
1426:                    self.input_batch.num_tokens_no_spec[req_index] = end_token_index
1427:
1428:            # Add spec_token_ids to token_ids_cpu.
1429:            self.input_batch.update_req_spec_token_ids(req_state, scheduled_spec_tokens)
1430:            # Restore scheduler-side draft count after ngram trimming.
1431:            if original_num_spec_per_req:
1432:                orig = original_num_spec_per_req.get(req_id, 0)
1433:                if orig != req_state.prev_num_draft_len:
1434:                    req_state.prev_num_draft_len = orig
1435:
1436:        # Add the new or resumed requests to the persistent batch.
1437:        # The smaller empty indices are filled first.
1438:        for request in reqs_to_add:
1439:            self.input_batch.add_request(request)
1440:            self.input_batch.update_req_spec_token_ids(request, scheduled_spec_tokens)
1441:
1442:        # Condense the batched states if there are gaps left by removed requests
1443:        self.input_batch.condense()
1444:        # Allow attention backend to reorder the batch, potentially
1445:        self._may_reorder_batch(scheduler_output)
1446:        # Refresh batch metadata with any pending updates.
1447:        self.input_batch.refresh_metadata()
1448:
1449:        # Incrementally update ngram_gpu tensors after batch is stable
1450:        if is_ngram_gpu:
1451:            update_ngram_gpu_tensors_incremental(
1452:                self.input_batch,
1453:                self.token_ids_gpu_tensor,
1454:                self.num_tokens_no_spec_gpu,
1455:                ngram_gpu_new_reqs,
1456:                self.device,
1457:                _pinned_idx_buf=self._ngram_pinned_idx_buf,
1458:                _pinned_val_buf=self._ngram_pinned_val_buf,
1459:            )
1460:
1461:        if deferred_spec_decode_corrections:
1462:
1463:            def correct_spec_decode_token_counts():
1464:                valid_sampled_token_count = self._get_valid_sampled_token_count()
1465:                if not valid_sampled_token_count:
1466:                    return
1467:                prev_req_id_to_index = self.input_batch.prev_req_id_to_index
1468:                if not prev_req_id_to_index:
1469:                    return
1470:                for (
1471:                    req_id,
1472:                    optimistic_num_accepted,
1473:                    req_state,
1474:                ) in deferred_spec_decode_corrections:
1475:                    prev_req_index = prev_req_id_to_index.get(req_id)
1476:                    if prev_req_index is None:
1477:                        continue
1478:                    num_accepted = valid_sampled_token_count[prev_req_index] - 1
1479:                    correction = optimistic_num_accepted - num_accepted
1480:                    req_state.num_computed_tokens -= correction
1481:                    cur_req_index = self.input_batch.req_id_to_index.get(req_id)
1482:                    if cur_req_index is None:
1483:                        continue
1484:                    self.input_batch.num_computed_tokens_cpu[cur_req_index] -= (
1485:                        correction
1486:                    )
1487:                    if is_ngram_gpu and correction > 0:
1488:                        self.input_batch.num_tokens_no_spec[cur_req_index] -= correction
1489:                        self.num_tokens_no_spec_gpu[cur_req_index] -= correction
1490:
1491:            return correct_spec_decode_token_counts
1492:        else:
1493:            return None
1494:
1495:    def _update_states_after_model_execute(
1496:        self, output_token_ids: torch.Tensor, scheduler_output: "SchedulerOutput"
1497:    ) -> None:
1498:        """Update the cached states after model execution.
1499:
1500:        This is used for MTP/EAGLE for hybrid models, as in linear attention,
1501:        only the last token's state is kept. In MTP/EAGLE, for draft tokens
1502:        the state are kept util we decide how many tokens are accepted for
1503:        each sequence, and a shifting is done during the next iteration
1504:        based on the number of accepted tokens.
1505:        """
1506:        if not self.speculative_config or not self.model_config.is_hybrid:
1507:            return
1508:
1509:        # Count the number of accepted tokens for each sequence.
1510:        # Valid tokens are contiguous from position 0, so counting non-(-1)
1511:        # tokens gives us the first -1 position (i.e., number of accepted).
1512:        num_reqs = output_token_ids.size(0)
1513:        self.num_accepted_tokens.gpu[:num_reqs] = (output_token_ids != -1).sum(dim=1)
1514:
1515:        if self.cache_config.mamba_cache_mode == "align":
1516:            # Fused GPU postprocess: state copies + per-request accepted-token
1517:            # update without CPU-GPU sync. The metadata
1518:            # (num_scheduled_tokens, num_draft_tokens, num_computed_tokens) is
1519:            # pre-staged to GPU buffers in _prepare_inputs.
1520:            mamba_utils.postprocess_mamba_align_gpu(
1521:                bufs=self._get_mamba_bufs(),
1522:                num_reqs=num_reqs,
1523:                num_accepted_tokens_gpu=self.num_accepted_tokens.gpu,
1524:                num_accepted_tokens_cpu_tensor=(
1525:                    self.input_batch.num_accepted_tokens_cpu_tensor
1526:                ),
1527:                input_batch=self.input_batch,
1528:                kv_cache_config=self.kv_cache_config,
1529:                forward_context=self.compilation_config.static_forward_context,
1530:                mamba_state_copy_funcs=self.model.get_mamba_state_copy_func(),
1531:            )
1532:
1533:            assert self.num_accepted_tokens_event is not None
1534:            self.num_accepted_tokens_event.record()
1535:        else:
1536:            self.input_batch.num_accepted_tokens_cpu_tensor[:num_reqs].copy_(
1537:                self.num_accepted_tokens.gpu[:num_reqs], non_blocking=True
1538:            )
1539:            assert self.num_accepted_tokens_event is not None
1540:            self.num_accepted_tokens_event.record()
1541:
1542:            if self.cache_config.mamba_cache_mode == "all":
1543:                mamba_utils.postprocess_mamba_all(
1544:                    scheduler_output,
1545:                    self.kv_cache_config,
1546:                    self.input_batch,
1547:                    self.requests,
1548:                    self.mamba_state_idx,
1549:                    self.num_spec_tokens,
1550:                    num_reqs,
1551:                )
1552:
1553:    def _update_streaming_request(
1554:        self, req_id: str, new_req_data: NewRequestData
1555:    ) -> CachedRequestState:
1556:        """Updates streaming session request from `scheduled_new_reqs`.
1557:
1558:        Removes the request from InputBatch (if present), updates the cached
1559:        state, and prepares it for re-addition to the batch.
1560:
1561:        NOTE: prompt_token_ids includes intermediate output tokens - tokens
1562:        previously generated but now are input context (part of the prompt).
1563:        """
1564:        self.input_batch.remove_request(req_id)
1565:        req_state = self.requests[req_id]
1566:
1567:        req_state.prompt_token_ids = new_req_data.prompt_token_ids
1568:        req_state.mm_features = new_req_data.mm_features
1569:        req_state.prompt_embeds = new_req_data.prompt_embeds
1570:        req_state.sampling_params = new_req_data.sampling_params
1571:        req_state.pooling_params = new_req_data.pooling_params
1572:        self.late_interaction_runner.register_request(req_id, req_state.pooling_params)
1573:        req_state.block_ids = new_req_data.block_ids
1574:        req_state.num_computed_tokens = new_req_data.num_computed_tokens
1575:        req_state.num_prompt_tokens = length_from_prompt_token_ids_or_embeds(
1576:            req_state.prompt_token_ids, req_state.prompt_embeds
1577:        )
1578:
1579:        # Clear `output_token_ids` as previous output tokens are now part of
1580:        # `prompt_token_ids`.
1581:        req_state.output_token_ids.clear()
1582:
1583:        if self.uses_mrope:
1584:            self._init_mrope_positions(req_state)
1585:
1586:        return req_state
1587:
1588:    def _init_mrope_positions(self, req_state: CachedRequestState):
1589:        model = self.get_model()
1590:        assert supports_mrope(model), "M-RoPE support is not implemented."
1591:        assert req_state.prompt_token_ids is not None, (
1592:            "M-RoPE requires prompt_token_ids to be available."
1593:        )
1594:        mrope_model = cast(SupportsMRoPE, model)
1595:
1596:        # `prompt_embeds` is a passthrough modality (no grid_thw), models'
1597:        # M-RoPE code assumes per-feature grid info, so filter it out. The
1598:        # prompt_embeds positions are treated as text positions for M-RoPE.
1599:        mrope_features = [
1600:            f for f in req_state.mm_features if f.modality != "prompt_embeds"
1601:        ]
1602:        req_state.mrope_positions, req_state.mrope_position_delta = (
1603:            mrope_model.get_mrope_input_positions(
1604:                req_state.prompt_token_ids,
1605:                mrope_features,
1606:            )
1607:        )
1608:
1609:    def _init_xdrope_positions(self, req_state: CachedRequestState):
1610:        model = self.get_model()
1611:        xdrope_model = cast(SupportsXDRoPE, model)
1612:        assert req_state.prompt_token_ids is not None, (
1613:            "XD-RoPE requires prompt_token_ids to be available."
1614:        )
1615:        assert supports_xdrope(model), "XD-RoPE support is not implemented."
1616:
1617:        req_state.xdrope_positions = xdrope_model.get_xdrope_input_positions(
1618:            req_state.prompt_token_ids,
1619:            req_state.mm_features,
1620:        )
1621:
1622:    def _extract_mm_kwargs(
1623:        self,
1624:        scheduler_output: "SchedulerOutput",
1625:    ) -> BatchedTensorInputs:
1626:        if not scheduler_output or not self.is_multimodal_raw_input_only_model:
1627:            return {}
1628:
1629:        mm_kwargs = list[tuple[str, MultiModalKwargsItem]]()
1630:        for req in scheduler_output.scheduled_new_reqs:
1631:            for feature in req.mm_features:
1632:                if feature.data is not None:
1633:                    mm_kwargs.append((feature.modality, feature.data))
1634:
1635:        # Input all modalities at once
1636:        mm_kwargs_combined: BatchedTensorInputs = {}
1637:        for _, _, mm_kwargs_batch in group_and_batch_mm_kwargs(
1638:            mm_kwargs,
1639:            device=self.device,
1640:            pin_memory=self.pin_memory,
1641:        ):
1642:            mm_kwargs_combined.update(mm_kwargs_batch)
1643:
1644:        return mm_kwargs_combined
1645:
1646:    def _dummy_mm_kwargs(self, num_seqs: int) -> BatchedTensorInputs:
1647:        if not self.is_multimodal_raw_input_only_model:
1648:            return {}
1649:
1650:        mm_budget = self.mm_budget
1651:        assert mm_budget is not None
1652:
1653:        if not mm_budget.mm_max_toks_per_item:
1654:            return {}  # No tower modalities (embed-only mode)
1655:
1656:        dummy_modality = mm_budget.get_modality_with_max_tokens()
1657:        return self._get_mm_dummy_batch(dummy_modality, num_seqs)
1658:
1659:    def _get_cumsum_and_arange(
1660:        self,
1661:        num_tokens: np.ndarray,
1662:        arange_out: np.ndarray,
1663:        cumsum_dtype: np.dtype | None = None,
1664:    ) -> np.ndarray:
1665:        """Get the cumulative sum and batched arange of the given array.
1666:        E.g., [2, 5, 3] -> [2, 7, 10], arange written to
1667:        arange_out[:10] as [0, 1, 0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 0, 1, 2].
1668:        Equivalent to but faster than:
1669:        np.concatenate([np.arange(n) for n in num_tokens])
1670:        """
1671:        # Step 1. [2, 5, 3] -> [2, 7, 10]
1672:        cu_num_tokens = np.cumsum(num_tokens, dtype=cumsum_dtype)
1673:        total_num_tokens = cu_num_tokens[-1]
1674:        # Step 2. [2, 7, 10] -> [0, 0, 2, 2, 2, 2, 2, 7, 7, 7]
1675:        cumsums_offsets = np.repeat(cu_num_tokens - num_tokens, num_tokens)
1676:        # Step 3. [0, 1, 0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 0, 1, 2]
1677:        np.subtract(
1678:            self.arange_np[:total_num_tokens],
1679:            cumsums_offsets,
1680:            out=arange_out[:total_num_tokens],
1681:        )
1682:
1683:        return cu_num_tokens
1684:
1685:    def _compute_prev_positions(self, num_reqs: int) -> None:
1686:        """Build prev_positions mapping: current pos -> previous pos (-1 if new).
1687:
1688:        Populates self.prev_positions.np[:num_reqs] with the mapping.
1689:        """
1690:        prev_req_id_to_index = self.input_batch.prev_req_id_to_index
1691:        prev_positions = self.prev_positions.np[:num_reqs]
1692:
1693:        if not prev_req_id_to_index:
1694:            prev_positions.fill(-1)
1695:            return
1696:
1697:        for i, req_id in enumerate(self.input_batch.req_ids[:num_reqs]):
1698:            prev_positions[i] = prev_req_id_to_index.get(req_id, -1)
1699:
1700:    def _prepare_input_ids(
1701:        self,
1702:        scheduler_output: "SchedulerOutput",
1703:        num_reqs: int,
1704:        total_num_scheduled_tokens: int,
1705:        cu_num_tokens: np.ndarray,
1706:    ) -> None:
1707:        """Prepare the input IDs for the current batch.
1708:
1709:        Carefully handles the `prev_sampled_token_ids` which can be cached
1710:        from the previous engine iteration, in which case those tokens on the
1711:        GPU need to be copied into the corresponding slots into input_ids.
1712:
1713:        Uses self.prev_positions[:num_reqs] which maps current pos -> prev pos
1714:        (-1 for new requests).
1715:        """
1716:
1717:        if self.input_batch.prev_sampled_token_ids is None:
1718:            # Normal scheduling case
1719:            self.input_ids.copy_to_gpu(total_num_scheduled_tokens)
1720:            if self.enable_prompt_embeds:
1721:                self.inputs_embeds.copy_to_gpu(total_num_scheduled_tokens)
1722:                self.is_token_ids.copy_to_gpu(total_num_scheduled_tokens)
1723:            return
1724:
1725:        # Async scheduling case, where some decode requests from the previous
1726:        # iteration won't have entries in input_ids_cpu and need to be copied
1727:        # on the GPU from prev_sampled_token_ids.
1728:        prev_positions = self.prev_positions.np[:num_reqs]
1729:        scheduled_spec_tokens = scheduler_output.scheduled_spec_decode_tokens
1730:        sample_flattened_indices: list[int] = []
1731:        spec_flattened_indices: list[int] = []
1732:        prev_draft_token_indices: list[int] = []
1733:        prev_indices: list[int] = []
1734:        common_indices_match = True
1735:        max_flattened_index = -1
1736:        total_num_spec_tokens = 0
1737:
1738:        for cur_index in range(num_reqs):
1739:            prev_index = prev_positions[cur_index]
1740:            if prev_index < 0:
1741:                continue
1742:            prev_indices.append(prev_index)
1743:            req_id = self.input_batch.req_ids[cur_index]
1744:            # We need to compute the flattened input_ids index of the
1745:            # last token in each common request.
1746:            draft_len = len(scheduled_spec_tokens.get(req_id, ()))
1747:            total_num_spec_tokens += draft_len
1748:            flattened_index = cu_num_tokens[cur_index].item() - 1
1749:            # example: cu_num_tokens = [2, 5, 8], draft_tokens = [1, 2, 2]
1750:            # sample_flattened_indices = [0, 2, 5]
1751:            # spec_flattened_indices = [1,   3, 4,    6, 7]
1752:            sample_flattened_indices.append(flattened_index - draft_len)
1753:            spec_flattened_indices.extend(
1754:                range(flattened_index - draft_len + 1, flattened_index + 1)
1755:            )
1756:            start = prev_index * self.num_spec_tokens
1757:            # prev_draft_token_indices is used to find which draft_tokens_id
1758:            # should be copied to input_ids
1759:            # example: prev draft_tokens_id [[1,2], [3,4], [5, 6]]
1760:            # flatten draft_tokens_id [1,2,3,4,5,6]
1761:            # draft_len of each request [1, 2, 1]
1762:            # then prev_draft_token_indices is [0,   2, 3,   4]
1763:            prev_draft_token_indices.extend(range(start, start + draft_len))
1764:            common_indices_match &= prev_index == flattened_index
1765:            max_flattened_index = max(max_flattened_index, flattened_index)
1766:
1767:        num_common_tokens = len(sample_flattened_indices)
1768:        total_without_spec = total_num_scheduled_tokens - total_num_spec_tokens
1769:        if num_common_tokens < total_without_spec:
1770:            # If not all requests are decodes from the last iteration,
1771:            # we need to copy the input_ids_cpu to the GPU first.
1772:            self.input_ids.copy_to_gpu(total_num_scheduled_tokens)
1773:            if self.enable_prompt_embeds:
1774:                self.inputs_embeds.copy_to_gpu(total_num_scheduled_tokens)
1775:                self.is_token_ids.copy_to_gpu(total_num_scheduled_tokens)
1776:        if num_common_tokens == 0:
1777:            # No requests in common with the previous iteration
1778:            # So input_ids.cpu will have all the input ids.
1779:            return
1780:        if common_indices_match and max_flattened_index == (num_common_tokens - 1):
1781:            # Common-case optimization: the batch is unchanged
1782:            # and no reordering happened.
1783:            # The indices are both the same permutation of 0..N-1 so
1784:            # we can copy directly using a single slice.
1785:            self.input_ids.gpu[:num_common_tokens].copy_(
1786:                self.input_batch.prev_sampled_token_ids[:num_common_tokens, 0],
1787:                non_blocking=True,
1788:            )
1789:            return
1790:        # Upload the index tensors asynchronously so the scatter can be non-blocking.
1791:        sampled_tokens_index_tensor = torch.tensor(
1792:            sample_flattened_indices, dtype=torch.int64, pin_memory=self.pin_memory
1793:        ).to(self.device, non_blocking=True)
1794:        prev_common_req_indices_tensor = torch.tensor(
1795:            prev_indices, dtype=torch.int64, pin_memory=self.pin_memory
1796:        ).to(self.device, non_blocking=True)
1797:        self.input_ids.gpu.scatter_(
1798:            dim=0,
1799:            index=sampled_tokens_index_tensor,
1800:            src=self.input_batch.prev_sampled_token_ids[
1801:                prev_common_req_indices_tensor, 0
1802:            ],
1803:        )
1804:
1805:        # Scatter the draft tokens after the sampled tokens are scattered.
1806:        if self._draft_token_ids is None or not spec_flattened_indices:
1807:            return
1808:
1809:        assert isinstance(self._draft_token_ids, torch.Tensor)
1810:        draft_tokens_index_tensor = torch.tensor(
1811:            spec_flattened_indices, dtype=torch.int64, pin_memory=self.pin_memory
1812:        ).to(self.device, non_blocking=True)
1813:        prev_draft_token_indices_tensor = torch.tensor(
1814:            prev_draft_token_indices, dtype=torch.int64, pin_memory=self.pin_memory
1815:        ).to(self.device, non_blocking=True)
1816:
1817:        # because input_ids dtype is torch.int32,
1818:        # so convert draft_token_ids to torch.int32 here.
1819:        draft_token_ids = self._draft_token_ids.to(dtype=torch.int32)
1820:
1821:        self.input_ids.gpu.scatter_(
1822:            dim=0,
1823:            index=draft_tokens_index_tensor,
1824:            src=draft_token_ids.flatten()[prev_draft_token_indices_tensor],
1825:        )
1826:
1827:    def _get_encoder_seq_lens(
1828:        self,
1829:        num_scheduled_tokens: dict[str, int],
1830:        kv_cache_spec: KVCacheSpec,
1831:        num_reqs: int,
1832:        for_cudagraph_capture: bool = False,
