1833:    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor | None, np.ndarray | None]:
1834:        if not isinstance(kv_cache_spec, CrossAttentionSpec):
1835:            return None, None
1836:
1837:        # Zero out buffer for padding requests that are not actually scheduled (CGs)
1838:        self.encoder_seq_lens.np[:num_reqs] = 0
1839:
1840:        # Build encoder_seq_lens array mapping request indices to
1841:        # encoder lengths for inputs scheduled in this batch
1842:        for req_id in num_scheduled_tokens:
1843:            req_index = self.input_batch.req_id_to_index[req_id]
1844:            req_state = self.requests[req_id]
1845:            if req_state.mm_features is None:
1846:                self.encoder_seq_lens.np[req_index] = 0
1847:                continue
1848:
1849:            # Get the total number of encoder input tokens for running encoder requests
1850:            # whether encoding is finished or not so that cross-attention knows how
1851:            # many encoder tokens to attend to.
1852:            encoder_input_tokens = sum(
1853:                feature.mm_position.length for feature in req_state.mm_features
1854:            )
1855:            self.encoder_seq_lens.np[req_index] = encoder_input_tokens
1856:        if for_cudagraph_capture:
1857:            # During CUDA graph capture, we need to use realistic encoder lengths
1858:            # so that max_seqlen_k is captured with the correct value.
1859:            max_encoder_len = getattr(
1860:                self.model_config.hf_config,
1861:                "max_source_positions",
1862:                self.max_encoder_len,
1863:            )
1864:            self.encoder_seq_lens.np[:num_reqs] = max_encoder_len
1865:
1866:        self.encoder_seq_lens.copy_to_gpu(num_reqs)
1867:        encoder_seq_lens = self.encoder_seq_lens.gpu[:num_reqs]
1868:        encoder_seq_lens_cpu = self.encoder_seq_lens.np[:num_reqs]
1869:
1870:        return encoder_seq_lens, encoder_seq_lens_cpu
1871:
1872:    def _prepare_inputs(
1873:        self,
1874:        scheduler_output: "SchedulerOutput",
1875:        num_scheduled_tokens: np.ndarray,
1876:    ) -> tuple[
1877:        torch.Tensor,
1878:        SpecDecodeMetadata | None,
1879:    ]:
1880:        """
1881:        Returns:
1882:            tuple[logits_indices, spec_decode_metadata]
1883:        """
1884:        total_num_scheduled_tokens = scheduler_output.total_num_scheduled_tokens
1885:        assert total_num_scheduled_tokens > 0
1886:        num_reqs = self.input_batch.num_reqs
1887:        assert num_reqs > 0
1888:
1889:        # OPTIMIZATION: Start copying the block table first.
1890:        # This way, we can overlap the copy with the following CPU operations.
1891:        self.input_batch.block_table.commit_block_table(num_reqs)
1892:
1893:        # Get request indices.
1894:        # E.g., [2, 5, 3] -> [0, 0, 1, 1, 1, 1, 1, 2, 2, 2]
1895:        req_indices = np.repeat(self.arange_np[:num_reqs], num_scheduled_tokens)
1896:
1897:        # cu_num_tokens: [2, 5, 3] -> [2, 7, 10]
1898:        # self.query_pos.np[:10]: [0, 1, 0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 0, 1, 2]
1899:        cu_num_tokens = self._get_cumsum_and_arange(
1900:            num_scheduled_tokens, self.query_pos.np
1901:        )
1902:
1903:        # Get positions.
1904:        positions_np = (
1905:            self.input_batch.num_computed_tokens_cpu[req_indices]
1906:            + self.query_pos.np[: cu_num_tokens[-1]]
1907:        )
1908:
1909:        # Calculate M-RoPE positions.
1910:        # Only relevant for models using M-RoPE (e.g, Qwen2-VL)
1911:        if self.uses_mrope:
1912:            self._calc_mrope_positions(scheduler_output)
1913:
1914:        # Calculate XD-RoPE positions.
1915:        # Only relevant for models using XD-RoPE (e.g, HunYuan-VL)
1916:        if self.uses_xdrope_dim > 0:
1917:            self._calc_xdrope_positions(scheduler_output)
1918:
1919:        # Get token indices.
1920:        # E.g., [0, 1, 0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 0, 1, 2]
1921:        # -> [0, 1, M, M + 1, M + 2, M + 3, M + 4, 2 * M, 2 * M + 1, 2 * M + 2]
1922:        # where M is the max_model_len.
1923:        token_indices = (
1924:            positions_np + req_indices * self.input_batch.token_ids_cpu.shape[1]
1925:        )
1926:        token_indices_tensor = torch.from_numpy(token_indices)
1927:
1928:        # NOTE(woosuk): We use torch.index_select instead of np.take here
1929:        # because torch.index_select is much faster than np.take for large
1930:        # tensors.
1931:        torch.index_select(
1932:            self.input_batch.token_ids_cpu_tensor.flatten(),
1933:            0,
1934:            token_indices_tensor,
1935:            out=self.input_ids.cpu[:total_num_scheduled_tokens],
1936:        )
1937:        if self.enable_prompt_embeds:
1938:            is_token_ids = self.input_batch.is_token_ids_tensor.flatten()
1939:            torch.index_select(
1940:                is_token_ids,
1941:                0,
1942:                token_indices_tensor,
1943:                out=self.is_token_ids.cpu[:total_num_scheduled_tokens],
1944:            )
1945:
1946:        # Because we did not pre-allocate a massive prompt_embeds CPU tensor on
1947:        # the InputBatch, we need to fill in the prompt embeds into the expected
1948:        # spots in the GpuModelRunner's pre-allocated prompt_embeds tensor.
1949:        if self.input_batch.req_prompt_embeds:
1950:            output_idx = 0
1951:            for req_idx in range(num_reqs):
1952:                num_sched = num_scheduled_tokens[req_idx]
1953:
1954:                # Skip if this request doesn't have embeddings
1955:                if req_idx not in self.input_batch.req_prompt_embeds:
1956:                    output_idx += num_sched
1957:                    continue
1958:
1959:                # Skip if no tokens scheduled
1960:                if num_sched <= 0:
1961:                    output_idx += num_sched
1962:                    continue
1963:
1964:                req_embeds = self.input_batch.req_prompt_embeds[req_idx]
1965:                start_pos = self.input_batch.num_computed_tokens_cpu[req_idx]
1966:
1967:                # Skip if trying to read beyond available embeddings
1968:                if start_pos >= req_embeds.shape[0]:
1969:                    output_idx += num_sched
1970:                    continue
1971:
1972:                # Copy available embeddings
1973:                end_pos = start_pos + num_sched
1974:                actual_end = min(end_pos, req_embeds.shape[0])
1975:                actual_num_sched = actual_end - start_pos
1976:
1977:                if actual_num_sched > 0:
1978:                    self.inputs_embeds.cpu[
1979:                        output_idx : output_idx + actual_num_sched
1980:                    ].copy_(req_embeds[start_pos:actual_end])
1981:
1982:                output_idx += num_sched
1983:
1984:        # Prepare the attention metadata.
1985:        self.query_start_loc.np[0] = 0
1986:        self.query_start_loc.np[1 : num_reqs + 1] = cu_num_tokens
1987:        # Note: pad query_start_loc to be non-decreasing, as kernels
1988:        # like FlashAttention requires that
1989:        self.query_start_loc.np[num_reqs + 1 :].fill(cu_num_tokens[-1])
1990:        self.query_start_loc.copy_to_gpu()
1991:        query_start_loc = self.query_start_loc.gpu[: num_reqs + 1]
1992:
1993:        # Compute optimistic seq_lens (assumes all draft tokens from previous
1994:        # iteration accepted). Store in optimistic_seq_lens_cpu for use by
1995:        # _build_attention_metadata (max_seq_len) and discard_request_mask.
1996:        # seq_lens (GPU) will be computed later using the same optimistic values.
1997:        torch.add(
1998:            self.input_batch.num_computed_tokens_cpu_tensor[:num_reqs],
1999:            torch.from_numpy(num_scheduled_tokens),
2000:            out=self.optimistic_seq_lens_cpu[:num_reqs],
2001:        )
2002:        self.optimistic_seq_lens_cpu[num_reqs:].fill_(0)
2003:
2004:        # Build prev_positions mapping: current pos -> prev pos (-1 if new).
2005:        # Used for gathering from previous iteration's GPU tensors.
2006:        prev_req_id_to_index = self.input_batch.prev_req_id_to_index
2007:        self._compute_prev_positions(num_reqs)
2008:
2009:        num_tokens = [self.requests[r].num_tokens for r in self.input_batch.req_ids]
2010:        num_tokens_np = np.array(num_tokens, dtype=np.int32)
2011:
2012:        # Record which requests should not be sampled,
2013:        # so that we could clear the sampled tokens before returning
2014:        self.discard_request_mask.np[:num_reqs] = (
2015:            self.optimistic_seq_lens_cpu[:num_reqs].numpy() < num_tokens_np
2016:        )
2017:        self.discard_request_mask.copy_to_gpu(num_reqs)
2018:
2019:        # Sync num_accepted_tokens from CPU (set by
2020:        # _update_states_after_model_execute for hybrid models).
2021:        if self.num_accepted_tokens_event is not None:
2022:            self.num_accepted_tokens_event.synchronize()
2023:            # Async mode: condense() reordered indices, use prev_positions mapping
2024:            if self.use_async_scheduling and prev_req_id_to_index:
2025:                prev_idx = self.prev_positions.np[:num_reqs]
2026:                new_mask = prev_idx < 0
2027:                self.num_accepted_tokens.np[:num_reqs] = (
2028:                    self.input_batch.num_accepted_tokens_cpu[
2029:                        np.where(new_mask, 0, prev_idx)
2030:                    ]
2031:                )
2032:                self.num_accepted_tokens.np[:num_reqs][new_mask] = 1
2033:                self.input_batch.num_accepted_tokens_cpu[:num_reqs] = (
2034:                    self.num_accepted_tokens.np[:num_reqs]
2035:                )
2036:            else:
2037:                # Non-async mode: use values directly
2038:                self.num_accepted_tokens.np[:num_reqs] = (
2039:                    self.input_batch.num_accepted_tokens_cpu[:num_reqs]
2040:                )
2041:            self.num_accepted_tokens.np[num_reqs:].fill(1)
2042:            self.num_accepted_tokens.copy_to_gpu()
2043:        else:
2044:            self.num_accepted_tokens.np.fill(1)
2045:            self.num_accepted_tokens.gpu.fill_(1)
2046:
2047:        if self.mamba_prev_last_scheduled_idx is not None:
2048:            mamba_utils.preprocess_mamba_all_specdec(
2049:                scheduler_output,
2050:                self.input_batch,
2051:                self.mamba_state_idx,
2052:                num_reqs,
2053:                self.mamba_prev_last_scheduled_idx,
2054:            )
2055:
2056:        # Update num_computed_tokens on GPU. In async spec decode,
2057:        # CPU values are optimistic (all drafts accepted). The kernel
2058:        # corrects on GPU using the previous step's
2059:        # valid_sampled_token_count_gpu. Otherwise, just copy from CPU.
2060:        if (
2061:            self.use_async_spec_decode
2062:            and self.valid_sampled_token_count_gpu is not None
2063:            and prev_req_id_to_index
2064:        ):
2065:            self.prev_positions.copy_to_gpu(num_reqs)
2066:            self.prev_num_draft_tokens.copy_to_gpu()
2067:            cpu_values = self.input_batch.num_computed_tokens_cpu_tensor[:num_reqs].to(
2068:                device=self.device, non_blocking=True
2069:            )
2070:            update_num_computed_tokens_for_batch_change(
2071:                self.num_computed_tokens,
2072:                self.num_accepted_tokens.gpu[:num_reqs],
2073:                self.prev_positions.gpu[:num_reqs],
2074:                self.valid_sampled_token_count_gpu,
2075:                self.prev_num_draft_tokens.gpu,
2076:                cpu_values,
2077:            )
2078:        else:
2079:            self.num_computed_tokens[:num_reqs].copy_(
2080:                self.input_batch.num_computed_tokens_cpu_tensor[:num_reqs],
2081:                non_blocking=True,
2082:            )
2083:
2084:        self.req_indices.np[:total_num_scheduled_tokens] = req_indices
2085:        self.req_indices.copy_to_gpu(total_num_scheduled_tokens)
2086:        req_indices_gpu = self.req_indices.gpu[:total_num_scheduled_tokens]
2087:
2088:        self.query_pos.copy_to_gpu(total_num_scheduled_tokens)
2089:        self.num_scheduled_tokens.np[:num_reqs] = num_scheduled_tokens
2090:        self.num_scheduled_tokens.copy_to_gpu(num_reqs)
2091:        num_scheduled_tokens_gpu = self.num_scheduled_tokens.gpu[:num_reqs]
2092:        self.positions[:total_num_scheduled_tokens] = (
2093:            self.num_computed_tokens[req_indices_gpu].to(torch.int64)
2094:            + self.query_pos.gpu[:total_num_scheduled_tokens]
2095:        )
2096:        self.seq_lens[:num_reqs] = (
2097:            self.num_computed_tokens[:num_reqs] + num_scheduled_tokens_gpu
2098:        )
2099:        self.seq_lens[num_reqs:].fill_(0)
2100:
2101:        self.input_batch.block_table.compute_slot_mapping(
2102:            num_reqs,
2103:            self.query_start_loc.gpu[: num_reqs + 1],
2104:            self.positions[:total_num_scheduled_tokens],
2105:        )
2106:
2107:        # Copy the tensors to the GPU.
2108:        self._prepare_input_ids(
2109:            scheduler_output,
2110:            num_reqs,
2111:            total_num_scheduled_tokens,
2112:            cu_num_tokens,
2113:        )
2114:
2115:        if self.uses_mrope:
2116:            # Only relevant for models using M-RoPE (e.g, Qwen2-VL)
2117:            self.mrope_positions.gpu[:, :total_num_scheduled_tokens].copy_(
2118:                self.mrope_positions.cpu[:, :total_num_scheduled_tokens],
2119:                non_blocking=True,
2120:            )
2121:        elif self.uses_xdrope_dim > 0:
2122:            # Only relevant for models using XD-RoPE (e.g, HunYuan-VL)
2123:            self.xdrope_positions.gpu[:, :total_num_scheduled_tokens].copy_(
2124:                self.xdrope_positions.cpu[:, :total_num_scheduled_tokens],
2125:                non_blocking=True,
2126:            )
2127:        if self.use_async_spec_decode and (self.uses_mrope or self.uses_xdrope_dim > 0):
2128:            drift = self.num_computed_tokens[req_indices_gpu].to(
2129:                torch.int64
2130:            ) - self.input_batch.num_computed_tokens_cpu_tensor[req_indices].to(
2131:                device=self.device, dtype=torch.int64, non_blocking=True
2132:            )
2133:            target = self.mrope_positions if self.uses_mrope else self.xdrope_positions
2134:            target.gpu[:, :total_num_scheduled_tokens] += drift
2135:
2136:        use_spec_decode = len(scheduler_output.scheduled_spec_decode_tokens) > 0
2137:        if not use_spec_decode:
2138:            # NOTE(woosuk): Due to chunked prefills, the batch may contain
2139:            # partial requests. While we should not sample any token
2140:            # from these partial requests, we do so for simplicity.
2141:            # We will ignore the sampled tokens from the partial requests.
2142:            # TODO: Support prompt logprobs.
2143:            logits_indices = query_start_loc[1:] - 1
2144:            spec_decode_metadata = None
2145:            num_sampled_tokens = np.ones(num_reqs, dtype=np.int32)
2146:        else:
2147:            # Get the number of draft tokens for each request.
2148:            # Iterate over the dictionary rather than all requests since not all
2149:            # requests have draft tokens.
2150:            num_draft_tokens = np.zeros(num_reqs, dtype=np.int32)
2151:            # For chunked prefills, use -1 as mask rather than 0, as guided
2152:            # decoding may rollback speculative tokens.
2153:            num_decode_draft_tokens = np.full(num_reqs, -1, dtype=np.int32)
2154:            for (
2155:                req_id,
2156:                draft_token_ids,
2157:            ) in scheduler_output.scheduled_spec_decode_tokens.items():
2158:                req_idx = self.input_batch.req_id_to_index[req_id]
2159:                draft_len = len(draft_token_ids)
2160:                num_draft_tokens[req_idx] = draft_len
2161:                if (
2162:                    self.input_batch.num_computed_tokens_cpu[req_idx]
2163:                    >= self.input_batch.num_prompt_tokens[req_idx]
2164:                ):
2165:                    num_decode_draft_tokens[req_idx] = draft_len
2166:            spec_decode_metadata = self._calc_spec_decode_metadata(
2167:                num_draft_tokens, cu_num_tokens
2168:            )
2169:            logits_indices = spec_decode_metadata.logits_indices
2170:            num_sampled_tokens = num_draft_tokens + 1
2171:            # For DECODE only cuda graph of some attention backends (e.g., GDN).
2172:            self.num_decode_draft_tokens.np[:num_reqs] = num_decode_draft_tokens
2173:            self.num_decode_draft_tokens.np[num_reqs:].fill(-1)
2174:            self.num_decode_draft_tokens.copy_to_gpu()
2175:
2176:        # Hot-Swap lora model
2177:        if self.lora_config:
2178:            assert (
2179:                np.sum(num_sampled_tokens)
2180:                <= self.vllm_config.scheduler_config.max_num_batched_tokens
2181:            )
2182:            self.set_active_loras(
2183:                self.input_batch, num_scheduled_tokens, num_sampled_tokens
2184:            )
2185:
2186:        return (
2187:            logits_indices,
2188:            spec_decode_metadata,
2189:        )
2190:
2191:    def _build_attention_metadata(
2192:        self,
2193:        num_tokens: int,
2194:        num_reqs: int,
2195:        max_query_len: int,
2196:        num_tokens_padded: int | None = None,
2197:        num_reqs_padded: int | None = None,
2198:        ubatch_slices: UBatchSlices | None = None,
2199:        logits_indices: torch.Tensor | None = None,
2200:        use_spec_decode: bool = False,
2201:        for_cudagraph_capture: bool = False,
2202:        num_scheduled_tokens: dict[str, int] | None = None,
2203:        cascade_attn_prefix_lens: list[list[int]] | None = None,
2204:        slot_mappings: dict[int, torch.Tensor] | None = None,
2205:    ) -> tuple[PerLayerAttnMetadata, CommonAttentionMetadata | None]:
2206:        """
2207:        Returns:
2208:            tuple[attn_metadata, spec_decode_common_attn_metadata]
2209:        """
2210:        # Attention metadata is not needed for attention free models
2211:        if len(self.kv_cache_config.kv_cache_groups) == 0:
2212:            return {}, None
2213:
2214:        num_tokens_padded = num_tokens_padded or num_tokens
2215:        num_reqs_padded = num_reqs_padded or num_reqs
2216:        assert num_reqs_padded is not None and num_tokens_padded is not None
2217:
2218:        attn_metadata: PerLayerAttnMetadata = {}
2219:        if ubatch_slices is not None:
2220:            attn_metadata = [dict() for _ in range(len(ubatch_slices))]
2221:
2222:        if for_cudagraph_capture:
2223:            # For some attention backends (e.g. FA) with sliding window models we need
2224:            # to make sure the backend see a max_seq_len that is larger to the sliding
2225:            # window size when capturing to make sure the correct kernel is selected.
2226:            max_seq_len = self.max_model_len
2227:        else:
2228:            max_seq_len = self.optimistic_seq_lens_cpu.numpy()[:num_reqs].max().item()
2229:
2230:        kv_cache_groups = self.kv_cache_config.kv_cache_groups
2231:
2232:        def _get_block_table(kv_cache_gid: int):
2233:            assert num_reqs_padded is not None and num_tokens_padded is not None
2234:            kv_cache_spec = kv_cache_groups[kv_cache_gid].kv_cache_spec
2235:            if isinstance(kv_cache_spec, EncoderOnlyAttentionSpec):
2236:                blk_table_tensor = torch.zeros(
2237:                    (num_reqs_padded, 1),
2238:                    dtype=torch.int32,
2239:                    device=self.device,
2240:                )
2241:            else:
2242:                blk_table = self.input_batch.block_table[kv_cache_gid]
2243:                blk_table_tensor = blk_table.get_device_tensor(num_reqs_padded)
2244:
2245:            # Fill unused block table entries with NULL_BLOCK_ID (null block)
2246:            # for CUDAGraph padding. Block 0 is reserved for padding.
2247:            blk_table_tensor[num_reqs:num_reqs_padded].fill_(NULL_BLOCK_ID)
2248:            return blk_table_tensor
2249:
2250:        assert slot_mappings is not None
2251:        block_table_gid_0 = _get_block_table(0)
2252:        slot_mapping_gid_0 = slot_mappings[0]
2253:
2254:        if self.routed_experts_initialized:
2255:            # Copy this step's attention slot_mapping into our private
2256:            # device buffer. The shared ``slot_mappings[attn_gid]`` is
2257:            # owned by the attention block table and will be overwritten
2258:            # by the next ``_prepare_inputs``; we need a stable snapshot
2259:            # because the async D2H may still be in flight on the copy
2260:            # stream when the next step runs.
2261:            attn_gid = self.routed_experts_attn_gid
2262:            slot_mapping_attn = slot_mappings[attn_gid]
2263:            self.routed_experts_slot_mapping_device[:num_tokens].copy_(
2264:                slot_mapping_attn[:num_tokens]
2265:            )
2266:
2267:        num_computed_tokens_cpu = self.input_batch.num_computed_tokens_cpu_tensor[
2268:            :num_reqs_padded
2269:        ]
2270:        num_prompt_tokens_cpu = self.input_batch.num_prompt_tokens_cpu_tensor[
2271:            :num_reqs_padded
2272:        ]
2273:        seq_lens_cpu = self.optimistic_seq_lens_cpu[:num_reqs_padded]
2274:        seq_lens_cpu_upper_bound = seq_lens_cpu
2275:
2276:        # is_prefilling: True if request is still in prefill phase.
2277:        # Used by mamba backends to distinguish actual decodes from
2278:        # short extends.
2279:        is_prefilling = num_computed_tokens_cpu < num_prompt_tokens_cpu
2280:        # Zero out padded rows so stale data from condense() doesn't
2281:        # misclassify padding as prefill in CUDA graph mode.
2282:        is_prefilling[num_reqs:] = False
2283:
2284:        if self.use_async_spec_decode:
2285:            # GPU tensors are authoritative in async mode.
2286:            seq_lens_cpu = None
2287:            num_computed_tokens_cpu = None
2288:
2289:        cm_base = CommonAttentionMetadata(
2290:            query_start_loc=self.query_start_loc.gpu[: num_reqs_padded + 1],
2291:            query_start_loc_cpu=self.query_start_loc.cpu[: num_reqs_padded + 1],
2292:            seq_lens=self.seq_lens[:num_reqs_padded],
2293:            _seq_lens_cpu=seq_lens_cpu,
2294:            _num_computed_tokens_cpu=num_computed_tokens_cpu,
2295:            seq_lens_cpu_upper_bound=seq_lens_cpu_upper_bound,
2296:            num_reqs=num_reqs_padded,
2297:            num_actual_tokens=num_tokens_padded,
2298:            max_query_len=max_query_len,
2299:            max_seq_len=max_seq_len,
2300:            block_table_tensor=block_table_gid_0,
2301:            slot_mapping=slot_mapping_gid_0,
2302:            causal=True,
2303:            is_prefilling=is_prefilling,
2304:            positions=self.positions[:num_tokens_padded],
2305:        )
2306:
2307:        if self.dcp_world_size > 1:
2308:            self.dcp_local_seq_lens.cpu[:num_reqs] = get_dcp_local_seq_lens(
2309:                self.optimistic_seq_lens_cpu[:num_reqs],
2310:                self.dcp_world_size,
2311:                self.dcp_rank,
2312:                self.parallel_config.cp_kv_cache_interleave_size,
2313:            )
2314:            self.dcp_local_seq_lens.cpu[num_reqs:].fill_(0)
2315:            self.dcp_local_seq_lens.copy_to_gpu(num_reqs_padded)
2316:
2317:            cm_base.dcp_local_seq_lens = self.dcp_local_seq_lens.gpu[:num_reqs_padded]
2318:            cm_base.dcp_local_seq_lens_cpu = self.dcp_local_seq_lens.cpu[
2319:                :num_reqs_padded
2320:            ]
2321:
2322:        if logits_indices is not None and self.cache_config.kv_sharing_fast_prefill:
2323:            cm_base.num_logits_indices = logits_indices.size(0)
2324:            cm_base.logits_indices_padded = self._prepare_kv_sharing_fast_prefill(
2325:                logits_indices
2326:            )
2327:
2328:        # Cache attention metadata builds across hybrid KV-cache groups
2329:        # The only thing that changes between different hybrid KV-cache groups when the
2330:        # same metadata builder and KVCacheSpec is the same is the block table, so we
2331:        # can cache the attention metadata builds and just update the block table using
2332:        # `builder.update_block_table` if the builder supports it.
2333:        cached_attn_metadata: dict[
2334:            tuple[KVCacheSpec, type[AttentionMetadataBuilder]], AttentionMetadata
2335:        ] = {}
2336:
2337:        def _build_attn_group_metadata(
2338:            kv_cache_gid: int,
2339:            attn_gid: int,
2340:            common_attn_metadata: CommonAttentionMetadata,
2341:            ubid: int | None = None,
2342:        ) -> None:
2343:            attn_group = self.attn_groups[kv_cache_gid][attn_gid]
2344:            builder = attn_group.get_metadata_builder(ubid or 0)
2345:            kv_cache_spec = kv_cache_groups[kv_cache_gid].kv_cache_spec
2346:            if isinstance(kv_cache_spec, UniformTypeKVCacheSpecs):
2347:                kv_cache_spec = kv_cache_spec.kv_cache_specs[attn_group.layer_names[0]]
2348:            cache_key = (kv_cache_spec, type(builder))
2349:
2350:            cascade_attn_prefix_len = (
2351:                cascade_attn_prefix_lens[kv_cache_gid][attn_gid]
2352:                if cascade_attn_prefix_lens
2353:                else 0
2354:            )
2355:
2356:            extra_attn_metadata_args = {}
2357:            if use_spec_decode and isinstance(
2358:                builder, (Mamba2AttentionMetadataBuilder, GDNAttentionMetadataBuilder)
2359:            ):
2360:                assert ubid is None, "UBatching not supported with GDN yet"
2361:                extra_attn_metadata_args = dict(
2362:                    num_accepted_tokens=self.num_accepted_tokens.gpu[:num_reqs_padded],
2363:                    num_decode_draft_tokens_cpu=self.num_decode_draft_tokens.cpu[
2364:                        :num_reqs_padded
2365:                    ],
2366:                )
2367:                if (
2368:                    isinstance(builder, Mamba2AttentionMetadataBuilder)
2369:                    and self.mamba_prev_last_scheduled_idx is not None
2370:                ):
2371:                    extra_attn_metadata_args["prev_last_scheduled_idx"] = (
2372:                        self.mamba_prev_last_scheduled_idx.gpu[:num_reqs_padded]
2373:                    )
2374:
2375:            if for_cudagraph_capture:
2376:                attn_metadata_i = builder.build_for_cudagraph_capture(
2377:                    common_attn_metadata
2378:                )
2379:            elif (
2380:                cache_key in cached_attn_metadata
2381:                and builder.supports_update_block_table
2382:            ):
2383:                attn_metadata_i = builder.update_block_table(
2384:                    cached_attn_metadata[cache_key],
2385:                    common_attn_metadata.block_table_tensor,
2386:                    common_attn_metadata.slot_mapping,
2387:                )
2388:            else:
2389:                attn_metadata_i = builder.build(
2390:                    common_prefix_len=cascade_attn_prefix_len,
2391:                    common_attn_metadata=common_attn_metadata,
2392:                    **extra_attn_metadata_args,
2393:                )
2394:                if builder.supports_update_block_table:
2395:                    cached_attn_metadata[cache_key] = attn_metadata_i
2396:
2397:            if ubid is None:
2398:                assert isinstance(attn_metadata, dict)
2399:                attn_metadata_dict = attn_metadata
2400:            else:
2401:                assert isinstance(attn_metadata, list)
2402:                attn_metadata_dict = attn_metadata[ubid]
2403:
2404:            for layer_name in attn_group.layer_names:
2405:                attn_metadata_dict[layer_name] = attn_metadata_i
2406:
2407:        # Prepare the attention metadata for each KV cache group and make layers
2408:        # in the same group share the same metadata.
2409:        spec_decode_common_attn_metadata = None
2410:        for kv_cache_gid, kv_cache_group in enumerate(kv_cache_groups):
2411:            cm = copy(cm_base)  # shallow copy
2412:
2413:            # Basically only the encoder seq_lens, block_table and slot_mapping change
2414:            # for each kv_cache_group.
2415:            cm.encoder_seq_lens, cm.encoder_seq_lens_cpu = self._get_encoder_seq_lens(
2416:                num_scheduled_tokens or {},
2417:                kv_cache_group.kv_cache_spec,
2418:                num_reqs_padded,
2419:                for_cudagraph_capture=for_cudagraph_capture,
2420:            )
2421:            if kv_cache_gid > 0:
2422:                cm.block_table_tensor = _get_block_table(kv_cache_gid)
2423:                cm.slot_mapping = slot_mappings[kv_cache_gid]
2424:
2425:            if self.speculative_config and spec_decode_common_attn_metadata is None:
2426:                if isinstance(
2427:                    self.drafter,
2428:                    (
2429:                        EagleProposer,
2430:                        DFlashProposer,
2431:                        Gemma4Proposer,
2432:                        ExtractHiddenStatesProposer,
2433:                    ),
2434:                ):
2435:                    if self.drafter.kv_cache_gid == kv_cache_gid:
2436:                        spec_decode_common_attn_metadata = cm
2437:                else:
2438:                    spec_decode_common_attn_metadata = cm
2439:            # Capture per-group block tables for multi-group proposers.
2440:            if self.speculative_config and isinstance(self.drafter, Step3p5MTPProposer):
2441:                self.drafter.set_per_group_attn_metadata(
2442:                    kv_cache_gid, cm.block_table_tensor, cm.slot_mapping
2443:                )
2444:            elif self.speculative_config and isinstance(self.drafter, Gemma4Proposer):
2445:                self.drafter.set_per_group_block_table(
2446:                    kv_cache_gid, cm.block_table_tensor
2447:                )
2448:
2449:            for attn_gid in range(len(self.attn_groups[kv_cache_gid])):
2450:                if ubatch_slices is not None:
2451:                    for ubid, _cm in enumerate(split_attn_metadata(ubatch_slices, cm)):
2452:                        _build_attn_group_metadata(kv_cache_gid, attn_gid, _cm, ubid)
2453:
2454:                else:
2455:                    _build_attn_group_metadata(kv_cache_gid, attn_gid, cm)
2456:
2457:        if self.is_mm_prefix_lm:
2458:            req_doc_ranges = {}
2459:
2460:            # Gemma4 bidi: skip ranges that exceed the sliding
2461:            # window. When image tokens > sliding_window, bidi causes
2462:            # early image tokens to attend to the entire image
2463:            # (e.g. 6 → 1092 targets), degrading spatial precision.
2464:            # Per-range filtering keeps bidi for small images/video
2465:            # frames while skipping oversized images.
2466:            hf_text_config = self.model_config.hf_text_config
2467:            _bidi_sw = getattr(hf_text_config, "sliding_window", None)
2468:
2469:            for req_id in self.input_batch.req_ids:
2470:                image_doc_ranges = []
2471:                req_state = self.requests[req_id]
2472:                for mm_feature in req_state.mm_features:
2473:                    if mm_feature.modality == "audio":
2474:                        continue
2475:                    pos_info = mm_feature.mm_position
2476:                    img_doc_range = pos_info.extract_embeds_range()
2477:                    for r in img_doc_range:
2478:                        if _bidi_sw is not None and (r[1] - r[0] + 1) > _bidi_sw:
2479:                            continue
2480:                        image_doc_ranges.append(r)
2481:                req_idx = self.input_batch.req_id_to_index[req_id]
2482:                req_doc_ranges[req_idx] = image_doc_ranges
2483:
2484:            # Set mm_prefix_range for all attention metadata
2485:            self._set_mm_prefix_range_for_metadata(attn_metadata, req_doc_ranges)
2486:
2487:        if spec_decode_common_attn_metadata is not None and (
2488:            num_reqs != num_reqs_padded or num_tokens != num_tokens_padded
2489:        ):
2490:            # Currently the drafter still only uses piecewise cudagraphs (and modifies
2491:            # the attention metadata in directly), and therefore does not want to use
2492:            # padded attention metadata.
2493:            spec_decode_common_attn_metadata = (
2494:                spec_decode_common_attn_metadata.unpadded(num_tokens, num_reqs)
2495:            )
2496:
2497:        return attn_metadata, spec_decode_common_attn_metadata
2498:
2499:    def _compute_cascade_attn_prefix_lens(
2500:        self,
2501:        num_scheduled_tokens: np.ndarray,
2502:        num_computed_tokens: np.ndarray,
2503:        num_common_prefix_blocks: list[int],
2504:    ) -> list[list[int]] | None:
2505:        """
2506:        Returns:
2507:            Optional[cascade_attn_prefix_lens]
2508:                cascade_attn_prefix_lens is 2D:
2509:                ``[kv_cache_group_id][attn_group_idx]``,
2510:                None if we should not use cascade attention
2511:        """
2512:
2513:        use_cascade_attn = False
2514:        num_kv_cache_groups = len(self.kv_cache_config.kv_cache_groups)
2515:        cascade_attn_prefix_lens: list[list[int]] = [
2516:            [] for _ in range(num_kv_cache_groups)
2517:        ]
2518:
2519:        for kv_cache_gid in range(num_kv_cache_groups):
2520:            for attn_group in self.attn_groups[kv_cache_gid]:
2521:                if isinstance(attn_group.kv_cache_spec, EncoderOnlyAttentionSpec):
2522:                    cascade_attn_prefix_len = 0
2523:                else:
2524:                    # 0 if cascade attention should not be used
2525:                    cascade_attn_prefix_len = self._compute_cascade_attn_prefix_len(
2526:                        num_scheduled_tokens,
2527:                        num_computed_tokens,
2528:                        num_common_prefix_blocks[kv_cache_gid],
2529:                        attn_group.kv_cache_spec,
2530:                        attn_group.get_metadata_builder(),
2531:                    )
2532:                cascade_attn_prefix_lens[kv_cache_gid].append(cascade_attn_prefix_len)
2533:                use_cascade_attn |= cascade_attn_prefix_len > 0
2534:
2535:        return cascade_attn_prefix_lens if use_cascade_attn else None
2536:
2537:    def _compute_cascade_attn_prefix_len(
2538:        self,
2539:        num_scheduled_tokens: np.ndarray,
2540:        num_computed_tokens: np.ndarray,
2541:        num_common_prefix_blocks: int,
2542:        kv_cache_spec: KVCacheSpec,
2543:        attn_metadata_builder: AttentionMetadataBuilder,
2544:    ) -> int:
2545:        """Compute the length of the common prefix for cascade attention.
2546:
2547:        NOTE(woosuk): The common prefix length returned by this function
2548:        represents the length used specifically for cascade attention, not the
2549:        actual number of tokens shared between requests. When cascade attention
2550:        is disabled (use_cascade=False), this function returns 0 even if
2551:        requests share common tokens. Additionally, the common prefix length is
2552:        truncated to a multiple of the block size and may be further truncated
2553:        due to implementation details explained below.
2554:
2555:        Args:
2556:            num_scheduled_tokens: Number of tokens scheduled per request.
2557:            num_common_prefix_blocks: Number of shared KV cache blocks.
2558:
2559:        Returns:
2560:            int: Length of common prefix in tokens.
2561:        """
2562:
2563:        common_prefix_len = num_common_prefix_blocks * kv_cache_spec.block_size
2564:        if common_prefix_len == 0:
2565:            # Common case.
2566:            return 0
2567:
2568:        # NOTE(woosuk): Cascade attention uses two attention kernels: one
2569:        # for the common prefix and the other for the rest. For the first
2570:        # kernel, we concatenate all the query tokens (possibly from
2571:        # different requests) and treat them as if they are from the same
2572:        # request. Then, we use bi-directional attention to process the
2573:        # common prefix in the KV cache. Importantly, this means that the
2574:        # first kernel does not do any masking.
2575:
2576:        # Consider the following example:
2577:        # Request 1's input query: [D, E, X]
2578:        # Request 1's kv cache: [A, B, C, D, E, X]
2579:        # Request 1's num_computed_tokens: 3 (i.e., [A, B, C])
2580:        # Request 2's input query: [E, Y]
2581:        # Request 2's kv cache: [A, B, C, D, E, Y]
2582:        # Request 2's num_computed_tokens: 4 (i.e., [A, B, C, D])
2583:
2584:        # If we use [A, B, C, D, E] as the common prefix, then the
2585:        # first kernel will compute the bi-directional attention between
2586:        # input query [D, E, X, E, Y] and common prefix [A, B, C, D, E].
2587:        # However, this is wrong because D in Request 1 should not attend to
2588:        # E in the common prefix (i.e., we need masking).
2589:        # To avoid this, [A, B, C, D] should be the common prefix.
2590:        # That is, the common prefix should be capped by the minimum
2591:        # num_computed_tokens among the requests, and plus one to include
2592:        # the first token of the query.
2593:
2594:        # In practice, we use [A, B, C] as the common prefix, instead of
2595:        # [A, B, C, D] (i.e., the common prefix is capped by the minimum
2596:        # num_computed_tokens, without plus one).
2597:        # This is because of an implementation detail: We want to always
2598:        # use two kernels for cascade attention. Let's imagine:
2599:        # Request 3's input query: [D]
2600:        # Request 3's kv cache: [A, B, C, D]
2601:        # Request 3's num_computed_tokens: 3 (i.e., [A, B, C])
2602:        # If we use [A, B, C, D] as the common prefix for Request 1-3,
2603:        # then Request 3 will be processed only by the first kernel,
2604:        # and the second kernel will get an empty input. While this is not
2605:        # a fundamental problem, our current implementation does not support
2606:        # this case.
2607:        common_prefix_len = min(common_prefix_len, num_computed_tokens.min())
2608:        # common_prefix_len should be a multiple of the block size.
2609:        common_prefix_len = (
2610:            common_prefix_len // kv_cache_spec.block_size * kv_cache_spec.block_size
2611:        )
2612:        use_sliding_window = isinstance(kv_cache_spec, SlidingWindowSpec) or (
2613:            isinstance(kv_cache_spec, FullAttentionSpec)
2614:            and kv_cache_spec.sliding_window is not None
2615:        )
2616:        use_local_attention = isinstance(kv_cache_spec, ChunkedLocalAttentionSpec) or (
2617:            isinstance(kv_cache_spec, FullAttentionSpec)
2618:            and kv_cache_spec.attention_chunk_size is not None
2619:        )
2620:        assert isinstance(kv_cache_spec, AttentionSpec)
2621:        use_cascade = attn_metadata_builder.use_cascade_attention(
2622:            common_prefix_len=common_prefix_len,
2623:            query_lens=num_scheduled_tokens,
2624:            num_query_heads=self.num_query_heads,
2625:            num_kv_heads=kv_cache_spec.num_kv_heads,
2626:            use_alibi=self.use_alibi,
2627:            use_sliding_window=use_sliding_window,
2628:            use_local_attention=use_local_attention,
2629:            num_sms=self.num_sms,
2630:            dcp_world_size=self.dcp_world_size,
2631:        )
2632:        return common_prefix_len if use_cascade else 0
2633:
2634:    def _calc_mrope_positions(self, scheduler_output: "SchedulerOutput"):
2635:        mrope_pos_ptr = 0
2636:        for index, req_id in enumerate(self.input_batch.req_ids):
2637:            req = self.requests[req_id]
2638:            assert req.mrope_positions is not None
2639:
2640:            num_computed_tokens = self.input_batch.num_computed_tokens_cpu[index]
2641:            num_scheduled_tokens = scheduler_output.num_scheduled_tokens[req_id]
2642:            num_prompt_tokens = length_from_prompt_token_ids_or_embeds(
2643:                req.prompt_token_ids, req.prompt_embeds
2644:            )
2645:
2646:            if num_computed_tokens + num_scheduled_tokens > num_prompt_tokens:
2647:                prompt_part_len = max(0, num_prompt_tokens - num_computed_tokens)
2648:                completion_part_len = max(0, num_scheduled_tokens - prompt_part_len)
2649:            else:
2650:                prompt_part_len = num_scheduled_tokens
2651:                completion_part_len = 0
2652:
2653:            assert num_scheduled_tokens == prompt_part_len + completion_part_len
2654:
2655:            if prompt_part_len > 0:
2656:                # prompt's mrope_positions are pre-computed
2657:                dst_start = mrope_pos_ptr
2658:                dst_end = mrope_pos_ptr + prompt_part_len
2659:                src_start = num_computed_tokens
2660:                src_end = num_computed_tokens + prompt_part_len
2661:
2662:                self.mrope_positions.cpu[:, dst_start:dst_end] = req.mrope_positions[
2663:                    :, src_start:src_end
2664:                ]
2665:                mrope_pos_ptr += prompt_part_len
2666:
2667:            if completion_part_len > 0:
2668:                # compute completion's mrope_positions on-the-fly
2669:                dst_start = mrope_pos_ptr
2670:                dst_end = mrope_pos_ptr + completion_part_len
2671:
2672:                assert req.mrope_position_delta is not None
2673:                MRotaryEmbedding.get_next_input_positions_tensor(
2674:                    out=self.mrope_positions.np,
2675:                    out_offset=dst_start,
2676:                    mrope_position_delta=req.mrope_position_delta,
2677:                    context_len=num_computed_tokens + prompt_part_len,
2678:                    num_new_tokens=completion_part_len,
2679:                )
2680:
2681:                mrope_pos_ptr += completion_part_len
2682:
2683:    def _calc_xdrope_positions(self, scheduler_output: "SchedulerOutput"):
2684:        xdrope_pos_ptr = 0
2685:        for index, req_id in enumerate(self.input_batch.req_ids):
2686:            req = self.requests[req_id]
2687:            assert req.xdrope_positions is not None
2688:
2689:            num_computed_tokens = self.input_batch.num_computed_tokens_cpu[index]
2690:            num_scheduled_tokens = scheduler_output.num_scheduled_tokens[req_id]
2691:            num_prompt_tokens = length_from_prompt_token_ids_or_embeds(
2692:                req.prompt_token_ids, req.prompt_embeds
2693:            )
2694:
2695:            if num_computed_tokens + num_scheduled_tokens > num_prompt_tokens:
2696:                prompt_part_len = max(0, num_prompt_tokens - num_computed_tokens)
2697:                completion_part_len = max(0, num_scheduled_tokens - prompt_part_len)
2698:            else:
2699:                prompt_part_len = num_scheduled_tokens
2700:                completion_part_len = 0
2701:
2702:            assert num_scheduled_tokens == prompt_part_len + completion_part_len
2703:
2704:            if prompt_part_len > 0:
2705:                # prompt's xdrope_positions are pre-computed
2706:                dst_start = xdrope_pos_ptr
2707:                dst_end = xdrope_pos_ptr + prompt_part_len
2708:                src_start = num_computed_tokens
2709:                src_end = num_computed_tokens + prompt_part_len
2710:
2711:                self.xdrope_positions.cpu[:, dst_start:dst_end] = req.xdrope_positions[
2712:                    :, src_start:src_end
2713:                ]
2714:                xdrope_pos_ptr += prompt_part_len
2715:
2716:            if completion_part_len > 0:
2717:                # compute completion's xdrope_positions on-the-fly
2718:                dst_start = xdrope_pos_ptr
2719:                dst_end = xdrope_pos_ptr + completion_part_len
2720:
2721:                XDRotaryEmbedding.get_next_input_positions_tensor(
2722:                    out=self.xdrope_positions.np,
2723:                    out_offset=dst_start,
2724:                    context_len=num_computed_tokens + prompt_part_len,
2725:                    num_new_tokens=completion_part_len,
2726:                )
2727:
2728:                xdrope_pos_ptr += completion_part_len
2729:
2730:    def _calc_spec_decode_metadata(
2731:        self,
2732:        num_draft_tokens: np.ndarray,
2733:        cu_num_scheduled_tokens: np.ndarray,
2734:    ) -> SpecDecodeMetadata:
2735:        # Inputs:
2736:        # cu_num_scheduled_tokens:  [  4, 104, 107, 207, 209]
2737:        # num_draft_tokens:         [  3,   0,   2,   0,   1]
2738:        # Outputs:
2739:        # cu_num_draft_tokens:      [  3,   3,   5,   5,   6]
2740:        # logits_indices:           [  0,   1,   2,   3, 103, 104, 105, 106,
2741:        #                            206, 207, 208]
2742:        # target_logits_indices:    [  0,   1,   2,   5,   6,   9]
2743:        # bonus_logits_indices:     [  3,   4,   7,   8,  10]
2744:
2745:        # Compute the logits indices.
2746:        # [4, 1, 3, 1, 2]
2747:        num_sampled_tokens = num_draft_tokens + 1
2748:
2749:        # Step 1.
2750:        # cu_num_sampled_tokens: [4, 5, 8, 9, 11]
2751:        # _arange_scratch[:11]: [0, 1, 2, 3, 0, 0, 1, 2, 0, 0, 1]
2752:        cu_num_sampled_tokens = self._get_cumsum_and_arange(
2753:            num_sampled_tokens, self._arange_scratch, cumsum_dtype=np.int32
2754:        )
2755:        # Step 2. [0, 0, 0, 0, 103, 104, 104, 104, 206, 207, 207]
2756:        logits_indices = np.repeat(
2757:            cu_num_scheduled_tokens - num_sampled_tokens, num_sampled_tokens
2758:        )
2759:        # Step 3. [0, 1, 2, 3, 103, 104, 105, 106, 206, 207, 208]
2760:        logits_indices += self._arange_scratch[: cu_num_sampled_tokens[-1]]
2761:
2762:        # Compute the bonus logits indices.
2763:        bonus_logits_indices = cu_num_sampled_tokens - 1
2764:
2765:        # Compute the draft logits indices.
2766:        # cu_num_draft_tokens: [3, 3, 5, 5, 6]
2767:        # _arange_scratch[:6]: [0, 1, 2, 0, 1, 0]
2768:        cu_num_draft_tokens = self._get_cumsum_and_arange(
2769:            num_draft_tokens, self._arange_scratch, cumsum_dtype=np.int32
2770:        )
2771:        # [0, 0, 0, 5, 5, 9]
2772:        target_logits_indices = np.repeat(
2773:            cu_num_sampled_tokens - num_sampled_tokens, num_draft_tokens
2774:        )
2775:        # [0, 1, 2, 5, 6, 9]
2776:        target_logits_indices += self._arange_scratch[: cu_num_draft_tokens[-1]]
2777:
2778:        # TODO: Optimize the CPU -> GPU copy.
2779:        cu_num_draft_tokens = torch.from_numpy(cu_num_draft_tokens).to(
2780:            self.device, non_blocking=True
2781:        )
2782:        cu_num_sampled_tokens = torch.from_numpy(cu_num_sampled_tokens).to(
2783:            self.device, non_blocking=True
2784:        )
2785:        logits_indices = torch.from_numpy(logits_indices).to(
2786:            self.device, non_blocking=True
2787:        )
2788:        target_logits_indices = torch.from_numpy(target_logits_indices).to(
2789:            self.device, non_blocking=True
2790:        )
2791:        bonus_logits_indices = torch.from_numpy(bonus_logits_indices).to(
2792:            self.device, non_blocking=True
2793:        )
2794:
2795:        # Compute the draft token ids.
2796:        # draft_token_indices:      [  1,   2,   3, 105, 106, 208]
2797:        draft_token_ids = self.input_ids.gpu[logits_indices]
2798:        draft_token_ids = draft_token_ids[target_logits_indices + 1]
2799:
2800:        return SpecDecodeMetadata(
2801:            draft_token_ids=draft_token_ids,
2802:            num_draft_tokens=num_draft_tokens.tolist(),
2803:            cu_num_draft_tokens=cu_num_draft_tokens,
2804:            cu_num_sampled_tokens=cu_num_sampled_tokens,
2805:            target_logits_indices=target_logits_indices,
2806:            bonus_logits_indices=bonus_logits_indices,
2807:            logits_indices=logits_indices,
2808:        )
2809:
2810:    def _prepare_kv_sharing_fast_prefill(
2811:        self,
2812:        logits_indices: torch.Tensor,
2813:    ) -> torch.Tensor:
2814:        assert self.kv_sharing_fast_prefill_logits_indices is not None
2815:        num_logits = logits_indices.shape[0]
2816:        assert num_logits > 0
2817:        self.kv_sharing_fast_prefill_logits_indices[:num_logits].copy_(logits_indices)
2818:        # There might have leftover indices in logits_indices[num_logits:]
2819:        # from previous iterations, whose values may be greater than the
2820:        # batch size in the current iteration. To ensure indices are always
2821:        # valid, fill the padded indices with the last index. Broadcast the
2822:        # scalar GPU-side to avoid a D2H sync on `.item()`.
2823:        self.kv_sharing_fast_prefill_logits_indices[num_logits:] = logits_indices[-1]
2824:        # Dispatch for the decoder portion of the model.
2825:        _, batch_desc = self.cudagraph_dispatcher.dispatch(
2826:            num_logits, invalid_modes={CUDAGraphMode.FULL}
2827:        )
2828:        num_logits_padded = batch_desc.num_tokens
2829:        logits_indices_padded = self.kv_sharing_fast_prefill_logits_indices[
2830:            :num_logits_padded
2831:        ]
2832:        return logits_indices_padded
2833:
2834:    def _batch_mm_inputs_from_scheduler(
2835:        self,
2836:        scheduler_output: "SchedulerOutput",
2837:    ) -> tuple[
2838:        list[str],
2839:        list[tuple[str, MultiModalKwargsItem]],
2840:        list[tuple[str, PlaceholderRange]],
2841:    ]:
2842:        """Batch multimodal inputs from scheduled encoder inputs.
2843:
2844:        Args:
2845:            scheduler_output: The scheduler output containing scheduled encoder
2846:                inputs.
2847:
2848:        Returns:
2849:            A tuple of (mm_hashes, mm_kwargs, mm_lora_refs) where:
2850:            - mm_hashes: List of multimodal hashes for each item
2851:            - mm_kwargs: List of multimodal kwargs for each item
2852:            - mm_lora_refs: List of (req_id, placeholder_range) for each item
2853:        """
2854:        scheduled_encoder_inputs = scheduler_output.scheduled_encoder_inputs
2855:        if not scheduled_encoder_inputs:
2856:            return [], [], []
2857:
2858:        mm_hashes = list[str]()
2859:        mm_kwargs = list[tuple[str, MultiModalKwargsItem]]()
2860:        # Multimodal LoRA reference info to map each multimodal item
2861:        # back to its request & position
2862:        mm_lora_refs = list[tuple[str, PlaceholderRange]]()
2863:        for req_id, encoder_input_ids in scheduled_encoder_inputs.items():
2864:            req_state = self.requests[req_id]
2865:
2866:            for mm_input_id in encoder_input_ids:
2867:                mm_feature = req_state.mm_features[mm_input_id]
2868:                if mm_feature.data is None:
2869:                    continue
2870:
2871:                mm_hashes.append(mm_feature.identifier)
2872:                mm_kwargs.append((mm_feature.modality, mm_feature.data))
2873:                mm_lora_refs.append((req_id, mm_feature.mm_position))
2874:
2875:        return mm_hashes, mm_kwargs, mm_lora_refs
2876:
2877:    def _execute_mm_encoder(
2878:        self, scheduler_output: "SchedulerOutput"
2879:    ) -> list[torch.Tensor]:
2880:        mm_hashes, mm_kwargs, mm_lora_refs = self._batch_mm_inputs_from_scheduler(
2881:            scheduler_output
2882:        )
2883:
2884:        if not mm_kwargs:
2885:            return []
2886:
2887:        # `prompt_embeds` is a passthrough modality, the tensor is already in
2888:        # the model embedding space, so no encoder runs. Inject each
2889:        # `prompt_embeds` tensor directly into the encoder cache here so that
2890:        # `_gather_mm_embeddings` can splice it via the standard `is_mm_embed`
2891:        # path.
2892:        pe_indices = [
2893:            i
2894:            for i, (modality, _) in enumerate(mm_kwargs)
2895:            if modality == "prompt_embeds"
2896:        ]
2897:        if pe_indices:
2898:            for i in pe_indices:
2899:                pe_tensor = mm_kwargs[i][1]["embedding"].data
2900:                assert isinstance(pe_tensor, torch.Tensor)
2901:
2902:                self.encoder_cache[mm_hashes[i]] = pe_tensor.to(self.device)
2903:                self.maybe_save_ec_to_connector(self.encoder_cache, mm_hashes[i])
2904:            # Filter out `prompt_embeds` items from mm_kwargs/mm_hashes/mm_lora_refs
2905:            # since they don't require further encoder processing.
2906:            mm_hashes = [h for i, h in enumerate(mm_hashes) if i not in pe_indices]
2907:            mm_kwargs = [k for i, k in enumerate(mm_kwargs) if i not in pe_indices]
2908:            mm_lora_refs = [
2909:                r for i, r in enumerate(mm_lora_refs) if i not in pe_indices
2910:            ]
2911:            if not mm_kwargs:
2912:                return []  # nothing left to encode after filtering out `prompt_embeds`
2913:
2914:        should_time = bool(
2915:            self.observability_config
2916:            and self.observability_config.enable_mm_processor_stats
2917:            and scheduler_output.scheduled_encoder_inputs
2918:        )
2919:
2920:        # Batch mm inputs as much as we can: if a request in the batch has
2921:        # multiple modalities or a different modality than the previous one,
2922:        # we process it separately to preserve item order.
2923:        # FIXME(ywang96): This is a hacky way to deal with multiple modalities
2924:        # in the same batch while still being able to benefit from batching
2925:        # multimodal inputs. The proper solution should be reordering the
2926:        # encoder outputs.
2927:        model = cast(SupportsMultiModal, self.model)
2928:
2929:        if self.lora_config and self.lora_manager.supports_tower_connector_lora():
2930:            # Build LoRA mappings independently for encoder inputs
2931:            # (encoder batch structure is different from main batch)
2932:            prompt_lora_mapping = []
2933:            token_lora_mapping = []
2934:            lora_requests = set()
2935:            encoder_token_counts = []
2936:
2937:            for req_id, pos_info in mm_lora_refs:
2938:                req_idx = self.input_batch.req_id_to_index[req_id]
2939:                lora_id = int(self.input_batch.request_lora_mapping[req_idx])
2940:
2941:                # Prefer pos_info.get_num_embeds to count precise MM embedding tokens.
2942:                num_tokens = self.model.get_num_mm_encoder_tokens(  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
2943:                    pos_info.get_num_embeds()
2944:                )
2945:                prompt_lora_mapping.append(lora_id)
2946:                token_lora_mapping.extend([lora_id] * num_tokens)
2947:                encoder_token_counts.append(num_tokens)
2948:
2949:                if lora_id > 0:
2950:                    lora_request = self.input_batch.lora_id_to_lora_request.get(lora_id)
2951:                    if lora_request is not None:
2952:                        lora_requests.add(lora_request)
2953:
2954:            # Set tower adapter mapping
2955:            tower_mapping = LoRAMapping(
2956:                tuple(token_lora_mapping),
2957:                tuple(prompt_lora_mapping),
2958:                is_prefill=True,
2959:                type=LoRAMappingType.TOWER,
2960:            )
2961:            self.lora_manager.set_active_adapters(lora_requests, tower_mapping)
2962:
2963:            # Only set connector mapping if the model actually has a connector.
2964:            # Some multimodal models inherit a stub `get_num_mm_connector_tokens`
2965:            # from `SupportsMultiModal`, which returns None and should not be
2966:            # treated as a signal that connector LoRA is supported.
2967:            mm_mapping = (
2968:                self.model.get_mm_mapping()  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
2969:                if hasattr(self.model, "get_mm_mapping")
2970:                else None
2971:            )
2972:            if (
2973:                mm_mapping is not None
2974:                and mm_mapping.connector
2975:                and hasattr(self.model, "get_num_mm_connector_tokens")
2976:            ):
2977:                post_op_counts = [
2978:                    self.model.get_num_mm_connector_tokens(num_tokens)  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
2979:                    for num_tokens in encoder_token_counts
2980:                ]
2981:
2982:                connector_token_mapping = np.repeat(
2983:                    np.array(prompt_lora_mapping, dtype=np.int32),
2984:                    np.array(post_op_counts, dtype=np.int32),
2985:                )
2986:                connector_mapping = LoRAMapping(
2987:                    index_mapping=tuple(connector_token_mapping.tolist()),
2988:                    prompt_mapping=tuple(prompt_lora_mapping),
2989:                    is_prefill=True,
2990:                    type=LoRAMappingType.CONNECTOR,
2991:                )
2992:
2993:                self.lora_manager.set_active_adapters(
2994:                    lora_requests,
2995:                    connector_mapping,
2996:                )
2997:
2998:        encoder_outputs: list[torch.Tensor] = []
2999:        # Track the current index in mm_kwargs/mm_lora_refs to map groups to request IDs
3000:        current_item_idx = 0
3001:        for modality, num_items, mm_kwargs_batch in group_and_batch_mm_kwargs(
3002:            mm_kwargs,
3003:            device=self.device,
3004:            pin_memory=self.pin_memory,
3005:        ):
3006:            batch_outputs: MultiModalEmbeddings
3007:
3008:            # EVS and dynamic res video related change.
3009:            # (ekhvedchenia): Temporary hack to limit peak memory usage when
3010:            # processing multimodal data. This solves the issue with scheduler
3011:            # putting too many video samples into a single batch. Scheduler
3012:            # uses pruned vision tokens count to compare it versus compute
3013:            # budget which is incorrect (Either input media size or non-pruned
3014:            # output vision tokens count should be considered)
3015:            # dynamic res video for nemotron temporarily uses this hack via
3016:            # requires_sequential_video_encoding
3017:            # because it doesn't yet support video batching.
3018:            # TODO(ywang96): Fix memory profiling to take EVS into account and
3019:            # remove this hack.
3020:            if (
3021:                (
3022:                    self.is_multimodal_pruning_enabled
3023:                    or self.requires_sequential_video_encoding
3024:                )
3025:                and modality == "video"
3026:                and num_items > 1
3027:            ):
3028:                batch_outputs_lst = list[torch.Tensor]()
3029:                for video_idx in range(num_items):
3030:                    video_mm_kwargs_item = mm_kwargs[current_item_idx + video_idx]
3031:                    with self.timed_encoder_operation(
3032:                        should_time, mm_lora_refs, current_item_idx + video_idx, 1
3033:                    ):
3034:                        _, _, micro_batch_mm_inputs = next(
3035:                            group_and_batch_mm_kwargs(
3036:                                [video_mm_kwargs_item],
3037:                                device=self.device,
3038:                                pin_memory=self.pin_memory,
3039:                            )
3040:                        )
3041:
3042:                        micro_batch_outputs = model.embed_multimodal(
3043:                            **micro_batch_mm_inputs
3044:                        )
3045:
3046:                        batch_outputs_lst.extend(micro_batch_outputs)
3047:
3048:                batch_outputs = batch_outputs_lst
3049:            else:
3050:                # Run the encoder.
3051:                # `batch_outputs` is either of the following:
3052:                # 1. A tensor of shape (num_items, feature_size, hidden_size)
3053:                # in case feature_size is fixed across all multimodal items.
3054:                # 2. A list or tuple (length: num_items) of tensors,
3055:                # each of shape (feature_size, hidden_size) in case the feature
3056:                # size is dynamic depending on the input multimodal items.
3057:
3058:                with self.timed_encoder_operation(
3059:                    should_time, mm_lora_refs, current_item_idx, num_items
3060:                ):
3061:                    cudagraph_output = None
3062:                    if (
3063:                        self.encoder_cudagraph_manager is not None
3064:                        and self.encoder_cudagraph_manager.supports_modality(modality)
3065:                    ):
3066:                        cudagraph_output = self.encoder_cudagraph_manager.execute(
3067:                            mm_kwargs_batch,
3068:                        )
3069:
3070:                    if cudagraph_output is not None:
3071:                        batch_outputs = cudagraph_output
3072:                    else:
3073:                        batch_outputs = model.embed_multimodal(**mm_kwargs_batch)
3074:
3075:            sanity_check_mm_encoder_outputs(batch_outputs, expected_num_items=num_items)
3076:            encoder_outputs.extend(batch_outputs)
3077:
3078:            current_item_idx += num_items
3079:
3080:        # Cache the encoder outputs by mm_hash
3081:        for mm_hash, output in zip(mm_hashes, encoder_outputs):
3082:            self.encoder_cache[mm_hash] = output
3083:            logger.debug("Finish execute for mm hash %s", mm_hash)
3084:            self.maybe_save_ec_to_connector(self.encoder_cache, mm_hash)
3085:
3086:        return encoder_outputs
3087:
3088:    def _gather_mm_embeddings(
3089:        self,
3090:        scheduler_output: "SchedulerOutput",
3091:        shift_computed_tokens: int = 0,
3092:    ) -> tuple[list[torch.Tensor], torch.Tensor]:
3093:        total_num_scheduled_tokens = scheduler_output.total_num_scheduled_tokens
3094:
3095:        mm_embeds = list[torch.Tensor]()
3096:        is_mm_embed = torch.zeros(
3097:            total_num_scheduled_tokens, dtype=torch.bool, device="cpu"
3098:        )
3099:
3100:        req_start_idx = 0
3101:        should_sync_mrope_positions = False
3102:        should_sync_xdrope_positions = False
3103:
3104:        for req_id in self.input_batch.req_ids:
3105:            mm_embeds_req: list[torch.Tensor] = []
3106:
3107:            num_scheduled_tokens = scheduler_output.num_scheduled_tokens[req_id]
3108:            req_state = self.requests[req_id]
3109:            num_computed_tokens = req_state.num_computed_tokens + shift_computed_tokens
3110:
3111:            mm_features = req_state.mm_features
3112:            lo, hi = get_mm_features_in_window(
3113:                mm_features,
3114:                start=num_computed_tokens,
3115:                end=num_computed_tokens + num_scheduled_tokens,
3116:            )
3117:            for i in range(lo, hi):
3118:                mm_feature = mm_features[i]
3119:                pos_info = mm_feature.mm_position
3120:                start_pos = pos_info.offset
3121:                num_encoder_tokens = pos_info.length
3122:
3123:                start_idx = max(num_computed_tokens - start_pos, 0)
3124:                end_idx = min(
3125:                    num_computed_tokens - start_pos + num_scheduled_tokens,
3126:                    num_encoder_tokens,
3127:                )
3128:                assert start_idx < end_idx
3129:                curr_embeds_start, curr_embeds_end = (
3130:                    pos_info.get_embeds_indices_in_range(start_idx, end_idx)
3131:                )
3132:                # If there are no embeddings in the current range, we skip
3133:                # gathering the embeddings.
3134:                if curr_embeds_start == curr_embeds_end:
3135:                    continue
3136:
3137:                mm_hash = mm_feature.identifier
3138:                encoder_output = self.encoder_cache.get(mm_hash, None)
3139:                assert encoder_output is not None, f"Encoder cache miss for {mm_hash}."
3140:
3141:                if (is_embed := pos_info.is_embed) is not None:
3142:                    is_embed = is_embed[start_idx:end_idx]
3143:                    mm_embeds_item = encoder_output[curr_embeds_start:curr_embeds_end]
3144:                else:
3145:                    mm_embeds_item = encoder_output[start_idx:end_idx]
3146:
3147:                req_start_pos = req_start_idx + start_pos - num_computed_tokens
3148:                # OR mask for overlapping mm_features (use_audio_in_video)
3149:                if is_embed is None:
3150:                    is_mm_embed[req_start_pos + start_idx : req_start_pos + end_idx] = (
3151:                        True
3152:                    )
3153:                else:
3154:                    is_mm_embed[
3155:                        req_start_pos + start_idx : req_start_pos + end_idx
3156:                    ] |= is_embed
3157:                mm_embeds_req.append(mm_embeds_item)
3158:
3159:            if self.is_multimodal_pruning_enabled and self.uses_mrope:
3160:                assert req_state.mrope_positions is not None
3161:                should_sync_mrope_positions = True
3162:                mm_embeds_req, new_mrope_positions, new_delta = (
3163:                    self.model.recompute_mrope_positions(
3164:                        input_ids=req_state.prompt_token_ids,
3165:                        multimodal_embeddings=mm_embeds_req,
3166:                        mrope_positions=req_state.mrope_positions,
3167:                        num_computed_tokens=req_state.num_computed_tokens,
3168:                    )
3169:                )
3170:                req_state.mrope_positions.copy_(new_mrope_positions)
3171:                req_state.mrope_position_delta = new_delta
3172:
3173:            mm_embeds.extend(mm_embeds_req)
3174:            req_start_idx += num_scheduled_tokens
3175:
3176:        if should_sync_mrope_positions:
3177:            self._calc_mrope_positions(scheduler_output)
3178:            self.mrope_positions.copy_to_gpu(total_num_scheduled_tokens)
3179:
3180:        if should_sync_xdrope_positions:
3181:            self._calc_xdrope_positions(scheduler_output)
3182:            self.xdrope_positions.copy_to_gpu(total_num_scheduled_tokens)
3183:
3184:        return mm_embeds, is_mm_embed
3185:
3186:    def get_model(self) -> nn.Module:
3187:        if not hasattr(self, "model"):
3188:            raise ValueError("Cannot get model before model has been initialized")
3189:        if isinstance(
3190:            self.model, (CUDAGraphWrapper, UBatchWrapper, BreakableCUDAGraphWrapper)
3191:        ):
3192:            # get raw model out of the cudagraph wrapper.
3193:            return self.model.unwrap()
3194:        return self.model

3196:    def apply_sparse_weight_patches(self, patches: Iterable[SparseWeightPatch]) -> None:
3197:        """Apply sparse flat-index patches directly to existing model params."""
3198:        model = self.get_model()
3199:        for patch in patches:
3200:            param = model.get_parameter(patch.name)
3201:            if not param.data.is_contiguous():
3202:                raise NotImplementedError(
3203:                    "Sparse weight updates currently require contiguous params: "
3204:                    f"{patch.name}"
3205:                )
3206:
3207:            if patch.indices.dtype != torch.int32:
3208:                raise ValueError(
3209:                    "Sparse weight updates currently require int32 indices: "
3210:                    f"{patch.name}"
3211:                )
3212:            if patch.indices.ndim != 1 or patch.values.ndim != 1:
3213:                raise ValueError(
3214:                    f"Sparse weight patches must be 1D flattened updates: {patch.name}"
3215:                )
3216:            if patch.indices.numel() != patch.values.numel():
3217:                raise ValueError(
3218:                    "`indices` and `values` must have matching lengths for "
3219:                    f"{patch.name}"
3220:                )
3221:            if patch.values.dtype != param.dtype:
3222:                raise ValueError(
3223:                    f"Sparse values dtype {patch.values.dtype} does not match "
3224:                    f"parameter dtype {param.dtype} for {patch.name}"
3225:                )
3226:
3227:            flat_param = param.data.view(-1)
3228:            flat_param.index_copy_(
3229:                0,
3230:                patch.indices.to(device=flat_param.device, dtype=torch.long),
3231:                patch.values.to(device=flat_param.device),
3232:            )

3234:    def get_supported_generation_tasks(self) -> list[GenerationTask]:
3235:        model = self.get_model()
3236:        supported_tasks = list[GenerationTask]()
3237:
3238:        if is_text_generation_model(model):
3239:            supported_tasks.append("generate")
3240:
3241:        if supports_transcription(model):
3242:            if model.supports_transcription_only:
3243:                return ["transcription"]
3244:
3245:            supported_tasks.append("transcription")
3246:
3247:        if supports_realtime(model):
3248:            supported_tasks.append("realtime")
3249:
3250:        return supported_tasks

3252:    def get_supported_pooling_tasks(self) -> list[PoolingTask]:
3253:        model = self.get_model()
3254:        if not is_pooling_model(model):
3255:            return []
3256:
3257:        return list(model.pooler.get_supported_tasks())

3259:    def get_supported_tasks(self) -> tuple[SupportedTask, ...]:
3260:        tasks = list[SupportedTask]()
3261:
3262:        if self.model_config.runner_type == "generate":
3263:            tasks.extend(self.get_supported_generation_tasks())
3264:        if self.model_config.runner_type == "pooling":
3265:            tasks.extend(self.get_supported_pooling_tasks())
3266:
3267:        return tuple(tasks)

3269:    def sync_and_gather_intermediate_tensors(
3270:        self,
3271:        num_tokens: int,
3272:        intermediate_tensors: IntermediateTensors | None,
3273:        sync_self: bool,
3274:    ) -> IntermediateTensors:
3275:        assert self.intermediate_tensors is not None
3276:
3277:        tp = self.vllm_config.parallel_config.tensor_parallel_size
3278:        is_rs = is_residual_scattered_for_sp(self.vllm_config, num_tokens)
3279:
3280:        # When sequence parallelism is enabled, the "residual" tensor is
3281:        # sharded across TP ranks. All-gather it here because downstream
3282:        # QKV + Attention needs the full residual before the SP split point.
3283:        if sync_self:
3284:            assert intermediate_tensors is not None
3285:            for k, v in intermediate_tensors.items():
3286:                is_scattered = k == "residual" and is_rs
3287:                if is_scattered:
3288:                    local_len = num_tokens // tp
3289:                    v = get_tp_group().all_gather(v[:local_len], dim=0)
3290:
3291:                self.intermediate_tensors[k][:num_tokens].copy_(
3292:                    v[:num_tokens], non_blocking=True
3293:                )
3294:
3295:        return IntermediateTensors(
3296:            {k: v[:num_tokens] for k, v in self.intermediate_tensors.items()}
3297:        )

3299:    def eplb_step(self, is_dummy: bool = False, is_profile: bool = False) -> None:
3300:        """
3301:        Step for the EPLB (Expert Parallelism Load Balancing) state.
3302:        """
3303:        if not self.parallel_config.enable_eplb or self.eep_eplb_suppressed:
3304:            return
3305:
3306:        assert self.eplb_state is not None
3307:        assert self._moe_model is not None
3308:        self.eplb_state.step(
3309:            is_dummy,
3310:            is_profile,
3311:            log_stats=self.parallel_config.eplb_config.log_balancedness,
3312:        )

3330:    def _pool(
3331:        self,
3332:        hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
3333:        num_scheduled_tokens: int,
3334:        num_scheduled_tokens_np: np.ndarray,
3335:        kv_connector_output: KVConnectorOutput | None,
3336:    ) -> ModelRunnerOutput | AsyncModelRunnerOutput:
3337:        num_reqs = self.input_batch.num_reqs
3338:        assert num_reqs == len(self.input_batch.pooling_params), (
3339:            "Either all or none of the requests in a batch must be pooling request"
3340:        )
3341:
3342:        hidden_states = hidden_states[:num_scheduled_tokens]
3343:        seq_lens_cpu = self.optimistic_seq_lens_cpu[:num_reqs]
3344:
3345:        pooling_metadata = self.input_batch.get_pooling_metadata()
3346:        pooling_metadata.build_pooling_cursor(
3347:            num_scheduled_tokens_np,
3348:            seq_lens_cpu,
3349:            device=hidden_states.device,
3350:            query_start_loc_gpu=self.query_start_loc.gpu[: num_reqs + 1],
3351:        )
3352:
3353:        model = cast(VllmModelForPooling, self.model)
3354:        raw_pooler_output: PoolerOutput = model.pooler(
3355:            hidden_states=hidden_states, pooling_metadata=pooling_metadata
3356:        )
3357:
3358:        finished_mask = [
3359:            seq_len == prompt_len
3360:            for seq_len, prompt_len in zip(seq_lens_cpu, pooling_metadata.prompt_lens)
3361:        ]
3362:        raw_pooler_output = self.late_interaction_runner.postprocess_pooler_output(
3363:            raw_pooler_output=raw_pooler_output,
3364:            pooling_params=pooling_metadata.pooling_params,
3365:            req_ids=self.input_batch.req_ids,
3366:            finished_mask=finished_mask,
3367:        )
3368:
3369:        model_runner_output = ModelRunnerOutput(
3370:            req_ids=self.input_batch.req_ids.copy(),
3371:            req_id_to_index=self.input_batch.req_id_to_index.copy(),
3372:            kv_connector_output=kv_connector_output,
3373:        )
3374:
3375:        if raw_pooler_output is None or not any(finished_mask):
3376:            model_runner_output.pooler_output = [None] * num_reqs
3377:            return model_runner_output
3378:
3379:        if not current_platform.is_cuda_alike():
3380:            # cpu/xpu runners cannot use the CUDA stream/event-based wrapper.
3381:            model_runner_output.pooler_output = _copy_pooler_output_to_cpu(
3382:                raw_pooler_output=raw_pooler_output,
3383:                finished_mask=finished_mask,
3384:            )
3385:            self._sync_device()
3386:            return model_runner_output
3387:
3388:        return AsyncGPUPoolingModelRunnerOutput(
3389:            model_runner_output=model_runner_output,
3390:            raw_pooler_output=raw_pooler_output,
3391:            finished_mask=finished_mask,
3392:            async_output_copy_stream=self._get_or_create_async_output_copy_stream(),
3393:        )

3395:    def _pad_for_sequence_parallelism(self, num_scheduled_tokens: int) -> int:
3396:        # Pad tokens to multiple of tensor_parallel_size when
3397:        # enabled collective fusion for SP
3398:        tp_size = self.vllm_config.parallel_config.tensor_parallel_size
3399:        if self.compilation_config.pass_config.enable_sp and tp_size > 1:
3400:            return round_up(num_scheduled_tokens, tp_size)
3401:        return num_scheduled_tokens
3402:
3403:    def _prepare_mm_inputs(
3404:        self, num_tokens: int
3405:    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor | None, torch.Tensor]:
3406:        if self.model.requires_raw_input_tokens:
3407:            input_ids = self.input_ids.gpu[:num_tokens]
3408:        else:
3409:            input_ids = None
3410:
3411:        inputs_embeds = self.inputs_embeds.gpu[:num_tokens]
3412:        return input_ids, inputs_embeds
3413:
3414:    def _preprocess(
3415:        self,
3416:        scheduler_output: "SchedulerOutput",
3417:        num_input_tokens: int,  # Padded
3418:        intermediate_tensors: IntermediateTensors | None = None,
3419:    ) -> tuple[
3420:        torch.Tensor | None,
3421:        torch.Tensor | None,
3422:        torch.Tensor,
3423:        IntermediateTensors | None,
3424:        dict[str, Any],
3425:        ECConnectorOutput | None,
3426:    ]:
3427:        num_scheduled_tokens = scheduler_output.total_num_scheduled_tokens
3428:        is_first_rank = get_pp_group().is_first_rank
3429:        is_encoder_decoder = self.model_config.is_encoder_decoder
3430:
3431:        # _prepare_inputs may reorder the batch, so we must gather multi
3432:        # modal outputs after that to ensure the correct order
3433:        ec_connector_output = None
3434:
3435:        if self.supports_mm_inputs and is_first_rank and not is_encoder_decoder:
3436:            # Run the multimodal encoder if any.
3437:            with self.maybe_get_ec_connector_output(
3438:                scheduler_output,
3439:                encoder_cache=self.encoder_cache,
3440:            ) as ec_connector_output:
3441:                self._execute_mm_encoder(scheduler_output)
3442:                mm_embeds, is_mm_embed = self._gather_mm_embeddings(scheduler_output)
3443:
3444:            # NOTE(woosuk): To unify token ids and soft tokens (vision
3445:            # embeddings), we always use embeddings (rather than token ids)
3446:            # as input to the multimodal model, even when the input is text.
3447:            inputs_embeds_scheduled = self.model.embed_input_ids(
3448:                self.input_ids.gpu[:num_scheduled_tokens],
3449:                multimodal_embeddings=mm_embeds,
3450:                is_multimodal=is_mm_embed,
3451:            )
3452:
3453:            # TODO(woosuk): Avoid the copy. Optimize.
3454:            self.inputs_embeds.gpu[:num_scheduled_tokens].copy_(inputs_embeds_scheduled)
3455:
3456:            input_ids, inputs_embeds = self._prepare_mm_inputs(num_input_tokens)
3457:            model_kwargs = {
3458:                **self._init_model_kwargs(),
3459:                **self._extract_mm_kwargs(scheduler_output),
3460:            }
3461:        elif self.enable_prompt_embeds and is_first_rank:
3462:            # Get the input embeddings for the tokens that are not input embeds,
3463:            # then put them into the appropriate positions.
3464:            # TODO(qthequartermasterman): Since even when prompt embeds are
3465:            # enabled, (a) not all requests will use prompt embeds, and (b)
3466:            # after the initial prompt is processed, the rest of the generated
3467:            # tokens will be token ids, it is not desirable to have the
3468:            # embedding layer outside of the CUDA graph all the time. The v0
3469:            # engine avoids this by "double compiling" the CUDA graph, once
