3470:            # with input_ids and again with inputs_embeds, for all num_tokens.
3471:            # If a batch only has token ids, then including the embedding layer
3472:            # in the CUDA graph will be more performant (like in the else case
3473:            # below).
3474:            is_token_ids = self.is_token_ids.np[:num_scheduled_tokens]
3475:            token_ids_idx_np = np.nonzero(is_token_ids)[0]
3476:            # Some tokens ids may need to become embeds
3477:            if token_ids_idx_np.size > 0:
3478:                token_ids_idx = torch.from_numpy(token_ids_idx_np)
3479:                token_ids_idx = token_ids_idx.to(self.device, non_blocking=True)
3480:                token_ids = self.input_ids.gpu[token_ids_idx]
3481:                tokens_to_embeds = self.model.embed_input_ids(input_ids=token_ids)
3482:                self.inputs_embeds.gpu[token_ids_idx] = tokens_to_embeds
3483:
3484:            inputs_embeds = self.inputs_embeds.gpu[:num_input_tokens]
3485:            model_kwargs = self._init_model_kwargs()
3486:            input_ids = None
3487:        else:
3488:            # For text-only models, we use token ids as input.
3489:            # While it is possible to use embeddings as input just like the
3490:            # multimodal models, it is not desirable for performance since
3491:            # then the embedding layer is not included in the CUDA graph.
3492:            input_ids = self.input_ids.gpu[:num_input_tokens]
3493:            inputs_embeds = None
3494:            model_kwargs = self._init_model_kwargs()
3495:
3496:        if self.uses_mrope:
3497:            positions = self.mrope_positions.gpu[:, :num_input_tokens]
3498:        elif self.uses_xdrope_dim > 0:
3499:            positions = self.xdrope_positions.gpu[:, :num_input_tokens]
3500:        else:
3501:            positions = self.positions[:num_input_tokens]
3502:            if num_input_tokens > num_scheduled_tokens:
3503:                self.positions[num_scheduled_tokens:num_input_tokens].zero_()
3504:
3505:        if is_first_rank:
3506:            intermediate_tensors = None
3507:        else:
3508:            assert intermediate_tensors is not None
3509:            intermediate_tensors = self.sync_and_gather_intermediate_tensors(
3510:                num_input_tokens, intermediate_tensors, True
3511:            )
3512:
3513:        if is_encoder_decoder and scheduler_output.scheduled_encoder_inputs:
3514:            # Run the encoder, just like we do with other multimodal inputs.
3515:            # For an encoder-decoder model, our processing here is a bit
3516:            # simpler, because the outputs are just passed to the decoder.
3517:            # We are not doing any prompt replacement. We also will only
3518:            # ever have a single encoder input.
3519:            encoder_outputs = self._execute_mm_encoder(scheduler_output)
3520:            model_kwargs.update({"encoder_outputs": encoder_outputs})
3521:
3522:        return (
3523:            input_ids,
3524:            inputs_embeds,
3525:            positions,
3526:            intermediate_tensors,
3527:            model_kwargs,
3528:            ec_connector_output,
3529:        )
3530:
3531:    def _sample(
3532:        self,
3533:        logits: torch.Tensor | None,
3534:        spec_decode_metadata: SpecDecodeMetadata | None,
3535:    ) -> SamplerOutput:
3536:        # Sample the next token and get logprobs if needed.
3537:        sampling_metadata = self.input_batch.sampling_metadata
3538:        # Update output token ids with tokens sampled in last step
3539:        # if async scheduling and required by current sampling params.
3540:        self.input_batch.update_async_output_token_ids()
3541:        if spec_decode_metadata is None:
3542:            return self.sampler(
3543:                logits=logits,
3544:                sampling_metadata=sampling_metadata,
3545:            )
3546:
3547:        # Update spec_token_ids with real draft tokens from pre step only when
3548:        # output_token_ids is needed (penalties or bad_words are in use).
3549:        if self.use_async_scheduling and self._draft_token_req_ids is not None:
3550:            draft_token_ids_cpu, _ = self._get_draft_token_ids_cpu()
3551:            self.input_batch.update_async_spec_token_ids(draft_token_ids_cpu)
3552:
3553:        draft_probs = self._get_spec_decode_draft_probs(spec_decode_metadata)
3554:        sampler_output = self.rejection_sampler(
3555:            spec_decode_metadata,
3556:            draft_probs,
3557:            logits,
3558:            sampling_metadata,
3559:        )
3560:        return sampler_output
3561:
3562:    def _bookkeeping_sync(
3563:        self,
3564:        scheduler_output: "SchedulerOutput",
3565:        sampler_output: SamplerOutput,
3566:        logits: torch.Tensor | None,
3567:        hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
3568:        num_scheduled_tokens: int,
3569:    ) -> tuple[
3570:        dict[str, int],
3571:        LogprobsLists | None,
3572:        list[list[int]],
3573:        dict[str, LogprobsTensors | None],
3574:        list[str],
3575:        dict[str, int],
3576:        list[int],
3577:    ]:
3578:        num_nans_in_logits = {}
3579:        if envs.VLLM_COMPUTE_NANS_IN_LOGITS:
3580:            num_nans_in_logits = self._get_nans_in_logits(logits)
3581:
3582:        num_reqs = self.input_batch.num_reqs
3583:        discard_sampled_tokens_req_indices = np.nonzero(
3584:            self.discard_request_mask.np[:num_reqs]
3585:        )[0]
3586:        for i in discard_sampled_tokens_req_indices:
3587:            gen = self.input_batch.generators.get(int(i))
3588:            if gen is not None:
3589:                gen.set_offset(gen.get_offset() - 4)
3590:
3591:        # Copy some objects so they don't get modified after returning.
3592:        # This is important when using async scheduling.
3593:        req_ids_output_copy = self.input_batch.req_ids.copy()
3594:        req_id_to_index_output_copy = self.input_batch.req_id_to_index.copy()
3595:
3596:        num_sampled_tokens = sampler_output.sampled_token_ids.shape[0]
3597:        sampled_token_ids = sampler_output.sampled_token_ids
3598:        logprobs_tensors = sampler_output.logprobs_tensors
3599:        invalid_req_indices = []
3600:        logprobs_lists = None
3601:        if not self.use_async_scheduling:
3602:            # Sync scheduling: issue routed experts D2H into the pinned
3603:            # CPU buffer BEFORE ``_to_list`` below. ``_to_list`` does
3604:            # ``event.synchronize()`` on the async copy stream which
3605:            # waits for every D2H queued on the default stream since
3606:            # the last sync, so this enqueue is naturally covered
3607:            # without requiring its own synchronize.
3608:            if self.routed_experts_initialized:
3609:                buf = self.routed_experts_capturer.get_device_buffer()
3610:                total = scheduler_output.total_num_scheduled_tokens
3611:                self.routed_experts_cpu[:total].copy_(buf[:total], non_blocking=True)
3612:                self.routed_experts_slot_mapping_cpu[:total].copy_(
3613:                    self.routed_experts_slot_mapping_device[:total],
3614:                    non_blocking=True,
3615:                )
3616:
3617:            # Get the valid generated tokens.
3618:            max_gen_len = sampled_token_ids.shape[-1]
3619:            if max_gen_len == 1:
3620:                # No spec decode tokens.
3621:                valid_sampled_token_ids = self._to_list(sampled_token_ids)
3622:                # Mask out the sampled tokens that should not be sampled.
3623:                for i in discard_sampled_tokens_req_indices:
3624:                    valid_sampled_token_ids[int(i)].clear()
3625:
3626:                if logprobs_tensors is not None:
3627:                    logprobs_lists = logprobs_tensors.tolists()
3628:            else:
3629:                # Includes spec decode tokens.
3630:                valid_sampled_token_ids, logprobs_lists = RejectionSampler.parse_output(
3631:                    sampled_token_ids,
3632:                    self.input_batch.vocab_size,
3633:                    discard_sampled_tokens_req_indices,
3634:                    logprobs_tensors=logprobs_tensors,
3635:                )
3636:        else:
3637:            valid_sampled_token_ids = []
3638:            invalid_req_indices = discard_sampled_tokens_req_indices.tolist()
3639:            invalid_req_indices_set = set(invalid_req_indices)
3640:
3641:            # Cache the sampled tokens on the GPU and avoid CPU sync.
3642:            # These will be copied into input_ids in the next step
3643:            # when preparing inputs.
3644:            # With spec decoding, this is done in propose_draft_token_ids().
3645:            if self.input_batch.prev_sampled_token_ids is None:
3646:                assert sampled_token_ids.shape[-1] == 1
3647:                self.input_batch.prev_sampled_token_ids = sampled_token_ids
3648:            self.input_batch.prev_req_id_to_index = {
3649:                req_id: i
3650:                for i, req_id in enumerate(self.input_batch.req_ids)
3651:                if i not in invalid_req_indices_set
3652:            }
3653:
3654:        # Cache the sampled tokens in the model runner, so that the scheduler
3655:        # doesn't need to send them back.
3656:        # NOTE(woosuk): As an exception, when using PP, the scheduler sends
3657:        # the sampled tokens back, because there's no direct communication
3658:        # between the first-stage worker and the last-stage worker.
3659:        req_ids = self.input_batch.req_ids
3660:        for req_idx in range(num_sampled_tokens):
3661:            if self.use_async_scheduling:
3662:                sampled_ids = [-1] if req_idx not in invalid_req_indices_set else None
3663:            else:
3664:                sampled_ids = valid_sampled_token_ids[req_idx]
3665:
3666:            num_sampled_ids: int = len(sampled_ids) if sampled_ids else 0
3667:
3668:            if not sampled_ids:
3669:                continue
3670:
3671:            start_idx = self.input_batch.num_tokens_no_spec[req_idx]
3672:            end_idx = start_idx + num_sampled_ids
3673:            assert end_idx <= self.max_model_len, (
3674:                "Sampled token IDs exceed the max model length. "
3675:                f"Total number of tokens: {end_idx} > max_model_len: "
3676:                f"{self.max_model_len}"
3677:            )
3678:
3679:            self.input_batch.token_ids_cpu[req_idx, start_idx:end_idx] = sampled_ids
3680:            self.input_batch.is_token_ids[req_idx, start_idx:end_idx] = True
3681:            self.input_batch.num_tokens_no_spec[req_idx] = end_idx
3682:
3683:            req_id = req_ids[req_idx]
3684:            req_state = self.requests[req_id]
3685:            req_state.output_token_ids.extend(sampled_ids)
3686:
3687:        # Compute prompt logprobs if needed.
3688:        prompt_logprobs_dict = self._get_prompt_logprobs_dict(
3689:            hidden_states[:num_scheduled_tokens],
3690:            scheduler_output.num_scheduled_tokens,
3691:        )
3692:
3693:        return (
3694:            num_nans_in_logits,
3695:            logprobs_lists,
3696:            valid_sampled_token_ids,
3697:            prompt_logprobs_dict,
3698:            req_ids_output_copy,
3699:            req_id_to_index_output_copy,
3700:            invalid_req_indices,
3701:        )
3702:
3703:    @contextmanager
3704:    def synchronize_input_prep(self):
3705:        if self.prepare_inputs_event is None:
3706:            yield
3707:            return
3708:
3709:        # Ensure prior step has finished with reused CPU tensors.
3710:        # This is required in the async scheduling case because
3711:        # the CPU->GPU transfer happens async.
3712:        self.prepare_inputs_event.synchronize()
3713:        try:
3714:            yield
3715:        finally:
3716:            self.prepare_inputs_event.record()
3717:
3718:    def _model_forward(
3719:        self,
3720:        input_ids: torch.Tensor | None = None,
3721:        positions: torch.Tensor | None = None,
3722:        intermediate_tensors: IntermediateTensors | None = None,
3723:        inputs_embeds: torch.Tensor | None = None,
3724:        **model_kwargs: dict[str, Any],
3725:    ) -> Any:
3726:        """Helper method to call the model forward pass.
3727:
3728:        This method can be overridden by subclasses for model execution.
3729:        Motivation: We can inspect only this method versus
3730:        the whole execute_model, which has additional logic.
3731:
3732:        Args:
3733:            input_ids: Input token IDs
3734:            positions: Token positions
3735:            intermediate_tensors: Tensors from previous pipeline stages
3736:            inputs_embeds: Input embeddings (alternative to input_ids)
3737:            **model_kwargs: Additional model arguments
3738:
3739:        Returns:
3740:            Model output tensor
3741:        """
3742:        return self.model(
3743:            input_ids=input_ids,
3744:            positions=positions,
3745:            intermediate_tensors=intermediate_tensors,
3746:            inputs_embeds=inputs_embeds,
3747:            **model_kwargs,
3748:        )
3749:
3750:    @staticmethod
3751:    def _is_uniform_decode(
3752:        max_num_scheduled_tokens: int,
3753:        uniform_decode_query_len: int,
3754:        num_tokens: int,
3755:        num_reqs: int,
3756:        force_uniform_decode: bool | None = None,
3757:    ) -> bool:
3758:        """
3759:        Checks if it's a decode batch with same amount scheduled tokens
3760:        across all requests.
3761:        """
3762:        return (
3763:            (
3764:                (max_num_scheduled_tokens == uniform_decode_query_len)
3765:                and (num_tokens == max_num_scheduled_tokens * num_reqs)
3766:            )
3767:            if force_uniform_decode is None
3768:            else force_uniform_decode
3769:        )
3770:
3771:    def _determine_batch_execution_and_padding(
3772:        self,
3773:        num_tokens: int,
3774:        num_reqs: int,
3775:        num_scheduled_tokens_np: np.ndarray,
3776:        max_num_scheduled_tokens: int,
3777:        use_cascade_attn: bool,
3778:        allow_microbatching: bool = True,
3779:        force_eager: bool = False,
3780:        # For cudagraph capture TODO(lucas): Refactor how we capture cudagraphs (will
3781:        # be improved in model runner v2)
3782:        force_uniform_decode: bool | None = None,
3783:        force_has_lora: bool | None = None,
3784:        force_num_active_loras: int | None = None,
3785:        num_encoder_reqs: int = 0,
3786:    ) -> tuple[
3787:        CUDAGraphMode,
3788:        BatchDescriptor,
3789:        bool,
3790:        torch.Tensor | None,
3791:        CUDAGraphStat | None,
3792:    ]:
3793:        uniform_decode = self._is_uniform_decode(
3794:            max_num_scheduled_tokens=max_num_scheduled_tokens,
3795:            uniform_decode_query_len=self.uniform_decode_query_len,
3796:            num_tokens=num_tokens,
3797:            num_reqs=num_reqs,
3798:            force_uniform_decode=force_uniform_decode,
3799:        )
3800:        # Encoder-decoder models only support CG for decoder_step > 0 (no enc_output
3801:        # is present). Also, chunked-prefill is disabled, so batch are uniform.
3802:        has_encoder_output = (
3803:            self.model_config.is_encoder_decoder and num_encoder_reqs > 0
3804:        )
3805:
3806:        # Compute LoRA state for cudagraph dispatch
3807:        num_active_loras = (
3808:            force_num_active_loras
3809:            if force_num_active_loras is not None
3810:            else len(self.input_batch.lora_id_to_lora_request)
3811:        )
3812:        has_lora = num_active_loras > 0 if force_has_lora is None else force_has_lora
3813:
3814:        num_tokens_padded = self._pad_for_sequence_parallelism(num_tokens)
3815:
3816:        def dispatch_cudagraph(num_tokens, disable_full=False, valid_modes=None):
3817:            return self.cudagraph_dispatcher.dispatch(
3818:                num_tokens=num_tokens,
3819:                has_lora=has_lora,
3820:                uniform_decode=uniform_decode,
3821:                num_active_loras=num_active_loras,
3822:                valid_modes={CUDAGraphMode.NONE} if force_eager else valid_modes,
3823:                invalid_modes={CUDAGraphMode.FULL} if disable_full else None,
3824:            )
3825:
3826:        cudagraph_mode, batch_descriptor = dispatch_cudagraph(
3827:            num_tokens_padded, disable_full=use_cascade_attn or has_encoder_output
3828:        )
3829:        num_tokens_padded = batch_descriptor.num_tokens
3830:        if self.compilation_config.pass_config.enable_sp:
3831:            assert (
3832:                batch_descriptor.num_tokens
3833:                % self.vllm_config.parallel_config.tensor_parallel_size
3834:                == 0
3835:            ), (
3836:                "Sequence parallelism requires num_tokens to be "
3837:                "a multiple of tensor parallel size"
3838:            )
3839:
3840:        # Extra coordination when running data-parallel since we need to coordinate
3841:        # across ranks
3842:        should_ubatch, num_tokens_across_dp = False, None
3843:        if self.vllm_config.parallel_config.data_parallel_size > 1:
3844:            should_ubatch, num_tokens_across_dp, synced_cudagraph_mode = (
3845:                coordinate_batch_across_dp(
3846:                    num_tokens_unpadded=num_tokens,
3847:                    parallel_config=self.parallel_config,
3848:                    allow_microbatching=allow_microbatching,
3849:                    num_tokens_padded=num_tokens_padded,
3850:                    uniform_decode=uniform_decode,
3851:                    cudagraph_mode=cudagraph_mode.value,
3852:                )
3853:            )
3854:
3855:            # Extract DP-synced values
3856:            if num_tokens_across_dp is not None:
3857:                dp_rank = self.parallel_config.data_parallel_rank
3858:                num_tokens_padded = int(num_tokens_across_dp[dp_rank].item())
3859:                # Re-dispatch with DP padding so we have the correct batch_descriptor
3860:                cudagraph_mode, batch_descriptor = dispatch_cudagraph(
3861:                    num_tokens_padded,
3862:                    valid_modes={CUDAGraphMode(synced_cudagraph_mode)},
3863:                )
3864:                # Assert to make sure the agreed upon token count is correct otherwise
3865:                # num_tokens_across_dp will no-longer be valid
3866:                assert batch_descriptor.num_tokens == num_tokens_padded
3867:
3868:        cudagraph_stats = None
3869:        if self.vllm_config.observability_config.cudagraph_metrics:
3870:            cudagraph_stats = CUDAGraphStat(
3871:                num_unpadded_tokens=num_tokens,
3872:                num_padded_tokens=batch_descriptor.num_tokens,
3873:                num_paddings=batch_descriptor.num_tokens - num_tokens,
3874:                runtime_mode=str(cudagraph_mode),
3875:            )
3876:
3877:        return (
3878:            cudagraph_mode,
3879:            batch_descriptor,
3880:            should_ubatch,
3881:            num_tokens_across_dp,
3882:            cudagraph_stats,
3883:        )
3884:
3885:    def _register_layerwise_nvtx_hooks(self) -> None:
3886:        """
3887:        Register layerwise NVTX hooks if --enable-layerwise-nvtx-tracing is enabled
3888:        to trace detailed information of each layer or module in the model.
3889:        """
3890:
3891:        if (
3892:            self.vllm_config.observability_config.enable_layerwise_nvtx_tracing
3893:            and not self.layerwise_nvtx_hooks_registered
3894:        ):
3895:            if self.compilation_config.cudagraph_mode != CUDAGraphMode.NONE:
3896:                logger.debug_once(
3897:                    "layerwise NVTX tracing is not supported when CUDA graph is "
3898:                    "turned off; you may observe part or all of the model "
3899:                    "missing NVTX markers"
3900:                )
3901:
3902:            # In STOCK_TORCH_COMPILE mode, after registering hooks here,
3903:            # the __call__ function of nn.module will be recompiled with
3904:            # fullgraph=True. Since nvtx.range_push/pop are not traceable
3905:            # by torch dynamo, we can't register hook functions here
3906:            # because hook functions will also be traced by torch dynamo.
3907:            if (
3908:                self.vllm_config.compilation_config.mode
3909:                == CompilationMode.STOCK_TORCH_COMPILE
3910:            ):
3911:                logger.debug_once(
3912:                    "layerwise NVTX tracing is not supported when "
3913:                    "CompilationMode is STOCK_TORCH_COMPILE, skipping "
3914:                    "function hooks registration"
3915:                )
3916:            else:
3917:                pyt_hooks = PytHooks()
3918:                pyt_hooks.register_hooks(self.model, self.model.__class__.__name__)
3919:                self.layerwise_nvtx_hooks_registered = True
3920:
3921:    def _get_slot_mappings(
3922:        self,
3923:        num_tokens_padded: int,
3924:        num_reqs_padded: int,
3925:        num_tokens_unpadded: int,
3926:        ubatch_slices: "UBatchSlices | None" = None,
3927:    ) -> tuple[
3928:        dict[int, torch.Tensor] | None,
3929:        dict[str, torch.Tensor] | list[dict[str, torch.Tensor]] | None,
3930:    ]:
3931:        """
3932:        Build slot mappings in both formats needed by the system.
3933:
3934:        Args:
3935:            num_tokens_padded: Total number of tokens (padded)
3936:            num_reqs_padded: Total number of requests (padded)
3937:            num_tokens_unpadded: Actual number of tokens (unpadded)
3938:            ubatch_slices: Optional ubatch slicing info for DBO
3939:
3940:        Returns:
3941:            A tuple of:
3942:            - slot_mappings_by_gid: dict[int, torch.Tensor] for attention metadata
3943:            - slot_mappings_by_layer: dict[str, torch.Tensor] or list for ForwardContext
3944:        """
3945:        if not (
3946:            hasattr(self, "kv_cache_config")
3947:            and self.kv_cache_config is not None
3948:            and len(self.kv_cache_config.kv_cache_groups) > 0
3949:        ):
3950:            return None, None
3951:
3952:        def _get_slot_mapping(kv_cache_gid: int):
3953:            assert num_reqs_padded is not None and num_tokens_padded is not None
3954:            kv_cache_spec = self.kv_cache_config.kv_cache_groups[
3955:                kv_cache_gid
3956:            ].kv_cache_spec
3957:            if isinstance(kv_cache_spec, EncoderOnlyAttentionSpec):
3958:                slot_mapping = torch.zeros(
3959:                    (num_tokens_padded,),
3960:                    dtype=torch.int64,
3961:                    device=self.device,
3962:                )
3963:            else:
3964:                blk_table = self.input_batch.block_table[kv_cache_gid]
3965:                slot_mapping = blk_table.slot_mapping.gpu[:num_tokens_padded]
3966:
3967:            # Fill unused with -1. Needed for reshape_and_cache in full cuda
3968:            # graph mode. `blk_table_tensor` -1 to match mamba PAD_SLOT_ID
3969:            slot_mapping[num_tokens_unpadded:num_tokens_padded].fill_(-1)
3970:
3971:            return slot_mapping
3972:
3973:        slot_mappings_by_gid = {
3974:            gid: _get_slot_mapping(gid)
3975:            for gid, _ in enumerate(self.kv_cache_config.kv_cache_groups)
3976:        }
3977:
3978:        slot_mappings_by_layer: dict[str, torch.Tensor] = {}
3979:        for gid, kv_cache_group in enumerate(self.kv_cache_config.kv_cache_groups):
3980:            slot_mapping = slot_mappings_by_gid[gid]
3981:            for layer_name in kv_cache_group.layer_names:
3982:                slot_mappings_by_layer[layer_name] = slot_mapping
3983:
3984:        if ubatch_slices is not None:
3985:            result: list[dict[str, torch.Tensor]] = []
3986:            for ubatch in ubatch_slices:
3987:                sliced_mappings: dict[str, torch.Tensor] = {}
3988:                for layer_name, slot_mapping in slot_mappings_by_layer.items():
3989:                    sliced_mappings[layer_name] = slot_mapping[ubatch.token_slice]
3990:                result.append(sliced_mappings)
3991:            return slot_mappings_by_gid, result
3992:
3993:        return slot_mappings_by_gid, slot_mappings_by_layer
3994:
3995:    def _is_all_reqs_chunked_prefill(self) -> bool:
3996:        """Check if all scheduled requests are marked to discard sampled tokens.
3997:
3998:        This is true when `discard_request_mask` is set for every scheduled
3999:        request (e.g., for chunked prefill requests that are not the last
4000:        prefill chunk)."""
4001:        num_reqs = self.input_batch.num_reqs
4002:        return bool(self.discard_request_mask.np[:num_reqs].all())
4003:
4004:    @torch.inference_mode()
4005:    def execute_model(
4006:        self,
4007:        scheduler_output: "SchedulerOutput",
4008:        intermediate_tensors: IntermediateTensors | None = None,
4009:    ) -> ModelRunnerOutput | AsyncModelRunnerOutput | IntermediateTensors | None:
4010:        if self.execute_model_state is not None:
4011:            raise RuntimeError(
4012:                "State error: sample_tokens() must be called "
4013:                "after execute_model() returns None."
4014:            )
4015:
4016:        if self.routed_experts_initialized:
4017:            self.routed_experts_capturer.clear_buffer()
4018:
4019:        # If ngram_gpu is used, we need to copy the scheduler_output to avoid
4020:        # the modification has influence on the scheduler_output in engine core process.
4021:        # The replace is much faster than deepcopy.
4022:        if (
4023:            self.speculative_config is not None
4024:            and self.speculative_config.use_ngram_gpu()
4025:        ):
4026:            num_scheduled_tokens_copy = scheduler_output.num_scheduled_tokens.copy()
4027:            spec_decode_tokens_copy = (
4028:                scheduler_output.scheduled_spec_decode_tokens.copy()
4029:            )
4030:            scheduler_output = replace(
4031:                scheduler_output,
4032:                num_scheduled_tokens=num_scheduled_tokens_copy,
4033:                scheduled_spec_decode_tokens=spec_decode_tokens_copy,
4034:            )
4035:
4036:        if has_kv_transfer_group():
4037:            kv_connector_metadata = scheduler_output.kv_connector_metadata
4038:            assert kv_connector_metadata is not None
4039:            get_kv_transfer_group().handle_preemptions(kv_connector_metadata)
4040:
4041:        num_scheduled_tokens = scheduler_output.total_num_scheduled_tokens
4042:        with (
4043:            record_function_or_nullcontext("gpu_model_runner: preprocess"),
4044:            self.synchronize_input_prep(),
4045:        ):
4046:            # Update persistent batch states.
4047:            deferred_state_corrections_fn = self._update_states(scheduler_output)
4048:
4049:            if has_ec_transfer() and not get_ec_transfer().is_consumer:
4050:                with self.maybe_get_ec_connector_output(
4051:                    scheduler_output,
4052:                    encoder_cache=self.encoder_cache,
4053:                ) as ec_connector_output:
4054:                    self._execute_mm_encoder(scheduler_output)
4055:                    return make_empty_encoder_model_runner_output(scheduler_output)
4056:
4057:            if not num_scheduled_tokens:
4058:                if (
4059:                    self.parallel_config.distributed_executor_backend
4060:                    == "external_launcher"
4061:                    and self.parallel_config.data_parallel_size > 1
4062:                ):
4063:                    # this is a corner case when both external launcher
4064:                    # and DP are enabled, num_scheduled_tokens could be
4065:                    # 0, and has_unfinished_requests in the outer loop
4066:                    # returns True. before returning early here we call
4067:                    # dummy run to ensure coordinate_batch_across_dp
4068:                    # is called into to avoid out of sync issues.
4069:                    self._dummy_run(1)
4070:                if not has_kv_transfer_group():
4071:                    # Return empty ModelRunnerOutput if no work to do.
4072:                    return EMPTY_MODEL_RUNNER_OUTPUT
4073:                return self.kv_connector_no_forward(scheduler_output, self.vllm_config)
4074:
4075:            if self.cache_config.kv_sharing_fast_prefill:
4076:                assert not self.num_prompt_logprobs, (
4077:                    "--kv-sharing-fast-prefill produces incorrect "
4078:                    "logprobs for prompt tokens, tokens, please disable "
4079:                    "it when the requests need prompt logprobs"
4080:                )
4081:
4082:            num_reqs = self.input_batch.num_reqs
4083:            req_ids = self.input_batch.req_ids
4084:            tokens = [scheduler_output.num_scheduled_tokens[i] for i in req_ids]
4085:            num_scheduled_tokens_np = np.array(tokens, dtype=np.int32)
4086:            max_num_scheduled_tokens = int(num_scheduled_tokens_np.max())
4087:            num_tokens_unpadded = scheduler_output.total_num_scheduled_tokens
4088:
4089:            logits_indices, spec_decode_metadata = self._prepare_inputs(
4090:                scheduler_output,
4091:                num_scheduled_tokens_np,
4092:            )
4093:
4094:            cascade_attn_prefix_lens = None
4095:            # Disable cascade attention when using microbatching (DBO)
4096:            if self.cascade_attn_enabled and not self.parallel_config.use_ubatching:
4097:                # Pre-compute cascade attention prefix lengths
4098:                cascade_attn_prefix_lens = self._compute_cascade_attn_prefix_lens(
4099:                    num_scheduled_tokens_np,
4100:                    self.input_batch.num_computed_tokens_cpu[:num_reqs],
4101:                    scheduler_output.num_common_prefix_blocks,
4102:                )
4103:
4104:            (
4105:                cudagraph_mode,
4106:                batch_desc,
4107:                should_ubatch,
4108:                num_tokens_across_dp,
4109:                cudagraph_stats,
4110:            ) = self._determine_batch_execution_and_padding(
4111:                num_tokens=num_tokens_unpadded,
4112:                num_reqs=num_reqs,
4113:                num_scheduled_tokens_np=num_scheduled_tokens_np,
4114:                max_num_scheduled_tokens=max_num_scheduled_tokens,
4115:                use_cascade_attn=cascade_attn_prefix_lens is not None,
4116:                num_encoder_reqs=len(scheduler_output.scheduled_encoder_inputs),
4117:            )
4118:
4119:            logger.debug(
4120:                "Running batch with cudagraph_mode: %s, batch_descriptor: %s, "
4121:                "should_ubatch: %s, num_tokens_across_dp: %s",
4122:                cudagraph_mode,
4123:                batch_desc,
4124:                should_ubatch,
4125:                num_tokens_across_dp,
4126:            )
4127:
4128:            num_tokens_padded = batch_desc.num_tokens
4129:            num_reqs_padded = (
4130:                batch_desc.num_reqs if batch_desc.num_reqs is not None else num_reqs
4131:            )
4132:            ubatch_slices, ubatch_slices_padded = maybe_create_ubatch_slices(
4133:                should_ubatch,
4134:                num_scheduled_tokens_np,
4135:                num_tokens_padded,
4136:                num_reqs_padded,
4137:                self.parallel_config.num_ubatches,
4138:            )
4139:
4140:            logger.debug(
4141:                "ubatch_slices: %s, ubatch_slices_padded: %s",
4142:                ubatch_slices,
4143:                ubatch_slices_padded,
4144:            )
4145:
4146:            # True if any attention backend handles KV cache update separately
4147:            # from forward() (i.e., forward_includes_kv_cache_update=False). When true,
4148:            # slot_mappings must use padded dimensions to match the key/value tensors.
4149:            has_separate_kv_update = not all(
4150:                all(
4151:                    g.backend.forward_includes_kv_cache_update
4152:                    for g in self.attn_groups[id]
4153:                )
4154:                for id, spec in enumerate(self.kv_cache_config.kv_cache_groups)
4155:                if not isinstance(spec.kv_cache_spec, EncoderOnlyAttentionSpec)
4156:            )
4157:            pad_attn = cudagraph_mode == CUDAGraphMode.FULL
4158:
4159:            if self.cache_config.mamba_cache_mode == "align":
4160:                # preprocess_mamba reads req_state.num_computed_tokens (CPU)
4161:                # to decide copy operations, so we must apply deferred
4162:                # corrections before it runs.
4163:                if deferred_state_corrections_fn:
4164:                    deferred_state_corrections_fn()
4165:                    deferred_state_corrections_fn = None
4166:                mamba_bufs = self._get_mamba_bufs()
4167:                mamba_utils.preprocess_mamba(
4168:                    scheduler_output,
4169:                    self.kv_cache_config,
4170:                    self.cache_config,
4171:                    self.mamba_state_idx,
4172:                    self.input_batch,
4173:                    self.requests,
4174:                    self.compilation_config.static_forward_context,
4175:                    self.model.get_mamba_state_copy_func(),
4176:                    mamba_bufs.preprocess,
4177:                )
4178:                # preprocess_mamba resets num_accepted_tokens_cpu to 1
4179:                # for requests whose state was copied to a new block.
4180:                # Re-sync to GPU so the mamba kernel reads from the
4181:                # correct initial state slot (init_token_idx = 0).
4182:                self.num_accepted_tokens.np[:num_reqs] = (
4183:                    self.input_batch.num_accepted_tokens_cpu[:num_reqs]
4184:                )
4185:                self.num_accepted_tokens.copy_to_gpu(num_reqs)
4186:
4187:                # Stage per-request inputs for the fused postprocess kernel
4188:                # only when that kernel will actually run. The kernel is
4189:                # gated on spec-decode + hybrid (see MambaBuffers.create);
4190:                # without it, ``mamba_bufs.postprocess_align`` is None and
4191:                # the staging buffers don't exist.
4192:                if mamba_bufs.postprocess_align is not None:
4193:                    mamba_utils.stage_postprocess_inputs_to_gpu(
4194:                        mamba_bufs.postprocess_align,
4195:                        scheduler_output,
4196:                        self.input_batch.req_ids,
4197:                        num_reqs,
4198:                        self.requests,
4199:                        self.mamba_state_idx,
4200:                    )
4201:
4202:            use_spec_decode = len(scheduler_output.scheduled_spec_decode_tokens) > 0
4203:            ubatch_slices_attn = ubatch_slices_padded if pad_attn else ubatch_slices
4204:
4205:            slot_mappings_by_group, slot_mappings = self._get_slot_mappings(
4206:                num_tokens_padded=num_tokens_padded
4207:                if pad_attn or has_separate_kv_update
4208:                else num_tokens_unpadded,
4209:                num_reqs_padded=(
4210:                    num_reqs_padded if pad_attn or has_separate_kv_update else num_reqs
4211:                ),
4212:                num_tokens_unpadded=num_tokens_unpadded,
4213:                ubatch_slices=ubatch_slices_padded,
4214:            )
4215:
4216:            attn_metadata, spec_decode_common_attn_metadata = (
4217:                self._build_attention_metadata(
4218:                    num_tokens=num_tokens_unpadded,
4219:                    num_tokens_padded=num_tokens_padded if pad_attn else None,
4220:                    num_reqs=num_reqs,
4221:                    num_reqs_padded=num_reqs_padded if pad_attn else None,
4222:                    max_query_len=max_num_scheduled_tokens,
4223:                    ubatch_slices=ubatch_slices_attn,
4224:                    logits_indices=logits_indices,
4225:                    use_spec_decode=use_spec_decode,
4226:                    num_scheduled_tokens=scheduler_output.num_scheduled_tokens,
4227:                    cascade_attn_prefix_lens=cascade_attn_prefix_lens,
4228:                    slot_mappings=slot_mappings_by_group,
4229:                )
4230:            )
4231:
4232:            (
4233:                input_ids,
4234:                inputs_embeds,
4235:                positions,
4236:                intermediate_tensors,
4237:                model_kwargs,
4238:                ec_connector_output,
4239:            ) = self._preprocess(
4240:                scheduler_output, num_tokens_padded, intermediate_tensors
4241:            )
4242:
4243:        # Set cudagraph mode to none if calc_kv_scales is true.
4244:        # KV scales calculation involves dynamic operations that are incompatible
4245:        # with CUDA graph capture.
4246:        if self.calculate_kv_scales:
4247:            cudagraph_mode = CUDAGraphMode.NONE
4248:            # Mark KV scales as calculated after the first forward pass
4249:            self.calculate_kv_scales = False
4250:
4251:        # Encoder-decoder models can only compile the pure decode steps where no
4252:        # encoder inputs are present. Use eager for the first pass.
4253:        num_encoder_reqs = len(scheduler_output.scheduled_encoder_inputs)
4254:        has_encoder_input = (
4255:            self.model_config.is_encoder_decoder and num_encoder_reqs > 0
4256:        )
4257:
4258:        # Run the model.
4259:        # Use persistent buffers for CUDA graphs.
4260:        # When spec decode is enabled, defer connector finalization
4261:        # (wait_for_save + clear metadata) until after draft model runs.
4262:        defer_kv_connector_finalize = self.speculative_config is not None
4263:        with (
4264:            set_forward_context(
4265:                attn_metadata,
4266:                self.vllm_config,
4267:                num_tokens=num_tokens_padded,
4268:                num_tokens_across_dp=num_tokens_across_dp,
4269:                cudagraph_runtime_mode=cudagraph_mode,
4270:                batch_descriptor=batch_desc,
4271:                ubatch_slices=ubatch_slices_padded,
4272:                slot_mapping=slot_mappings,
4273:                skip_compiled=has_encoder_input,
4274:            ),
4275:            record_function_or_nullcontext("gpu_model_runner: forward"),
4276:            self.maybe_get_kv_connector_output(
4277:                scheduler_output,
4278:                defer_finalize=defer_kv_connector_finalize,
4279:            ) as kv_connector_output,
4280:        ):
4281:            model_output = self._model_forward(
4282:                input_ids=input_ids,
4283:                positions=positions,
4284:                intermediate_tensors=intermediate_tensors,
4285:                inputs_embeds=inputs_embeds,
4286:                **model_kwargs,
4287:            )
4288:
4289:        with record_function_or_nullcontext("gpu_model_runner: postprocess"):
4290:            if self.use_aux_hidden_state_outputs:
4291:                # True when EAGLE 3 is used.
4292:                hidden_states, aux_hidden_states = model_output
4293:            else:
4294:                # Common case.
4295:                hidden_states = model_output
4296:                aux_hidden_states = None
4297:
4298:            if not self.broadcast_pp_output:
4299:                # Common case.
4300:                if not get_pp_group().is_last_rank:
4301:                    # Return the intermediate tensors.
4302:                    assert isinstance(hidden_states, IntermediateTensors)
4303:                    self.kv_connector_output = kv_connector_output
4304:                    return hidden_states
4305:
4306:                if self.is_pooling_model:
4307:                    # Return the pooling output.
4308:                    return self._pool(
4309:                        hidden_states,
4310:                        num_scheduled_tokens,
4311:                        num_scheduled_tokens_np,
4312:                        kv_connector_output,
4313:                    )
4314:
4315:                sample_hidden_states = hidden_states[logits_indices]
4316:                logits = self.model.compute_logits(sample_hidden_states)
4317:            else:
4318:                # Rare case.
4319:                assert not self.is_pooling_model
4320:
4321:                sample_hidden_states = hidden_states[logits_indices]
4322:                if not get_pp_group().is_last_rank:
4323:                    all_gather_tensors = {
4324:                        "residual": not is_residual_scattered_for_sp(
4325:                            self.vllm_config, num_tokens_padded
4326:                        )
4327:                    }
4328:                    get_pp_group().send_tensor_dict(
4329:                        hidden_states.tensors,
4330:                        all_gather_group=get_tp_group(),
4331:                        all_gather_tensors=all_gather_tensors,
4332:                    )
4333:                    logits = None
4334:                else:
4335:                    logits = self.model.compute_logits(sample_hidden_states)
4336:
4337:                model_output_broadcast_data: dict[str, Any] = {}
4338:                if logits is not None:
4339:                    model_output_broadcast_data["logits"] = logits.contiguous()
4340:
4341:                broadcasted = get_pp_group().broadcast_tensor_dict(
4342:                    model_output_broadcast_data, src=len(get_pp_group().ranks) - 1
4343:                )
4344:                assert broadcasted is not None
4345:                logits = broadcasted["logits"]
4346:
4347:        self.execute_model_state = ExecuteModelState(
4348:            scheduler_output,
4349:            logits,
4350:            spec_decode_metadata,
4351:            spec_decode_common_attn_metadata,
4352:            hidden_states,
4353:            sample_hidden_states,
4354:            aux_hidden_states,
4355:            ec_connector_output,
4356:            cudagraph_stats,
4357:            slot_mappings,
4358:        )
4359:        self.kv_connector_output = kv_connector_output
4360:
4361:        # Now the batch has been launched we can wait for corrections from the
4362:        # previous model forward without breaking async scheduling.
4363:        if deferred_state_corrections_fn:
4364:            deferred_state_corrections_fn()
4365:
4366:        return None
4367:
4368:    def _input_fits_in_drafter(
4369:        self, common_attn_metadata: CommonAttentionMetadata | None
4370:    ) -> bool:
4371:        if common_attn_metadata is None:
4372:            return False
4373:        assert self.speculative_config is not None
4374:        # DFlash queries one extra token (the bonus token) beyond num_spec_tokens
4375:        num_drafter_query_tokens = self.num_spec_tokens + (
4376:            1 if self.speculative_config.use_dflash() else 0
4377:        )
4378:        return (
4379:            common_attn_metadata.max_seq_len + num_drafter_query_tokens
4380:            <= self.effective_drafter_max_model_len
4381:        )
4382:
4383:    @torch.inference_mode
4384:    def sample_tokens(
4385:        self, grammar_output: "GrammarOutput | None"
4386:    ) -> ModelRunnerOutput | AsyncModelRunnerOutput | IntermediateTensors:
4387:        if self.execute_model_state is None:
4388:            kv_connector_output = self.kv_connector_output
4389:            self.kv_connector_output = None
4390:            # receive sampled token ids from the last PP rank.
4391:            if self.use_async_scheduling and not get_pp_group().is_last_rank:
4392:                self._pp_receive_prev_sampled_token_ids_to_input_batch()
4393:            # In case of PP with kv transfer, we need to pass through the
4394:            # kv_connector_output
4395:            return ModelRunnerOutput.with_kv_conn_output_only(kv_connector_output)
4396:
4397:        # Unpack ephemeral state.
4398:        (
4399:            scheduler_output,
4400:            logits,
4401:            spec_decode_metadata,
4402:            spec_decode_common_attn_metadata,
4403:            hidden_states,
4404:            sample_hidden_states,
4405:            aux_hidden_states,
4406:            ec_connector_output,
4407:            cudagraph_stats,
4408:            slot_mappings,
4409:        ) = self.execute_model_state
4410:        # Clear ephemeral state.
4411:        self.execute_model_state = None
4412:
4413:        # Apply structured output bitmasks if present.
4414:        if grammar_output is not None:
4415:            apply_grammar_bitmask(
4416:                scheduler_output, grammar_output, self.input_batch, logits
4417:            )
4418:
4419:        with record_function_or_nullcontext("gpu_model_runner: sample"):
4420:            sampler_output = self._sample(logits, spec_decode_metadata)
4421:
4422:        self._update_states_after_model_execute(
4423:            sampler_output.sampled_token_ids, scheduler_output
4424:        )
4425:        if self.use_async_scheduling:
4426:            pp = get_pp_group()
4427:            # For torchrun external_launcher PP mode with broadcast_pp_output=True,
4428:            # PP outputs have been broadcasted to all ranks at logits computation.
4429:            # Therefore, here is no need to send sampled token ids again in this case.
4430:            if not self.broadcast_pp_output and pp.world_size > 1 and pp.is_last_rank:
4431:                self._pp_broadcast_prev_sampled_token_ids(
4432:                    sampler_output.sampled_token_ids
4433:                )
4434:
4435:        self._draft_token_ids = None
4436:        self._draft_probs = None
4437:        self._draft_prob_req_ids = None
4438:        self._draft_token_req_ids = None
4439:        self.valid_sampled_token_count_gpu = None
4440:        self.input_batch.prev_sampled_token_ids = None
4441:
4442:        def propose_draft_token_ids(sampled_token_ids):
4443:            assert spec_decode_common_attn_metadata is not None
4444:            with record_function_or_nullcontext("gpu_model_runner: draft"):
4445:                self._draft_token_ids = self.propose_draft_token_ids(
4446:                    scheduler_output,
4447:                    sampled_token_ids,
4448:                    self.input_batch.sampling_metadata,
4449:                    hidden_states,
4450:                    sample_hidden_states,
4451:                    aux_hidden_states,
4452:                    spec_decode_metadata,
4453:                    spec_decode_common_attn_metadata,
4454:                    slot_mappings,
4455:                )
4456:                self._copy_draft_token_ids_to_cpu(scheduler_output)
4457:
4458:        spec_config = self.speculative_config
4459:        propose_drafts_after_bookkeeping = False
4460:        if spec_config is not None:
4461:            # Decide whether to run the drafter or zero out draft tokens.
4462:            input_fits_in_drafter = self._input_fits_in_drafter(
4463:                spec_decode_common_attn_metadata
4464:            )
4465:            use_gpu_toks = (
4466:                spec_config.use_eagle()
4467:                or spec_config.uses_draft_model()
4468:                or spec_config.uses_extract_hidden_states()
4469:            ) and not spec_config.disable_padded_drafter_batch
4470:            if use_gpu_toks:
4471:                # EAGLE/DraftModel speculative decoding can use the GPU sampled tokens
4472:                # as inputs, and does not need to wait for bookkeeping to finish.
4473:                assert isinstance(
4474:                    self.drafter,
4475:                    EagleProposer
4476:                    | DFlashProposer
4477:                    | DraftModelProposer
4478:                    | ExtractHiddenStatesProposer
4479:                    | Gemma4Proposer,
4480:                )
4481:                sampled_token_ids = sampler_output.sampled_token_ids
4482:                if input_fits_in_drafter:
4483:                    propose_draft_token_ids(sampled_token_ids)
4484:                elif self.valid_sampled_token_count_event is not None:
4485:                    assert spec_decode_common_attn_metadata is not None
4486:                    next_token_ids, valid_sampled_tokens_count = (
4487:                        self.drafter.prepare_next_token_ids_padded(
4488:                            sampled_token_ids,
4489:                            self.requests,
4490:                            self.input_batch,
4491:                            self.discard_request_mask.gpu,
4492:                        )
4493:                    )
4494:                    self._copy_valid_sampled_token_count(
4495:                        next_token_ids, valid_sampled_tokens_count
4496:                    )
4497:            elif (
4498:                spec_config.use_ngram_gpu()
4499:                and not spec_config.disable_padded_drafter_batch
4500:            ):
4501:                assert isinstance(self.drafter, NgramProposerGPU)
4502:                sampled_token_ids = sampler_output.sampled_token_ids
4503:                if input_fits_in_drafter:
4504:                    propose_draft_token_ids(sampled_token_ids)
4505:                elif self.valid_sampled_token_count_event is not None:
4506:                    assert spec_decode_common_attn_metadata is not None
4507:                    next_token_ids, valid_sampled_tokens_count, _ = (
4508:                        self.drafter.update_token_ids_ngram(
4509:                            sampled_token_ids,
4510:                            self.input_batch,
4511:                            self.token_ids_gpu_tensor,
4512:                            self.num_tokens_no_spec_gpu,
4513:                            self.discard_request_mask.gpu,
4514:                        )
4515:                    )
4516:                    self._copy_valid_sampled_token_count(
4517:                        next_token_ids, valid_sampled_tokens_count
4518:                    )
4519:            else:
4520:                propose_drafts_after_bookkeeping = input_fits_in_drafter
4521:
4522:            if not input_fits_in_drafter:
4523:                # Zero out draft tokens so the scheduler doesn't schedule
4524:                # stale drafts from the previous step.
4525:                # For Nemotron-H: it is necessary to zero out the draft tokens,
4526:                # otherwise the stale tokens will corrupt Mamba recurrent
4527:                # state and logprobs for sequences near max_model_len.
4528:                self._draft_token_ids = torch.zeros(
4529:                    1, device=self.device, dtype=torch.int32
4530:                ).expand(len(self.input_batch.req_ids), self.num_spec_tokens)
4531:                self._draft_probs = None
4532:                self._draft_prob_req_ids = None
4533:                self._copy_draft_token_ids_to_cpu(scheduler_output, zeros_only=True)
4534:
4535:        with record_function_or_nullcontext("gpu_model_runner: bookkeep"):
4536:            (
4537:                num_nans_in_logits,
4538:                logprobs_lists,
4539:                valid_sampled_token_ids,
4540:                prompt_logprobs_dict,
4541:                req_ids_output_copy,
4542:                req_id_to_index_output_copy,
4543:                invalid_req_indices,
4544:            ) = self._bookkeeping_sync(
4545:                scheduler_output,
4546:                sampler_output,
4547:                logits,
4548:                hidden_states,
4549:                scheduler_output.total_num_scheduled_tokens,
4550:            )
4551:
4552:        if propose_drafts_after_bookkeeping:
4553:            # ngram and other speculative decoding methods use the sampled
4554:            # tokens on the CPU, so they are run after bookkeeping.
4555:            propose_draft_token_ids(valid_sampled_token_ids)
4556:
4557:        # Finalize KV connector (wait_for_save + clear metadata) after
4558:        # draft model runs. Deferred from target model forward to allow
4559:        # draft model to also save its KV cache.
4560:        if spec_config is not None:
4561:            self.finalize_kv_connector()
4562:
4563:        with record_function_or_nullcontext("gpu_model_runner: eplb"):
4564:            self.eplb_step()
4565:
4566:        # self.kv_connector_output may be modified during drafting
4567:        kv_connector_output = self.kv_connector_output
4568:        self.kv_connector_output = None
4569:
4570:        with record_function_or_nullcontext("gpu_model_runner: ModelRunnerOutput"):
4571:            output = ModelRunnerOutput(
4572:                req_ids=req_ids_output_copy,
4573:                req_id_to_index=req_id_to_index_output_copy,
4574:                sampled_token_ids=valid_sampled_token_ids,
4575:                logprobs=logprobs_lists,
4576:                prompt_logprobs_dict=prompt_logprobs_dict,
4577:                kv_connector_output=kv_connector_output,
4578:                ec_connector_output=ec_connector_output
4579:                if self.supports_mm_inputs
4580:                else None,
4581:                num_nans_in_logits=num_nans_in_logits,
4582:                cudagraph_stats=cudagraph_stats,
4583:                routed_experts=None,
4584:            )
4585:
4586:        if not self.use_async_scheduling:
4587:            if self.routed_experts_initialized:
4588:                # Sync path: D2H was issued in ``_bookkeeping_sync`` and
4589:                # synchronized by ``_to_list``'s event.synchronize(), so
4590:                # the pinned buffers are ready to be wrapped as numpy.
4591:                total = scheduler_output.total_num_scheduled_tokens
4592:                output.routed_experts = RoutedExpertsLists(
4593:                    routing_data=self.routed_experts_cpu[:total].numpy(),
4594:                    slot_mapping=self.routed_experts_slot_mapping_cpu[:total].numpy(),
4595:                )
4596:            return output
4597:
4598:        with record_function_or_nullcontext(
4599:            "gpu_model_runner: AsyncGPUModelRunnerOutput"
4600:        ):
4601:            # Async path: produce a device-side snapshot that the async
4602:            # copy stream can D2H later. Both tensors must be private
4603:            # clones because:
4604:            #   - ``routing_data`` source is the shared capturer buffer,
4605:            #     which is ``clear_buffer()``-ed at the start of the
4606:            #     next step on the default stream.
4607:            #   - ``slot_mapping`` source is our own
4608:            #     ``routed_experts_slot_mapping_device``, which the
4609:            #     next ``_prepare_inputs`` overwrites on the default
4610:            #     stream while the D2H is still pending on the copy
4611:            #     stream.
4612:            # Without clones, the copy stream would read torn data.
4613:            routed_experts_snapshot = None
4614:            if self.routed_experts_initialized:
4615:                buf = self.routed_experts_capturer.get_device_buffer()
4616:                total = scheduler_output.total_num_scheduled_tokens
4617:                routed_experts_snapshot = RoutedExpertsTensors(
4618:                    routing_data=buf[:total].clone(),
4619:                    slot_mapping=self.routed_experts_slot_mapping_device[
4620:                        :total
4621:                    ].clone(),
4622:                )
4623:
4624:            async_output = AsyncGPUModelRunnerOutput(
4625:                model_runner_output=output,
4626:                sampled_token_ids=sampler_output.sampled_token_ids,
4627:                logprobs_tensors=sampler_output.logprobs_tensors,
4628:                invalid_req_indices=invalid_req_indices,
4629:                async_output_copy_stream=self._get_or_create_async_output_copy_stream(),
4630:                vocab_size=self.input_batch.vocab_size,
4631:                routed_experts=routed_experts_snapshot,
4632:            )
4633:        with record_function_or_nullcontext(
4634:            "gpu_model_runner: set_async_sampled_token_ids"
4635:        ):
4636:            # Save ref of sampled_token_ids CPU tensor if the batch contains
4637:            # any requests with sampling params that require output ids.
4638:            self.input_batch.set_async_sampled_token_ids(
4639:                async_output.sampled_token_ids_cpu,
4640:                async_output.async_copy_ready_event,
4641:            )
4642:
4643:        return async_output
4644:
4645:    def _pp_broadcast_prev_sampled_token_ids(
4646:        self, sampled_token_ids: torch.Tensor
4647:    ) -> None:
4648:        """Broadcast sampled token ids (GPU) from last PP stage"""
4649:        pp = get_pp_group()
4650:        assert pp.is_last_rank
4651:        # `prev_sampled_token_ids` is expected to have shape [num_reqs, 1].
4652:        assert sampled_token_ids.dim() == 2 and sampled_token_ids.shape[-1] == 1, (
4653:            "PP+async expects sampled_token_ids to have shape [num_reqs, 1]"
4654:        )
4655:        # Skip for chunked prefill: sampled tokens are dummy
4656:        # and will be discarded, no need to broadcast.
4657:        if not self._is_all_reqs_chunked_prefill():
4658:            torch.distributed.broadcast(
4659:                sampled_token_ids, src=pp.rank, group=pp.device_group
4660:            )
4661:
4662:    def _pp_receive_prev_sampled_token_ids_to_input_batch(self) -> None:
4663:        """Receive sampled token ids broadcast from last PP stage"""
4664:        pp = get_pp_group()
4665:        assert not pp.is_last_rank
4666:        num_reqs = self.input_batch.num_reqs
4667:        # `prev_sampled_token_ids` is expected to have shape [num_reqs, 1].
4668:        recv = torch.empty((num_reqs, 1), dtype=torch.int32, device=self.device)
4669:        # skip for chunked prefill.
4670:        if not self._is_all_reqs_chunked_prefill():
4671:            torch.distributed.broadcast(recv, src=pp.last_rank, group=pp.device_group)
4672:        self.input_batch.prev_sampled_token_ids = recv
4673:
4674:        # construct `prev_req_id_to_index` here so `_prepare_input_ids`
4675:        # can map req_id -> previous batch row
4676:        discard_req_indices = np.nonzero(self.discard_request_mask.np[:num_reqs])[0]
4677:        discard_req_indices_set = set(discard_req_indices)
4678:        prev_req_id_to_index: dict[str, int] = {}
4679:        for i, req_id in enumerate(self.input_batch.req_ids):
4680:            if i in discard_req_indices_set:
4681:                continue
4682:            prev_req_id_to_index[req_id] = i
4683:            # PP+async scheduling: advance per-request local cached output length by
4684:            # appending a placeholder (-1) token id.
4685:            if (req_state := self.requests.get(req_id)) is not None:
4686:                req_state.output_token_ids.append(-1)
4687:            pos = self.input_batch.num_tokens_no_spec[i]
4688:            self.input_batch.is_token_ids[i, pos] = True
4689:            self.input_batch.num_tokens_no_spec[i] = pos + 1
4690:        self.input_batch.prev_req_id_to_index = prev_req_id_to_index
4691:
4692:    def take_draft_token_ids(self) -> DraftTokenIds | None:
4693:        if not self.num_spec_tokens or not self._draft_token_req_ids:
4694:            return None
4695:        draft_token_ids, req_ids = self._get_draft_token_ids_cpu()
4696:        return DraftTokenIds(req_ids, draft_token_ids)
4697:
4698:    def _copy_draft_token_ids_to_cpu(
4699:        self, scheduler_output: "SchedulerOutput", zeros_only: bool = False
4700:    ) -> None:
4701:        # Check if we need to copy draft tokens to CPU. In async scheduling,
4702:        # we only copy when needed for structured output, penalties or bad_words.
4703:        if self.use_async_scheduling and not (
4704:            scheduler_output.has_structured_output_requests
4705:            or self.input_batch.sampling_metadata.output_token_ids
4706:        ):
4707:            return
4708:        # We must also set the corresponding request ids.
4709:        self._draft_token_req_ids = self.input_batch.req_ids.copy()
4710:
4711:        draft_token_ids: torch.Tensor = self._draft_token_ids
4712:        if not torch.is_tensor(draft_token_ids):
4713:            return
4714:        assert self.draft_token_ids_event is not None
4715:        assert self.draft_token_ids_copy_stream is not None
4716:        assert self.draft_token_ids_cpu is not None
4717:        default_stream = torch.cuda.current_stream()
4718:        num_reqs = draft_token_ids.shape[0]
4719:        with torch.cuda.stream(self.draft_token_ids_copy_stream):
4720:            if not zeros_only:
4721:                # Trigger async copy of draft token ids to cpu.
4722:                self.draft_token_ids_copy_stream.wait_stream(default_stream)
4723:                self.draft_token_ids_cpu[:num_reqs].copy_(
4724:                    draft_token_ids, non_blocking=True
4725:                )
4726:            else:
4727:                # No copy needed, just zero-out cpu tensor.
4728:                self.draft_token_ids_cpu[:num_reqs] = 0
4729:            self.draft_token_ids_event.record()
4730:
4731:    def _get_draft_token_ids_cpu(self) -> tuple[list[list[int]], list[str]]:
4732:        if isinstance(self._draft_token_ids, list):
4733:            return self._draft_token_ids, self.input_batch.req_ids
4734:        req_ids = self._draft_token_req_ids
4735:        if req_ids is None:
4736:            return [], []
4737:        assert self.draft_token_ids_event is not None
4738:        assert self.draft_token_ids_cpu is not None
4739:        self.draft_token_ids_event.synchronize()
4740:        return self.draft_token_ids_cpu[: len(req_ids)].tolist(), req_ids
4741:
4742:    def _copy_valid_sampled_token_count(
4743:        self, next_token_ids: torch.Tensor, valid_sampled_tokens_count: torch.Tensor
4744:    ) -> None:
4745:        if self.valid_sampled_token_count_event is None:
4746:            return
4747:
4748:        default_stream = torch.cuda.current_stream()
4749:        # Initialize a new stream to overlap the copy operation with
4750:        # prepare_input of draft model.
4751:        with torch.cuda.stream(self.valid_sampled_token_count_copy_stream):
4752:            self.valid_sampled_token_count_copy_stream.wait_stream(default_stream)  # type: ignore
4753:            counts = valid_sampled_tokens_count
4754:            counts_cpu = self.valid_sampled_token_count_cpu
4755:            assert counts_cpu is not None
4756:            counts_cpu[: counts.shape[0]].copy_(counts, non_blocking=True)
4757:            self.valid_sampled_token_count_event.record()
4758:
4759:        if self.use_async_spec_decode:
4760:            # Stash for GPU-side correction in _prepare_inputs.
4761:            self.valid_sampled_token_count_gpu = valid_sampled_tokens_count
4762:        self.input_batch.prev_sampled_token_ids = next_token_ids.unsqueeze(1)
4763:
4764:    def _get_valid_sampled_token_count(self) -> list[int]:
4765:        # Wait until valid_sampled_tokens_count is copied to cpu,
4766:        prev_sampled_token_ids = self.input_batch.prev_sampled_token_ids
4767:        sampled_count_event = self.valid_sampled_token_count_event
4768:        if sampled_count_event is None or prev_sampled_token_ids is None:
4769:            return []
4770:
4771:        counts_cpu = self.valid_sampled_token_count_cpu
4772:        assert counts_cpu is not None
4773:        sampled_count_event.synchronize()
4774:        return counts_cpu[: prev_sampled_token_ids.shape[0]].tolist()
4775:
4776:    def _get_spec_decode_draft_probs(
4777:        self, spec_decode_metadata: SpecDecodeMetadata
4778:    ) -> torch.Tensor | None:
4779:        if self._draft_probs is None or self._draft_prob_req_ids is None:
4780:            return None
4781:
4782:        row_by_req_id = {
4783:            req_id: idx for idx, req_id in enumerate(self._draft_prob_req_ids)
4784:        }
4785:        draft_probs_rows: list[torch.Tensor] = []
4786:        for req_id, num_draft in zip(
4787:            self.input_batch.req_ids, spec_decode_metadata.num_draft_tokens
4788:        ):
4789:            if num_draft == 0:
4790:                continue
4791:            row_idx = row_by_req_id.get(req_id)
4792:            if row_idx is None:
4793:                logger.warning(
4794:                    "Missing cached draft probabilities for request %s; "
4795:                    "falling back to legacy speculative rejection behavior.",
4796:                    req_id,
4797:                )
4798:                return None
4799:            draft_probs_rows.append(self._draft_probs[row_idx, :num_draft])
4800:
4801:        if not draft_probs_rows:
4802:            return None
4803:        return torch.cat(draft_probs_rows, dim=0).contiguous()
4804:
4805:    def propose_draft_token_ids(
4806:        self,
4807:        scheduler_output: "SchedulerOutput",
4808:        sampled_token_ids: torch.Tensor | list[list[int]],
4809:        sampling_metadata: SamplingMetadata,
4810:        hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
4811:        sample_hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
4812:        aux_hidden_states: list[torch.Tensor] | None,
4813:        spec_decode_metadata: SpecDecodeMetadata | None,
4814:        common_attn_metadata: CommonAttentionMetadata,
4815:        slot_mappings: dict[str, torch.Tensor] | list[dict[str, torch.Tensor]] | None,
4816:    ) -> list[list[int]] | torch.Tensor:
4817:        num_scheduled_tokens = scheduler_output.total_num_scheduled_tokens
4818:        spec_config = self.speculative_config
4819:        assert spec_config is not None
4820:        self._draft_probs = None
4821:        self._draft_prob_req_ids = None
4822:        if spec_config.method == "ngram":
4823:            from vllm.v1.spec_decode.ngram_proposer import NgramProposer
4824:
4825:            assert isinstance(sampled_token_ids, list)
4826:            assert isinstance(self.drafter, NgramProposer)
4827:            draft_token_ids = self.drafter.propose(
4828:                sampled_token_ids,
4829:                self.input_batch.num_tokens_no_spec,
4830:                self.input_batch.token_ids_cpu,
4831:                slot_mappings=slot_mappings,
4832:            )
4833:        elif spec_config.method == "custom_class":
4834:            assert isinstance(sampled_token_ids, list)
4835:            draft_token_ids = cast(Any, self.drafter).propose(
4836:                sampled_token_ids,
4837:                self.input_batch.num_tokens_no_spec,
4838:                self.input_batch.token_ids_cpu,
4839:                slot_mappings=slot_mappings,
4840:            )
4841:        elif spec_config.use_ngram_gpu():
4842:            assert isinstance(self.drafter, NgramProposerGPU)
4843:            (
4844:                next_token_ids,
4845:                valid_sampled_tokens_count,
4846:                valid_sampled_token_ids_gpu,
4847:            ) = self.drafter.update_token_ids_ngram(
4848:                sampled_token_ids,
4849:                self.input_batch,
4850:                self.token_ids_gpu_tensor,
4851:                self.num_tokens_no_spec_gpu,
4852:                self.discard_request_mask.gpu,
4853:            )
4854:            self._copy_valid_sampled_token_count(
4855:                next_token_ids, valid_sampled_tokens_count
4856:            )
4857:
4858:            batch_size = next_token_ids.shape[0]
4859:
4860:            draft_token_ids, num_valid_draft_tokens = self.drafter.propose(
4861:                self.num_tokens_no_spec_gpu[:batch_size],
4862:                self.token_ids_gpu_tensor[:batch_size],
4863:                valid_sampled_token_ids_gpu,
4864:                valid_sampled_tokens_count,
4865:            )
4866:
4867:            # Cache valid draft counts for scheduler-side trimming.
4868:            self._num_valid_draft_tokens = num_valid_draft_tokens
4869:
4870:            # Async D2H copy on a dedicated stream.
4871:            copy_num_valid_draft_tokens(
4872:                self._num_valid_draft_tokens_cpu,
4873:                self._num_valid_draft_tokens_copy_stream,
4874:                self._num_valid_draft_tokens_event,
4875:                self._num_valid_draft_tokens,
4876:                self.input_batch.num_reqs,
4877:            )
4878:        elif spec_config.method == "suffix":
4879:            assert isinstance(sampled_token_ids, list)
4880:            assert isinstance(self.drafter, SuffixDecodingProposer)
4881:            draft_token_ids = self.drafter.propose(
4882:                self.input_batch, sampled_token_ids, slot_mappings=slot_mappings
4883:            )
4884:        elif spec_config.method == "medusa":
4885:            assert isinstance(sampled_token_ids, list)
4886:            assert isinstance(self.drafter, MedusaProposer)
4887:
4888:            if sample_hidden_states.shape[0] == len(sampled_token_ids):
4889:                # The input to the target model does not include draft tokens.
4890:                hidden_states = sample_hidden_states
4891:            else:
4892:                indices = []
4893:                offset = 0
4894:                assert spec_decode_metadata is not None, (
4895:                    "No spec decode metadata for medusa"
4896:                )
4897:                for num_draft, tokens in zip(
4898:                    spec_decode_metadata.num_draft_tokens, sampled_token_ids
4899:                ):
4900:                    indices.append(offset + len(tokens) - 1)
4901:                    offset += num_draft + 1
4902:                indices = torch.tensor(indices, device=self.device)
4903:                hidden_states = sample_hidden_states[indices]
4904:
4905:            draft_token_ids = self.drafter.propose(
4906:                target_hidden_states=hidden_states,
4907:                sampling_metadata=sampling_metadata,
4908:                slot_mappings=slot_mappings,
4909:            )
4910:        elif spec_config.uses_extract_hidden_states():
4911:            assert isinstance(self.drafter, ExtractHiddenStatesProposer)
4912:            assert isinstance(sampled_token_ids, torch.Tensor), (
4913:                "sampled_token_ids should be a torch.Tensor for "
4914:                "extract_hidden_states method."
4915:            )
4916:            if not self.use_aux_hidden_state_outputs or aux_hidden_states is None:
4917:                raise ValueError(
4918:                    "aux_hidden_states are required when using `extract_hidden_states`"
4919:                )
4920:            target_hidden_states = [h[:num_scheduled_tokens] for h in aux_hidden_states]
4921:
4922:            draft_token_ids = self.drafter.propose(
4923:                sampled_token_ids=sampled_token_ids,
4924:                target_hidden_states=target_hidden_states,
4925:                common_attn_metadata=common_attn_metadata,
4926:                slot_mappings=slot_mappings,
4927:            )
4928:            next_token_ids, valid_sampled_tokens_count = (
4929:                self.drafter.prepare_next_token_ids_padded(
4930:                    sampled_token_ids,
4931:                    self.requests,
4932:                    self.input_batch,
4933:                    self.discard_request_mask.gpu,
4934:                )
4935:            )
4936:            self._copy_valid_sampled_token_count(
4937:                next_token_ids, valid_sampled_tokens_count
4938:            )
4939:
4940:        elif (
4941:            spec_config.use_eagle()
4942:            or spec_config.use_dflash()
4943:            or spec_config.uses_draft_model()
4944:        ):
4945:            assert isinstance(
4946:                self.drafter,
4947:                EagleProposer | DFlashProposer | DraftModelProposer | Gemma4Proposer,
4948:            )
4949:
4950:            if spec_config.disable_padded_drafter_batch:
4951:                # When padded-batch is disabled, the sampled_token_ids should be
4952:                # the cpu-side list[list[int]] of valid sampled tokens for each
4953:                # request, with invalid requests having empty lists.
4954:                assert isinstance(sampled_token_ids, list), (
4955:                    "sampled_token_ids should be a python list when"
4956:                    "padded-batch is disabled."
4957:                )
4958:                next_token_ids = self.drafter.prepare_next_token_ids_cpu(
4959:                    sampled_token_ids,
4960:                    self.requests,
4961:                    self.input_batch,
4962:                    scheduler_output.num_scheduled_tokens,
4963:                )
4964:            else:
4965:                # When using padded-batch, the sampled_token_ids should be
4966:                # the gpu tensor of sampled tokens for each request, of shape
4967:                # (num_reqs, num_spec_tokens + 1) with rejected tokens having
4968:                # value -1.
4969:                assert isinstance(sampled_token_ids, torch.Tensor), (
4970:                    "sampled_token_ids should be a torch.Tensor when"
4971:                    "padded-batch is enabled."
4972:                )
4973:                next_token_ids, valid_sampled_tokens_count = (
4974:                    self.drafter.prepare_next_token_ids_padded(
4975:                        sampled_token_ids,
4976:                        self.requests,
4977:                        self.input_batch,
4978:                        self.discard_request_mask.gpu,
4979:                    )
4980:                )
4981:                self._copy_valid_sampled_token_count(
4982:                    next_token_ids, valid_sampled_tokens_count
4983:                )
4984:
4985:            # Let the target override the hidden state fed to the drafter
4986:            # (e.g. DeepSeek V4 MTP needs the pre-hc_head residual). Safe to
4987:            # rebind here: hidden_states was already consumed for sampling
4988:            # above and is not used again in this branch.
4989:            alt = getattr(
4990:                self.get_model(), "get_mtp_target_hidden_states", lambda: None
4991:            )()
4992:            if alt is not None:
4993:                hidden_states = alt
4994:
4995:            num_rejected_tokens_gpu = None
4996:            if spec_decode_metadata is None:
4997:                token_indices_to_sample = None
4998:                # input_ids can be None for multimodal models.
4999:                target_token_ids = self.input_ids.gpu[:num_scheduled_tokens]
5000:                target_positions = self._get_positions(num_scheduled_tokens)
5001:                if self.use_aux_hidden_state_outputs:
5002:                    assert aux_hidden_states is not None
5003:                    target_hidden_states = torch.cat(
5004:                        [h[:num_scheduled_tokens] for h in aux_hidden_states], dim=-1
5005:                    )
5006:                else:
5007:                    target_hidden_states = hidden_states[:num_scheduled_tokens]
5008:            else:
5009:                if spec_config.disable_padded_drafter_batch:
5010:                    token_indices_to_sample = None
5011:                    common_attn_metadata, token_indices = self.drafter.prepare_inputs(
5012:                        common_attn_metadata,
5013:                        sampled_token_ids,
5014:                        spec_decode_metadata.num_draft_tokens,
5015:                    )
5016:                    target_token_ids = self.input_ids.gpu[token_indices]
5017:                    target_positions = self._get_positions(token_indices)
5018:                    if self.use_aux_hidden_state_outputs:
5019:                        assert aux_hidden_states is not None
5020:                        target_hidden_states = torch.cat(
5021:                            [h[token_indices] for h in aux_hidden_states], dim=-1
5022:                        )
5023:                    else:
5024:                        target_hidden_states = hidden_states[token_indices]
5025:                else:
5026:                    (
5027:                        common_attn_metadata,
5028:                        token_indices_to_sample,
5029:                        num_rejected_tokens_gpu,
5030:                    ) = self.drafter.prepare_inputs_padded(
5031:                        common_attn_metadata,
5032:                        spec_decode_metadata,
5033:                        valid_sampled_tokens_count,
5034:                    )
5035:                    total_num_tokens = common_attn_metadata.num_actual_tokens
5036:                    # When padding the batch, token_indices is just a range
5037:                    target_token_ids = self.input_ids.gpu[:total_num_tokens]
5038:                    target_positions = self._get_positions(total_num_tokens)
5039:                    if self.use_aux_hidden_state_outputs:
5040:                        assert aux_hidden_states is not None
5041:                        target_hidden_states = torch.cat(
5042:                            [h[:total_num_tokens] for h in aux_hidden_states], dim=-1
5043:                        )
5044:                    else:
5045:                        target_hidden_states = hidden_states[:total_num_tokens]
5046:
5047:            if self.supports_mm_inputs and self.drafter.supports_mm_inputs:
5048:                mm_embed_inputs = self._gather_mm_embeddings(
5049:                    scheduler_output,
5050:                    shift_computed_tokens=1,
5051:                )
5052:            else:
5053:                mm_embed_inputs = None
5054:
5055:            draft_token_ids = self.drafter.propose(
5056:                target_token_ids=target_token_ids,
5057:                target_positions=target_positions,
5058:                target_hidden_states=target_hidden_states,
5059:                next_token_ids=next_token_ids,
5060:                token_indices_to_sample=token_indices_to_sample,
5061:                sampling_metadata=sampling_metadata,
5062:                common_attn_metadata=common_attn_metadata,
5063:                mm_embed_inputs=mm_embed_inputs,
5064:                num_rejected_tokens_gpu=num_rejected_tokens_gpu,
5065:                slot_mappings=slot_mappings,
5066:            )
5067:            if hasattr(self.drafter, "take_last_draft_probs"):
5068:                draft_probs = self.drafter.take_last_draft_probs()
5069:                if draft_probs is not None:
5070:                    self._draft_probs = draft_probs
5071:                    self._draft_prob_req_ids = self.input_batch.req_ids.copy()
5072:
5073:        return draft_token_ids
5074:
5075:    def update_config(self, overrides: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
5076:        allowed_config_names = {"load_config", "model_config"}
5077:        for config_name, config_overrides in overrides.items():
5078:            assert config_name in allowed_config_names, (
5079:                f"Config `{config_name}` not supported. "
