5080:                f"Allowed configs: {allowed_config_names}"
5081:            )
5082:            config = getattr(self, config_name)
5083:            new_config = update_config(config, config_overrides)
5084:            setattr(self, config_name, new_config)
5085:
5086:    @instrument(span_name="Loading (GPU)")
5087:    def load_model(self, load_dummy_weights: bool = False) -> None:
5088:        """
5089:        Args:
5090:            load_dummy_weights: load dummy weights instead of real weights.
5091:        """
5092:        logger.info_once(
5093:            "Starting to load model %s...",
5094:            self.model_config.model,
5095:            scope="global",
5096:        )
5097:
5098:        if self.parallel_config.enable_eplb:
5099:            self.eplb_state = EplbState(self.parallel_config, self.device)
5100:            eplb_models = 0
5101:
5102:        try:
5103:            with DeviceMemoryProfiler() as m:
5104:                time_before_load = time.perf_counter()
5105:                if load_dummy_weights:
5106:                    self.load_config.load_format = "dummy"
5107:                model_loader = get_model_loader(self.load_config)
5108:                self.model = model_loader.load_model(
5109:                    vllm_config=self.vllm_config, model_config=self.model_config
5110:                )
5111:                if self.lora_config:
5112:                    self.model = self.load_lora_model(
5113:                        self.model, self.vllm_config, self.device
5114:                    )
5115:                if hasattr(self, "drafter"):
5116:                    logger.info_once("Loading drafter model...")
5117:                    if hasattr(self.drafter, "load_model"):
5118:                        self.drafter.load_model(self.model)
5119:                    if (
5120:                        hasattr(self.drafter, "model")
5121:                        and is_mixture_of_experts(self.drafter.model)
5122:                        and self.parallel_config.enable_eplb
5123:                    ):
5124:                        assert not self.parallel_config.enable_elastic_ep, (
5125:                            "Elastic EP is not supported with drafter model."
5126:                        )
5127:                        spec_config = self.vllm_config.speculative_config
5128:                        assert spec_config is not None
5129:                        assert spec_config.draft_model_config is not None
5130:                        logger.info_once(
5131:                            "EPLB is enabled for drafter model %s.",
5132:                            spec_config.draft_model_config.model,
5133:                        )
5134:                        if self.eplb_state is None:
5135:                            self.eplb_state = EplbState(
5136:                                self.parallel_config, self.device
5137:                            )
5138:                        self.eplb_state.add_model(
5139:                            self.drafter.model,
5140:                            spec_config.draft_model_config,
5141:                        )
5142:                        eplb_models += 1
5143:
5144:                self._setup_eagle3_aux_hidden_state_outputs()
5145:
5146:                # Resolve the MoE model, unwrapping VLM wrappers if needed.
5147:                # VLM models (e.g. KimiK25ForConditionalGeneration) wrap the
5148:                # actual MoE language model but don't implement
5149:                # MixtureOfExperts themselves.
5150:                moe_candidate = self.model
5151:                if not is_mixture_of_experts(moe_candidate) and isinstance(
5152:                    moe_candidate, SupportsMultiModal
5153:                ):
5154:                    moe_candidate = moe_candidate.get_language_model()
5155:                if is_mixture_of_experts(moe_candidate):
5156:                    self._moe_model = moe_candidate
5157:
5158:                if (
5159:                    self._moe_model is not None
5160:                    and self.parallel_config.enable_eplb
5161:                    and not load_dummy_weights
5162:                ):
5163:                    logger.info_once(
5164:                        "EPLB is enabled for model %s.",
5165:                        self.model_config.model,
5166:                    )
5167:                    assert self.eplb_state is not None
5168:                    self.eplb_state.add_model(
5169:                        self._moe_model,
5170:                        self.model_config,
5171:                    )
5172:                    eplb_models += 1
5173:
5174:                time_after_load = time.perf_counter()
5175:            self.model_memory_usage = m.consumed_memory
5176:        except torch.cuda.OutOfMemoryError as e:
5177:            msg = (
5178:                "Failed to load model - not enough GPU memory. "
5179:                "Try lowering --gpu-memory-utilization to free memory for weights, "
5180:                "increasing --tensor-parallel-size, or using --quantization. "
5181:                "See https://docs.vllm.ai/en/latest/configuration/conserving_memory/ "
5182:                "for more tips."
5183:            )
5184:            combined_msg = f"{msg} (original error: {e})"
5185:            logger.error(combined_msg)
5186:            raise e
5187:        logger.info_once(
5188:            "Model loading took %s GiB memory and %.6f seconds",
5189:            format_gib(self.model_memory_usage),
5190:            time_after_load - time_before_load,
5191:        )
5192:        if not load_dummy_weights:
5193:            prepare_communication_buffer_for_model(self.model)
5194:            if (drafter := getattr(self, "drafter", None)) and (
5195:                drafter_model := getattr(drafter, "model", None)
5196:            ):
5197:                prepare_communication_buffer_for_model(drafter_model)
5198:        mm_config = self.model_config.multimodal_config
5199:        self.is_multimodal_pruning_enabled = (
5200:            supports_multimodal_pruning(self.get_model())
5201:            and mm_config is not None
5202:            and mm_config.is_multimodal_pruning_enabled()
5203:        )
5204:        self.requires_sequential_video_encoding = hasattr(
5205:            self.get_model(), "requires_sequential_video_encoding"
5206:        )  # Temporary hack for dynamic res video w/o support for bs>1 yet
5207:
5208:        if (
5209:            self._moe_model is not None
5210:            and self.parallel_config.enable_eplb
5211:            and not load_dummy_weights
5212:            and self.eplb_state is not None
5213:            and self.eplb_state.is_async
5214:        ):
5215:            self.eplb_state.start_async_loop()
5216:
5217:        if (
5218:            self.vllm_config.compilation_config.mode
5219:            == CompilationMode.STOCK_TORCH_COMPILE
5220:        ):
5221:            from vllm.env_override import _apply_constrain_to_fx_strides_patch
5222:
5223:            _apply_constrain_to_fx_strides_patch()
5224:            backend = self.vllm_config.compilation_config.init_backend(self.vllm_config)
5225:            compilation_counter.stock_torch_compile_count += 1
5226:            self.model.compile(fullgraph=True, backend=backend)
5227:            return
5228:        # for other compilation modes, cudagraph behavior is controlled by
5229:        # CudagraphWrapper and CudagraphDispatcher of vllm.
5230:
5231:        # wrap the model with full cudagraph wrapper if needed.
5232:        cudagraph_mode = self.compilation_config.cudagraph_mode
5233:        assert cudagraph_mode is not None
5234:        if (
5235:            is_breakable_cudagraph_enabled()
5236:            and cudagraph_mode != CUDAGraphMode.NONE
5237:            and not self.parallel_config.use_ubatching
5238:        ):
5239:            self.model = BreakableCUDAGraphWrapper(self.model, self.vllm_config)
5240:            drafter = getattr(self, "drafter", None)
5241:            if drafter is not None and hasattr(drafter, "model"):
5242:                drafter.model = BreakableCUDAGraphWrapper(
5243:                    drafter.model, self.vllm_config
5244:                )
5245:        elif (
5246:            cudagraph_mode.has_full_cudagraphs()
5247:            and not self.parallel_config.use_ubatching
5248:        ):
5249:            self.model = CUDAGraphWrapper(
5250:                self.model, self.vllm_config, runtime_mode=CUDAGraphMode.FULL
5251:            )
5252:        elif self.parallel_config.use_ubatching:
5253:            if cudagraph_mode.has_full_cudagraphs():
5254:                self.model = UBatchWrapper(
5255:                    self.model, self.vllm_config, CUDAGraphMode.FULL, self.device
5256:                )
5257:            else:
5258:                self.model = UBatchWrapper(
5259:                    self.model, self.vllm_config, CUDAGraphMode.NONE, self.device
5260:                )
5261:
5262:        get_offloader().post_init()
5263:
5264:    def _setup_eagle3_aux_hidden_state_outputs(self) -> None:
5265:        if not self.use_aux_hidden_state_outputs:
5266:            return
5267:
5268:        if not supports_eagle3(self.get_model()):
5269:            raise RuntimeError(
5270:                "Model does not support EAGLE3 interface but "
5271:                "aux_hidden_state_outputs was requested"
5272:            )
5273:        # Try to get auxiliary layers from speculative config,
5274:        # otherwise use model's default layers
5275:        aux_layers = self._get_eagle3_aux_layers_from_config()
5276:        if aux_layers:
5277:            logger.info(
5278:                "Using auxiliary layers from speculative config: %s", aux_layers
5279:            )
5280:        else:
5281:            aux_layers = self.model.get_eagle3_default_aux_hidden_state_layers()
5282:
5283:        self.model.set_aux_hidden_state_layers(aux_layers)
5284:
5285:    def _get_eagle3_aux_layers_from_config(self) -> tuple[int, ...] | None:
5286:        """Extract Eagle3 auxiliary layer indices from speculative config.
5287:
5288:        These indices specify which hidden states from the base model should
5289:        be used as auxiliary inputs for the Eagle3 drafter model during
5290:        speculative decoding.
5291:
5292:        Returns:
5293:            Tuple of layer indices if found in draft model config,
5294:            None otherwise.
5295:        """
5296:        if not (self.speculative_config and self.speculative_config.draft_model_config):
5297:            return None
5298:
5299:        hf_config = self.speculative_config.draft_model_config.hf_config
5300:
5301:        layer_ids = getattr(hf_config, "eagle_aux_hidden_state_layer_ids", None)
5302:        if not layer_ids:
5303:            dflash_config = getattr(hf_config, "dflash_config", None)
5304:            eagle_config = getattr(hf_config, "eagle_config", None)
5305:
5306:            if dflash_config and isinstance(dflash_config, dict):
5307:                # Add 1 to convert DFlash's aux layer id semantics
5308:                layer_ids = [
5309:                    i + 1 for i in (dflash_config.get("target_layer_ids") or [])
5310:                ]
5311:
5312:            if eagle_config and isinstance(eagle_config, dict):
5313:                layer_ids = eagle_config.get("eagle_aux_hidden_state_layer_ids")
5314:
5315:        if layer_ids and isinstance(layer_ids, (list, tuple)):
5316:            return tuple(layer_ids)
5317:
5318:        return None
5319:
5320:    def reload_weights(
5321:        self,
5322:        weights_iterator: Iterable[tuple[str, torch.Tensor]] | None = None,
5323:        weights_path: str | None = None,
5324:        is_checkpoint_format: bool = True,
5325:    ) -> None:
5326:        """
5327:        Reload weights from a weights iterator or from disk
5328:
5329:        Args:
5330:            weights_iterator: weights to load into model
5331:            weights_path: path to load weights from if weights_iterator is not
5332:                provided. Use path of original model if neither is provided.
5333:            is_checkpoint_format: set to False if weights have already been
5334:                processed into kernel format (repacking, renaming, etc.)
5335:        """
5336:        # TODO(@kylesayrs): generalize to all runners and loaders
5337:        # argument validation
5338:        if weights_iterator is None and not is_checkpoint_format:
5339:            logger.warning(
5340:                "Reloading from disk means that weights will be in checkpoint format. "
5341:                "Please use `is_checkpoint_format=True` "
5342:                "to avoid weight reloading errors"
5343:            )
5344:
5345:        model = self.get_model()
5346:        weights_to_load = {name for name, _ in model.named_parameters()}
5347:        counter_before_reloading = time.perf_counter()
5348:
5349:        # load weights from disk if none are provided
5350:        if weights_iterator is None:
5351:            model_loader = get_model_loader(self.load_config)
5352:            if not hasattr(model_loader, "get_all_weights"):
5353:                raise NotImplementedError(
5354:                    f"Model reloading with `{self.load_config.load_format}` format"
5355:                )
5356:
5357:            if weights_path is not None:
5358:                self.model_config.model = weights_path
5359:            weights_iterator = model_loader.get_all_weights(self.model_config, model)
5360:            weights_iterator = cast(
5361:                Iterable[tuple[str, torch.Tensor]], weights_iterator
5362:            )
5363:
5364:        # begin loading weights
5365:        logger.info_once("Reloading weights inplace...")
5366:        if is_checkpoint_format:
5367:            # load weights from checkpoint/ original model format
5368:            initialize_layerwise_reload(model)
5369:            loaded_weights = model.load_weights(weights_iterator)
5370:            finalize_layerwise_reload(model, self.model_config)
5371:
5372:        else:
5373:            # load weights from kernel format
5374:            logger.warning_once(
5375:                "Reloading with `is_checkpoint_format=True` requires that "
5376:                "weights be in kernel format and already sharded",
5377:            )
5378:            loaded_weights = set()
5379:            for name, loaded_weight in weights_iterator:
5380:                param = model.get_parameter(name)  # TODO: buffers?
5381:                param.copy_(loaded_weight)
5382:                loaded_weights.add(name)
5383:
5384:        # logging and validation
5385:        counter_after_reloading = time.perf_counter()
5386:        diff_seconds = counter_after_reloading - counter_before_reloading
5387:        logger.info_once(
5388:            "Reloading and processing weights took %.2f seconds",
5389:            diff_seconds,
5390:        )
5391:        if self.model_config.quantization is None and loaded_weights is not None:
5392:            weights_not_loaded = weights_to_load - loaded_weights
5393:            if weights_not_loaded:
5394:                logger.warning(
5395:                    "Following weights were not loaded from checkpoint: %s",
5396:                    weights_not_loaded,
5397:                )
5398:
5399:    def _get_prompt_logprobs_dict(
5400:        self,
5401:        hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
5402:        num_scheduled_tokens: dict[str, int],
5403:    ) -> dict[str, LogprobsTensors | None]:
5404:        num_prompt_logprobs_dict = self.num_prompt_logprobs
5405:        if not num_prompt_logprobs_dict:
5406:            return {}
5407:
5408:        prompt_logprobs_dict: dict[str, LogprobsTensors | None] = {}
5409:
5410:        # Since prompt logprobs are a rare feature, prioritize simple,
5411:        # maintainable loop over optimal performance.
5412:        completed_prefill_reqs = []
5413:        for req_id, num_prompt_logprobs in num_prompt_logprobs_dict.items():
5414:            num_tokens = num_scheduled_tokens.get(req_id)
5415:            if num_tokens is None:
5416:                # This can happen if the request was preempted in prefill stage.
5417:                continue
5418:
5419:            # Get metadata for this request.
5420:            request = self.requests[req_id]
5421:            if request.prompt_token_ids is None:
5422:                # Prompt logprobs is incompatible with prompt embeddings
5423:                continue
5424:
5425:            num_prompt_tokens = len(request.prompt_token_ids)
5426:            prompt_token_ids = torch.tensor(request.prompt_token_ids).to(
5427:                self.device, non_blocking=True
5428:            )
5429:
5430:            # Set up target LogprobsTensors object.
5431:            logprobs_tensors = request.in_progress_prompt_logprobs_cpu
5432:            if logprobs_tensors is None:
5433:                # Create empty logprobs CPU tensors for the entire prompt.
5434:                # If chunked, we'll copy in slice by slice.
5435:                logprobs_tensors = LogprobsTensors.empty_cpu(
5436:                    num_prompt_tokens - 1, num_prompt_logprobs + 1
5437:                )
5438:                request.in_progress_prompt_logprobs_cpu = logprobs_tensors
5439:
5440:            # Determine number of logits to retrieve.
5441:            start_idx = request.num_computed_tokens
5442:            start_tok = start_idx + 1
5443:            num_remaining_tokens = num_prompt_tokens - start_tok
5444:            if num_tokens <= num_remaining_tokens:
5445:                # This is a chunk, more tokens remain.
5446:                # In the == case, there are no more prompt logprobs to produce
5447:                # but we want to defer returning them to the next step where we
5448:                # have new generated tokens to return.
5449:                num_logits = num_tokens
5450:            else:
5451:                # This is the last chunk of prompt tokens to return.
5452:                num_logits = num_remaining_tokens
5453:                completed_prefill_reqs.append(req_id)
5454:                prompt_logprobs_dict[req_id] = logprobs_tensors
5455:
5456:            if num_logits <= 0:
5457:                # This can happen for the final chunk if we prefilled exactly
5458:                # (num_prompt_tokens - 1) tokens for this request in the prior
5459:                # step. There are no more prompt logprobs to produce.
5460:                continue
5461:
5462:            # Get the logits corresponding to this req's prompt tokens.
5463:            # If this is a partial request (i.e. chunked prefill),
5464:            # then there is prompt logprob generated for each index.
5465:            req_idx = self.input_batch.req_id_to_index[req_id]
5466:            offset = self.query_start_loc.np[req_idx].item()
5467:            prompt_hidden_states = hidden_states[offset : offset + num_logits]
5468:            logits = self.model.compute_logits(prompt_hidden_states)
5469:
5470:            # Get the "target" tokens for each index. For prompt at index i,
5471:            # the token at prompt index i+1 is the "sampled" token we want
5472:            # to gather the logprob for.
5473:            tgt_token_ids = prompt_token_ids[start_tok : start_tok + num_logits]
5474:
5475:            # Compute prompt logprobs.
5476:            logprobs = self.sampler.compute_logprobs(logits)
5477:            token_ids, logprobs, ranks, _ = self.sampler.gather_logprobs(
5478:                logprobs, num_prompt_logprobs, tgt_token_ids
5479:            )
5480:
5481:            # Transfer GPU->CPU async.
5482:            chunk_slice = slice(start_idx, start_idx + num_logits)
5483:            logprobs_tensors.logprob_token_ids[chunk_slice].copy_(
5484:                token_ids, non_blocking=True
5485:            )
5486:            logprobs_tensors.logprobs[chunk_slice].copy_(logprobs, non_blocking=True)
5487:            logprobs_tensors.selected_token_ranks[chunk_slice].copy_(
5488:                ranks, non_blocking=True
5489:            )
5490:
5491:        # Remove requests that have completed prefill from the batch
5492:        # num_prompt_logprobs_dict.
5493:        for req_id in completed_prefill_reqs:
5494:            del num_prompt_logprobs_dict[req_id]
5495:            self.requests[req_id].in_progress_prompt_logprobs_cpu = None
5496:
5497:        # Must synchronize the non-blocking GPU->CPU transfers.
5498:        if prompt_logprobs_dict:
5499:            self._sync_device()
5500:
5501:        return prompt_logprobs_dict
5502:
5503:    def _get_nans_in_logits(
5504:        self,
5505:        logits: torch.Tensor | None,
5506:    ) -> dict[str, int]:
5507:        try:
5508:            if logits is None:
5509:                return {req_id: 0 for req_id in self.input_batch.req_ids}
5510:
5511:            num_nans_in_logits = {}
5512:            num_nans_for_index = logits.isnan().sum(dim=-1).cpu().numpy()
5513:            for req_id in self.input_batch.req_ids:
5514:                req_index = self.input_batch.req_id_to_index[req_id]
5515:                num_nans_in_logits[req_id] = (
5516:                    int(num_nans_for_index[req_index])
5517:                    if num_nans_for_index is not None and req_index < logits.shape[0]
5518:                    else 0
5519:                )
5520:            return num_nans_in_logits
5521:        except IndexError:
5522:            return {}
5523:
5524:    @contextmanager
5525:    def maybe_randomize_inputs(
5526:        self, input_ids: torch.Tensor | None, inputs_embeds: torch.Tensor | None
5527:    ):
5528:        """
5529:        Randomize input_ids if VLLM_RANDOMIZE_DP_DUMMY_INPUTS is set.
5530:        This is to help balance expert-selection
5531:         - during profile_run
5532:         - during DP rank dummy run
5533:        """
5534:
5535:        dp_size = self.vllm_config.parallel_config.data_parallel_size
5536:        randomize_inputs = envs.VLLM_RANDOMIZE_DP_DUMMY_INPUTS and dp_size > 1
5537:        if not randomize_inputs:
5538:            yield
5539:        elif input_ids is not None:
5540:
5541:            @functools.cache
5542:            def rand_input_ids() -> torch.Tensor:
5543:                return torch.randint_like(
5544:                    self.input_ids.gpu,
5545:                    low=0,
5546:                    high=self.model_config.get_vocab_size(),
5547:                )
5548:
5549:            logger.debug_once("Randomizing dummy input_ids for DP Rank")
5550:            input_ids.copy_(rand_input_ids()[: input_ids.size(0)], non_blocking=True)
5551:            yield
5552:            input_ids.fill_(0)
5553:        else:
5554:
5555:            @functools.cache
5556:            def rand_inputs_embeds() -> torch.Tensor:
5557:                return torch.randn_like(
5558:                    self.inputs_embeds.gpu,
5559:                )
5560:
5561:            assert inputs_embeds is not None
5562:            logger.debug_once("Randomizing dummy inputs_embeds for DP Rank")
5563:            inputs_embeds.copy_(
5564:                rand_inputs_embeds()[: inputs_embeds.size(0)], non_blocking=True
5565:            )
5566:            yield
5567:            inputs_embeds.fill_(0)
5568:
5569:    def _get_mm_dummy_batch(
5570:        self,
5571:        modality: str,
5572:        max_items_per_batch: int,
5573:    ) -> BatchedTensorInputs:
5574:        """Dummy data for profiling and precompiling multimodal models."""
5575:        assert self.mm_budget is not None
5576:
5577:        # Don't use `max_items_per_batch` here to avoid redundant computation
5578:        dummy_mm_inputs = self.mm_registry.get_dummy_mm_inputs(
5579:            self.model_config,
5580:            mm_counts={modality: 1},
5581:            cache=self.mm_budget.cache,
5582:        )
5583:        dummy_mm_item = dummy_mm_inputs["mm_kwargs"][modality][0]
5584:
5585:        # We use the cache so that the item is saved to the cache,
5586:        # but not read from the cache
5587:        assert dummy_mm_item is not None, "Item should not already be cached"
5588:
5589:        return next(
5590:            mm_kwargs_batch
5591:            for _, _, mm_kwargs_batch in group_and_batch_mm_kwargs(
5592:                [(modality, dummy_mm_item)] * max_items_per_batch,
5593:                device=self.device,
5594:                pin_memory=self.pin_memory,
5595:            )
5596:        )
5597:
5598:    @torch.inference_mode()
5599:    def _dummy_run(
5600:        self,
5601:        num_tokens: int,
5602:        cudagraph_runtime_mode: CUDAGraphMode | None = None,
5603:        force_attention: bool = False,
5604:        uniform_decode: bool = False,
5605:        allow_microbatching: bool = True,
5606:        skip_eplb: bool = False,
5607:        is_profile: bool = False,
5608:        create_mixed_batch: bool = False,
5609:        remove_lora: bool = True,
5610:        is_graph_capturing: bool = False,
5611:        num_active_loras: int = 0,
5612:        profile_seq_lens: int | None = None,
5613:    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
5614:        """
5615:        Run a dummy forward pass to warm up/profile run or capture the
5616:        CUDA graph for the model.
5617:
5618:        Args:
5619:            num_tokens: Number of tokens to run the dummy forward pass.
5620:            cudagraph_runtime_mode: used to control the behavior.
5621:                - if not set will determine the cudagraph mode based on using
5622:                    the self.cudagraph_dispatcher.
5623:                - CUDAGraphMode.NONE: No cudagraph, for warm up and profile run
5624:                - CUDAGraphMode.PIECEWISE: Piecewise cudagraph.
5625:                - CUDAGraphMode.FULL: Full cudagraph, attention metadata is
5626:                    needed.
5627:            force_attention: If True, always create attention metadata. Used to
5628:                warm up attention backend when mode is NONE.
5629:            uniform_decode: If True, the batch is a uniform decode batch.
5630:            skip_eplb: If True, skip EPLB state update.
5631:            is_profile: If True, this is a profile run.
5632:            create_mixed_batch: If True, create a mixed batch with both decode
5633:                (1 token) and prefill (multiple tokens) requests.
5634:            remove_lora: If False, dummy LoRAs are not destroyed after the run
5635:            num_active_loras: Number of distinct active LoRAs to capture for.
5636:                LoRA is activated when num_active_loras > 0.
5637:            profile_seq_lens: If provided, use this value for seq_lens instead
5638:                of max_query_len. Used to profile attention workspace that
5639:                scales with context length.
5640:        """
5641:        mm_config = self.vllm_config.model_config.multimodal_config
5642:        if mm_config and mm_config.mm_encoder_only:
5643:            # The current dummy run only covers LM execution, so we can skip it.
5644:            # mm encoder dummy run may need to add in the future.
5645:            return torch.tensor([]), torch.tensor([])
5646:
5647:        assert (
5648:            cudagraph_runtime_mode is None
5649:            or cudagraph_runtime_mode.is_valid_runtime_mode()
5650:        )
5651:
5652:        # If cudagraph_mode.decode_mode() == FULL and
5653:        # cudagraph_mode.separate_routine(). This means that we are using
5654:        # different graphs and/or modes for mixed prefill-decode batches vs.
5655:        # uniform decode batches. A uniform decode batch means that all
5656:        # requests have identical query length, except a potential virtual
5657:        # request (shorter) in the batch account for padding.
5658:        # Uniform decode batch could either be common pure decode, where
5659:        # max_query_len == 1, or speculative decode, where
5660:        # max_query_len == 1 + num_spec_decode_tokens.
5661:
5662:        # When setting max_query_len = 1, we switch to and capture the optimized
5663:        # routine of FA2 for pure decode, i.e., Flashdecode + an optimization
5664:        # for GQA/MQA.
5665:        max_query_len = self.uniform_decode_query_len if uniform_decode else num_tokens
5666:
5667:        # Set num_scheduled_tokens based on num_tokens and max_num_seqs
5668:        # for dummy run with LoRA so that the num_reqs collectively
5669:        # has num_tokens in total.
5670:        assert num_tokens <= self.max_num_tokens
5671:        max_num_reqs = self.scheduler_config.max_num_seqs
5672:        if create_mixed_batch:
5673:            assert not uniform_decode
5674:            # Create mixed batch:
5675:            # first half decode tokens, second half one prefill
5676:            num_decode_tokens = min(max_num_reqs - 1, num_tokens // 2)
5677:            num_prefill_tokens = num_tokens - num_decode_tokens
5678:            num_reqs = num_decode_tokens + 1
5679:
5680:            # Create decode requests (1 token each) followed by prefill request
5681:            num_scheduled_tokens_list = [1] * num_decode_tokens + [num_prefill_tokens]
5682:            # Note: Overriding max_query_len to be the prefill tokens
5683:            max_query_len = num_prefill_tokens
5684:        elif uniform_decode:
5685:            assert not create_mixed_batch
5686:            num_reqs = min(max_num_reqs, cdiv(num_tokens, max_query_len))
5687:            num_scheduled_tokens_list = [max_query_len] * num_reqs
5688:            if num_tokens % max_query_len != 0:
5689:                num_scheduled_tokens_list[-1] = num_tokens % max_query_len
5690:        else:
5691:            num_reqs = min(num_tokens, max_num_reqs)
5692:            min_tokens_per_req = num_tokens // num_reqs
5693:            num_scheduled_tokens_list = [min_tokens_per_req] * num_reqs
5694:            num_scheduled_tokens_list[-1] += num_tokens % num_reqs
5695:
5696:        assert sum(num_scheduled_tokens_list) == num_tokens
5697:        assert len(num_scheduled_tokens_list) == num_reqs
5698:        num_scheduled_tokens = np.array(num_scheduled_tokens_list, dtype=np.int32)
5699:        num_tokens_unpadded = int(num_scheduled_tokens.sum())
5700:
5701:        num_sampled_tokens = np.ones(num_reqs, dtype=np.int32)
5702:
5703:        _cudagraph_mode, batch_desc, should_ubatch, num_tokens_across_dp, _ = (
5704:            self._determine_batch_execution_and_padding(
5705:                num_tokens=num_tokens_unpadded,
5706:                num_reqs=num_reqs,
5707:                num_scheduled_tokens_np=num_scheduled_tokens,
5708:                max_num_scheduled_tokens=max_query_len,
5709:                use_cascade_attn=False,
5710:                allow_microbatching=allow_microbatching,
5711:                force_eager=is_profile
5712:                or (cudagraph_runtime_mode == CUDAGraphMode.NONE),
5713:                # `force_uniform_decode` is used for cudagraph capture; because for
5714:                # capturing mixed prefill-decode batches, we sometimes use
5715:                # num_tokens == num_reqs which looks like a uniform decode batch to the
5716:                # dispatcher; but we actually want to capture a piecewise cudagraph
5717:                force_uniform_decode=uniform_decode,
5718:                # `force_has_lora` is used for cudagraph capture; because LoRA is
5719:                # activated later in the context manager, but we need to know the
5720:                # LoRA state when determining the batch descriptor for capture
5721:                force_has_lora=num_active_loras > 0,
5722:                # `force_num_active_loras` is used for cudagraph capture; because we
5723:                # need to capture graphs for specific num_active_loras counts
5724:                force_num_active_loras=num_active_loras,
5725:            )
5726:        )
5727:
5728:        if cudagraph_runtime_mode is None:
5729:            cudagraph_runtime_mode = _cudagraph_mode
5730:        else:
5731:            assert cudagraph_runtime_mode == _cudagraph_mode, (
5732:                f"Cudagraph runtime mode mismatch in dummy_run. "
5733:                f"Expected {_cudagraph_mode}, but got {cudagraph_runtime_mode}."
5734:            )
5735:
5736:        num_tokens_padded = batch_desc.num_tokens
5737:        num_reqs_padded = (
5738:            batch_desc.num_reqs if batch_desc.num_reqs is not None else num_reqs
5739:        )
5740:        ubatch_slices, ubatch_slices_padded = maybe_create_ubatch_slices(
5741:            should_ubatch,
5742:            num_scheduled_tokens,
5743:            num_tokens_padded,
5744:            num_reqs_padded,
5745:            self.vllm_config.parallel_config.num_ubatches,
5746:        )
5747:        logger.debug(
5748:            "ubatch_slices: %s, ubatch_slices_padded: %s",
5749:            ubatch_slices,
5750:            ubatch_slices_padded,
5751:        )
5752:
5753:        attn_metadata: PerLayerAttnMetadata | None = None
5754:
5755:        slot_mappings_by_group, slot_mappings = self._get_slot_mappings(
5756:            num_tokens_padded=num_tokens_padded,
5757:            num_reqs_padded=num_reqs_padded,
5758:            num_tokens_unpadded=num_tokens_unpadded,
5759:            ubatch_slices=ubatch_slices_padded,
5760:        )
5761:
5762:        # Dummy runs have no real slot assignments — fill with -1 so
5763:        # concat_and_cache kernels skip the KV write.
5764:        if slot_mappings_by_group is not None:
5765:            for sm in slot_mappings_by_group.values():
5766:                sm.fill_(-1)
5767:
5768:        # _dummy_run shares pinned CPU buffers (seq_lens, query_start_loc,
5769:        # etc.) with execute_model.  It must participate in the same event
5770:        # protocol so that back-to-back dummy/real steps don't overwrite
5771:        # pinned memory while a prior non_blocking H2D DMA is still reading.
5772:        with self.synchronize_input_prep():
5773:            # If force_attention is True, we always capture attention.
5774:            # Otherwise, it only happens for cudagraph_runtime_mode=FULL.
5775:            if force_attention or cudagraph_runtime_mode == CUDAGraphMode.FULL:
5776:                if profile_seq_lens is not None:
5777:                    seq_lens = profile_seq_lens  # type: ignore[assignment]
5778:                elif create_mixed_batch:
5779:                    # In the mixed batch mode (used for FI warmup), we use
5780:                    # shorter sequence lengths to run faster.
5781:                    # TODO(luka) better system for describing dummy batches
5782:                    seq_lens = torch.tensor(  # type: ignore[assignment]
5783:                        [1] * num_decode_tokens + [num_prefill_tokens + 1],
5784:                        dtype=torch.int,
5785:                    )
5786:                else:
5787:                    seq_lens = max_query_len  # type: ignore[assignment]
5788:                self.optimistic_seq_lens_cpu[:num_reqs] = seq_lens
5789:                self.optimistic_seq_lens_cpu[num_reqs:].fill_(0)
5790:                self.seq_lens.copy_(self.optimistic_seq_lens_cpu, non_blocking=True)
5791:
5792:                cum_num_tokens = self._get_cumsum_and_arange(
5793:                    num_scheduled_tokens, self.query_pos.np
5794:                )
5795:                self.query_start_loc.np[1 : num_reqs + 1] = cum_num_tokens
5796:                self.query_start_loc.np[num_reqs + 1 : num_reqs_padded + 1].fill(
5797:                    cum_num_tokens[-1]
5798:                )
5799:                self.query_start_loc.copy_to_gpu()
5800:
5801:                # Sync block table CPU->GPU so cleared rows from
5802:                # remove_request() are visible to the attention metadata
5803:                # builder. Without this, stale block IDs from finished
5804:                # requests can corrupt Mamba state.
5805:                self.input_batch.block_table.commit_block_table(num_reqs_padded)
5806:
5807:                pad_attn = cudagraph_runtime_mode == CUDAGraphMode.FULL
5808:                attn_metadata, _ = self._build_attention_metadata(
5809:                    num_tokens=num_tokens_unpadded,
5810:                    num_tokens_padded=num_tokens_padded if pad_attn else None,
5811:                    num_reqs=num_reqs_padded,
5812:                    max_query_len=max_query_len,
5813:                    ubatch_slices=(ubatch_slices_padded if pad_attn else ubatch_slices),
5814:                    for_cudagraph_capture=is_graph_capturing,
5815:                    slot_mappings=slot_mappings_by_group,
5816:                    use_spec_decode=self.speculative_config is not None,
5817:                )
5818:
5819:        with self.maybe_dummy_run_with_lora(
5820:            self.lora_config,
5821:            num_scheduled_tokens,
5822:            num_sampled_tokens,
5823:            remove_lora,
5824:            num_active_loras,
5825:        ):
5826:            # Make sure padding doesn't exceed max_num_tokens
5827:            assert num_tokens_padded <= self.max_num_tokens
5828:            model_kwargs = self._init_model_kwargs()
5829:            if self.supports_mm_inputs and not self.model_config.is_encoder_decoder:
5830:                input_ids, inputs_embeds = self._prepare_mm_inputs(num_tokens_padded)
5831:
5832:                model_kwargs = {
5833:                    **model_kwargs,
5834:                    **self._dummy_mm_kwargs(num_reqs),
5835:                }
5836:            elif self.enable_prompt_embeds:
5837:                input_ids = None
5838:                inputs_embeds = self.inputs_embeds.gpu[:num_tokens_padded]
5839:                model_kwargs = self._init_model_kwargs()
5840:            else:
5841:                input_ids = self.input_ids.gpu[:num_tokens_padded]
5842:                inputs_embeds = None
5843:
5844:            if self.uses_mrope:
5845:                positions = self.mrope_positions.gpu[:, :num_tokens_padded]
5846:            elif self.uses_xdrope_dim > 0:
5847:                positions = self.xdrope_positions.gpu[:, :num_tokens_padded]
5848:            else:
5849:                positions = self.positions[:num_tokens_padded]
5850:
5851:            if get_pp_group().is_first_rank:
5852:                intermediate_tensors = None
5853:            else:
5854:                if self.intermediate_tensors is None:
5855:                    self.intermediate_tensors = (
5856:                        self.model.make_empty_intermediate_tensors(
5857:                            batch_size=self.max_num_tokens,
5858:                            dtype=self.model_config.dtype,
5859:                            device=self.device,
5860:                        )
5861:                    )
5862:
5863:                intermediate_tensors = self.sync_and_gather_intermediate_tensors(
5864:                    num_tokens_padded, None, False
5865:                )
5866:
5867:            if ubatch_slices_padded is not None:
5868:                # Adjust values to reflect a single ubatch.
5869:                # TODO(sage,lucas): this is cruft that should be addressed in
5870:                #  the padding refactor.
5871:                num_tokens_padded = ubatch_slices_padded[0].num_tokens
5872:                if num_tokens_across_dp is not None:
5873:                    num_tokens_across_dp[:] = num_tokens_padded
5874:
5875:            with (
5876:                self.maybe_randomize_inputs(input_ids, inputs_embeds),
5877:                set_forward_context(
5878:                    attn_metadata,
5879:                    self.vllm_config,
5880:                    num_tokens=num_tokens_padded,
5881:                    num_tokens_across_dp=num_tokens_across_dp,
5882:                    cudagraph_runtime_mode=cudagraph_runtime_mode,
5883:                    batch_descriptor=batch_desc,
5884:                    ubatch_slices=ubatch_slices_padded,
5885:                    slot_mapping=slot_mappings,
5886:                ),
5887:            ):
5888:                outputs = self.model(
5889:                    input_ids=input_ids,
5890:                    positions=positions,
5891:                    intermediate_tensors=intermediate_tensors,
5892:                    inputs_embeds=inputs_embeds,
5893:                    **model_kwargs,
5894:                )
5895:
5896:            if self.use_aux_hidden_state_outputs:
5897:                hidden_states, _ = outputs
5898:            else:
5899:                hidden_states = outputs
5900:
5901:            if self.speculative_config and (
5902:                self.speculative_config.use_eagle()
5903:                or self.speculative_config.uses_draft_model()
5904:                or self.speculative_config.uses_extract_hidden_states()
5905:            ):
5906:                assert isinstance(
5907:                    self.drafter,
5908:                    EagleProposer
5909:                    | DFlashProposer
5910:                    | DraftModelProposer
5911:                    | ExtractHiddenStatesProposer
5912:                    | Gemma4Proposer,
5913:                )
5914:                assert self.speculative_config is not None
5915:                # Eagle currently only supports PIECEWISE cudagraphs.
5916:                # Therefore only use cudagraphs if the main model uses PIECEWISE
5917:                # NOTE(lucas): this is a hack, need to clean up.
5918:                use_cudagraphs = (
5919:                    (
5920:                        is_graph_capturing
5921:                        and cudagraph_runtime_mode == CUDAGraphMode.PIECEWISE
5922:                    )
5923:                    or (
5924:                        not is_graph_capturing
5925:                        and cudagraph_runtime_mode != CUDAGraphMode.NONE
5926:                    )
5927:                ) and not self.speculative_config.enforce_eager
5928:
5929:                # Note(gnovack) - We need to disable cudagraphs for one of the two
5930:                # lora cases when cudagraph_specialize_lora is enabled. This is a
5931:                # short term mitigation for issue mentioned in
5932:                # https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/28334
5933:                if (
5934:                    self.compilation_config.cudagraph_specialize_lora
5935:                    and num_active_loras > 0
5936:                ):
5937:                    use_cudagraphs = False
5938:
5939:                self.drafter.dummy_run(
5940:                    num_tokens,
5941:                    use_cudagraphs=use_cudagraphs,
5942:                    is_graph_capturing=is_graph_capturing,
5943:                    slot_mappings=slot_mappings,
5944:                )
5945:
5946:        # We register layerwise NVTX hooks here after the first dynamo tracing is
5947:        # done to avoid nvtx operations in hook functions being traced by
5948:        # torch dynamo and causing graph breaks.
5949:        # Note that for DYNAMO_ONCE and VLLM_COMPILE mode,
5950:        # compiled model's dynamo tracing is only done once and the compiled model's
5951:        # __call__ function is replaced by calling the compiled function.
5952:        # So it's safe to register hooks here. Hooks will be registered to
5953:        # both compiled and uncompiled models but they will never
5954:        # be called on the compiled model execution path.
5955:        self._register_layerwise_nvtx_hooks()
5956:
5957:        # This is necessary to avoid blocking DP.
5958:        # For dummy runs, we typically skip EPLB since we don't have any real
5959:        # requests to process.
5960:        # However, in DP settings, there may be cases when some DP ranks do
5961:        # not have any requests to process, so they're executing dummy batches.
5962:        # In such cases, we still have to trigger EPLB to make sure
5963:        # ranks execute the rearrangement in synchronization.
5964:        if not skip_eplb:
5965:            self.eplb_step(is_dummy=True, is_profile=is_profile)
5966:
5967:        logit_indices = np.cumsum(num_scheduled_tokens) - 1
5968:        logit_indices_device = torch.from_numpy(logit_indices).to(
5969:            self.device, non_blocking=True
5970:        )
5971:        return hidden_states, hidden_states[logit_indices_device]
5972:
5973:    @torch.inference_mode()
5974:    def _dummy_sampler_run(
5975:        self,
5976:        hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
5977:    ) -> torch.Tensor:
5978:        # The dummy hidden states may contain special values,
5979:        # like `inf` or `nan`.
5980:        # To avoid breaking the sampler, we use a random tensor here instead.
5981:
5982:        mm_config = self.vllm_config.model_config.multimodal_config
5983:        if mm_config and mm_config.mm_encoder_only:
5984:            # MM Encoder only model no need to run sampler.
5985:            return torch.tensor([])
5986:
5987:        hidden_states = torch.rand_like(hidden_states)
5988:
5989:        logits = self.model.compute_logits(hidden_states)
5990:        num_reqs = logits.size(0)
5991:
5992:        dummy_tensors = lambda v: torch.full((num_reqs,), v, device=self.device)
5993:
5994:        dummy_metadata = SamplingMetadata(
5995:            temperature=dummy_tensors(0.5),
5996:            all_greedy=False,
5997:            all_random=False,
5998:            top_p=dummy_tensors(0.9),
5999:            top_k=dummy_tensors(logits.size(1) - 1),
6000:            generators={},
6001:            max_num_logprobs=None,
6002:            logprob_token_ids=None,
6003:            no_penalties=True,
6004:            prompt_token_ids=None,
6005:            frequency_penalties=dummy_tensors(0.1),
6006:            presence_penalties=dummy_tensors(0.1),
6007:            repetition_penalties=dummy_tensors(0.1),
6008:            output_token_ids=[[] for _ in range(num_reqs)],
6009:            spec_token_ids=[[] for _ in range(num_reqs)],
6010:            allowed_token_ids_mask=None,
6011:            bad_words_token_ids={},
6012:            logitsprocs=LogitsProcessors(),
6013:        )
6014:        try:
6015:            sampler_output = self.sampler(
6016:                logits=logits, sampling_metadata=dummy_metadata
6017:            )
6018:            # Also warm forward_native (taken when generators dict is non-empty),
6019:            # but skip the extra call in 'processed_logits' / 'processed_logprobs'
6020:            # modes — there TopKTopPSampler binds forward = forward_native at
6021:            # init time, so the warmup call is redundant and only inflates peak
6022:            # memory during profile_run.
6023:            # No .clone() of logits: warmup output is discarded, so any in-place
6024:            # mutation by forward_native does not affect correctness.
6025:            if self.sampler.logprobs_mode not in (
6026:                "processed_logits",
6027:                "processed_logprobs",
6028:            ):
6029:                self.sampler(
6030:                    logits=logits,
6031:                    sampling_metadata=replace(
6032:                        dummy_metadata,
6033:                        generators={
6034:                            0: torch.Generator(device=self.device).manual_seed(0)
6035:                        },
6036:                    ),
6037:                )
6038:        except RuntimeError as e:
6039:            if "out of memory" in str(e):
6040:                raise RuntimeError(
6041:                    "CUDA out of memory occurred when warming up sampler with "
6042:                    f"{num_reqs} dummy requests. Please try lowering "
6043:                    "`max_num_seqs` or `gpu_memory_utilization` when "
6044:                    "initializing the engine."
6045:                ) from e
6046:            else:
6047:                raise e
6048:        if self.speculative_config:
6049:            draft_token_ids = [[0] for _ in range(num_reqs)]
6050:            dummy_spec_decode_metadata = SpecDecodeMetadata.make_dummy(
6051:                draft_token_ids, self.device
6052:            )
6053:
6054:            num_tokens = sum(len(ids) for ids in draft_token_ids)
6055:            draft_probs = None
6056:            if (
6057:                self.speculative_config.rejection_sample_method == "standard"
6058:                and self.speculative_config.draft_sample_method == "probabilistic"
6059:            ):
6060:                draft_probs = torch.rand(
6061:                    num_tokens,
6062:                    logits.shape[-1],
6063:                    device=self.device,
6064:                    dtype=torch.float32,
6065:                )
6066:                draft_probs = torch.softmax(draft_probs, dim=-1)
6067:            logits = torch.randn(
6068:                num_tokens + num_reqs,
6069:                logits.shape[-1],
6070:                device=self.device,
6071:                dtype=logits.dtype,
6072:            )
6073:            self.rejection_sampler(
6074:                dummy_spec_decode_metadata,
6075:                draft_probs,
6076:                logits,
6077:                dummy_metadata,
6078:            )
6079:        return sampler_output
6080:
6081:    def _dummy_pooler_run_task(
6082:        self,
6083:        hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
6084:        task: PoolingTask,
6085:    ) -> PoolerOutput:
6086:        num_tokens = hidden_states.shape[0]
6087:        max_num_reqs = self.scheduler_config.max_num_seqs
6088:        num_reqs = min(num_tokens, max_num_reqs)
6089:        min_tokens_per_req = num_tokens // num_reqs
6090:        num_scheduled_tokens_np = np.full(num_reqs, min_tokens_per_req)
6091:        num_scheduled_tokens_np[-1] += num_tokens % num_reqs
6092:        assert np.sum(num_scheduled_tokens_np) == num_tokens
6093:        assert len(num_scheduled_tokens_np) == num_reqs
6094:
6095:        req_num_tokens = num_tokens // num_reqs
6096:
6097:        dummy_prompt_lens = torch.from_numpy(num_scheduled_tokens_np)
6098:        dummy_token_ids = torch.zeros(
6099:            (num_reqs, req_num_tokens), dtype=torch.int32, device=self.device
6100:        )
6101:
6102:        model = cast(VllmModelForPooling, self.get_model())
6103:        dummy_pooling_params = PoolingParams(task=task)
6104:        dummy_pooling_params.verify(self.model_config)
6105:        to_update = model.pooler.get_pooling_updates(task)
6106:        to_update.apply(dummy_pooling_params)
6107:
6108:        dummy_metadata = PoolingMetadata(
6109:            prompt_lens=dummy_prompt_lens,
6110:            prompt_token_ids=dummy_token_ids,
6111:            prompt_token_ids_cpu=dummy_token_ids.cpu(),
6112:            pooling_params=[dummy_pooling_params] * num_reqs,
6113:            pooling_states=[PoolingStates() for i in range(num_reqs)],
6114:        )
6115:
6116:        dummy_metadata.build_pooling_cursor(
6117:            num_scheduled_tokens_np,
6118:            seq_lens_cpu=dummy_prompt_lens,
6119:            device=hidden_states.device,
6120:        )
6121:
6122:        try:
6123:            return model.pooler(
6124:                hidden_states=hidden_states, pooling_metadata=dummy_metadata
6125:            )
6126:        except RuntimeError as e:
6127:            if "out of memory" in str(e):
6128:                raise RuntimeError(
6129:                    "CUDA out of memory occurred when warming up pooler "
6130:                    f"({task=}) with {num_reqs} dummy requests. Please try "
6131:                    "lowering `max_num_seqs` or `gpu_memory_utilization` when "
6132:                    "initializing the engine."
6133:                ) from e
6134:            else:
6135:                raise e
6136:
6137:    @torch.inference_mode()
6138:    def _dummy_pooler_run(
6139:        self,
6140:        hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
6141:    ) -> PoolerOutput:
6142:        mm_config = self.vllm_config.model_config.multimodal_config
6143:        if mm_config and mm_config.mm_encoder_only:
6144:            # MM Encoder only model not need to run pooler.
6145:            return torch.tensor([])
6146:
6147:        # Find the task that has the largest output for subsequent steps
6148:        supported_pooling_tasks = self.get_supported_pooling_tasks()
6149:
6150:        if not supported_pooling_tasks:
6151:            raise RuntimeError(
6152:                f"Model {self.model_config.model} does not support "
6153:                "any pooling tasks. See "
6154:                "https://docs.vllm.ai/en/latest/models/pooling_models.html "
6155:                "to learn more."
6156:            )
6157:
6158:        output_size = dict[PoolingTask, float]()
6159:        for task in supported_pooling_tasks:
6160:            # Run a full batch with each task to ensure none of them OOMs
6161:            output = self._dummy_pooler_run_task(hidden_states, task)
6162:            output_size[task] = sum(o.nbytes for o in output if o is not None)
6163:            del output  # Allow GC
6164:
6165:        max_task = max(output_size.items(), key=lambda x: x[1])[0]
6166:        return self._dummy_pooler_run_task(hidden_states, max_task)
6167:
6168:    def profile_run(self) -> None:
6169:        # Profile with multimodal encoder & encoder cache.
6170:        if self.supports_mm_inputs:
6171:            mm_config = self.model_config.multimodal_config
6172:            if mm_config is not None and mm_config.skip_mm_profiling:
6173:                logger.info(
6174:                    "Skipping memory profiling for multimodal encoder and "
6175:                    "encoder cache."
6176:                )
6177:            else:
6178:                mm_budget = self.mm_budget
6179:                assert mm_budget is not None
6180:
6181:                if (encoder_budget := mm_budget.get_encoder_budget()) > 0:
6182:                    if not mm_budget.mm_max_toks_per_item:
6183:                        # All modality limits are 0 — embedding-only mode.
6184:                        # Budget is non-zero for embedding storage, but
6185:                        # there's no encoder to profile.
6186:                        logger.info(
6187:                            "Skipping encoder profiling for embedding-only "
6188:                            "mode (all modality limits=0 with "
6189:                            "enable_mm_embeds=True).",
6190:                        )
6191:                    else:
6192:                        # NOTE: Currently model is profiled with a single
6193:                        # non-text modality with the max possible input
6194:                        # tokens even when it supports multiple.
6195:                        dummy_modality = mm_budget.get_modality_with_max_tokens()
6196:                        max_mm_items_per_batch = mm_budget.mm_max_items_per_batch[
6197:                            dummy_modality
6198:                        ]
6199:
6200:                        logger.info_once(
6201:                            "Encoder cache will be initialized with a "
6202:                            "budget of %s tokens, and profiled with "
6203:                            "%s %s items of the maximum feature size.",
6204:                            encoder_budget,
6205:                            max_mm_items_per_batch,
6206:                            dummy_modality,
6207:                        )
6208:
6209:                        # Create dummy batch of multimodal inputs.
6210:                        batched_dummy_mm_inputs = self._get_mm_dummy_batch(
6211:                            dummy_modality,
6212:                            max_mm_items_per_batch,
6213:                        )
6214:
6215:                        # Run multimodal encoder.
6216:                        dummy_encoder_outputs = self.model.embed_multimodal(
6217:                            **batched_dummy_mm_inputs
6218:                        )
6219:
6220:                        sanity_check_mm_encoder_outputs(
6221:                            dummy_encoder_outputs,
6222:                            expected_num_items=max_mm_items_per_batch,
6223:                        )
6224:                        for i, output in enumerate(dummy_encoder_outputs):
6225:                            self.encoder_cache[f"tmp_{i}"] = output
6226:
6227:        # Add `is_profile` here to pre-allocate communication buffers
6228:        hidden_states, last_hidden_states = self._dummy_run(
6229:            self.max_num_tokens, is_profile=True
6230:        )
6231:        if get_pp_group().is_last_rank:
6232:            if self.is_pooling_model:
6233:                output = self._dummy_pooler_run(hidden_states)
6234:            else:
6235:                output = self._dummy_sampler_run(last_hidden_states)
6236:        else:
6237:            output = None
6238:        self._sync_device()
6239:        del hidden_states, output
6240:        self.encoder_cache.clear()
6241:        gc.collect()
6242:
6243:    def _init_minimal_kv_cache_for_profiling(self) -> None:
6244:        from vllm.v1.core.kv_cache_utils import (
6245:            get_kv_cache_config_from_groups,
6246:            get_kv_cache_groups,
6247:        )
6248:
6249:        kv_cache_spec = self.get_kv_cache_spec()
6250:        KVCacheSpecRegistry.check_kv_cache_spec_registry(kv_cache_spec)
6251:        kv_cache_groups = get_kv_cache_groups(self.vllm_config, kv_cache_spec)
6252:        min_blocks = self.compilation_config.max_cudagraph_capture_size or 1
6253:
6254:        # Temporarily change num_gpu_blocks_override to allocate a minimal KV cache
6255:        saved_override = self.cache_config.num_gpu_blocks_override
6256:        self.cache_config.num_gpu_blocks_override = min_blocks
6257:        minimal_config = get_kv_cache_config_from_groups(
6258:            self.vllm_config, kv_cache_groups, available_memory=0
6259:        )
6260:        self.cache_config.num_gpu_blocks_override = saved_override
6261:
6262:        self.initialize_kv_cache(minimal_config, is_profiling=True)
6263:        self.cache_config.num_gpu_blocks = minimal_config.num_blocks
6264:
6265:        logger.debug("Initialized minimal KV cache for CUDA graph profiling")
6266:
6267:    @staticmethod
6268:    @contextmanager
6269:    def _freeze_gc():
6270:        gc.collect()
6271:        should_freeze = not envs.VLLM_ENABLE_CUDAGRAPH_GC
6272:        if should_freeze:
6273:            gc.freeze()
6274:        try:
6275:            yield
6276:        finally:
6277:            if should_freeze:
6278:                gc.unfreeze()
6279:                gc.collect()
6280:
6281:    def shutdown(self) -> None:
6282:        """Release GPU tensors (model weights, KV caches, workspace) so that
6283:        memory is reclaimable when running in the same process."""
6284:        from vllm.model_executor.layers.rotary_embedding import _ROPE_DICT
6285:        from vllm.v1.worker.workspace import reset_workspace_manager
6286:
6287:        # Calls torch.accelerator.synchronize()
6288:        self._cleanup_profiling_kv_cache()
6289:        if current_platform.is_rocm():
6290:            # Drop captured graphs before distributed teardown. On ROCm, delayed
6291:            # graph destruction can surface HSA faults in the next engine startup.
6292:            CUDAGraphWrapper.clear_all_graphs()
6293:            BreakableCUDAGraphWrapper.clear_all_graphs()
6294:            self.encoder_cudagraph_manager = None
6295:        self.compilation_config.static_forward_context.clear()
6296:        self.model = None  # type: ignore[assignment]
6297:        _ROPE_DICT.clear()
6298:
6299:        reset_workspace_manager()
6300:        if current_platform.is_rocm():
6301:            gc.collect()
6302:            torch.accelerator.empty_cache()
6303:            torch.accelerator.synchronize()
6304:
6305:    def _cleanup_profiling_kv_cache(self) -> None:
6306:        torch.accelerator.synchronize()
6307:        if hasattr(self, "kv_caches") and self.kv_caches:
6308:            for i in range(len(self.kv_caches)):
6309:                self.kv_caches[i] = None  # type: ignore
6310:            self.kv_caches.clear()
6311:        if hasattr(self, "cross_layers_kv_cache"):
6312:            self.cross_layers_kv_cache = None
6313:            self.cross_layers_attn_backend = None
6314:        if hasattr(self, "attn_groups"):
6315:            self.attn_groups.clear()
6316:        if hasattr(self, "kv_cache_config"):
6317:            delattr(self, "kv_cache_config")
6318:        self.cache_config.num_gpu_blocks = None
6319:
6320:        for layer in self.compilation_config.static_forward_context.values():
6321:            if hasattr(layer, "kv_cache"):
6322:                kv_cache = layer.kv_cache
6323:                layer.kv_cache = (
6324:                    torch.tensor([]) if isinstance(kv_cache, torch.Tensor) else []
6325:                )
6326:            # Clean up quantized KV cache scale views
6327:            # (int8_per_token_head, fp8_per_token_head)
6328:            if hasattr(layer, "impl"):
6329:                if hasattr(layer.impl, "_k_scale_cache"):
6330:                    layer.impl._k_scale_cache = None
6331:                if hasattr(layer.impl, "_v_scale_cache"):
6332:                    layer.impl._v_scale_cache = None
6333:
6334:        gc.collect()
6335:        torch.accelerator.empty_cache()
6336:
6337:        logger.debug("Cleaned up profiling KV cache and CUDA graphs")
6338:
6339:    @torch.inference_mode()
6340:    def _create_encoder_cudagraph_manager(self) -> "EncoderCudaGraphManager | None":
6341:        if not (
6342:            self.compilation_config.cudagraph_mm_encoder and self.supports_mm_inputs
6343:        ):
6344:            return None
6345:
6346:        # Use get_model() to unwrap CUDAGraphWrapper/UBatchWrapper, because
6347:        # @runtime_checkable Protocol isinstance() checks do not work through
6348:        # __getattr__ forwarding.
6349:        from vllm.model_executor.models.interfaces import (
6350:            SupportsEncoderCudaGraph,
6351:            supports_encoder_cudagraph,
6352:        )
6353:        from vllm.v1.worker.encoder_cudagraph import (
6354:            EncoderCudaGraphManager,
6355:        )
6356:
6357:        raw_model = self.get_model()
6358:        if not supports_encoder_cudagraph(raw_model):
6359:            return None
6360:
6361:        return EncoderCudaGraphManager(
6362:            vllm_config=self.vllm_config,
6363:            device=self.device,
6364:            dtype=self.dtype,
6365:            model=cast(SupportsEncoderCudaGraph, raw_model),
6366:        )
6367:
6368:    @torch.inference_mode()
6369:    def _maybe_init_encoder_cudagraph_manager(self) -> None:
6370:        if self.encoder_cudagraph_manager is None:
6371:            self.encoder_cudagraph_manager = self._create_encoder_cudagraph_manager()
6372:            if self.encoder_cudagraph_manager is not None:
6373:                logger.info("Initialized EncoderCudaGraphManager for vision encoder")
6374:
6375:    @torch.inference_mode()
6376:    def profile_cudagraph_memory(self) -> int:
6377:        with set_current_vllm_config(self.vllm_config):
6378:            self._init_minimal_kv_cache_for_profiling()
6379:
6380:        saved_num_cudagraph_captured = compilation_counter.num_cudagraph_captured
6381:
6382:        capture_descs = self.cudagraph_dispatcher.get_capture_descs()
6383:        # Use a temporary manager for memory profiling. The persistent manager
6384:        # is initialized later so it does not keep profiling-only graph state.
6385:        encoder_cudagraph_manager = self._create_encoder_cudagraph_manager()
6386:
6387:        decoder_graphs = sum(len(descs) for _, descs in capture_descs)
6388:        encoder_graphs = (
6389:            encoder_cudagraph_manager.get_num_graphs_to_capture()
6390:            if encoder_cudagraph_manager is not None
6391:            else 0
6392:        )
6393:        total_graphs = decoder_graphs + encoder_graphs
6394:        if total_graphs == 0:
6395:            logger.debug("No CUDA graphs will be captured, skipping profiling")
6396:            self._cleanup_profiling_kv_cache()
6397:            return 0
6398:
6399:        graph_groups = [
6400:            *(
6401:                f"{mode.name}={len(descs)} (largest={descs[0].num_tokens})"
6402:                for mode, descs in capture_descs
6403:                if descs
6404:            ),
6405:        ]
6406:        if encoder_graphs > 0:
6407:            graph_groups.append(
6408:                f"ENCODER={encoder_graphs} "
6409:                f"(largest={encoder_cudagraph_manager.token_budgets[-1]})"
6410:            )
6411:
6412:        logger.info("Profiling CUDA graph memory: %s", ", ".join(graph_groups))
6413:
6414:        # Use a temporary pool for profiling to avoid fragmentation in the main pool.
6415:        profiling_pool = current_platform.graph_pool_handle()
6416:        encoder_profiling_pool = current_platform.graph_pool_handle()
6417:        original_pools: dict[int, Any] = {}
6418:        all_wrappers = list(CUDAGraphWrapper._all_instances) + list(
6419:            BreakableCUDAGraphWrapper._all_instances
6420:        )
6421:        for instance in all_wrappers:
6422:            original_pools[id(instance)] = instance.graph_pool
6423:            instance.graph_pool = profiling_pool
6424:
6425:        shared_memory_estimate = {}
6426:        per_graph_estimate = {}
6427:        encoder_memory_estimate = 0
6428:
6429:        # Cleanup-only guard: CUDA graph capture errors should still propagate
6430:        # because encoder graph capture is opt-in.
6431:        try:
6432:            set_cudagraph_capturing_enabled(True)
6433:            with self._freeze_gc(), graph_capture(device=self.device):
6434:                torch.accelerator.synchronize()
6435:                torch.accelerator.empty_cache()
6436:
6437:                for mode, descs in capture_descs:
6438:                    profile_descs = descs[:2]
6439:                    mem_samples: list[int] = []
6440:
6441:                    for i, desc in enumerate(profile_descs):
6442:                        mem_before = torch.cuda.mem_get_info()[0]
6443:                        self._warmup_and_capture(
6444:                            desc,
6445:                            cudagraph_runtime_mode=mode,
6446:                            profile_seq_lens=(
6447:                                min(
6448:                                    self.max_model_len,
6449:                                    self.max_num_tokens // desc.num_tokens,
6450:                                )
6451:                                if mode == CUDAGraphMode.FULL and i == 0
6452:                                else None
6453:                            ),
6454:                        )
6455:                        torch.accelerator.synchronize()
6456:                        free_after = torch.cuda.mem_get_info()[0]
6457:                        mem_samples.append(mem_before - free_after)
6458:
6459:                    first_capture = mem_samples[0]
6460:                    # Use at least 1 MiB per graph for driver overhead
6461:                    per_graph = max(
6462:                        mem_samples[1] if len(mem_samples) > 1 else 0, 1 << 20
6463:                    )
6464:
6465:                    shared_memory_estimate[mode] = first_capture
6466:                    per_graph_estimate[mode] = per_graph * (len(descs) - 1)
6467:
6468:                    logger.debug(
6469:                        "Estimated %s CUDA graph memory: "
6470:                        "%.2f MiB first-capture + (%d-1) × %.2f MiB per-graph",
6471:                        mode.name,
6472:                        first_capture / (1 << 20),
6473:                        len(descs),
6474:                        per_graph / (1 << 20),
6475:                    )
6476:
6477:                if encoder_cudagraph_manager is not None:
6478:                    mem_before = torch.cuda.mem_get_info()[0]
6479:                    encoder_cudagraph_manager.capture(graph_pool=encoder_profiling_pool)
6480:                    torch.accelerator.synchronize()
6481:                    free_after = torch.cuda.mem_get_info()[0]
6482:                    encoder_memory_estimate = max(mem_before - free_after, 0)
6483:
6484:                    logger.debug(
6485:                        "Estimated encoder CUDA graph memory: %.2f MiB for %d graphs",
6486:                        encoder_memory_estimate / (1 << 20),
6487:                        encoder_graphs,
6488:                    )
6489:        finally:
6490:            set_cudagraph_capturing_enabled(False)
6491:            CUDAGraphWrapper.clear_all_graphs()
6492:            BreakableCUDAGraphWrapper.clear_all_graphs()
6493:            if encoder_cudagraph_manager is not None:
6494:                encoder_cudagraph_manager.clear()
6495:            all_wrappers = list(CUDAGraphWrapper._all_instances) + list(
6496:                BreakableCUDAGraphWrapper._all_instances
6497:            )
6498:            for instance in all_wrappers:
6499:                if id(instance) in original_pools:
6500:                    instance.graph_pool = original_pools[id(instance)]
6501:            for key_set in self.cudagraph_dispatcher.cudagraph_keys.values():
6502:                key_set.clear()
6503:            self.cudagraph_dispatcher.keys_initialized = False
6504:            self.maybe_remove_all_loras(self.lora_config)
6505:            self._cleanup_profiling_kv_cache()
6506:            compilation_counter.num_cudagraph_captured = saved_num_cudagraph_captured
6507:
6508:        # FULL and PIECEWISE graphs share the global pool at runtime and are
6509:        # never replayed concurrently, so the pool overlays their memory.
6510:        # Take the max to avoid double-counting the overlap.
6511:        decoder_estimate = max(shared_memory_estimate.values(), default=0) + sum(
6512:            per_graph_estimate.values()
6513:        )
6514:        # Encoder graphs use a manager-local pool at runtime, separate from the
6515:        # decoder pool, so add their estimate instead of overlaying it.
6516:        total_estimate = decoder_estimate + encoder_memory_estimate
6517:        logger.info(
6518:            "Estimated CUDA graph memory: %.2f GiB total",
6519:            total_estimate / (1 << 30),
6520:        )
6521:
6522:        return int(total_estimate)
6523:
6524:    @instrument(span_name="Capture model")
6525:    def capture_model(self) -> int:
6526:        if self.compilation_config.cudagraph_mode == CUDAGraphMode.NONE:
6527:            logger.warning(
6528:                "Skipping CUDA graph capture. To turn on CUDA graph capture, "
6529:                "ensure `cudagraph_mode` was not manually set to `NONE`"
6530:            )
6531:            return 0
6532:
6533:        # Initialize encoder CUDA graph manager if enabled.
6534:        self._maybe_init_encoder_cudagraph_manager()
6535:
6536:        compilation_counter.num_gpu_runner_capture_triggers += 1
6537:
6538:        start_time = time.perf_counter()
6539:
6540:        # Trigger CUDA graph capture for specific shapes.
6541:        # Capture the large shapes first so that the smaller shapes
6542:        # can reuse the memory pool allocated for the large shapes.
6543:        set_cudagraph_capturing_enabled(True)
6544:        with self._freeze_gc(), graph_capture(device=self.device):
6545:            torch.accelerator.synchronize()
6546:            torch.accelerator.empty_cache()
6547:            start_free_gpu_memory = torch.cuda.mem_get_info()[0]
6548:
6549:            for (
6550:                runtime_mode,
6551:                batch_descs,
6552:            ) in self.cudagraph_dispatcher.get_capture_descs():
6553:                self._capture_cudagraphs(
6554:                    batch_descriptors=batch_descs,
6555:                    cudagraph_runtime_mode=runtime_mode,
6556:                )
6557:                torch.accelerator.synchronize()
6558:
6559:            # Capture encoder CUDA graphs if enabled
6560:            if self.encoder_cudagraph_manager is not None:
6561:                encoder_graph_pool = current_platform.graph_pool_handle()
6562:                self.encoder_cudagraph_manager.capture(graph_pool=encoder_graph_pool)
6563:
6564:            torch.accelerator.synchronize()
6565:            end_free_gpu_memory = torch.cuda.mem_get_info()[0]
6566:
6567:        # Disable cudagraph capturing globally, so any unexpected cudagraph
6568:        # capturing will be detected and raise an error after here.
6569:        # Note: We don't put it into graph_capture context manager because
6570:        # we may do lazy capturing in future that still allows capturing
6571:        # after here.
6572:        set_cudagraph_capturing_enabled(False)
6573:
6574:        torch.accelerator.synchronize()
6575:        torch.accelerator.empty_cache()
6576:
6577:        # Lock workspace to prevent resizing during execution.
6578:        # Max workspace sizes should have been captured during warmup/profiling.
6579:        lock_workspace()
6580:
6581:        end_time = time.perf_counter()
6582:        elapsed_time = end_time - start_time
6583:        cuda_graph_size = start_free_gpu_memory - end_free_gpu_memory
6584:        # This usually takes 5~20 seconds.
6585:        logger.info_once(
6586:            "Graph capturing finished in %.0f secs, took %.2f GiB",
6587:            elapsed_time,
6588:            cuda_graph_size / (1 << 30),
6589:        )
6590:        return cuda_graph_size
6591:
6592:    def _warmup_and_capture(
6593:        self,
6594:        desc: BatchDescriptor,
6595:        cudagraph_runtime_mode: CUDAGraphMode,
6596:        profile_seq_lens: int | None = None,
6597:        allow_microbatching: bool = False,
6598:        num_warmups: int | None = None,
6599:    ):
6600:        if num_warmups is None:
6601:            num_warmups = self.compilation_config.cudagraph_num_of_warmups
6602:        force_attention = cudagraph_runtime_mode == CUDAGraphMode.FULL
6603:        for _ in range(num_warmups):
6604:            self._dummy_run(
6605:                desc.num_tokens,
6606:                cudagraph_runtime_mode=CUDAGraphMode.NONE,
6607:                force_attention=force_attention,
6608:                uniform_decode=desc.uniform,
6609:                allow_microbatching=allow_microbatching,
6610:                skip_eplb=True,
6611:                remove_lora=False,
6612:                num_active_loras=desc.num_active_loras,
6613:                profile_seq_lens=profile_seq_lens,
6614:            )
6615:        self._dummy_run(
6616:            desc.num_tokens,
6617:            cudagraph_runtime_mode=cudagraph_runtime_mode,
6618:            uniform_decode=desc.uniform,
6619:            allow_microbatching=allow_microbatching,
6620:            skip_eplb=True,
6621:            remove_lora=False,
6622:            num_active_loras=desc.num_active_loras,
6623:            is_graph_capturing=True,
6624:            profile_seq_lens=profile_seq_lens,
6625:        )
6626:
6627:    def _capture_cudagraphs(
6628:        self,
6629:        batch_descriptors: list[BatchDescriptor],
6630:        cudagraph_runtime_mode: CUDAGraphMode,
6631:    ):
6632:        assert (
6633:            cudagraph_runtime_mode != CUDAGraphMode.NONE
6634:            and cudagraph_runtime_mode.is_valid_runtime_mode()
6635:        ), f"Invalid cudagraph runtime mode: {cudagraph_runtime_mode}"
6636:
6637:        if not batch_descriptors:
6638:            return
6639:
6640:        uniform_decode = batch_descriptors[0].uniform
6641:
6642:        # Only rank 0 should print progress bar during capture
6643:        if is_global_first_rank():
6644:            batch_descriptors = tqdm(
6645:                batch_descriptors,
6646:                disable=not self.load_config.use_tqdm_on_load,
6647:                desc="Capturing CUDA graphs ({}, {})".format(
6648:                    "decode" if uniform_decode else "mixed prefill-decode",
6649:                    cudagraph_runtime_mode.name,
6650:                ),
6651:            )
6652:
6653:        # We skip EPLB here since we don't want to record dummy metrics
6654:        for batch_desc in batch_descriptors:
6655:            # We currently only capture ubatched graphs when its a FULL
6656:            # cudagraph, a uniform decode batch, and the number of tokens
6657:            # is above the threshold. Otherwise we just capture a non-ubatched
6658:            # version of the graph
6659:            allow_microbatching = (
6660:                self.parallel_config.use_ubatching
6661:                and cudagraph_runtime_mode == CUDAGraphMode.FULL
6662:                and uniform_decode
6663:                and check_ubatch_thresholds(
6664:                    config=self.vllm_config.parallel_config,
6665:                    num_tokens=batch_desc.num_tokens,
6666:                    uniform_decode=uniform_decode,
6667:                )
6668:            )
6669:            self._warmup_and_capture(
6670:                batch_desc,
6671:                cudagraph_runtime_mode=cudagraph_runtime_mode,
6672:                allow_microbatching=allow_microbatching,
6673:            )
6674:            torch.accelerator.synchronize()
6675:        self.maybe_remove_all_loras(self.lora_config)
6676:
6677:    def initialize_attn_backend(
6678:        self,
6679:        kv_cache_config: KVCacheConfig,
6680:        is_profiling: bool = False,
6681:    ) -> None:
6682:        """
6683:        Initialize the attention backends and attention metadata builders.
6684:        """
6685:        assert len(self.attn_groups) == 0, "Attention backends are already initialized"
6686:
6687:        class AttentionGroupKey(NamedTuple):
6688:            """Deduplication key for attention groups within a KV cache group.
6689:
6690:            Splits on per-rank ``num_heads_q`` in addition to backend + spec
6691:            so layers with different Q-head counts (e.g. a spec-decode draft
6692:            with fewer attention heads than its target) get separate metadata
6693:            builders. The builders' scratch (e.g. ``softmax_segm_*`` in
6694:            ``triton_attn``, ``num_qo_heads`` in FlashInfer) is sized by
6695:            ``num_heads_q`` and assumes uniformity within the group; see
6696:            ``get_num_attention_heads_from_layers`` in
6697:            ``vllm/v1/attention/backends/utils.py``.
6698:            """
6699:
6700:            attn_backend: type[AttentionBackend]
6701:            kv_cache_spec: KVCacheSpec
6702:            num_heads_q: int
6703:
6704:        def get_attn_backends_for_group(
6705:            kv_cache_group_spec: KVCacheGroupSpec,
6706:        ) -> tuple[dict[AttentionGroupKey, list[str]], set[type[AttentionBackend]]]:
6707:            layer_type = cast(type[Any], AttentionLayerBase)
6708:            layers = get_layers_from_vllm_config(
6709:                self.vllm_config, layer_type, kv_cache_group_spec.layer_names
6710:            )
6711:            attn_backends = {}
6712:            attn_backend_layers = defaultdict(list)
6713:            # Dedupe based on full class name; this is a bit safer than
6714:            # using the class itself as the key because when we create dynamic
6715:            # attention backend subclasses (e.g. ChunkedLocalAttention) unless
6716:            # they are cached correctly, there will be different objects per
6717:            # layer.
6718:            for layer_name in kv_cache_group_spec.layer_names:
6719:                attn_backend = layers[layer_name].get_attn_backend()
6720:
6721:                if layer_name in self.kv_sharing_fast_prefill_eligible_layers:
6722:                    attn_backend = create_fast_prefill_custom_backend(
6723:                        "FastPrefill",
6724:                        attn_backend,  # type: ignore[arg-type]
6725:                    )
6726:
