6727:                full_cls_name = attn_backend.full_cls_name()
6728:                layer_kv_cache_spec = kv_cache_group_spec.kv_cache_spec
6729:                if isinstance(layer_kv_cache_spec, UniformTypeKVCacheSpecs):
6730:                    layer_kv_cache_spec = layer_kv_cache_spec.kv_cache_specs[layer_name]
6731:                # Non-Attention layer types (e.g. Mamba1, ShortConv) do not
6732:                # expose ``num_heads``; fall back to 0 so they cluster as
6733:                # before. Such layers never coexist with Attention in a
6734:                # single KV cache group (different KVCacheSpec), so the
6735:                # fallback can never spuriously merge them with attention
6736:                # layers.
6737:                num_heads_q = getattr(layers[layer_name], "num_heads", 0)
6738:                key = (full_cls_name, layer_kv_cache_spec, num_heads_q)
6739:                attn_backends[key] = AttentionGroupKey(
6740:                    attn_backend, layer_kv_cache_spec, num_heads_q
6741:                )
6742:                attn_backend_layers[key].append(layer_name)
6743:            return (
6744:                {attn_backends[k]: v for k, v in attn_backend_layers.items()},
6745:                set(group_key.attn_backend for group_key in attn_backends.values()),
6746:            )
6747:
6748:        def create_attn_groups(
6749:            attn_backends_map: dict[AttentionGroupKey, list[str]],
6750:            kv_cache_group_id: int,
6751:        ) -> list[AttentionGroup]:
6752:            attn_groups: list[AttentionGroup] = []
6753:            for key, layer_names in attn_backends_map.items():
6754:                attn_group = AttentionGroup(
6755:                    key.attn_backend,
6756:                    layer_names,
6757:                    key.kv_cache_spec,
6758:                    kv_cache_group_id,
6759:                )
6760:
6761:                attn_groups.append(attn_group)
6762:            return attn_groups
6763:
6764:        attention_backend_maps = []
6765:        attention_backend_list = []
6766:        for kv_cache_group_spec in kv_cache_config.kv_cache_groups:
6767:            attn_backends = get_attn_backends_for_group(kv_cache_group_spec)
6768:            attention_backend_maps.append(attn_backends[0])
6769:            attention_backend_list.append(attn_backends[1])
6770:
6771:        # Resolve cudagraph_mode before actually initialize metadata_builders
6772:        self._check_and_update_cudagraph_mode(
6773:            attention_backend_list,
6774:            kv_cache_config.kv_cache_groups,
6775:            is_profiling=is_profiling,
6776:        )
6777:
6778:        # Check if attention backend supports PCP&DCP and related features.
6779:        check_attention_cp_compatibility(self.vllm_config)
6780:
6781:        for i, attn_backend_map in enumerate(attention_backend_maps):
6782:            self.attn_groups.append(create_attn_groups(attn_backend_map, i))
6783:
6784:    def initialize_metadata_builders(
6785:        self, kv_cache_config: KVCacheConfig, kernel_block_sizes: list[int]
6786:    ) -> None:
6787:        """
6788:        Create the metadata builders for all KV cache groups and attn groups.
6789:        """
6790:        for kv_cache_group_id in range(len(kv_cache_config.kv_cache_groups)):
6791:            for attn_group in self.attn_groups[kv_cache_group_id]:
6792:                attn_group.create_metadata_builders(
6793:                    self.vllm_config,
6794:                    self.device,
6795:                    kernel_block_sizes[kv_cache_group_id]
6796:                    if kv_cache_group_id < len(kernel_block_sizes)
6797:                    else None,
6798:                    num_metadata_builders=1
6799:                    if not self.parallel_config.use_ubatching
6800:                    else self.parallel_config.num_ubatches,
6801:                )
6802:        # Calculate reorder batch threshold (if needed)
6803:        # Note (tdoublep): do this *after* constructing builders,
6804:        # because some of them change the threshold at init time.
6805:        self.calculate_reorder_batch_threshold()
6806:
6807:        # Initialize drafter attention backend
6808:        if self.speculative_config and (
6809:            self.speculative_config.use_eagle()
6810:            or self.speculative_config.uses_draft_model()
6811:        ):
6812:            assert isinstance(
6813:                self.drafter,
6814:                EagleProposer | DFlashProposer | DraftModelProposer | Gemma4Proposer,
6815:            )
6816:            self.drafter.initialize_attn_backend(kv_cache_config, kernel_block_sizes)
6817:
6818:    def _check_and_update_cudagraph_mode(
6819:        self,
6820:        attention_backends: list[set[type[AttentionBackend]]],
6821:        kv_cache_groups: list[KVCacheGroupSpec],
6822:        is_profiling: bool = False,
6823:    ) -> None:
6824:        """
6825:        Resolve the cudagraph_mode when there are multiple attention
6826:        groups with potential conflicting CUDA graph support.
6827:        Then initialize the cudagraph_dispatcher based on the resolved
6828:        cudagraph_mode.
6829:        """
6830:        min_cg_support = AttentionCGSupport.ALWAYS
6831:        min_cg_attn_backend = None
6832:
6833:        for attn_backend_set, kv_cache_group in zip(
6834:            attention_backends, kv_cache_groups
6835:        ):
6836:            for attn_backend in attn_backend_set:
6837:                builder_cls = attn_backend.get_builder_cls()
6838:
6839:                cg_support = builder_cls.get_cudagraph_support(
6840:                    self.vllm_config, kv_cache_group.kv_cache_spec
6841:                )
6842:                if cg_support.value < min_cg_support.value:
6843:                    min_cg_support = cg_support
6844:                    min_cg_attn_backend = attn_backend.__name__
6845:        cudagraph_mode = self.compilation_config.resolve_cudagraph_mode_and_sizes(
6846:            min_cg_support,
6847:            min_cg_attn_backend,
6848:            self.uniform_decode_query_len,
6849:            self.parallel_config.tensor_parallel_size,
6850:            self.kv_cache_config,
6851:            self.max_num_reqs,
6852:            is_profiling=is_profiling,
6853:        )
6854:        # Trigger cudagraph dispatching keys initialization after
6855:        # resolved cudagraph mode.
6856:        self.cudagraph_dispatcher.initialize_cudagraph_keys(
6857:            cudagraph_mode, self.uniform_decode_query_len
6858:        )
6859:
6860:        # Initialize drafter's cudagraph dispatcher if using spec decode.
6861:        if self.speculative_config and (
6862:            self.speculative_config.use_eagle()
6863:            or self.speculative_config.uses_extract_hidden_states()
6864:        ):
6865:            assert isinstance(
6866:                self.drafter,
6867:                EagleProposer
6868:                | DFlashProposer
6869:                | ExtractHiddenStatesProposer
6870:                | Gemma4Proposer,
6871:            )
6872:            self.drafter.initialize_cudagraph_keys(cudagraph_mode)
6873:
6874:    def calculate_reorder_batch_threshold(self) -> None:
6875:        """
6876:        Choose the minimum reorder batch threshold from all attention groups.
6877:        Backends should be able to support lower threshold then what they request
6878:        just may have a performance penalty due to that backend treating decodes
6879:        as prefills.
6880:        """
6881:        min_none_high = lambda a, b: a if b is None else b if a is None else min(a, b)
6882:
6883:        reorder_batch_thresholds: list[int | None] = [
6884:            group.get_metadata_builder().reorder_batch_threshold
6885:            for group in self._attn_group_iterator()
6886:        ]
6887:        # If there are no attention groups (attention-free model) or no backend
6888:        # reports a threshold, leave reordering disabled.
6889:        if len(reorder_batch_thresholds) == 0:
6890:            self.reorder_batch_threshold = None
6891:            return
6892:        self.reorder_batch_threshold = reduce(min_none_high, reorder_batch_thresholds)  # type: ignore[assignment]
6893:
6894:    def _set_mm_prefix_range_for_metadata(
6895:        self,
6896:        attn_metadata: Any,
6897:        req_doc_ranges: dict[int, list[tuple[int, int]]],
6898:    ) -> None:
6899:        """Set mm_prefix_range for all attention metadata objects.
6900:
6901:        This method handles both list and non-list attention metadata,
6902:        computing mm_prefix_range_tensor once and sharing it across all
6903:        metadata objects to avoid redundant host-to-device transfers.
6904:        """
6905:        from vllm.v1.attention.backends.triton_attn import (
6906:            TritonAttentionMetadata,
6907:        )
6908:
6909:        # Get all metadata objects from either list or dict structure
6910:        metadata_list = []
6911:        if isinstance(attn_metadata, list):
6912:            for ub_metadata in attn_metadata:
6913:                metadata_list.extend(ub_metadata.values())
6914:        else:
6915:            metadata_list.extend(attn_metadata.values())
6916:
6917:        # Set mm_prefix_range for all metadata and compute tensor once
6918:        shared_tensor = None
6919:        for metadata in metadata_list:
6920:            metadata.mm_prefix_range = req_doc_ranges  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
6921:
6922:            # Only compute tensor for TritonAttentionMetadata
6923:            if isinstance(metadata, TritonAttentionMetadata):
6924:                if shared_tensor is None:
6925:                    shared_tensor = (
6926:                        TritonAttentionMetadata.compute_mm_prefix_range_tensor(
6927:                            req_doc_ranges,
6928:                            metadata.seq_lens.shape[0],  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
6929:                            metadata.seq_lens.device,  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
6930:                        )
6931:                    )
6932:                metadata.mm_prefix_range_tensor = shared_tensor
6933:
6934:    def may_reinitialize_input_batch(
6935:        self, kv_cache_config: KVCacheConfig, kernel_block_sizes: list[int]
6936:    ) -> None:
6937:        """
6938:        Re-initialize the input batch if the block sizes are different from
6939:        what it was originally created with. This happens when the final
6940:        block size (determined after model loading) differs from the
6941:        placeholder used during __init__, or when there are multiple
6942:        KV cache groups.
6943:
6944:        Args:
6945:            kv_cache_config: The KV cache configuration.
6946:            kernel_block_sizes: The kernel block sizes for each KV cache group.
6947:        """
6948:        block_sizes = []
6949:        max_num_blocks = []
6950:        max_model_len = max(self.max_model_len, self.max_encoder_len)
6951:        for kv_cache_group in kv_cache_config.kv_cache_groups:
6952:            if isinstance(kv_cache_group.kv_cache_spec, EncoderOnlyAttentionSpec):
6953:                continue
6954:            block_size = kv_cache_group.kv_cache_spec.block_size
6955:            block_sizes.append(block_size)
6956:            max_num_blocks_per_req = cdiv(
6957:                max_model_len, block_size * get_total_cp_world_size()
6958:            )
6959:            if isinstance(kv_cache_group.kv_cache_spec, MambaSpec):
6960:                max_num_blocks_per_req = (
6961:                    max_num_blocks_per_req
6962:                    if self.cache_config.enable_prefix_caching
6963:                    else 1
6964:                ) + kv_cache_group.kv_cache_spec.num_speculative_blocks
6965:            max_num_blocks.append(max_num_blocks_per_req)
6966:
6967:        if (
6968:            block_sizes != self._init_block_sizes
6969:            or kernel_block_sizes != self._init_kernel_block_sizes
6970:        ):
6971:            self._init_block_sizes = block_sizes
6972:            self._init_kernel_block_sizes = kernel_block_sizes
6973:            self.input_batch = InputBatch(
6974:                max_num_reqs=self.max_num_reqs,
6975:                max_model_len=max_model_len,
6976:                max_num_batched_tokens=self.max_num_tokens,
6977:                device=self.device,
6978:                pin_memory=self.pin_memory,
6979:                vocab_size=self.model_config.get_vocab_size(),
6980:                block_sizes=block_sizes,
6981:                kernel_block_sizes=kernel_block_sizes,
6982:                max_num_blocks_per_req=max_num_blocks,
6983:                num_spec_tokens=self.num_spec_tokens,
6984:                logitsprocs=self.input_batch.logitsprocs,
6985:                logitsprocs_need_output_token_ids=self.input_batch.logitsprocs_need_output_token_ids,
6986:                is_pooling_model=self.is_pooling_model,
6987:                reasoning_config=self.vllm_config.reasoning_config,
6988:            )
6989:
6990:        assert self._init_block_sizes == block_sizes, (
6991:            f"InputBatch block_sizes {self._init_block_sizes} != "
6992:            f"kv_cache block_sizes {block_sizes}"
6993:        )
6994:        assert self._init_kernel_block_sizes == kernel_block_sizes, (
6995:            f"InputBatch kernel_block_sizes {self._init_kernel_block_sizes} "
6996:            f"!= kv_cache kernel_block_sizes {kernel_block_sizes}"
6997:        )
6998:
6999:    def _allocate_kv_cache_tensors(
7000:        self, kv_cache_config: KVCacheConfig
7001:    ) -> dict[str, torch.Tensor]:
7002:        """
7003:        Initializes the KV cache buffer with the correct size. The buffer needs
7004:        to be reshaped to the desired shape before being used by the models.
7005:
7006:        Args:
7007:            kv_cache_config: The KV cache config
7008:        Returns:
7009:            dict[str, torch.Tensor]: A map between layer names to their
7010:            corresponding memory buffer for KV cache.
7011:        """
7012:        kv_cache_raw_tensors: dict[str, torch.Tensor] = {}
7013:        for kv_cache_tensor in kv_cache_config.kv_cache_tensors:
7014:            tensor = torch.zeros(
7015:                kv_cache_tensor.size, dtype=torch.int8, device=self.device
7016:            )
7017:            for layer_name in kv_cache_tensor.shared_by:
7018:                kv_cache_raw_tensors[layer_name] = tensor
7019:
7020:        layer_names = set()
7021:        for group in kv_cache_config.kv_cache_groups:
7022:            for layer_name in group.layer_names:
7023:                if layer_name in self.runner_only_attn_layers:
7024:                    continue
7025:                layer_names.add(layer_name)
7026:        assert layer_names == set(kv_cache_raw_tensors.keys()), (
7027:            "Some layers are not correctly initialized"
7028:        )
7029:        return kv_cache_raw_tensors
7030:
7031:    def _attn_group_iterator(self) -> Iterator[AttentionGroup]:
7032:        return itertools.chain.from_iterable(self.attn_groups)
7033:
7034:    def _kv_cache_spec_attn_group_iterator(self) -> Iterator[AttentionGroup]:
7035:        if not self.kv_cache_config.kv_cache_groups:
7036:            return
7037:        for attn_groups in self.attn_groups:
7038:            yield from attn_groups
7039:
7040:    def _reshape_kv_cache_tensors(
7041:        self,
7042:        kv_cache_raw_tensors: dict[str, torch.Tensor],
7043:        kernel_block_sizes: list[int],
7044:    ) -> dict[str, torch.Tensor]:
7045:        """
7046:        Reshape the KV cache tensors to the desired shape and dtype.
7047:
7048:        Args:
7049:            kv_cache_raw_tensors: The KV cache buffer of each layer, with
7050:                correct size but uninitialized shape.
7051:            kernel_block_sizes: The kernel block sizes for each KV cache group.
7052:        Returns:
7053:            Dict[str, torch.Tensor]: A map between layer names to their
7054:            corresponding memory buffer for KV cache.
7055:        """
7056:        kv_caches: dict[str, torch.Tensor] = {}
7057:        has_attn, has_mamba = False, False
7058:        for group in self._kv_cache_spec_attn_group_iterator():
7059:            kv_cache_spec = group.kv_cache_spec
7060:            attn_backend = group.backend
7061:            if group.kv_cache_group_id == len(kernel_block_sizes):
7062:                # There may be a last group for layers without kv cache.
7063:                continue
7064:            kernel_block_size = kernel_block_sizes[group.kv_cache_group_id]
7065:            for layer_name in group.layer_names:
7066:                if layer_name in self.runner_only_attn_layers:
7067:                    continue
7068:                raw_tensor = kv_cache_raw_tensors[layer_name]
7069:                assert raw_tensor.numel() % kv_cache_spec.page_size_bytes == 0
7070:                num_blocks = raw_tensor.numel() // kv_cache_spec.page_size_bytes
7071:                if isinstance(kv_cache_spec, AttentionSpec):
7072:                    has_attn = True
7073:                    num_blocks_per_kv_block = (
7074:                        kv_cache_spec.block_size // kernel_block_size
7075:                    )
7076:                    kernel_num_blocks = num_blocks * num_blocks_per_kv_block
7077:
7078:                    # For MLA with compression, storage_block_size != block_size
7079:                    if kv_cache_spec.storage_block_size != kv_cache_spec.block_size:
7080:                        shape_block_size = kv_cache_spec.storage_block_size
7081:                    else:
7082:                        shape_block_size = kernel_block_size
7083:
7084:                    kv_cache_shape = attn_backend.get_kv_cache_shape(
7085:                        kernel_num_blocks,
7086:                        shape_block_size,
7087:                        kv_cache_spec.num_kv_heads,
7088:                        kv_cache_spec.head_size,
7089:                        cache_dtype_str=self.cache_config.cache_dtype,
7090:                    )
7091:                    dtype = kv_cache_spec.dtype
7092:                    try:
7093:                        kv_cache_stride_order = attn_backend.get_kv_cache_stride_order()
7094:                        assert len(kv_cache_stride_order) == len(kv_cache_shape)
7095:                    except (AttributeError, NotImplementedError):
7096:                        kv_cache_stride_order = tuple(range(len(kv_cache_shape)))
7097:                    # The allocation respects the backend-defined stride order
7098:                    # to ensure the semantic remains consistent for each
7099:                    # backend. We first obtain the generic kv cache shape and
7100:                    # then permute it according to the stride order which could
7101:                    # result in a non-contiguous tensor.
7102:                    kv_cache_shape = tuple(
7103:                        kv_cache_shape[i] for i in kv_cache_stride_order
7104:                    )
7105:                    # Maintain original KV shape view.
7106:                    inv_order = [
7107:                        kv_cache_stride_order.index(i)
7108:                        for i in range(len(kv_cache_stride_order))
7109:                    ]
7110:
7111:                    raw_tensor = kv_cache_raw_tensors[layer_name].view(dtype)
7112:                    if kv_cache_spec.page_size_padded is not None:
7113:                        # Use strided view to handle page_size_bytes that
7114:                        # include padding. This follows
7115:                        # the same pattern as MambaSpec handling below.
7116:                        # NOTE: This assumes kv_cache_shape[0] == num_blocks
7117:                        # (i.e. the first physical dimension is the block
7118:                        # index), which holds for MLA backends but NOT for
7119:                        # standard attention backends whose shape starts with
7120:                        # a K/V dimension of size 2.
7121:                        dtype_size = get_dtype_size(dtype)
7122:                        page_stride = kv_cache_spec.page_size_bytes // dtype_size
7123:                        strides = list(torch.empty(kv_cache_shape).stride())
7124:                        strides[inv_order[0]] = page_stride
7125:                        kv_cache = torch.as_strided(
7126:                            raw_tensor,
7127:                            size=kv_cache_shape,
7128:                            stride=tuple(strides),
7129:                        )
7130:                    else:
7131:                        # No padding — safe to use a contiguous view.
7132:                        kv_cache = raw_tensor.view(kv_cache_shape)
7133:                    kv_caches[layer_name] = kv_cache.permute(*inv_order)
7134:
7135:                elif isinstance(kv_cache_spec, MambaSpec):
7136:                    has_mamba = True
7137:                    raw_tensor = kv_cache_raw_tensors[layer_name]
7138:                    state_tensors = []
7139:                    storage_offset_bytes = 0
7140:                    for shape, dtype in zip(kv_cache_spec.shapes, kv_cache_spec.dtypes):
7141:                        dtype_size = get_dtype_size(dtype)
7142:                        num_element_per_page = (
7143:                            kv_cache_spec.page_size_bytes // dtype_size
7144:                        )
7145:                        target_shape = (num_blocks, *shape)
7146:                        stride = torch.empty(target_shape).stride()
7147:                        target_stride = (num_element_per_page, *stride[1:])
7148:                        assert storage_offset_bytes % dtype_size == 0
7149:                        tensor = torch.as_strided(
7150:                            raw_tensor.view(dtype),
7151:                            size=target_shape,
7152:                            stride=target_stride,
7153:                            storage_offset=storage_offset_bytes // dtype_size,
7154:                        )
7155:                        state_tensors.append(tensor)
7156:                        storage_offset_bytes += stride[0] * dtype_size
7157:
7158:                    kv_caches[layer_name] = state_tensors
7159:                else:
7160:                    raise NotImplementedError
7161:
7162:        if has_attn and has_mamba:
7163:            self._update_hybrid_attention_mamba_layout(kv_caches, kernel_block_sizes)
7164:
7165:        return kv_caches
7166:
7167:    def _update_hybrid_attention_mamba_layout(
7168:        self, kv_caches: dict[str, torch.Tensor], kernel_block_sizes: list[int]
7169:    ) -> None:
7170:        """
7171:        Update the layout of attention layers from (2, num_blocks, ...) to
7172:        (num_blocks, 2, ...).
7173:
7174:        Args:
7175:            kv_caches: The KV cache buffer of each layer.
7176:            kernel_block_sizes: The kernel block sizes for each KV cache group.
7177:        """
7178:
7179:        for group in self._kv_cache_spec_attn_group_iterator():
7180:            kv_cache_spec = group.kv_cache_spec
7181:            if not isinstance(kv_cache_spec, AttentionSpec):
7182:                continue
7183:            block_dim = group.backend.get_kv_cache_block_dim(
7184:                kernel_block_sizes[group.kv_cache_group_id],
7185:                kv_cache_spec.num_kv_heads,
7186:                kv_cache_spec.head_size,
7187:                cache_dtype_str=self.cache_config.cache_dtype,
7188:            )
7189:            # block_dim: 0 means (num_blocks, 2, ...); 1 means (2, num_blocks, ...).
7190:            if block_dim == 0:
7191:                continue
7192:            assert block_dim == 1
7193:            for layer_name in group.layer_names:
7194:                kv_cache = kv_caches[layer_name]
7195:                hidden_size = kv_cache.shape[2:].numel()
7196:                kv_cache.as_strided_(
7197:                    size=kv_cache.shape,
7198:                    stride=(hidden_size, 2 * hidden_size, *kv_cache.stride()[2:]),
7199:                )
7200:
7201:    def initialize_kv_cache_tensors(
7202:        self, kv_cache_config: KVCacheConfig, kernel_block_sizes: list[int]
7203:    ) -> dict[str, torch.Tensor]:
7204:        """
7205:        Initialize the memory buffer for KV cache.
7206:
7207:        Args:
7208:            kv_cache_config: The KV cache config
7209:            kernel_block_sizes: The kernel block sizes for each KV cache group.
7210:
7211:        Returns:
7212:            Dict[str, torch.Tensor]: A map between layer names to their
7213:            corresponding memory buffer for KV cache.
7214:        """
7215:
7216:        # Try creating KV caches optimized for kv-connector transfers
7217:        cache_dtype = self.cache_config.cache_dtype
7218:        if self.use_uniform_kv_cache(self.attn_groups, cache_dtype):
7219:            kv_caches, cross_layers_kv_cache, attn_backend = (
7220:                self.allocate_uniform_kv_caches(
7221:                    kv_cache_config,
7222:                    self.attn_groups,
7223:                    cache_dtype,
7224:                    self.device,
7225:                    kernel_block_sizes,
7226:                )
7227:            )
7228:            self.cross_layers_kv_cache = cross_layers_kv_cache
7229:            self.cross_layers_attn_backend = attn_backend
7230:        else:
7231:            # Fallback to the general case
7232:            # Initialize the memory buffer for KV cache
7233:            kv_cache_raw_tensors = self._allocate_kv_cache_tensors(kv_cache_config)
7234:
7235:            # Change the memory buffer to the desired shape
7236:            kv_caches = self._reshape_kv_cache_tensors(
7237:                kv_cache_raw_tensors, kernel_block_sizes
7238:            )
7239:
7240:        # Set up cross-layer KV cache sharing
7241:        for layer_name, target_layer_name in self.shared_kv_cache_layers.items():
7242:            logger.debug("%s reuses KV cache of %s", layer_name, target_layer_name)
7243:            kv_caches[layer_name] = kv_caches[target_layer_name]
7244:
7245:        num_attn_module = (
7246:            2 if self.model_config.hf_config.model_type == "longcat_flash" else 1
7247:        )
7248:        bind_kv_cache(
7249:            kv_caches,
7250:            self.compilation_config.static_forward_context,
7251:            self.kv_caches,
7252:            num_attn_module,
7253:        )
7254:        return kv_caches
7255:
7256:    def maybe_add_kv_sharing_layers_to_kv_cache_groups(
7257:        self, kv_cache_config: KVCacheConfig
7258:    ) -> None:
7259:        """
7260:        Add layers that re-use KV cache to KV cache group of its target layer.
7261:        Mapping of KV cache tensors happens in `initialize_kv_cache_tensors()`
7262:        """
7263:        if not self.shared_kv_cache_layers:
7264:            # No cross-layer KV sharing, return
7265:            return
7266:
7267:        add_kv_sharing_layers_to_kv_cache_groups(
7268:            self.shared_kv_cache_layers,
7269:            kv_cache_config.kv_cache_groups,
7270:            self.runner_only_attn_layers,
7271:        )
7272:
7273:        if self.cache_config.kv_sharing_fast_prefill:
7274:            # In You Only Cache Once (https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.05254) or other
7275:            # similar KV sharing setups, only the layers that generate KV caches
7276:            # are involved in the prefill phase, enabling prefill to early exit.
7277:            attn_layers = get_layers_from_vllm_config(self.vllm_config, Attention)
7278:            for layer_name in reversed(attn_layers):
7279:                if layer_name in self.shared_kv_cache_layers:
7280:                    self.kv_sharing_fast_prefill_eligible_layers.add(layer_name)
7281:                else:
7282:                    break
7283:
7284:    def initialize_kv_cache(
7285:        self,
7286:        kv_cache_config: KVCacheConfig,
7287:        is_profiling: bool = False,
7288:    ) -> None:
7289:        """
7290:        Initialize KV cache based on `kv_cache_config`.
7291:        Args:
7292:            kv_cache_config: Configuration for the KV cache, including the KV
7293:            cache size of each layer
7294:        """
7295:        kv_cache_config = deepcopy(kv_cache_config)
7296:        self.kv_cache_config = kv_cache_config
7297:        self._mamba_bufs = None
7298:        self.may_add_encoder_only_layers_to_kv_cache_config()
7299:        self.maybe_add_kv_sharing_layers_to_kv_cache_groups(kv_cache_config)
7300:        self.initialize_attn_backend(kv_cache_config, is_profiling=is_profiling)
7301:        initialize_mamba_ssu_backend(
7302:            self.vllm_config.mamba_config, self.kv_cache_config
7303:        )
7304:        # The kernel block size for all KV cache groups. For example, if
7305:        # kv_cache_manager uses block_size 256 for a given group, but the attention
7306:        # backends for that group only supports block_size 64, we will return
7307:        # kernel_block_size 64 and split the 256-token-block to 4 blocks with 64
7308:        # tokens each.
7309:        kernel_block_sizes = prepare_kernel_block_sizes(
7310:            kv_cache_config, self.attn_groups
7311:        )
7312:        self._kernel_block_sizes = kernel_block_sizes
7313:
7314:        # create metadata builders
7315:        self.initialize_metadata_builders(kv_cache_config, kernel_block_sizes)
7316:
7317:        # Reinitialize need to after initialize_attn_backend
7318:        self.may_reinitialize_input_batch(kv_cache_config, kernel_block_sizes)
7319:        kv_caches = self.initialize_kv_cache_tensors(
7320:            kv_cache_config, kernel_block_sizes
7321:        )
7322:
7323:        if (
7324:            self.speculative_config
7325:            and self.speculative_config.uses_extract_hidden_states()
7326:        ):
7327:            assert isinstance(self.drafter, ExtractHiddenStatesProposer)
7328:            # validate all draft model layers belong to the same kv cache
7329:            # group
7330:            self.drafter.validate_same_kv_cache_group(kv_cache_config)
7331:
7332:        if has_kv_transfer_group() and not is_profiling:
7333:            kv_transfer_group = get_kv_transfer_group()
7334:            if self.cross_layers_kv_cache is not None:
7335:                assert self.cross_layers_attn_backend is not None
7336:                kv_transfer_group.register_cross_layers_kv_cache(
7337:                    self.cross_layers_kv_cache, self.cross_layers_attn_backend
7338:                )
7339:            else:
7340:                kv_transfer_group.register_kv_caches(kv_caches)
7341:            kv_transfer_group.set_host_xfer_buffer_ops(copy_kv_blocks)
7342:
7343:    def _get_attention_kv_cache_gid(self) -> int:
7344:        """Find the KV cache group index for attention layers.
7345:
7346:        Must match :attr:`RoutedExpertsManager.attn_gid` in the scheduler:
7347:        both pick the first ``FullAttentionSpec`` group so hybrid models
7348:        (Mamba / linear-attention layers that use other AttentionSpec
7349:        subclasses) end up indexing the same slot layout on both sides.
7350:        Falls back to 0 only for legacy single-group configs.
7351:        """
7352:        for gid, group in enumerate(self.kv_cache_config.kv_cache_groups):
7353:            if isinstance(group.kv_cache_spec, FullAttentionSpec):
7354:                return gid
7355:        return 0
7356:
7357:    def init_routed_experts_capturer(self):
7358:        logger.info(
7359:            "Initializing routed experts capturer, enable_return_routed_experts: %s",
7360:            self.model_config.enable_return_routed_experts,
7361:        )
7362:        self.routed_experts_capturer = RoutedExpertsCapturer(
7363:            max_num_batched_tokens=self.scheduler_config.max_num_batched_tokens,
7364:            vllm_config=self.vllm_config,
7365:        )
7366:        self.routed_experts_attn_gid = self._get_attention_kv_cache_gid()
7367:        self._bind_routed_experts_capturer(self.routed_experts_capturer)
7368:
7369:        # Pinned CPU buffer for non-blocking D2H of ``routing_data`` on
7370:        # the sync scheduling path. Shape / dtype mirror the device
7371:        # capturer exactly so ``copy_`` is a straight memcpy.
7372:        self.routed_experts_cpu = torch.empty(
7373:            self.routed_experts_capturer.device_buffer.shape,
7374:            dtype=self.routed_experts_capturer.device_buffer.dtype,
7375:            device="cpu",
7376:            pin_memory=self.pin_memory,
7377:        )
7378:        # ``slot_mapping`` dtype is fixed to int64 by
7379:        # ``block_table.slot_mapping``; we mirror that here.
7380:        max_tokens = self.scheduler_config.max_num_batched_tokens
7381:        self.routed_experts_slot_mapping_cpu = torch.empty(
7382:            (max_tokens,),
7383:            dtype=torch.int64,
7384:            device="cpu",
7385:            pin_memory=self.pin_memory,
7386:        )
7387:        # Private device buffer so the shared ``block_table.slot_mapping``
7388:        # can be overwritten by the next ``_prepare_inputs`` while the
7389:        # D2H is still pending on the copy stream. Written in
7390:        # ``_prepare_inputs``, read in ``_bookkeeping_sync`` (sync path)
7391:        # or cloned into a snapshot (async path).
7392:        self.routed_experts_slot_mapping_device = torch.empty(
7393:            (max_tokens,),
7394:            dtype=torch.int64,
7395:            device=self.device,
7396:        )
7397:        self.routed_experts_initialized = True
7398:
7399:    def _bind_routed_experts_capturer(self, capturer: RoutedExpertsCapturer) -> None:
7400:        from vllm.model_executor.layers.fused_moe.layer import FusedMoE
7401:        from vllm.model_executor.layers.fused_moe.router.base_router import (
7402:            BaseRouter,
7403:        )
7404:
7405:        for module in self.compilation_config.static_forward_context.values():
7406:            if isinstance(module, FusedMoE) and isinstance(module.router, BaseRouter):
7407:                layer_id = module.layer_id
7408:
7409:                def _capture_fn(topk_ids, _layer_id=layer_id, _capturer=capturer):
7410:                    _capturer.capture(_layer_id, topk_ids)
7411:
7412:                module.router.set_capture_fn(_capture_fn)
7413:
7414:    def may_add_encoder_only_layers_to_kv_cache_config(self) -> None:
7415:        """
7416:        Add encoder-only layers to the KV cache config.
7417:        """
7418:        block_size = self.vllm_config.cache_config.block_size
7419:        encoder_only_attn_specs: dict[AttentionSpec, list[str]] = defaultdict(list)
7420:        attn_layers = get_layers_from_vllm_config(self.vllm_config, Attention)
7421:        for layer_name, attn_module in attn_layers.items():
7422:            if attn_module.attn_type == AttentionType.ENCODER_ONLY:
7423:                attn_spec: AttentionSpec = EncoderOnlyAttentionSpec(
7424:                    block_size=block_size,
7425:                    num_kv_heads=attn_module.num_kv_heads,
7426:                    head_size=attn_module.head_size,
7427:                    dtype=self.kv_cache_dtype,
7428:                )
7429:                encoder_only_attn_specs[attn_spec].append(layer_name)
7430:                self.runner_only_attn_layers.add(layer_name)
7431:        if len(encoder_only_attn_specs) > 0:
7432:            assert len(encoder_only_attn_specs) == 1, (
7433:                "Only support one encoder-only attention spec now"
7434:            )
7435:            spec, layer_names = encoder_only_attn_specs.popitem()
7436:            self.kv_cache_config.kv_cache_groups.append(
7437:                KVCacheGroupSpec(layer_names=layer_names, kv_cache_spec=spec)
7438:            )
7439:
7440:    def get_kv_cache_spec(self) -> dict[str, KVCacheSpec]:
7441:        """
7442:        Generates the KVCacheSpec by parsing the kv cache format from each
7443:        Attention module in the static forward context.
7444:        Returns:
7445:            KVCacheSpec: A dictionary mapping layer names to their KV cache
7446:            format. Layers that do not need KV cache are not included.
7447:        """
7448:        if has_ec_transfer() and not get_ec_transfer().is_consumer:
7449:            return {}
7450:        kv_cache_spec: dict[str, KVCacheSpec] = {}
7451:        layer_type = cast(type[Any], AttentionLayerBase)
7452:        attn_layers = get_layers_from_vllm_config(self.vllm_config, layer_type)
7453:        for layer_name, attn_module in attn_layers.items():
7454:            if isinstance(attn_module, Attention) and (
7455:                kv_tgt_layer := attn_module.kv_sharing_target_layer_name
7456:            ):
7457:                # The layer doesn't need its own KV cache and will use that of
7458:                # the target layer. We skip creating a KVCacheSpec for it, so
7459:                # that KV cache management logic will act as this layer does
7460:                # not exist, and doesn't allocate KV cache for the layer. This
7461:                # enables the memory saving of cross-layer kv sharing, allowing
7462:                # a given amount of memory to accommodate longer context lengths
7463:                # or enable more requests to be processed simultaneously.
7464:                self.shared_kv_cache_layers[layer_name] = kv_tgt_layer
7465:                continue
7466:            # Skip modules that don't need KV cache (eg encoder-only attention)
7467:            if spec := attn_module.get_kv_cache_spec(self.vllm_config):
7468:                kv_cache_spec[layer_name] = spec
7469:
7470:        return kv_cache_spec
7471:
7472:    def _to_list(self, sampled_token_ids: torch.Tensor) -> list[list[int]]:
7473:        # This is a short term mitigation for issue mentioned in
7474:        # https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/22754.
7475:        # `tolist` would trigger a cuda wise stream sync, which
7476:        # would block other copy ops from other cuda streams.
7477:        # A cuda event sync would avoid such a situation. Since
7478:        # this is in the critical path of every single model
7479:        # forward loop, this has caused perf issue for a disagg
7480:        # setup.
7481:        pinned = self.sampled_token_ids_pinned_cpu[: sampled_token_ids.shape[0]]
7482:        pinned.copy_(sampled_token_ids, non_blocking=True)
7483:        self.transfer_event.record()
7484:        self.transfer_event.synchronize()
7485:        return pinned.tolist()
7486:
7487:    def get_encoder_timing_stats(self) -> dict[str, dict[str, float | int]]:
7488:        """
7489:        Get encoder timing stats for all requests and clear the registry.
7490:
7491:        Returns:
7492:            Dictionary mapping request_id to stats dict.
7493:        """
7494:        with self._encoder_timing_lock:
7495:            stats = {
7496:                req_id: stats_obj.to_dict()
7497:                for req_id, stats_obj in self.encoder_timing_registry.items()
7498:            }
7499:            self.encoder_timing_registry.clear()
7500:            return stats
7501:
7502:    @contextmanager
7503:    def timed_encoder_operation(
7504:        self,
7505:        should_time: bool,
7506:        group_lora_refs: list[tuple[str, Any]],
7507:        current_item_idx: int,
7508:        num_items: int,
7509:    ):
7510:        """
7511:        Context manager to time encoder forward operations.
7512:
7513:        Args:
7514:            should_time: Whether timing is enabled
7515:            group_lora_refs: Full list of (request_id, pos_info) tuples
7516:            current_item_idx: Starting index for this group
7517:            num_items: Number of items in this group
7518:        """
7519:        if not should_time:
7520:            yield
7521:            return
7522:
7523:        group_refs = group_lora_refs[current_item_idx : current_item_idx + num_items]
7524:        group_request_ids = {req_id for req_id, _ in group_refs}
7525:
7526:        torch.accelerator.synchronize()
7527:        start_time = time.perf_counter()
7528:
7529:        try:
7530:            yield
7531:        finally:
7532:            torch.accelerator.synchronize()
7533:            elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time
7534:
7535:            per_request_time = elapsed / max(len(group_request_ids), 1)
7536:
7537:            with self._encoder_timing_lock:
7538:                for req_id in group_request_ids:
7539:                    if req_id not in self.encoder_timing_registry:
7540:                        self.encoder_timing_registry[req_id] = EncoderTimingStats()
7541:
7542:                    stats = self.encoder_timing_registry[req_id]
7543:                    stats.encoder_forward_secs += per_request_time
7544:                    stats.num_encoder_calls += 1
7545:
7546:
7547:@dataclass
7548:class EncoderTimingStats:
7549:    """Per-request timing statistics for encoder forward pass."""
7550:
7551:    encoder_forward_secs: float = 0.0
7552:    """Time spent in vision encoder forward pass (seconds)."""
7553:
7554:    num_encoder_calls: int = 0
7555:    """Number of times encoder was called for this request."""
7556:
7557:    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, float | int]:
7558:        return {
7559:            "encoder_forward_secs": self.encoder_forward_secs,
7560:            "num_encoder_calls": self.num_encoder_calls,
7561:        }

59:@contextmanager
60:def patch_tensor_parallel_group(tp_group):
61:    """Temporarily swap the global TP group for draft-model spec decode.
62:
63:    vllm-ascend local implementation for swapping the global TP group so the
64:    draft model can run with a TP degree that differs from the target model.
65:    """
66:    old_tp_group = _ps.get_tp_group()
67:    _ps._TP_STATE_PATCHED = True
68:    _ps._TP = tp_group
69:    try:
70:        yield
71:    finally:
72:        _ps._TP_STATE_PATCHED = False
73:        _ps._TP = old_tp_group

83:def _maybe_eager_context(vllm_config):
84:    target_compilation_config = vllm_config.compilation_config
85:    draft_compilation_config = replace(
86:        target_compilation_config,
87:        mode=CompilationMode.NONE,
88:    )
89:    # Model layers use these registries even when compilation is disabled.
90:    draft_compilation_config.static_forward_context = target_compilation_config.static_forward_context
91:    draft_compilation_config.static_all_moe_layers = target_compilation_config.static_all_moe_layers
92:    vllm_config.compilation_config = draft_compilation_config
93:    try:
94:        yield
95:    finally:
96:        vllm_config.compilation_config = target_compilation_config

99:# split hidden states along dimension of sequence
100:def split_inputs_tp_to_sp(hidden_states, out):
101:    # tp and sp share the same group
102:    group = get_tp_group()
103:
104:    world_size = group.world_size
105:    rank = group.rank
106:
107:    num_tokens = hidden_states.shape[0]
108:    # the size per rank after padded
109:    padded_num_tokens_per_rank = (num_tokens + world_size - 1) // world_size
110:    # compute the start and end of slice
111:    start = padded_num_tokens_per_rank * rank
112:    end = padded_num_tokens_per_rank * (rank + 1)
113:
114:    # copy only hidden_states in current rank
115:    hidden_states_curr_rank = hidden_states[start:end]
116:    out[: hidden_states_curr_rank.shape[0]] = hidden_states_curr_rank
117:    return out[:padded_num_tokens_per_rank]

119:
120:def greedy_sample(logits: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
121:    tp_group = get_tp_group()
122:    B, V_local = logits.shape
123:    rank = tp_group.rank_in_group
124:
125:    local_max_logits, local_max_indices = logits.max(dim=-1)
126:
127:    local_global_idx = local_max_indices + rank * V_local  # [B]
128:
129:    # [B, world_size]
130:    gathered_logits = tp_group.all_gather(local_max_logits.unsqueeze(-1), dim=-1)
131:    gathered_global_idx = tp_group.all_gather(local_global_idx.unsqueeze(-1), dim=-1)  # [B, world_size]
132:    global_max_rank = gathered_logits.argmax(dim=-1)  # [B]
133:    target_argmax = gathered_global_idx.gather(dim=-1, index=global_max_rank.unsqueeze(-1)).squeeze(-1)  # [B]
134:    return target_argmax

139:def _is_glm_model(model_config) -> bool:
140:    """Return True if the target model belongs to the GLM series.
141:
142:    Detection is based on the model_type string (covers glm, chatglm, glm4,
143:    glm4_moe, glm4_moe_lite, glm4_1v, glm_ocr, glm_moe_dsa, etc).
144:    """
145:    hf_text_config = getattr(model_config, "hf_text_config", None)
146:    model_type = getattr(hf_text_config, "model_type", "") or ""
147:    return "glm" in str(model_type).lower()

191:        draft_hf_config = draft_model_config.hf_config if draft_model_config is not None else None
192:        self._share_mtp_indices = getattr(draft_hf_config, "index_share_for_mtp_iteration", False)
193:
194:        # NOTE:
195:        # `draft_tensor_parallel_size` does not take effect for Eagle:
196:        # the draft model uses the same TP size as the target model in practice.
197:        # so we applied this patch to set tp=1 of draft model separately.
198:        # Due to verification of `_verify_and_get_draft_tp` in vllm,
199:        # the value of `draft_tensor_parallel_size` here will either be 1 separately
200:        # or the same as target model.

202:        # is not 1 and differs from target model, this part should be rewritten.
203:        if vllm_config.parallel_config.tensor_parallel_size != self.speculative_config.draft_tensor_parallel_size:
204:            tp_group = init_model_parallel_group(
205:                [[get_world_group().rank]],
206:                get_world_group().rank,
207:                torch.distributed.get_backend(get_world_group().device_group),
208:                use_message_queue_broadcaster=True,
209:                group_name="tp",
210:            )

212:        else:
213:            self.tp_group_context = nullcontext()
214:

216:        self._raise_if_padded_drafter_batch_disabled_and_full_graph_enabled()
217:
218:        # GLM series models: speculative decoding does not yet support running
219:        # the draft model in graph mode. Force the draft model to always use
220:        # eager mode. This is equivalent to the user adding
221:        # `"enforce_eager": true` to the `--speculative-config`, and keeps
222:        # the target model's graph-mode setting untouched.
223:        # TODO(lilinsiman): Remove this code segment after future versions of the GLM
224:        # series models support graph input for speculative inference.
225:        if _is_glm_model(self.vllm_config.model_config):
226:            if self.use_cuda_graph:
227:                logger.warning(

229:                    "not support graph mode. The draft model has been "
230:                    "automatically switched to eager mode "
231:                    "(enforce_eager=true). Graph mode support for GLM "
232:                    "speculative decoding will be added in a future release. "
233:                )

238:        if not self.use_cuda_graph and enable_sp(vllm_config):
239:            self.maybe_eager_context = _maybe_eager_context(vllm_config)
240:
241:        self.token_indices_to_sample = torch.zeros(
242:            self.vllm_config.scheduler_config.max_num_batched_tokens, dtype=torch.int32, device=device
243:        )
244:        metadata_lens = self.runner.max_num_tokens + 2 * self.pcp_size * self.runner.max_num_reqs
245:        num_mtp_draft_slots = max(self.num_speculative_tokens - 1, 0) * self.runner.max_num_reqs
246:        slot_mapping_lens = metadata_lens + num_mtp_draft_slots
247:        self.slot_mapping_group = [
248:            torch.zeros(slot_mapping_lens, dtype=torch.int32, device=device, pin_memory=self.runner.pin_memory)
249:            for _ in range(self.num_speculative_tokens)
250:        ]

253:        self.seq_lens_group = [

255:            for _ in range(self.num_speculative_tokens)
256:        ]
257:        self.query_start_loc_group = [
258:            torch.zeros(metadata_lens, dtype=torch.int32, device=device, pin_memory=self.runner.pin_memory)
259:            for _ in range(self.num_speculative_tokens)
260:        ]

263:        # since final block table tensor is not ready in __init__, it is delayed until dummy_run
264:        self.block_table_tensor_clone: torch.Tensor | None = None

267:        self.is_multimodal_model = self.vllm_config.model_config.is_multimodal_model
268:        if self.uses_mrope:
269:            self.mrope_positions = torch.zeros((3, self.max_num_tokens + 1), dtype=torch.int32, device=device)
270:        elif self.uses_xdrope_dim > 0 and self.draft_uses_xdrope_dim > 0:
271:            self.xdrope_positions = torch.zeros(
272:                (self.uses_xdrope_dim, self.max_num_tokens + 1),
273:                dtype=torch.int32,

275:            )

278:            self.positions = torch.zeros(self.max_num_tokens, dtype=torch.int32, device=device)
279:

284:    def _raise_if_padded_drafter_batch_disabled_and_full_graph_enabled(self):
285:        if (
286:            self.speculative_config.disable_padded_drafter_batch
287:            and self.use_cuda_graph
288:            and self.compilation_config.cudagraph_mode.has_full_cudagraphs()
289:        ):
290:            raise NotImplementedError(
291:                "Speculative Decoding with cudagraph mode containing full cudagraphs only "
292:                "supports padded drafter batch. Please unset "
293:                "disable_padded_drafter_batch in the speculative_config."
294:            )

296:    def _get_model(self) -> nn.Module:
297:        """
298:        Default method to call get_model(). Can be overridden by subclasses which
299:        need to customize model loading.
300:        """
301:        from vllm.compilation.backends import set_model_tag
302:
303:        draft_vllm_config = self._create_draft_vllm_config()
304:        draft_load_config = self.speculative_config.draft_load_config
305:        logger.info(
306:            "[spec_decode/base] Loading draft model: method=%s, load_format=%s, model=%s",
307:            self.method,
308:            getattr(draft_load_config, "load_format", None),
309:            getattr(self.speculative_config.draft_model_config, "model", None),
310:        )
311:        with set_model_tag("eagle_head"):
312:            model = get_model(
313:                vllm_config=draft_vllm_config,
314:                model_config=self.speculative_config.draft_model_config,
315:                load_config=self.speculative_config.draft_load_config,
316:            )
317:        return model
318:
319:    def load_model(self, model: nn.Module) -> None:
320:        assert get_pp_group().is_last_rank, f"{self.method} drafter must be loaded on the last pipeline stage."
321:
322:        target_attn_layer_names = set(get_layers_from_vllm_config(self.vllm_config, AttentionLayerBase).keys())
323:
324:        with self.maybe_eager_context:
325:            self.model = self._get_model()
326:
327:        # Find draft layers (attention layers added by draft model)
328:        all_attn_layers = get_layers_from_vllm_config(
329:            self.vllm_config,
330:            AttentionLayerBase,  # type: ignore[type-abstract]
331:        )
332:        all_indexer_layer_names = set(get_layers_from_vllm_config(self.vllm_config, DeepseekV32IndexerCache).keys())
333:        # Filter to only layers that have KV cache specs.
334:        self._draft_attn_layer_names = {
335:            name
336:            for name in (set(all_attn_layers.keys()) - target_attn_layer_names)
337:            if all_attn_layers[name].get_kv_cache_spec(self.vllm_config) is not None
338:        } - all_indexer_layer_names
339:
340:        self.attn_layer_names = list(sorted(self._draft_attn_layer_names))
341:        draft_attn_layers_dict = get_layers_from_vllm_config(self.vllm_config, AttentionLayerBase)
342:        # initialized for mamba models
343:        self.kernel_block_size = (
344:            draft_attn_layers_dict[self.attn_layer_names[0]].get_attn_backend().get_supported_kernel_block_sizes()[0]
345:        )
346:
347:        self.piece_all_attn_layer_name = []
348:        for _ in range(self.num_speculative_tokens):
349:            self.piece_all_attn_layer_name.append([name for name in self.attn_layer_names])
350:
351:        if supports_multimodal(model):
352:            # handle multimodality
353:            if self.get_model_name(model) in [
354:                "Qwen2_5_VLForConditionalGeneration",
355:                "Qwen3VLForConditionalGeneration",
356:                "Qwen3VLMoeForConditionalGeneration",
357:                "Qwen3_5ForConditionalGeneration",
358:                "Qwen3_5MoeForConditionalGeneration",
359:                "Step3p7ForConditionalGeneration",
360:            ]:
361:                self.model.config.image_token_index = model.config.image_token_id
362:            elif self.get_model_name(model) == "PixtralForConditionalGeneration":
363:                self.model.config.image_token_index = model.config.vision_config.image_token_id
364:            elif self.get_model_name(model) == "KimiK25ForConditionalGeneration":
365:                self.model.config.image_token_index = model.config.media_placeholder_token_id
366:            else:
367:                self.model.config.image_token_index = model.config.image_token_index
368:            target_language_model = model.get_language_model()
369:        else:
370:            target_language_model = model
371:
372:        # share embed_tokens with the target model if needed
373:        self._maybe_share_embeddings(target_language_model)
374:        self._maybe_share_topk_indices(target_language_model)
375:        self._maybe_share_lm_head(model)
376:
377:        if (
378:            self.parallel_drafting
379:            and self.pass_hidden_states_to_model
380:            and self.parallel_drafting_hidden_state_tensor is not None
381:        ):
382:            self.parallel_drafting_hidden_state_tensor.copy_(
383:                self.model.combine_hidden_states(self.model.mask_hidden.view(3 * self.hidden_size))
384:                if self.eagle3_use_aux_hidden_state
385:                else self.model.mask_hidden.view(self.hidden_size)
386:            )
387:
388:    def _maybe_share_embeddings(self, target_language_model: nn.Module) -> None:
389:        """
390:        Some draft models may not have their own embedding layers, and some may
391:        have a duplicate copy of the target model's embedding layers. In these cases,
392:        we share the target model's embedding layers with the draft model to save
393:        memory.
394:        """
395:        if get_pp_group().world_size == 1:
396:            if hasattr(target_language_model.model, "embed_tokens"):
397:                target_embed_tokens = target_language_model.model.embed_tokens
398:            elif hasattr(target_language_model.model, "embedding"):
399:                target_embed_tokens = target_language_model.model.embedding
400:            else:
401:                raise AttributeError("Target model does not have 'embed_tokens' or 'embedding' attribute")
402:            # If pp>1, the weights of mtp and the main model's embedding are not on the same device.
403:            # check if mtp model use main model's embedding and LMhead
404:            share_embeddings = False
405:            if hasattr(self.model, "has_own_embed_tokens"):
406:                # EAGLE model
407:                if not self.model.has_own_embed_tokens:
408:                    share_embeddings = True
409:                    logger.info(
410:                        "[spec_decode/base] Detected EAGLE model without its own"
411:                        " embed_tokens in the checkpoint. Sharing target model"
412:                        " embedding weights with the draft model."
413:                    )
414:                elif (
415:                    isinstance(target_embed_tokens.weight, torch.Tensor)
416:                    and isinstance(self.model.model.embed_tokens.weight, torch.Tensor)
417:                    # TODO: Offload to CPU for comparison to avoid extra NPU memory
418:                    # usage in CI testing environments with limited NPU memory
419:                    and torch.equal(
420:                        target_embed_tokens.weight.cpu(),
421:                        self.model.model.embed_tokens.weight.cpu(),
422:                    )
423:                ):
424:                    share_embeddings = True
425:                    logger.info(
426:                        "[spec_decode/base] Detected EAGLE model with embed_tokens"
427:                        " identical to the target model. Sharing target model embedding"
428:                        " weights with the draft model."
429:                    )
430:                else:
431:                    logger.info(
432:                        "[spec_decode/base] Detected EAGLE model with distinct"
433:                        " embed_tokens weights. Keeping separate embedding weights"
434:                        " from the target model."
435:                    )
436:            else:
437:                # MTP model
438:                share_embeddings = not self.use_compress
439:                if share_embeddings:
440:                    logger.info(
441:                        "[spec_decode/base] Detected MTP model. Sharing target model"
442:                        " embedding weights with the draft model."
443:                    )
444:
445:            if share_embeddings:
446:                if hasattr(self.model.model, "embed_tokens"):
447:                    del self.model.model.embed_tokens
448:                self.model.model.embed_tokens = target_embed_tokens
449:        else:
450:            logger.info(
451:                "[spec_decode/base] PP>1: draft model loaded its own vocab embedding"
452:                " weights instead of sharing them with the target model."
453:            )
454:
455:    # share lm_head with the target model if needed
456:    def _maybe_share_lm_head(self, model: nn.Module) -> None:
457:        # some model definition do not define lm_head explicitly
458:        # and reuse embed_tokens for lm_head, e.g., CohereForCausalLM
459:        if self.method in ("eagle", "dflash"):
460:            # For DFlash drafters trained with a reduced draft vocabulary, the
461:            # draft model ships its own lm_head of shape [draft_vocab_size,
462:            # hidden] whose rows map to a trained subset of the target vocab via
463:            # the draft_id_to_target_id (d2t) buffer. Overwriting it with the
464:            # target lm_head ([target_vocab_size, hidden]) makes the draft emit
465:            # logits over the wrong vocabulary, so the verifier rejects almost
466:            # every speculative token. Keep the draft's own lm_head in that case.
467:            draft_has_own_lm_head = (
468:                self.method == "dflash" and getattr(self.model, "draft_id_to_target_id", None) is not None
469:            )
470:            if draft_has_own_lm_head:
471:                logger.info(
472:                    "[spec_decode/base] DFlash draft uses d2t vocab remapping;"
473:                    " keeping the draft's own lm_head instead of sharing the target"
474:                    " lm_head."
475:                )
476:            else:
477:                logger.info("[spec_decode/base] Loading EAGLE/DFLASH LM head weights from the target model.")
478:                if hasattr(model, "lm_head"):
479:                    self.model.lm_head = model.lm_head
480:                elif hasattr(model, "get_language_model") and hasattr(model.get_language_model(), "lm_head"):
481:                    self.model.lm_head = model.get_language_model().lm_head
482:                else:
483:                    logger.warning(
484:                        "[spec_decode/base] Target model has no accessible lm_head"
485:                        " for sharing. Draft model will use its own lm_head."
486:                        " This may cause incorrect logits if the draft lm_head"
487:                        " is not trained."
488:                    )
489:
490:        if self.method == "mtp" and self.vllm_config.model_config.is_deepseek_mla:
491:            for _, layer_module in self.model.model.layers.items():
492:                if torch.equal(layer_module.shared_head.head.weight, model.lm_head.weight):
493:                    layer_module.shared_head.head = model.lm_head
494:
495:        if self.vllm_config.compilation_config.cudagraph_mode.has_full_cudagraphs() and self.use_cuda_graph:
496:            logger.info(
497:                "[spec_decode/base] Wrapping draft model with ACLGraphWrapper:"
498:                " runtime_mode=FULL, use_eagle=%s, enable_enpu=%s",
499:                self.use_eagle,
500:                self.enable_enpu,
501:            )
502:            self.update_stream = torch.npu.Stream()
503:            self._runnable = ACLGraphWrapper(
504:                self._run_merged_draft,
505:                self.vllm_config,
506:                runtime_mode=CUDAGraphMode.FULL,
507:                use_eagle=self.use_eagle,
508:                enable_enpu=self.enable_enpu,
509:            )
510:
511:    def _maybe_share_topk_indices(self, target_language_model: nn.Module) -> None:
512:        if hasattr(target_language_model.model, "topk_indices_buffer"):
513:            if hasattr(self.model.model, "topk_indices_buffer"):
514:                del self.model.model.topk_indices_buffer
515:            self.model.model.topk_indices_buffer = target_language_model.model.topk_indices_buffer
516:            logger.info(
517:                "[spec_decode/base] Detected MTP model with topk_indices_buffer."
518:                " Sharing target model topk_indices_buffer with the draft model."
519:            )
520:            target_buffer = target_language_model.model.topk_indices_buffer
521:            draft_model = getattr(self.model, "model", None)
522:            if target_buffer is not None and draft_model is not None:
523:                for _, module in draft_model.named_modules():
524:                    if hasattr(module, "topk_indices_buffer"):
525:                        module.topk_indices_buffer = target_buffer
526:
527:    def get_model(self) -> nn.Module:
528:        # get raw model out of the aclgraph wrapper.
529:        if isinstance(self.model, ACLGraphWrapper):
530:            return self.model.unwrap()
531:        return self.model
532:
533:    def shallow_copy_metadata(self, attn_metadata):
534:        # Currently, new objects will be assigned to the lists in attn_metadata
535:        # when update. So we can use the shallow copy.
536:        return copy.copy(attn_metadata)
537:
538:    def _freeze_draft_index_attn_metadata(self, attn_metadata):
539:        decode_metadata = getattr(attn_metadata, "decode", None)
540:        if decode_metadata is not None:
541:            if decode_metadata.sas_metadata is not None:
542:                decode_metadata.sas_metadata = decode_metadata.sas_metadata.clone()
543:        return attn_metadata
544:
545:    @torch.inference_mode()
546:    def dummy_run(
547:        self,
548:        num_tokens: int,
549:        with_prefill: bool = False,
550:        in_graph_capturing: bool = False,
551:        num_reqs: int = 0,
552:        num_tokens_across_dp: torch.Tensor | None = None,
553:        aclgraph_runtime_mode: CUDAGraphMode = CUDAGraphMode.NONE,
554:        batch_descriptor=None,
555:        dummy_compute_logits=lambda hidden_states: None,
556:        is_profile=False,
557:    ):
558:        (
559:            num_tokens,
560:            num_tokens_across_dp,
561:            _,
562:        ) = self.runner._sync_metadata_across_dp(num_tokens, is_draft_model=True)
563:        pcp_manager = getattr(self.runner, "pcp_manager", None)
564:
565:        multi_steps_attn_metadata = []
566:        if not self.use_cuda_graph:
567:            aclgraph_runtime_mode = CUDAGraphMode.NONE
568:
569:        # init block table tensor clone is only available after profile run and is only used for graph mode
570:        if (
571:            self.pcp_size * self.dcp_size > 1
572:            and self.use_cuda_graph
573:            and not is_profile
574:            and self.block_table_tensor_clone is None
575:        ):
576:            self.block_table_tensor_clone = torch.zeros(
577:                (
578:                    self.runner.max_num_tokens + 2 * self.pcp_size * self.runner.max_num_reqs,
579:                    self.runner.input_batch.block_table[0].get_device_tensor().shape[1],
580:                ),
581:                dtype=torch.int32,
582:                device=self.device,
583:                pin_memory=self.runner.pin_memory,
584:            )
585:
586:        if aclgraph_runtime_mode == CUDAGraphMode.FULL and len(self.runner.attn_groups) > 0:
587:            num_computed_tokens_cpu = self.runner.input_batch.num_computed_tokens_cpu_tensor[:num_reqs]
588:
589:            # num_reqs is already the padded version
590:            self.query_start_loc.cpu[: num_reqs + 1].copy_(self.runner.query_start_loc.cpu[: num_reqs + 1])
591:            copy_snapshot_to_gpu(self.query_start_loc)
592:
593:            common_attn_metadata = AscendCommonAttentionMetadata(
594:                query_start_loc=self.query_start_loc.gpu[: num_reqs + 1],
595:                query_start_loc_cpu=self.query_start_loc.cpu[: num_reqs + 1],
596:                seq_lens_cpu=self.runner.optimistic_seq_lens_cpu,
597:                _seq_lens_cpu=self.runner.optimistic_seq_lens_cpu,
598:                seq_lens_cpu_upper_bound=self.runner.optimistic_seq_lens_cpu,
599:                seq_lens=self.runner.seq_lens[:num_reqs],
600:                num_reqs=num_reqs,
601:                num_actual_tokens=num_tokens,
602:                num_input_tokens=num_tokens,
603:                max_query_len=self.num_speculative_tokens + 1,
604:                num_computed_tokens_cpu=num_computed_tokens_cpu,
605:                actual_seq_lengths_q=self.runner.actual_seq_lengths_q,
606:                block_table_tensor=self.runner.input_batch.block_table[self.kv_cache_gid].get_device_tensor()[
607:                    :num_reqs
608:                ],
609:                # This is used to hold a position.
610:                slot_mapping=self.runner.input_batch.block_table[self.kv_cache_gid].slot_mapping.gpu,
611:                positions=self.runner.positions,
612:                positions_cpu=self.runner._dsa_positions_cpu_buf if self.use_compress else None,
613:                attn_state=self.runner.attn_state,
614:                decode_token_per_req=self.runner.decode_token_per_req,
615:                is_prefilling=torch.zeros(num_reqs, dtype=torch.bool),
616:                max_seq_len=0,
617:            )
618:            if pcp_manager is not None:
619:                # update long_seq related params and flatten block_table
620:                common_attn_metadata.prefill_context_parallel_metadata = pcp_manager.long_seq_metadata
621:
622:            assert len(self.draft_attn_groups) > 0
623:            builder = self.draft_attn_groups[0].get_metadata_builder()
624:            kv_cache_spec = self.draft_attn_groups[0].kv_cache_spec
625:            # update the tensor's address for each step.
626:            for draft_index in range(self.num_speculative_tokens):
627:                common_attn_metadata = self.shallow_copy_metadata(common_attn_metadata)
628:                extra_attn_metadata_args: dict = {}
629:                if self.use_compress:
630:                    extra_attn_metadata_args.update(
631:                        prefill_ratio_to_sas_metadata=dict(),
632:                        decode_ratio_to_sas_metadata=dict(),
633:                        common_ratio_to_sas_metadata=dict(),
634:                        block_size=kv_cache_spec.block_size,
635:                    )
636:                # Set the real slot_mapping.
637:                slot_mapping_lens = common_attn_metadata.slot_mapping.shape[0]
638:                self.slot_mapping_group[draft_index][:slot_mapping_lens].copy_(common_attn_metadata.slot_mapping)
639:                self.slot_mapping_group[draft_index][slot_mapping_lens:].fill_(PADDING_SLOT_ID)
640:                common_attn_metadata.slot_mapping = self.slot_mapping_group[draft_index]
641:                self.seq_lens_group[draft_index][:num_reqs].copy_(common_attn_metadata.seq_lens)
642:                self.seq_lens_group[draft_index][num_reqs:].fill_(0)
643:                common_attn_metadata.seq_lens = self.seq_lens_group[draft_index][:num_reqs]
644:                self.query_start_loc_group[draft_index][: num_reqs + 1].copy_(common_attn_metadata.query_start_loc)
645:                self.query_start_loc_group[draft_index][num_reqs + 1 :].fill_(0)
646:                common_attn_metadata.query_start_loc = self.query_start_loc_group[draft_index][: num_reqs + 1]
647:                if self.pcp_size * self.dcp_size > 1 and draft_index > 0:
648:                    assert self.block_table_tensor_clone is not None, "block_table_tensor_clone is not init"
649:                    common_attn_metadata.block_table_tensor = self.block_table_tensor_clone[:num_reqs]
650:                if not self.use_compress or draft_index == 0:
651:                    attn_metadata_eagle = builder.build_for_graph_capture(
652:                        common_attn_metadata,
653:                        AscendAttentionState.SpecDecoding
654:                        if self.method == "mtp"
655:                        else AscendAttentionState.ChunkedPrefill,
656:                        **extra_attn_metadata_args,
657:                    )
658:                else:
659:                    attn_metadata_eagle = builder.build_for_drafting(
660:                        common_attn_metadata,
661:                        draft_index,
662:                        **extra_attn_metadata_args,
663:                    )
664:                per_layer_attn_metadata = dict()
665:                for layer_name in self.attn_layer_names:
666:                    per_layer_attn_metadata[layer_name] = attn_metadata_eagle
667:                multi_steps_attn_metadata.append(per_layer_attn_metadata)
668:
669:        model_positions = self._get_positions(num_tokens)
670:
671:        batch_size = max(num_tokens // (self.num_speculative_tokens + 1), 1)
672:        # TODO: temporarily hack here, we should find out batch_size for profile_run
673:        if is_profile:
674:            batch_size = min(batch_size, self.runner.max_num_reqs)
675:
676:        if self.supports_mm_inputs:
677:            mm_embeds, is_mm_embed = (None, None)
678:            inputs_embeds = self.model.embed_input_ids(
679:                self.input_ids[:num_tokens], multimodal_embeddings=mm_embeds, is_multimodal=is_mm_embed
680:            )
681:            self.inputs_embeds[:num_tokens] = inputs_embeds
682:            inputs_embeds = self.inputs_embeds[:num_tokens]
683:        else:
684:            inputs_embeds = None
685:
686:        self.token_indices_to_sample.fill_(0)
687:
688:        with set_ascend_forward_context(
689:            multi_steps_attn_metadata[0] if multi_steps_attn_metadata else None,
690:            self.vllm_config,
691:            num_tokens=num_tokens,
692:            num_tokens_across_dp=num_tokens_across_dp,
693:            num_actual_tokens=0,
694:            in_profile_run=is_profile,
695:            batch_descriptor=batch_descriptor,
696:            aclgraph_runtime_mode=aclgraph_runtime_mode,
697:            is_draft_model=True,
698:            draft_attn_metadatas=multi_steps_attn_metadata,
699:        ):
700:            # Reset MOE layer index before first model call
701:            forward_context = get_forward_context()
702:            if forward_context is not None:
703:                forward_context.moe_layer_index = 0
704:
705:            self._runnable(
706:                num_input_tokens=num_tokens,
707:                batch_size=batch_size,
708:                token_indices_to_sample=self.token_indices_to_sample[: batch_size * self.extra_slots_per_request],
709:                # The target_position's address is same as the model_positions's
710:                target_positions=model_positions,
711:                inputs_embeds=inputs_embeds,
712:                multi_steps_attn_metadata=multi_steps_attn_metadata,
713:                num_tokens=num_tokens,
714:            )
715:            forward_context = get_forward_context()
716:            if forward_context.cudagraph_runtime_mode == CUDAGraphMode.FULL and not _EXTRA_CTX.capturing:
717:                self._update_full_graph_params(forward_context, num_tokens, multi_steps_attn_metadata)
718:
719:    def _update_full_graph_params_if_needed(
720:        self,
721:        forward_context: ForwardContext,
722:        num_input_tokens: int,
723:        multi_steps_attn_metadata: list[dict[str, Any]],
724:    ) -> None:
725:        if forward_context.cudagraph_runtime_mode == CUDAGraphMode.FULL:
726:            self._update_full_graph_params(forward_context, num_input_tokens, multi_steps_attn_metadata)
727:
728:    def _propose(
729:        self,
730:        # [num_tokens]
731:        target_token_ids: torch.Tensor,
732:        # [num_tokens] or [3, num_tokens] when M-RoPE is enabled
733:        target_positions: torch.Tensor,
734:        # [num_tokens, hidden_size]
735:        target_hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
736:        # [batch_size]
737:        next_token_ids: torch.Tensor,
738:        token_indices_to_sample: torch.Tensor | None,
739:        common_attn_metadata: CommonAttentionMetadata,
740:        target_model_batch_desc: BatchDescriptor,
741:        sampling_metadata: SamplingMetadata,
742:        mm_embed_inputs: tuple[list[torch.Tensor], torch.Tensor] | None = None,
743:        req_scheduled_tokens=None,
744:        long_seq_metadata=None,
745:        num_prefill_reqs=0,
746:        num_decode_reqs=0,
747:        scheduler_output: SchedulerOutput = None,
748:        num_scheduled_tokens: int = 0,
749:        num_rejected_tokens_gpu: torch.Tensor | None = None,
750:    ) -> torch.Tensor:
751:        batch_size = common_attn_metadata.batch_size()
752:
753:        if token_indices_to_sample is None:
754:            token_indices_to_sample = common_attn_metadata.query_start_loc[1:] - 1
755:
756:        if self.method in ("eagle3", "dflash"):
757:            assert isinstance(
758:                self.get_model(),
759:                (
760:                    Eagle3LlamaForCausalLM,
761:                    DFlashQwen3ForCausalLM,
762:                    Eagle3VwnLlamaForCausalLM,
763:                    Eagle3DeepseekV2ForCausalLM,
764:                ),
765:            )
766:            target_hidden_states = self.model.combine_hidden_states(target_hidden_states)
767:            assert target_hidden_states.shape[-1] == self.hidden_size
768:
769:        num_tokens, token_indices_to_sample, common_attn_metadata, long_seq_args = self.set_inputs_first_pass(
770:            target_token_ids=target_token_ids,
771:            next_token_ids=next_token_ids,
772:            target_positions=target_positions,
773:            target_hidden_states=target_hidden_states,
774:            token_indices_to_sample=token_indices_to_sample,
775:            cad=common_attn_metadata,
776:            num_rejected_tokens_gpu=num_rejected_tokens_gpu,
777:            req_scheduled_tokens=req_scheduled_tokens,
778:            long_seq_metadata=long_seq_metadata,
779:            num_prefill_reqs=num_prefill_reqs,
780:            num_decode_reqs=num_decode_reqs,
781:        )
782:        assert self.runner is not None
783:        pcp_manager = getattr(self.runner, "pcp_manager", None)
784:        if pcp_manager is not None:
785:            assert long_seq_args is not None
786:            _, ori_token_indices_to_sample = long_seq_args
787:
788:        has_lora = len(self.runner.input_batch.lora_id_to_lora_request) > 0
789:        uniform_decode = target_model_batch_desc.uniform
790:
791:        if self.use_cuda_graph:
792:            _, batch_descriptor = self.runner.cudagraph_dispatcher.dispatch(
793:                num_tokens=num_tokens, uniform_decode=uniform_decode, has_lora=has_lora
794:            )
795:            num_input_tokens = batch_descriptor.num_tokens
796:        else:
797:            num_input_tokens = num_tokens
798:
799:        (
800:            num_input_tokens,
801:            num_tokens_across_dp,
802:            _,
803:        ) = self.runner._sync_metadata_across_dp(num_input_tokens, is_draft_model=True)
804:
805:        if self.use_cuda_graph:
806:            aclgraph_runtime_mode, batch_descriptor = self.runner.cudagraph_dispatcher.dispatch(
807:                num_tokens=num_input_tokens, uniform_decode=uniform_decode, has_lora=has_lora
808:            )
809:            num_input_tokens = batch_descriptor.num_tokens
810:        else:
811:            aclgraph_runtime_mode = CUDAGraphMode.NONE
812:            batch_descriptor = None
813:
814:        if aclgraph_runtime_mode == CUDAGraphMode.FULL:
815:            # TODO: Due to the inconsistency between the proposer `dispatcher` and model runner, this padding
816:            # should have been done in model runner but not. For example, at prefill stage, target model
817:            # is run in eager mode currently, which means `_pad_query_start_loc_for_fia` is not called,
818:            # while draft model is run in graph model, which means we should pad the `query_start_loc`.
819:            # Need to be fixed in the future.
820:            num_reqs = common_attn_metadata.query_start_loc.shape[0]
821:            self.query_start_loc.gpu[:num_reqs].copy_(common_attn_metadata.query_start_loc)
822:            self.query_start_loc.cpu[:num_reqs].copy_(common_attn_metadata.query_start_loc_cpu)
823:            num_reqs_padded = self.runner._pad_query_start_loc_for_fia(
824:                self.query_start_loc,
825:                num_input_tokens,
826:                batch_descriptor.num_reqs if batch_descriptor.num_reqs is not None else common_attn_metadata.num_reqs,
827:                common_attn_metadata.num_reqs,
828:                aclgraph_runtime_mode,
829:                batch_descriptor.num_reqs,
830:            )
831:            common_attn_metadata.num_reqs = num_reqs_padded
832:            common_attn_metadata.query_start_loc = self.query_start_loc.gpu[: num_reqs_padded + 1]
833:            common_attn_metadata.query_start_loc_cpu = self.query_start_loc.cpu[: num_reqs_padded + 1]
834:            slicing_length = (
835:                num_reqs_padded * self.decode_threshold if self.pcp_size * self.dcp_size > 1 else num_reqs_padded
836:            )
837:            common_attn_metadata.block_table_tensor = self._adjust_tensor(
838:                common_attn_metadata.block_table_tensor, slicing_length
839:            )
840:            if self.method == "dflash":
841:                common_attn_metadata.seq_lens = self._adjust_tensor(common_attn_metadata.seq_lens, num_reqs_padded)
842:            else:
843:                common_attn_metadata.seq_lens = self._adjust_tensor(self.runner.seq_lens, num_reqs_padded)
844:                common_attn_metadata.seq_lens_cpu = self._adjust_tensor(
845:                    self.runner.optimistic_seq_lens_cpu, num_reqs_padded
846:                )
847:                # Keep the upstream-canonical mirror length-aligned with the
848:                # padded subclass field, but only if the caller already
849:                # populated it (production cm_base does; some unit-test mocks
