850:                # leave it None and assert it stays None). ``.clone()`` keeps
851:                # the two fields independent so per-step in-place updates in
852:                # ``attn_update_stack_num_spec_norm`` don't double-count.
853:                if common_attn_metadata._seq_lens_cpu is not None:
854:                    common_attn_metadata._seq_lens_cpu = common_attn_metadata.seq_lens_cpu.clone()
855:            if common_attn_metadata.num_computed_tokens_cpu is not None:
856:                common_attn_metadata.num_computed_tokens_cpu = self._adjust_tensor(
857:                    common_attn_metadata.num_computed_tokens_cpu, num_reqs_padded
858:                )
859:
860:            if pcp_manager is not None and self.pcp_size > 1:
861:                pcp_manager.mask_spec_decode_restore_idx_for_graph(
862:                    common_attn_metadata.prefill_context_parallel_metadata.pcp_allgather_restore_idx
863:                )
864:        else:
865:            num_reqs_padded = common_attn_metadata.num_reqs
866:            # In the below scenario, padding has been applied by _pad_query_start_loc_for_fia in the model runner.
867:            # We need to unpad here for eager mode to maintain compatibility.
868:            if not self.vllm_config.model_config.use_mla and self.pcp_size * self.dcp_size == 1:
869:                common_attn_metadata.block_table_tensor = self._adjust_tensor(
870:                    common_attn_metadata.block_table_tensor, num_reqs_padded
871:                )
872:
873:        if self.supports_mm_inputs:
874:            mm_embeds, is_mm_embed = mm_embed_inputs or (None, None)
875:            inputs_embeds = self.model.embed_input_ids(
876:                self.input_ids[:num_tokens], multimodal_embeddings=mm_embeds, is_multimodal=is_mm_embed
877:            )
878:            self.inputs_embeds[:num_tokens] = inputs_embeds
879:            inputs_embeds = self.inputs_embeds[:num_input_tokens]
880:        else:
881:            inputs_embeds = None
882:
883:        # Update slot_mapping for different speculative.
884:        # NOTE: Currently, we only remake the slot_mapping, because it's the
885:        # only tensor which will be used in current FIA.
886:        # Strictly speaking, `query_start_loc`, `seq_lens` should also have
887:        # their memory allocated separately for each step just like `slot_mapping`.
888:        slot_mapping_lens = common_attn_metadata.slot_mapping.shape[0]
889:        self.slot_mapping_group[0][:slot_mapping_lens].copy_(common_attn_metadata.slot_mapping)
890:        self.slot_mapping_group[0][slot_mapping_lens:].fill_(-1)
891:        common_attn_metadata.slot_mapping = self.slot_mapping_group[0]
892:
893:        self.seq_lens_group[0][:num_reqs_padded].copy_(common_attn_metadata.seq_lens)
894:        self.seq_lens_group[0][num_reqs_padded:].fill_(0)
895:        common_attn_metadata.seq_lens = self.seq_lens_group[0][:num_reqs_padded]
896:
897:        self.query_start_loc_group[0][: num_reqs_padded + 1].copy_(common_attn_metadata.query_start_loc)
898:        self.query_start_loc_group[0][num_reqs_padded + 1 :].fill_(0)
899:        common_attn_metadata.query_start_loc = self.query_start_loc_group[0][: num_reqs_padded + 1]
900:
901:        common_attn_metadata.num_input_tokens = num_input_tokens
902:        # FIXME(woosuk): The below two ops cause synchronization. Optimize.
903:        assert len(self.draft_attn_groups) > 0
904:        builder = self.draft_attn_groups[0].get_metadata_builder()
905:        extra_attn_metadata_args: dict = {}
906:        if self.use_compress:
907:            extra_attn_metadata_args = dict(
908:                prefill_ratio_to_sas_metadata=dict(),
909:                decode_ratio_to_sas_metadata=dict(),
910:                common_ratio_to_sas_metadata=dict(),
911:                block_size=self.draft_attn_groups[0].kv_cache_spec.block_size,
912:            )
913:        attn_metadata = builder.build(0, common_attn_metadata, self.runner.get_model(), **extra_attn_metadata_args)
914:
915:        if hasattr(attn_metadata, "causal") and not attn_metadata.causal:
916:            attn_metadata.attn_mask = None
917:
918:        if self.uses_mrope:
919:            used_update_positions = self.mrope_positions[:, token_indices_to_sample]
920:        else:
921:            used_update_positions = self.positions[token_indices_to_sample]
922:        per_layer_attn_metadata = dict()
923:        # The first step of speculative.
924:        for layer_name in self.attn_layer_names:
925:            per_layer_attn_metadata[layer_name] = attn_metadata
926:        multi_steps_attn_metadata = [per_layer_attn_metadata]
927:
928:        # Copy the old attn_metadata and update
929:        attn_metadata_i = per_layer_attn_metadata[self.attn_layer_names[0]]
930:
931:        # Clone the data so that when calculating the data at position 2 and position 3
932:        # in the merged graph, it does not affect position 1
933:        # FIXME(lilinsiman)
934:        if self.pcp_size * self.dcp_size > 1 and self.use_cuda_graph:
935:            assert self.block_table_tensor_clone is not None, "block_table_tensor_clone is not init"
936:            self.block_table_tensor_clone[: common_attn_metadata.block_table_tensor.shape[0]] = (
937:                common_attn_metadata.block_table_tensor
938:            )
939:            common_attn_metadata.block_table_tensor = self.block_table_tensor_clone[
940:                : common_attn_metadata.block_table_tensor.shape[0]
941:            ]
942:        else:
943:            common_attn_metadata.block_table_tensor = common_attn_metadata.block_table_tensor.clone()
944:
945:        metadata_has_prefill = bool(getattr(attn_metadata_i, "num_prefills", 0))
946:        is_prefill_batch = num_prefill_reqs > 0 or metadata_has_prefill
947:        pcp_mtp_inputs = None
948:        draft_cp_kwargs = {
949:            "ori_seq_len": None,
950:            "ori_seq_len_cpu": None,
951:            "slot_indices": None,
952:            "mtp_slot_mapping": None,
953:        }
954:        if pcp_manager is not None:
955:            pcp_mtp_inputs = pcp_manager.prepare_spec_decode_mtp_drafting_inputs(
956:                common_attn_metadata=common_attn_metadata,
957:                attn_metadata=attn_metadata_i,
958:                ori_token_indices_to_sample=ori_token_indices_to_sample,
959:                batch_size=batch_size,
960:                num_decode_reqs=num_decode_reqs,
961:                is_prefill_batch=is_prefill_batch,
962:                num_speculative_tokens=self.num_speculative_tokens,
963:            )
964:            if pcp_mtp_inputs is not None:
965:                draft_cp_kwargs.update(
966:                    ori_seq_len=pcp_mtp_inputs.seq_lens,
967:                    ori_seq_len_cpu=pcp_mtp_inputs.seq_lens_cpu,
968:                    slot_indices=pcp_mtp_inputs.slot_indices,
969:                    mtp_slot_mapping=pcp_mtp_inputs.slot_mapping,
970:                )
971:
972:        should_update_next_steps = not self.parallel_drafting and (
973:            self.pcp_size * self.dcp_size == 1 or pcp_mtp_inputs is not None
974:        )
975:        if should_update_next_steps:
976:            # Copy the old attn_metadata and update
977:            for draft_index in range(1, self.num_speculative_tokens):
978:                per_layer_attn_metadata = dict()
979:                for attn_group in self.draft_attn_groups:
980:                    common_attn_metadata, attn_metadata = self.attn_update_stack_num_spec_norm(
981:                        draft_index,
982:                        attn_metadata,
983:                        common_attn_metadata,
984:                        batch_size,
985:                        num_input_tokens,
986:                        used_update_positions,
987:                        aclgraph_runtime_mode,
988:                        **draft_cp_kwargs,
989:                        attn_group=attn_group,
990:                    )
991:                    for layer_name in self.attn_layer_names:
992:                        per_layer_attn_metadata[layer_name] = attn_metadata
993:                multi_steps_attn_metadata.append(per_layer_attn_metadata)
994:
995:        token_indices_to_sample_len = token_indices_to_sample.shape[0]
996:        self.token_indices_to_sample[:token_indices_to_sample_len].copy_(token_indices_to_sample)
997:        self.token_indices_to_sample[token_indices_to_sample_len:].fill_(0)
998:
999:        with set_ascend_forward_context(
1000:            multi_steps_attn_metadata[0],
1001:            self.vllm_config,
1002:            num_tokens=num_input_tokens,
1003:            num_tokens_across_dp=num_tokens_across_dp,
1004:            num_actual_tokens=num_tokens,
1005:            batch_descriptor=batch_descriptor,
1006:            aclgraph_runtime_mode=aclgraph_runtime_mode,
1007:            is_draft_model=True,
1008:            draft_attn_metadatas=multi_steps_attn_metadata,
1009:            eplb_heat_collection_status=(
1010:                self.runner.eplb_heat_collection_status if self.runner.dynamic_eplb else False
1011:            ),
1012:        ):
1013:            # Reset MOE layer index for forward pass
1014:            forward_context = get_forward_context()
1015:            if forward_context is not None:
1016:                forward_context.moe_layer_index = 0
1017:
1018:            model_inputs: dict[str, Any] = {
1019:                "num_input_tokens": num_input_tokens,
1020:                "batch_size": batch_size,
1021:                "token_indices_to_sample": self.token_indices_to_sample[:token_indices_to_sample_len],
1022:                "target_positions": target_positions,
1023:                "inputs_embeds": inputs_embeds,
1024:                "multi_steps_attn_metadata": multi_steps_attn_metadata,
1025:                "num_tokens": num_tokens,
1026:                "is_prefill": is_prefill_batch,
1027:            }
1028:            runnable = cast(Callable[..., Any], self._runnable)
1029:            run_draft: Callable[[], Any] = partial(runnable, **model_inputs)
1030:
1031:            if self.enable_enpu:
1032:                self._update_full_graph_params_if_needed(forward_context, num_input_tokens, multi_steps_attn_metadata)
1033:                draft_token_ids = run_draft()
1034:            else:
1035:                draft_token_ids = run_draft()
1036:                self._update_full_graph_params_if_needed(forward_context, num_input_tokens, multi_steps_attn_metadata)
1037:        return draft_token_ids
1038:
1039:    def compute_draft_token_ids(self, hidden_states: torch.Tensor):
1040:        if self.method in ("eagle3", "dflash"):
1041:            logits = self.model.logits_processor(self.model.lm_head, hidden_states)
1042:            if not hasattr(self.model, "draft_id_to_target_id") or self.model.draft_id_to_target_id is None:
1043:                return greedy_sample(logits)
1044:            logits = logits.contiguous()
1045:            next_token = greedy_sample(logits)
1046:            bias = torch.index_select(self.model.draft_id_to_target_id, dim=0, index=next_token.view(-1)).view(
1047:                next_token.shape
1048:            )
1049:            return next_token + bias
1050:        else:
1051:            logits = self.model.compute_logits(hidden_states)
1052:            return greedy_sample(logits)
1053:
1054:    def _run_merged_draft(
1055:        self,
1056:        num_input_tokens,
1057:        batch_size,
1058:        token_indices_to_sample,
1059:        target_positions,
1060:        inputs_embeds,
1061:        multi_steps_attn_metadata,
1062:        num_tokens,
1063:        is_prefill=None,
1064:    ) -> torch.Tensor:
1065:        # The lifecycle of `input_ids`, `positions`, `hidden_states` runs through all
1066:        # speculative tokens' proposings. `model_input_ids`, `model_positions` and
1067:        # `model_hidden_states` represent the speculative model inputs.
1068:        model_input_ids = self.input_ids[:num_input_tokens]
1069:        model_positions = self._get_positions(num_input_tokens)
1070:
1071:        if self.method == "dflash":
1072:            model_kwargs = self.build_model_inputs_first_pass(num_input_tokens)
1073:        else:
1074:            model_kwargs = {
1075:                "input_ids": model_input_ids,
1076:                "positions": model_positions,
1077:                "inputs_embeds": inputs_embeds,
1078:            }
1079:
1080:            if self.pass_hidden_states_to_model:
1081:                model_hidden_states = self.hidden_states[:num_input_tokens]
1082:                model_hidden_states, model_positions = self.maybe_pad_and_reduce(model_hidden_states, model_positions)
1083:                model_kwargs["hidden_states"] = model_hidden_states
1084:                if self.method == "mtp":
1085:                    model_kwargs["positions"] = model_positions
1086:
1087:        # step 0
1088:        draft_model = getattr(self.model, "model", None)
1089:        if self._share_mtp_indices and draft_model is not None and hasattr(draft_model, "set_skip_topk"):
1090:            draft_model.set_skip_topk(False)
1091:
1092:        ret_hidden_states = self.model(**model_kwargs)
1093:        if not self.model_returns_tuple():
1094:            last_hidden_states = ret_hidden_states
1095:            hidden_states = last_hidden_states
1096:        else:
1097:            last_hidden_states, hidden_states = ret_hidden_states
1098:
1099:        # step 1+ skip indexer
1100:        draft_model = getattr(self.model, "model", None)
1101:        if self._share_mtp_indices and draft_model is not None and hasattr(draft_model, "set_skip_topk"):
1102:            draft_model.set_skip_topk(True)
1103:
1104:        if self.method != "dflash":
1105:            last_hidden_states, model_positions, hidden_states = self.maybe_all_gather_and_unpad(
1106:                last_hidden_states, model_positions, hidden_states
1107:            )
1108:
1109:        num_indices = token_indices_to_sample.shape[0]
1110:        if lmhead_tp_enable():
1111:            max_num_reqs_across_dp = (
1112:                self.vllm_config.scheduler_config.max_num_seqs * self.runner.uniform_decode_query_len
1113:            )
1114:            # It is necessary to evaluate the case where num_indices becomes large
1115:            # in the context of the dummy‑run accompaniment of p‑eagle.
1116:            if num_indices > max_num_reqs_across_dp:
1117:                ori_token_indices_to_sample = token_indices_to_sample
1118:            else:
1119:                ori_token_indices_to_sample = None
1120:
1121:        pcp_manager = getattr(self.runner, "pcp_manager", None)
1122:        if pcp_manager is not None and self.pcp_size > 1:
1123:            # remove graph padding before all_gather
1124:            hidden_states = pcp_manager.get_restore_hidden_states(
1125:                hidden_states,
1126:                num_input_tokens=num_input_tokens,
1127:            )
1128:            if self.method == "mtp":
1129:                last_hidden_states = hidden_states
1130:            else:
1131:                # eagle and eagle3 need allgather last_hidden_states.
1132:                last_hidden_states = pcp_manager.get_restore_hidden_states(
1133:                    last_hidden_states,
1134:                    num_input_tokens=num_input_tokens,
1135:                )
1136:
1137:        if lmhead_tp_enable():
1138:            token_indices_to_sample = nn.functional.pad(
1139:                token_indices_to_sample, (0, max_num_reqs_across_dp - num_indices)
1140:            )
1141:
1142:        sample_hidden_states = last_hidden_states[token_indices_to_sample]
1143:
1144:        if get_ascend_config().enable_reduce_sample:
1145:            if self.method in ("eagle3", "dflash", "mtp"):
1146:                draft_token_ids = self.compute_draft_token_ids(sample_hidden_states)
1147:                if lmhead_tp_enable():
1148:                    draft_token_ids, token_indices_to_sample = self._align_tensor_and_indices(
1149:                        draft_token_ids,
1150:                        num_indices,
1151:                        token_indices_to_sample,
1152:                        ori_token_indices_to_sample,
1153:                        is_logits=False,
1154:                    )
1155:            else:
1156:                logits = self.model.compute_logits(sample_hidden_states)
1157:                if lmhead_tp_enable():
1158:                    logits = get_lmhead_tp_group().all_to_all(logits)
1159:                else:
1160:                    logits = self.model.model.logits_processor._gather_logits(logits)
1161:                if lmhead_tp_enable():
1162:                    logits, token_indices_to_sample = self._align_tensor_and_indices(
1163:                        logits,
1164:                        num_indices,
1165:                        token_indices_to_sample,
1166:                        ori_token_indices_to_sample,
1167:                        is_logits=True,
1168:                    )
1169:                draft_token_ids = logits.argmax(dim=-1)
1170:        else:
1171:            logits = self.model.compute_logits(sample_hidden_states)
1172:            if lmhead_tp_enable():
1173:                logits, token_indices_to_sample = self._align_tensor_and_indices(
1174:                    logits,
1175:                    num_indices,
1176:                    token_indices_to_sample,
1177:                    ori_token_indices_to_sample,
1178:                    is_logits=True,
1179:                )
1180:            draft_token_ids = logits.argmax(dim=-1)
1181:
1182:        # Early exit if there is only one draft token to be generated.
1183:        if self.num_speculative_tokens == 1 or self.parallel_drafting:
1184:            # [batch_size, 1]
1185:            return draft_token_ids.view(-1, self.num_speculative_tokens)
1186:
1187:        if self.pcp_size > 1 and is_prefill:
1188:            draft_token_ids_list = []
1189:            for _ in range(self.num_speculative_tokens):
1190:                draft_token_ids_list.append(draft_token_ids)
1191:            return torch.stack(draft_token_ids_list, dim=1)
1192:
1193:        # The logits are split and then merged only when lmhead_tp_enable() is enabled.
1194:        # As a result, the batch size length becomes the actual length 32.
1195:        # However, when lmhead_tp_enable() is disabled, the batch size uses the length after padding.
1196:        # To decouple the scenarios, a judgment is required.
1197:        # That is, the batch size needs to be modified only when lmhead_tp_enable() is enabled.
1198:        if lmhead_tp_enable() and self.method == "mtp":
1199:            batch_size = draft_token_ids.shape[0]
1200:
1201:        # Generate the remaining draft tokens.
1202:        draft_token_ids_tensor = torch.zeros(
1203:            (self.num_speculative_tokens, *draft_token_ids.shape), dtype=draft_token_ids.dtype, device=self.device
1204:        )
1205:        draft_token_ids_tensor[0] = draft_token_ids
1206:        if self.uses_mrope:
1207:            positions = self.mrope_positions[:, token_indices_to_sample]
1208:        else:
1209:            positions = self.positions[token_indices_to_sample]
1210:        hidden_states = hidden_states[token_indices_to_sample]
1211:        token_indices_to_sample = self.arange[:batch_size]
1212:
1213:        input_batch_size = num_input_tokens if (self.method == "mtp" or self.use_cuda_graph) else batch_size
1214:
1215:        forward_context = get_forward_context()
1216:        _EXTRA_CTX.num_tokens = input_batch_size
1217:        _EXTRA_CTX.num_accept_tokens = batch_size
1218:
1219:        for draft_index in range(self.num_speculative_tokens - 1):
1220:            # Reset MOE layer index for each draft step iteration
1221:            forward_context = get_forward_context()
1222:            if forward_context is not None:
1223:                forward_context.moe_layer_index = 0
1224:
1225:            # Update the inputs.
1226:            # cast to int32 is crucial when eagle model is compiled.
1227:            # tensor.argmax() returns int64 by default.
1228:            input_ids = draft_token_ids_tensor[draft_index]
1229:            positions += 1
1230:
1231:            # NOTE(woosuk): We should handle the case where the draft model
1232:            # generates tokens beyond the max model length. Since it is complex
1233:            # to remove such requests from the batch, we keep them in the batch
1234:            # but adjust the position ids and slot mappings to avoid the
1235:            # out-of-range access during the model execution. The draft tokens
1236:            # generated with this adjustment should be ignored.
1237:            if self.uses_mrope:
1238:                exceeds_max_model_len = positions[0] >= self.vllm_config.model_config.max_model_len
1239:                # Mask out the position ids that exceed the max model length.
1240:                # Otherwise, we may get out-of-range error in RoPE.
1241:                clamped_positions = torch.where(
1242:                    exceeds_max_model_len.unsqueeze(0), torch.zeros_like(positions), positions
1243:                )
1244:            else:
1245:                exceeds_max_model_len = positions >= self.vllm_config.model_config.max_model_len
1246:                clamped_positions = torch.where(exceeds_max_model_len, 0, positions)
1247:
1248:            # copy inputs to buffer for cudagraph
1249:            self.input_ids[:batch_size] = input_ids
1250:            self._set_positions(batch_size, clamped_positions)
1251:            self.hidden_states[:batch_size] = hidden_states.view(batch_size, -1)
1252:            if self.supports_mm_inputs:
1253:                self.inputs_embeds[:batch_size] = self.model.embed_input_ids(input_ids)
1254:
1255:                input_ids = self.input_ids[:input_batch_size]
1256:                inputs_embeds = self.inputs_embeds[:input_batch_size]
1257:            else:
1258:                input_ids = self.input_ids[:input_batch_size]
1259:                inputs_embeds = None
1260:
1261:            # Run the model.
1262:
1263:            # The lifecycle of `input_ids`, `positions`, `hidden_states` runs through all
1264:            # speculative tokens' proposings. `model_input_ids`, `model_positions` and
1265:            # `model_hidden_states` represent the speculative model inputs.
1266:            model_input_ids = self.input_ids[:input_batch_size]
1267:            model_positions = self._get_positions(input_batch_size)
1268:            model_hidden_states = self.hidden_states[:input_batch_size]
1269:
1270:            model_hidden_states, model_positions = self.maybe_pad_and_reduce(model_hidden_states, model_positions)
1271:
1272:            forward_context.attn_metadata = (
1273:                multi_steps_attn_metadata[draft_index + 1] if multi_steps_attn_metadata else None
1274:            )
1275:
1276:            model_kwargs = {
1277:                "input_ids": model_input_ids,
1278:                "positions": model_positions,
1279:                "inputs_embeds": inputs_embeds,
1280:            }
1281:            if self.pass_hidden_states_to_model:
1282:                model_kwargs["hidden_states"] = model_hidden_states
1283:
1284:            ret_hidden_states = self.model(**model_kwargs)
1285:            if not self.model_returns_tuple():
1286:                last_hidden_states = ret_hidden_states
1287:                hidden_states = last_hidden_states
1288:            else:
1289:                last_hidden_states, hidden_states = ret_hidden_states
1290:
1291:            last_hidden_states, model_positions, hidden_states = self.maybe_all_gather_and_unpad(
1292:                last_hidden_states, model_positions, hidden_states
1293:            )
1294:
1295:            num_indices = token_indices_to_sample.shape[0]
1296:            if lmhead_tp_enable():
1297:                max_num_reqs_across_dp = (
1298:                    self.vllm_config.scheduler_config.max_num_seqs * self.runner.uniform_decode_query_len
1299:                )
1300:                token_indices_to_sample = nn.functional.pad(
1301:                    token_indices_to_sample,
1302:                    (0, max_num_reqs_across_dp - num_indices),
1303:                )
1304:
1305:            sample_hidden_states = last_hidden_states[token_indices_to_sample]
1306:            if get_ascend_config().enable_reduce_sample:
1307:                if self.method in ("eagle3", "dflash", "mtp"):
1308:                    draft_token_ids = self.compute_draft_token_ids(sample_hidden_states)
1309:                    if lmhead_tp_enable() and num_indices < draft_token_ids.shape[0]:
1310:                        draft_token_ids = draft_token_ids[:num_indices]
1311:                        token_indices_to_sample = token_indices_to_sample[:num_indices]
1312:                else:
1313:                    logits = self.model.compute_logits(sample_hidden_states)
1314:                    if lmhead_tp_enable():
1315:                        logits = get_lmhead_tp_group().all_to_all(logits)
1316:                    else:
1317:                        logits = self.model.model.logits_processor._gather_logits(logits)
1318:                    if lmhead_tp_enable() and num_indices < logits.shape[0]:
1319:                        logits = logits[:num_indices]
1320:                        token_indices_to_sample = token_indices_to_sample[:num_indices]
1321:                    draft_token_ids = logits.argmax(dim=-1)
1322:            else:
1323:                logits = self.model.compute_logits(sample_hidden_states)
1324:                if lmhead_tp_enable() and num_indices < logits.shape[0]:
1325:                    logits = logits[:num_indices]
1326:                    token_indices_to_sample = token_indices_to_sample[:num_indices]
1327:                draft_token_ids = logits.argmax(dim=-1)
1328:
1329:            # TODO(wenlong): get more than one token for tree attention
1330:            hidden_states = hidden_states[:batch_size]
1331:            draft_token_ids_tensor[draft_index + 1] = draft_token_ids
1332:
1333:        # [batch_size, num_speculative_tokens]
1334:        draft_token_ids = draft_token_ids_tensor.swapaxes(0, 1)
1335:        return draft_token_ids
1336:
1337:    def set_inputs_first_pass(
1338:        self,
1339:        target_token_ids: torch.Tensor,
1340:        next_token_ids: torch.Tensor,
1341:        target_positions: torch.Tensor,
1342:        target_hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
1343:        token_indices_to_sample: torch.Tensor | None,
1344:        cad: CommonAttentionMetadata,
1345:        num_rejected_tokens_gpu: torch.Tensor | None,
1346:        req_scheduled_tokens=None,
1347:        long_seq_metadata=None,
1348:        num_prefill_reqs=0,
1349:        num_decode_reqs=0,
1350:    ) -> tuple[int, torch.Tensor, CommonAttentionMetadata, tuple[Any, Any] | None]:
1351:        if not self.needs_extra_input_slots:
1352:            # Default EAGLE pathway: no reshaping of input tensors needed.
1353:            # Simply rotate the input ids and leave the positions unchanged,
1354:            # Inserting the next token ids at the last slot in each request.
1355:            if token_indices_to_sample is None:
1356:                token_indices_to_sample = cad.query_start_loc[1:] - 1
1357:
1358:            num_tokens = target_token_ids.shape[0]
1359:            # Shift the input ids by one token.
1360:            # E.g., [a1, b1, b2, c1, c2, c3] -> [b1, b2, c1, c2, c3, c3]
1361:            self.input_ids[: num_tokens - 1] = target_token_ids[1:]
1362:            # Replace the last token with the next token.
1363:            # E.g., [b1, b2, c1, c2, c3, c3] -> [a2, b2, b3, c2, c3, c4]
1364:            self.input_ids[token_indices_to_sample] = next_token_ids
1365:
1366:            assert self.runner is not None
1367:            pcp_manager = getattr(self.runner, "pcp_manager", None)
1368:            long_seq_args = None
1369:            if pcp_manager is not None:
1370:                first_pass_inputs = pcp_manager.prepare_spec_decode_first_pass_inputs(
1371:                    input_ids=self.input_ids[:num_tokens],
1372:                    target_positions=target_positions,
1373:                    target_hidden_states=target_hidden_states,
1374:                    token_indices_to_sample=token_indices_to_sample,
1375:                    common_attn_metadata=cad,
1376:                    long_seq_metadata=long_seq_metadata,
1377:                    req_scheduled_tokens=req_scheduled_tokens,
1378:                    req_ids=self.runner.input_batch.req_ids,
1379:                    logits_indices=self.runner.logits_indices,
1380:                    num_tokens=num_tokens,
1381:                    num_prefill_reqs=num_prefill_reqs,
1382:                    num_decode_reqs=num_decode_reqs,
1383:                    uses_mrope=self.uses_mrope,
1384:                )
1385:                num_tokens = first_pass_inputs.num_tokens
1386:                target_positions = first_pass_inputs.target_positions
1387:                target_hidden_states = first_pass_inputs.target_hidden_states
1388:                token_indices_to_sample = first_pass_inputs.token_indices_to_sample
1389:                self.input_ids[:num_tokens].copy_(first_pass_inputs.input_ids)
1390:                long_seq_args = first_pass_inputs.long_seq_args
1391:
1392:            # copy inputs to buffer for cudagraph
1393:            if self.uses_xdrope_dim > 0 and self.draft_uses_xdrope_dim == 0:
1394:                target_positions = target_positions[0]
1395:
1396:            self._set_positions(num_tokens, target_positions)
1397:            self.hidden_states[:num_tokens] = target_hidden_states.view(num_tokens, -1)
1398:
1399:            return num_tokens, token_indices_to_sample, cad, long_seq_args
1400:        else:
1401:            assert self.is_rejected_token_mask is not None
1402:            assert self.is_masked_token_mask is not None
1403:            # 1.
1404:            # Call the CopyAndExpandEagleInputs AscendC operator to copy
1405:            # input_ids and positions into the correct slots in the
1406:            # preallocated buffers self.input_ids, self.positions.
1407:            batch_size = cad.batch_size()
1408:            total_num_input_tokens = target_token_ids.shape[0]
1409:            total_num_output_tokens = total_num_input_tokens + (self.net_num_new_slots_per_request * batch_size)
1410:
1411:            query_start_loc = cad.query_start_loc
1412:            query_end_loc = cad.query_start_loc[1:] - 1
1413:            if num_rejected_tokens_gpu is not None:
1414:                query_end_loc = query_end_loc - num_rejected_tokens_gpu
1415:
1416:            (
1417:                out_input_ids,
1418:                out_positions,
1419:                out_is_rejected_token_mask,
1420:                out_is_masked_token_mask,
1421:                token_indices_to_sample,
1422:                out_hidden_state_mapping,
1423:            ) = torch.ops._C_ascend.npu_copy_and_expand_eagle_inputs(
1424:                target_token_ids,
1425:                target_positions.to(torch.int32),
1426:                next_token_ids,
1427:                query_start_loc,
1428:                query_end_loc,
1429:                0,  # padding_token_id
1430:                self.parallel_drafting_token_id,
1431:                self.extra_slots_per_request,
1432:                self.pass_hidden_states_to_model,
1433:                total_num_output_tokens,
1434:            )
1435:
1436:            # Copy returned tensors into pre-allocated buffers
1437:            self.input_ids[:total_num_output_tokens].copy_(out_input_ids)
1438:            self.positions[:total_num_output_tokens].copy_(out_positions)
1439:            self.is_rejected_token_mask[:total_num_output_tokens].copy_(out_is_rejected_token_mask)
1440:            self.is_masked_token_mask[:total_num_output_tokens].copy_(out_is_masked_token_mask)
1441:            if self.pass_hidden_states_to_model:
1442:                assert self.parallel_drafting_hidden_state_tensor is not None
1443:                self.hidden_states[out_hidden_state_mapping] = target_hidden_states
1444:                # Use torch.where to avoid DtoH sync from boolean indexing
1445:                mask = self.is_masked_token_mask[:total_num_output_tokens]
1446:                torch.where(
1447:                    mask.unsqueeze(1),  # type: ignore
1448:                    self.parallel_drafting_hidden_state_tensor,
1449:                    self.hidden_states[:total_num_output_tokens],
1450:                    out=self.hidden_states[:total_num_output_tokens],
1451:                )
1452:
1453:            # 2.
1454:            # Recompute the slot mapping based on the new positions and
1455:            # rejection mask.
1456:            # Use the first draft attention group's kv_cache_spec for block_size
1457:            # (all draft layers share the same kv-cache group)
1458:            assert len(self.draft_attn_groups) > 0
1459:            block_size = self.draft_attn_groups[0].kv_cache_spec.block_size
1460:
1461:            new_slot_mapping = compute_new_slot_mapping(
1462:                cad=cad,
1463:                new_positions=self.positions[:total_num_output_tokens],
1464:                is_rejected_token_mask=self.is_rejected_token_mask[:total_num_output_tokens],
1465:                block_size=block_size,
1466:                num_new_tokens=self.net_num_new_slots_per_request,
1467:                max_model_len=self.max_model_len,
1468:            )
1469:
1470:            # 3. Update the common attention metadata with the new (meta)data
1471:            new_cad = extend_all_queries_by_N(
1472:                cad,
1473:                N=self.net_num_new_slots_per_request,
1474:                arange=self.arange,
1475:                new_slot_mapping=new_slot_mapping,
1476:            )
1477:            # ``extend_all_queries_by_N`` adds N to every per-row GPU
1478:            # ``seq_lens`` but cannot touch the host-side mirrors (it
1479:            # only knows about upstream's deprecated ``_seq_lens_cpu``
1480:            # field; the Ascend subclass also has its own
1481:            # ``seq_lens_cpu`` field, which would be silently stale
1482:            # after the dataclass ``replace``).
1483:            #
1484:            # NPU attention backends (MLA, AscendAttention, SFA) read
1485:            # those CPU mirrors as kernel input, so they MUST be in
1486:            # sync with the GPU view. We compute the +N update on CPU
1487:            # to avoid an extra GPU->CPU sync (which was the original
1488:            # FIXME): every consumer that needs the post-extend value
1489:            # already had a valid pre-extend mirror, so a CPU-only
1490:            # ``+N`` keeps both in lock-step at zero device-side cost.
1491:            N = self.net_num_new_slots_per_request
1492:            if cad._seq_lens_cpu is not None:
1493:                new_cad._seq_lens_cpu = cad._seq_lens_cpu + N
1494:            elif cad.seq_lens_cpu is not None:
1495:                # Parent field absent but Ascend subclass field set:
1496:                # populate ``_seq_lens_cpu`` so upstream code paths
1497:                # that prefer the parent field still get a fresh value.
1498:                new_cad._seq_lens_cpu = cad.seq_lens_cpu + N
1499:            if cad.seq_lens_cpu is not None:
1500:                new_cad.seq_lens_cpu = cad.seq_lens_cpu + N
1501:
1502:            return total_num_output_tokens, token_indices_to_sample, new_cad, None
1503:
1504:    def model_returns_tuple(self) -> bool:
1505:        if self.method == "mtp":
1506:            # DeepSeek-family MTP (deepseek_mtp.py) recycles the post-final-
1507:            # norm hidden, so its forward returns (logit_hidden,
1508:            # recycle_hidden). Other MTP families return a single tensor.
1509:            draft_model_config = getattr(self, "draft_model_config", None)
1510:            hf_config = getattr(draft_model_config, "hf_config", None)
1511:            architectures = getattr(hf_config, "architectures", []) or []
1512:            return "DeepSeekMTPModel" in architectures
1513:        return self.method not in ("mtp", "draft_model", "dflash")
1514:
1515:    def attn_update_stack_num_spec_norm(
1516:        self,
1517:        # `draft_index` must start from `1`, no `0`
1518:        draft_index,
1519:        old_attn_metadata,
1520:        old_common_metadata,
1521:        batch_size,
1522:        input_batch_size,
1523:        used_update_positions,
1524:        aclgraph_runtime_mode,
1525:        ori_seq_len=None,
1526:        ori_seq_len_cpu=None,
1527:        slot_indices=None,
1528:        mtp_slot_mapping=None,
1529:        attn_group=None,
1530:    ):
1531:        assert draft_index > 0
1532:        assert attn_group is not None, "vllm-ascend v0.17.0rc1 requires attn_group"
1533:        common_attn_metadata = self.shallow_copy_metadata(old_common_metadata)
1534:
1535:        if draft_index == 1:
1536:            if aclgraph_runtime_mode == CUDAGraphMode.FULL:
1537:                common_attn_metadata.num_reqs = input_batch_size
1538:                common_attn_metadata.block_table_tensor = self._adjust_tensor(
1539:                    common_attn_metadata.block_table_tensor, input_batch_size
1540:                )
1541:                common_attn_metadata.seq_lens = self._adjust_tensor(common_attn_metadata.seq_lens, input_batch_size)
1542:                common_attn_metadata.seq_lens_cpu = self._adjust_tensor(
1543:                    common_attn_metadata.seq_lens_cpu, input_batch_size
1544:                )
1545:                if common_attn_metadata._seq_lens_cpu is not None:
1546:                    common_attn_metadata._seq_lens_cpu = self._adjust_tensor(
1547:                        common_attn_metadata._seq_lens_cpu, input_batch_size
1548:                    )
1549:                if common_attn_metadata.num_computed_tokens_cpu is not None:
1550:                    common_attn_metadata.num_computed_tokens_cpu = self._adjust_tensor(
1551:                        common_attn_metadata.num_computed_tokens_cpu, input_batch_size
1552:                    )
1553:                common_attn_metadata.query_start_loc = self.arange[: input_batch_size + 1]
1554:                common_attn_metadata.query_start_loc_cpu = torch.from_numpy(
1555:                    self.token_arange_np[: input_batch_size + 1]
1556:                ).clone()
1557:            else:
1558:                common_attn_metadata.query_start_loc = self.arange[: batch_size + 1]
1559:                common_attn_metadata.query_start_loc_cpu = torch.from_numpy(
1560:                    self.token_arange_np[: batch_size + 1]
1561:                ).clone()
1562:
1563:            common_attn_metadata.num_actual_tokens = batch_size
1564:            common_attn_metadata.max_query_len = 1
1565:            common_attn_metadata.decode_token_per_req = 1
1566:            common_attn_metadata.attn_state = (
1567:                AscendAttentionState.SpecDecoding if self.method == "mtp" else AscendAttentionState.ChunkedPrefill
1568:            )
1569:            common_attn_metadata.graph_pad_size = -1
1570:            common_attn_metadata.num_input_tokens = input_batch_size
1571:
1572:        # The loop part
1573:        used_update_positions += 1
1574:
1575:        # Clone the data so that when calculating the data at position 2 and position 3
1576:        # in the merged graph, it does not affect position 1
1577:        # FIXME(lilinsiman)
1578:        common_attn_metadata.seq_lens = common_attn_metadata.seq_lens.clone()
1579:        if common_attn_metadata.seq_lens_cpu is not None:
1580:            common_attn_metadata.seq_lens_cpu = common_attn_metadata.seq_lens_cpu.clone()
1581:        if common_attn_metadata._seq_lens_cpu is not None:
1582:            common_attn_metadata._seq_lens_cpu = common_attn_metadata._seq_lens_cpu.clone()
1583:        if common_attn_metadata.num_computed_tokens_cpu is not None:
1584:            common_attn_metadata.num_computed_tokens_cpu = common_attn_metadata.num_computed_tokens_cpu.clone()
1585:        common_attn_metadata.positions = common_attn_metadata.positions.clone()
1586:
1587:        # NOTE(woosuk): We should handle the case where the draft model
1588:        # generates tokens beyond the max model length. Since it is complex
1589:        # to remove such requests from the batch, we keep them in the batch
1590:        # but adjust the position ids and slot mappings to avoid the
1591:        # out-of-range access during the model execution. The draft tokens
1592:        # generated with this adjustment should be ignored.
1593:        if self.uses_mrope:
1594:            exceeds_max_model_len = used_update_positions[0] >= self.max_model_len
1595:            # Mask out the position ids that exceed the max model length.
1596:            # Otherwise, we may get out-of-range error in RoPE.
1597:            clamped_positions = torch.where(
1598:                exceeds_max_model_len.unsqueeze(0), torch.zeros_like(used_update_positions), used_update_positions
1599:            )
1600:        else:
1601:            exceeds_max_model_len = used_update_positions >= self.max_model_len
1602:            clamped_positions = torch.where(exceeds_max_model_len, 0, used_update_positions)
1603:
1604:        # For data integrity when async scheduling, we shouldn't use in place
1605:        # operations in case they are modified in next step's `prepare_input`
1606:        # of main model.
1607:        # Increment the sequence lengths.
1608:        common_attn_metadata.seq_lens[:batch_size] += 1
1609:        # For the requests that exceed the max model length, we set the
1610:        # sequence length to 1 to minimize their overheads in attention.
1611:        exceeds_mask = common_attn_metadata.seq_lens[:batch_size] > self.max_model_len
1612:        common_attn_metadata.seq_lens[:batch_size].masked_fill_(exceeds_mask, 1)
1613:        if common_attn_metadata.seq_lens_cpu is not None:
1614:            common_attn_metadata.seq_lens_cpu[:batch_size] = common_attn_metadata.seq_lens_cpu[:batch_size] + 1
1615:            exceeds_mask_cpu = common_attn_metadata.seq_lens_cpu[:batch_size] > self.max_model_len
1616:            common_attn_metadata.seq_lens_cpu[:batch_size].masked_fill_(exceeds_mask_cpu, 1)
1617:        if common_attn_metadata._seq_lens_cpu is not None:
1618:            common_attn_metadata._seq_lens_cpu[:batch_size] = common_attn_metadata._seq_lens_cpu[:batch_size] + 1
1619:            exceeds_mask_internal_cpu = common_attn_metadata._seq_lens_cpu[:batch_size] > self.max_model_len
1620:            common_attn_metadata._seq_lens_cpu[:batch_size].masked_fill_(exceeds_mask_internal_cpu, 1)
1621:        if common_attn_metadata.num_computed_tokens_cpu is not None:
1622:            common_attn_metadata.num_computed_tokens_cpu[:batch_size] += 1
1623:        if self.uses_mrope:
1624:            common_attn_metadata.positions[:batch_size].copy_(clamped_positions[0])
1625:        else:
1626:            common_attn_metadata.positions[:batch_size].copy_(clamped_positions)
1627:
1628:        pcp_manager = getattr(self.runner, "pcp_manager", None)
1629:        if pcp_manager is not None:
1630:            kv_cache_spec = getattr(attn_group, "kv_cache_spec", self.draft_attn_groups[0].kv_cache_spec)
1631:            # update slot_mapping
1632:            slot_indices += self.pcp_size
1633:            slot_mapping = mtp_slot_mapping[slot_indices]
1634:            self.slot_mapping_group[draft_index][: batch_size * self.pcp_size] = slot_mapping
1635:            self.slot_mapping_group[draft_index][batch_size * self.pcp_size :].fill_(PADDING_SLOT_ID)
1636:            common_attn_metadata.slot_mapping = self.slot_mapping_group[draft_index]
1637:        else:
1638:            # NOTE: In vllm, `block_size = attn_metadata_builder.kv_cache_spec.block_size`.
1639:            # However, in vllm-ascend, the above value can be multiple of `kernel_block_size`,
1640:            # which is not correct for computing `slot_mapping` below.
1641:            if self.has_gdn:
1642:                block_size = self.kernel_block_size
1643:            else:
1644:                block_size = self.block_size
1645:
1646:            # Compute the slot mapping.
1647:            if self.uses_mrope:
1648:                block_numbers = clamped_positions[0] // block_size
1649:            else:
1650:                block_numbers = clamped_positions // block_size
1651:            block_ids = old_common_metadata.block_table_tensor.gather(dim=1, index=block_numbers.view(-1, 1))
1652:            block_ids = block_ids.view(-1)
1653:            if self.uses_mrope:
1654:                slot_mapping = block_ids * block_size + clamped_positions[0] % block_size
1655:            else:
1656:                slot_mapping = block_ids * block_size + clamped_positions % block_size
1657:
1658:            # Mask out the slot mappings that exceed the max model length.
1659:            # Otherwise, the KV cache will be inadvertently updated with the
1660:            # padding tokens.
1661:            slot_mapping.masked_fill_(exceeds_max_model_len, PADDING_SLOT_ID)
1662:            self.slot_mapping_group[draft_index][: slot_mapping.shape[0]].copy_(slot_mapping.to(torch.int32))
1663:            self.slot_mapping_group[draft_index][slot_mapping.shape[0] :].fill_(PADDING_SLOT_ID)
1664:            # Set the address of the attn_metadata.slot_mapping to the self.slot_mapping_group[idx]
1665:            common_attn_metadata.slot_mapping = self.slot_mapping_group[draft_index]
1666:
1667:        self.seq_lens_group[draft_index][: common_attn_metadata.seq_lens.shape[0]].copy_(common_attn_metadata.seq_lens)
1668:        self.seq_lens_group[draft_index][common_attn_metadata.seq_lens.shape[0] :].fill_(0)
1669:        common_attn_metadata.seq_lens = self.seq_lens_group[draft_index][: common_attn_metadata.seq_lens.shape[0]]
1670:
1671:        self.query_start_loc_group[draft_index][: common_attn_metadata.query_start_loc.shape[0]].copy_(
1672:            common_attn_metadata.query_start_loc
1673:        )
1674:        self.query_start_loc_group[draft_index][common_attn_metadata.query_start_loc.shape[0] :].fill_(0)
1675:        common_attn_metadata.query_start_loc = self.query_start_loc_group[draft_index][
1676:            : common_attn_metadata.query_start_loc.shape[0]
1677:        ]
1678:
1679:        attn_metadata_builder = attn_group.get_metadata_builder()
1680:
1681:        extra_attn_metadata_args = {}
1682:        if self.use_compress:
1683:            extra_attn_metadata_args = dict(
1684:                prefill_ratio_to_sas_metadata=dict(),
1685:                decode_ratio_to_sas_metadata=dict(),
1686:                common_ratio_to_sas_metadata=dict(),
1687:                block_size=self.draft_attn_groups[0].kv_cache_spec.block_size,
1688:            )
1689:        attn_metadata = attn_metadata_builder.build_for_drafting(
1690:            common_attn_metadata,
1691:            draft_index,
1692:            **extra_attn_metadata_args,
1693:        )
1694:
1695:        if pcp_manager is not None:
1696:            pcp_manager.update_spec_decode_drafting_cp_metadata(
1697:                attn_metadata=attn_metadata,
1698:                kv_cache_spec=kv_cache_spec,
1699:                seq_lens=ori_seq_len,
1700:                draft_index=draft_index,
1701:                seq_lens_cpu=ori_seq_len_cpu,
1702:                attn_metadata_builder=attn_metadata_builder,
1703:            )
1704:
1705:        return common_attn_metadata, attn_metadata
1706:
1707:    def prepare_next_token_ids_padded(
1708:        self,
1709:        sampled_token_ids: torch.Tensor,
1710:        requests: dict[str, CachedRequestState],
1711:        gpu_input_batch: InputBatch,
1712:        discard_request_indices: torch.Tensor,
1713:        num_discarded_requests: int,
1714:    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
1715:        """
1716:        This function is used to prepare the inputs for speculative decoding.
1717:        It calculates the next token ids and the number of valid sampled tokens
1718:        for each request, considering the "discarded" requests whose next token
1719:        is not sampled and comes from `request.get_token_id()` instead.
1720:        It also accounts for the rejected tokens in `sampled_token_ids`.
1721:        This function must use device functions to operate on the inputs, and
1722:        should not introduce any blocking CPU-GPU synchronization.
1723:        """
1724:        # TODO(Ben): Combine this into a custom fused kernel
1725:
1726:        # Precompute get_token_id for when there is no valid next token
1727:        num_reqs = gpu_input_batch.num_reqs
1728:        seq_lens_list = (gpu_input_batch.num_tokens_no_spec[:num_reqs] - 1).tolist()
1729:        self.backup_next_token_ids.np[:num_reqs] = np.array(
1730:            [requests[gpu_input_batch.req_ids[i]].get_token_id(seq_lens_list[i]) for i in range(num_reqs)]
1731:        )
1732:        self.backup_next_token_ids.copy_to_gpu(num_reqs)
1733:
1734:        # Mask out the sampled tokens indices that should not be sampled.
1735:        discard_sampled_tokens_req_indices = discard_request_indices[:num_discarded_requests]
1736:
1737:        valid_sampled_token_ids_gpu = sampled_token_ids.clone()
1738:        valid_sampled_token_ids_gpu = DeviceOperator.index_fill(
1739:            valid_sampled_token_ids_gpu,
1740:            0,
1741:            discard_sampled_tokens_req_indices,
1742:            -1,
1743:        )
1744:
1745:        # Generate a mask for all valid tokens within those requests
1746:        valid_mask = (valid_sampled_token_ids_gpu != -1) & (valid_sampled_token_ids_gpu < gpu_input_batch.vocab_size)
1747:
1748:        # Count the number of valid tokens in each request
1749:        valid_sampled_tokens_count = valid_mask.sum(dim=1)
1750:
1751:        # Get the rightmost valid index per row
1752:        last_valid_indices = valid_sampled_tokens_count - 1
1753:        last_valid_indices_safe = torch.clamp(last_valid_indices, min=0)
1754:
1755:        # Get last valid token from each row
1756:        # (assume undefined state where there is no valid token)
1757:        selected_tokens = torch.gather(valid_sampled_token_ids_gpu, 1, last_valid_indices_safe.unsqueeze(1)).squeeze(1)
1758:
1759:        # Use last token if valid, pre-computed backup if not
1760:        batch_size = valid_sampled_token_ids_gpu.shape[0]
1761:        next_token_ids = torch.where(
1762:            last_valid_indices != -1,
1763:            selected_tokens,
1764:            self.backup_next_token_ids.gpu[:batch_size],
1765:        )
1766:
1767:        return next_token_ids, valid_sampled_tokens_count
1768:
1769:    def prepare_inputs(
1770:        self,
1771:        common_attn_metadata: CommonAttentionMetadata,
1772:        sampled_token_ids: list[list[int]],
1773:        num_draft_tokens: list[int],
1774:    ) -> tuple[CommonAttentionMetadata, torch.Tensor]:
1775:        """
1776:        This function is used to prepare the inputs for speculative decoding.
1777:        It updates to the common_attn_metadata to account for the rejected
1778:        tokens (and newly sampled tokens). It also returns the token indices
1779:        of the tokens that should be fed to the speculator.
1780:        """
1781:        # E.g.
1782:        #  common_attn_metadata.query_start_loc{_cpu}:
1783:        #       [0, q1, q1 + q2, q1 + q2 + q3]
1784:        #  common_attn_metadata.seq_lens{_cpu}: [s1, s2, s3]
1785:        #  num_rejected_tokens: [n1, n2, n3]
1786:        # This function computes the intermediate values:
1787:        #  num_tokens_per_req: [q1 - n1, q2 - n2, q3 - n3]
1788:        # And returns:
1789:        #  common_attn_metadata.query_start_loc{_cpu}:
1790:        #       [0, q1 - n1, q1 + q2 - n1 - n2, q1 + q2 + q3 - n1 - n2 - n3]
1791:        #  common_attn_metadata.seq_lens{_cpu}:
1792:        #       [s1 - n1 + 1, s2 - n2 + 1, s3 - n3 + 1]
1793:        #  token_indices: [0, 1, ..., q1 - n1 - 1,
1794:        #                 q1, q1 + 1, ..., q1 + q2 - n2 - 1,
1795:        #                 q1 + q2, q1 + q2 + 1, ..., q1 + q2 + q3 - n3 - 1]
1796:
1797:        num_actual_reqs = len(num_draft_tokens)
1798:        num_rejected_tokens = [
1799:            n + 1 - len(sampled_token_ids[i]) if n > 0 else 0 for i, n in enumerate(num_draft_tokens)
1800:        ]
1801:        num_rejected_tokens = torch.tensor(num_rejected_tokens, dtype=torch.int32)
1802:
1803:        device = common_attn_metadata.query_start_loc.device
1804:        query_start_loc_cpu = common_attn_metadata.query_start_loc_cpu[: num_actual_reqs + 1]
1805:        # Prefer the upstream-canonical ``_seq_lens_cpu``; fall back to the
1806:        # Ascend subclass field. In async-spec mode the model runner only
1807:        # populates ``_seq_lens_cpu`` (optimistic_seq_lens_cpu) and leaves
1808:        # ``seq_lens_cpu`` as None, so an unguarded read here would crash.
1809:        if common_attn_metadata._seq_lens_cpu is not None:
1810:            seq_lens_cpu = common_attn_metadata._seq_lens_cpu[:num_actual_reqs]
1811:        else:
1812:            seq_lens_cpu = common_attn_metadata.seq_lens_cpu[:num_actual_reqs]
1813:        new_seq_lens_cpu = seq_lens_cpu - num_rejected_tokens
1814:
1815:        # [0, q1, q1 + q2, q1 + q2 + q3] -> [q1, q2, q3]
1816:        new_query_len_per_req = query_start_loc_cpu[1:] - query_start_loc_cpu[:-1]
1817:        # [q1, q2, q3] -> [q1 - n1, q2 - n2, q3 - n3]
1818:        new_num_tokens_per_req = new_query_len_per_req - num_rejected_tokens
1819:        new_num_tokens_per_req_np = new_num_tokens_per_req.numpy()
1820:
1821:        # [q1 - n1, q2 - n2, q3 - n3] ->
1822:        # [0, q1 - n1, q1 + q2 - n1 - n2, q1 + q2 + q3 - n1 - n2 - n3]
1823:        new_query_start_loc_cpu = torch.zeros(
1824:            query_start_loc_cpu.shape,
1825:            dtype=torch.int32,
1826:            pin_memory=is_pin_memory_available(),
1827:        )
1828:        new_query_start_loc_np = new_query_start_loc_cpu.numpy()
1829:        np.cumsum(new_num_tokens_per_req_np, out=new_query_start_loc_np[1:])
1830:
1831:        total_num_tokens = new_query_start_loc_np[-1]
1832:        # Example assuming num_tokens_per_req_np = [2, 4, 3]
1833:        # this implies that `new_query_start_locs` is:
1834:        # [0, 2, 6, 9] ->
1835:        # [0, 0, 2, 2, 2, 2, 6, 6, 6]
1836:        #  _r1_  ____r2____  ___r3__
1837:        new_query_start_locs_expanded = np.repeat(new_query_start_loc_np[:-1], new_num_tokens_per_req_np)
1838:        # [0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8] ->
1839:        # [0, 1, 0, 1, 2, 3, 0, 1, 2]
1840:        #  _r1_  ____r2____  ___r3__
1841:        token_offsets = self.token_arange_np[:total_num_tokens] - new_query_start_locs_expanded
1842:
1843:        # Expand starting positions to match token pattern
1844:        # [0, q1, q1 + q2] ->
1845:        # [0, 0, q1, q1, q1, q1, q1 + q2, q1 + q2, q1 + q2]
1846:        #  _r1_  _____r2_______  ___________r3____________
1847:        old_query_start_locs_expanded = np.repeat(query_start_loc_cpu[:-1].numpy(), new_num_tokens_per_req_np)
1848:        # Final token indices are:
1849:        # [0, 1,                                // req 1
1850:        #  q1 + 0, q1 + 1, q1 + 2, q1 + 3,       // req 2
1851:        #  q1 + q2 + 0, q1 + q2 + 1, q1 + q2 + 2] // req 3
1852:        token_indices_np = token_offsets + old_query_start_locs_expanded
1853:        token_indices = torch.from_numpy(token_indices_np).to(device, non_blocking=True)
1854:
1855:        common_attn_metadata.slot_mapping[: token_indices.shape[0]].copy_(
1856:            common_attn_metadata.slot_mapping[token_indices]
1857:        )
1858:        common_attn_metadata.slot_mapping[token_indices.shape[0] :].fill_(-1)
1859:
1860:        # NOTE: Currently positions and seq_lens are not used in attn forward
1861:        # so we do not need to fixed them. But if they are used in the future,
1862:        # we should fixed them.
1863:        # Mirror ``new_seq_lens_cpu`` into the upstream-canonical
1864:        # ``_seq_lens_cpu`` slot so consumers preferring the parent field
1865:        # (e.g. attention_cp builder) see the rejection-adjusted value.
1866:        spec_common_attn_metadata = AscendCommonAttentionMetadata(
1867:            query_start_loc=new_query_start_loc_cpu.to(device, non_blocking=True),
1868:            query_start_loc_cpu=new_query_start_loc_cpu,
1869:            seq_lens=new_seq_lens_cpu.to(device, non_blocking=True),
1870:            seq_lens_cpu=new_seq_lens_cpu,
1871:            _seq_lens_cpu=new_seq_lens_cpu,
1872:            num_computed_tokens_cpu=common_attn_metadata.num_computed_tokens_cpu,
1873:            _num_computed_tokens_cpu=common_attn_metadata._num_computed_tokens_cpu,
1874:            seq_lens_cpu_upper_bound=new_seq_lens_cpu,
1875:            num_reqs=common_attn_metadata.num_reqs,
1876:            num_actual_tokens=total_num_tokens,
1877:            num_input_tokens=common_attn_metadata.num_input_tokens,
1878:            max_query_len=new_query_len_per_req.max().item(),
1879:            block_table_tensor=common_attn_metadata.block_table_tensor,
1880:            slot_mapping=common_attn_metadata.slot_mapping,
1881:            actual_seq_lengths_q=self.runner.actual_seq_lengths_q,
1882:            positions=common_attn_metadata.positions[token_indices],
1883:            positions_cpu=common_attn_metadata.positions_cpu[token_indices]
1884:            if common_attn_metadata.positions_cpu is not None
1885:            else None,
1886:            attn_state=self.runner.attn_state,
1887:            decode_token_per_req=self.runner.decode_token_per_req,
1888:            is_prefilling=common_attn_metadata.is_prefilling,
1889:            max_seq_len=0,
1890:        )
1891:        return spec_common_attn_metadata, token_indices
1892:
1893:    def prepare_inputs_padded(
1894:        self,
1895:        common_attn_metadata: CommonAttentionMetadata,
1896:        spec_decode_metadata: SpecDecodeMetadata,
1897:        valid_sampled_tokens_count: torch.Tensor,
1898:    ) -> tuple[CommonAttentionMetadata, torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
1899:        """
1900:        This function is used to prepare the inputs for speculative decoding
1901:        It updates the common_attn_metadata for speculative decoding,
1902:        but does not consider the rejected tokens. Instead, all tokens
1903:        are included as inputs to the speculator, with the rejected tokens
1904:        used as padding and filtered out later by `token_indices_to_sample`.
1905:        No blocking CPU operations should be introduced in this function.
1906:        """
1907:        if HAS_TRITON:
1908:            num_reqs = common_attn_metadata.num_reqs
1909:            device = valid_sampled_tokens_count.device
1910:
1911:            token_indices_to_sample = torch.empty((num_reqs,), dtype=torch.int32, device=device)
1912:            num_rejected_tokens_gpu = torch.empty((num_reqs,), dtype=torch.int32, device=device)
1913:            num_blocks_needed = triton.cdiv(num_reqs, _PREPARE_INPUTS_BLOCK_SIZE)
1914:            num_vector_core = get_vectorcore_num()
1915:            grid_size = min(num_blocks_needed, num_vector_core)
1916:            grid = (grid_size,)
1917:
1918:            prepare_inputs_padded_kernel[grid](
1919:                spec_decode_metadata.cu_num_draft_tokens,
1920:                valid_sampled_tokens_count,
1921:                common_attn_metadata.query_start_loc,
1922:                token_indices_to_sample,
1923:                num_rejected_tokens_gpu,
1924:                num_reqs,
1925:                BLOCK_SIZE=_PREPARE_INPUTS_BLOCK_SIZE,
1926:            )
1927:        else:
1928:            num_draft_tokens_gpu = torch.cat(
1929:                [
1930:                    spec_decode_metadata.cu_num_draft_tokens[0:1],
1931:                    spec_decode_metadata.cu_num_draft_tokens[1:] - spec_decode_metadata.cu_num_draft_tokens[:-1],
1932:                ]
1933:            )
1934:
1935:            num_rejected_tokens_gpu = torch.where(
1936:                num_draft_tokens_gpu > 0,
1937:                num_draft_tokens_gpu + 1 - valid_sampled_tokens_count,
1938:                torch.zeros_like(num_draft_tokens_gpu),
1939:            )
1940:
1941:            token_indices_to_sample = common_attn_metadata.query_start_loc[1:] - 1 - num_rejected_tokens_gpu
1942:
1943:        query_start_loc_cpu = common_attn_metadata.query_start_loc_cpu
1944:
1945:        new_query_len_per_req = query_start_loc_cpu[1:] - query_start_loc_cpu[:-1]
1946:
1947:        total_num_tokens = query_start_loc_cpu[-1].item()
1948:        token_indices = self.arange[:total_num_tokens]
1949:
1950:        # NOTE: Currently positions and seq_lens are not used in attn forward
1951:        # so we do not need to fixed them. But if they are used in the future,
1952:        # we should fixed them.
1953:        # ``prepare_inputs_padded`` does not change ``seq_lens`` (rejected
1954:        # tokens are kept as padding and filtered out later). Pass through
1955:        # both the subclass ``seq_lens_cpu`` field and the upstream-canonical
1956:        # ``_seq_lens_cpu`` field unchanged. In async-spec mode only the
1957:        # latter is populated (subclass field is None to signal "GPU is
1958:        # authoritative"); dropping ``_seq_lens_cpu`` here causes downstream
1959:        # backends (e.g. attention_cp) to crash on a None subscript.
1960:        spec_common_attn_metadata = AscendCommonAttentionMetadata(
1961:            query_start_loc=common_attn_metadata.query_start_loc,
1962:            query_start_loc_cpu=query_start_loc_cpu,
1963:            seq_lens_cpu=common_attn_metadata.seq_lens_cpu,
1964:            _seq_lens_cpu=common_attn_metadata._seq_lens_cpu,
1965:            seq_lens_cpu_upper_bound=common_attn_metadata.seq_lens_cpu_upper_bound,
1966:            num_reqs=common_attn_metadata.num_reqs,
1967:            num_actual_tokens=common_attn_metadata.num_actual_tokens if self.pcp_size > 1 else total_num_tokens,
1968:            num_input_tokens=common_attn_metadata.num_input_tokens,
1969:            max_query_len=new_query_len_per_req.max().item(),
1970:            actual_seq_lengths_q=self.runner.actual_seq_lengths_q,
1971:            block_table_tensor=common_attn_metadata.block_table_tensor,
1972:            slot_mapping=common_attn_metadata.slot_mapping,
1973:            positions=common_attn_metadata.positions,
1974:            positions_cpu=common_attn_metadata.positions_cpu,
1975:            attn_state=self.runner.attn_state,
1976:            decode_token_per_req=self.runner.decode_token_per_req,
1977:            num_computed_tokens_cpu=common_attn_metadata.num_computed_tokens_cpu,
1978:            _num_computed_tokens_cpu=common_attn_metadata._num_computed_tokens_cpu,
1979:            seq_lens=common_attn_metadata.seq_lens,
1980:            is_prefilling=common_attn_metadata.is_prefilling,
1981:            max_seq_len=0,
1982:        )
1983:
1984:        return spec_common_attn_metadata, token_indices, token_indices_to_sample, num_rejected_tokens_gpu
1985:
1986:    # update full-graph params for one spec token
1987:    def _update_full_graph_params(self, forward_context, num_tokens, draft_attn_metadatas=None):
1988:        assert len(self.draft_attn_groups) > 0
1989:        attn_backend = self.draft_attn_groups[0].backend
1990:        update_full_graph_params(
1991:            attn_backend,
1992:            self.update_stream,
1993:            forward_context,
1994:            num_tokens,
1995:            self.vllm_config,
1996:            self.vllm_config.speculative_config,
1997:            draft_attn_metadatas=draft_attn_metadatas,
1998:        )
1999:
2000:    # adjusting tensor into desired size
2001:    def _adjust_tensor(self, tensor, desired_size):
2002:        pad_size = desired_size - tensor.shape[0]
2003:        if pad_size > 0:
2004:            pad = [0] * (2 * tensor.dim() - 1) + [pad_size]
2005:            tensor = F.pad(tensor, pad, mode="constant", value=0)
2006:        else:
2007:            tensor = tensor[:desired_size]
2008:        return tensor
2009:
2010:    def maybe_pad_and_reduce(
2011:        self,
2012:        hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
2013:        positions: torch.Tensor,
2014:    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
2015:        if self.method == "mtp":
2016:            if _EXTRA_CTX.flash_comm_v1_enabled and not self.is_multimodal_model:
2017:                hidden_states = torch.ops.vllm.maybe_pad_and_reduce(hidden_states)
2018:                positions = positions.unsqueeze(-1)
2019:                positions = torch.ops.vllm.maybe_pad_and_reduce(positions)
2020:                positions = positions.squeeze(-1)
2021:        else:
2022:            if _EXTRA_CTX.flash_comm_v1_enabled:
2023:                hidden_states = split_inputs_tp_to_sp(hidden_states, hidden_states)
2024:        return hidden_states, positions
2025:
2026:    def maybe_all_gather_and_unpad(
2027:        self,
2028:        last_hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
2029:        positions: torch.Tensor,
2030:        hidden_states: torch.Tensor | None = None,
2031:    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor | None]:
2032:        if self.method == "mtp":
2033:            if self.enable_shared_expert_dp:
2034:                last_hidden_states = torch.ops.vllm.maybe_all_gather_and_maybe_unpad(
2035:                    last_hidden_states.contiguous(), True
2036:                )
2037:                # in mm model, positions not need allgather, because it not reduced before(see maybe_pad_and_reduce())
2038:                if not self.is_multimodal_model:
2039:                    positions = torch.ops.vllm.maybe_all_gather_and_maybe_unpad(positions.contiguous(), True)
2040:                if hidden_states is not None:
2041:                    hidden_states = last_hidden_states
2042:        else:
2043:            if _EXTRA_CTX.flash_comm_v1_enabled:
2044:                last_hidden_states = torch.ops.vllm.maybe_all_gather_and_maybe_unpad(
2045:                    last_hidden_states.contiguous(), True
2046:                )
2047:                if hidden_states is not None:
2048:                    hidden_states = torch.ops.vllm.maybe_all_gather_and_maybe_unpad(hidden_states.contiguous(), True)
2049:        return last_hidden_states, positions, hidden_states
2050:
2051:    # In the context of the dummy‑run accompaniment of p‑eagle, when num_indices becomes large,
2052:    # enabling the LM head feature causes token_indices_to_sample to switch from padding to trimming.
2053:    # The trimmed length may not be an integer multiple of the speculative length,
2054:    # in which case padding is required to restore it to the original length.
2055:    def _align_tensor_and_indices(
2056:        self,
2057:        tensor,
2058:        num_indices,
2059:        token_indices_to_sample,
2060:        ori_token_indices_to_sample,
2061:        is_logits=False,
2062:    ):
2063:        """
2064:        Align the tensor (either draft_token_ids or logits) and token_indices_to_sample
2065:        to the length specified by num_indices.
2066:
2067:        Args:
2068:        tensor: The tensor to be aligned (draft_token_ids or logits)
2069:        num_indices: The target length
2070:        token_indices_to_sample: The current index tensor
2071:        ori_token_indices_to_sample: The original index tensor (used for restoration)
2072:        is_logits: Whether the tensor is logits (affects the padding dimension and padding value)
2073:
2074:        Returns:
2075:        The adjusted tensor and token_indices_to_sample
2076:        """
2077:        if tensor.shape[0] == num_indices:
2078:            return tensor, token_indices_to_sample
2079:
2080:        if tensor.shape[0] > num_indices:
2081:            # Trim to the target length.
2082:            tensor = tensor[:num_indices]
2083:            token_indices_to_sample = token_indices_to_sample[:num_indices]
2084:        else:
2085:            # Padding to the target length.
2086:            pad_size = num_indices - tensor.shape[0]
2087:            if is_logits:
2088:                # logits: shape [seq_len, vocab_size], Padding at the end of the seq dimension.
2089:                tensor = nn.functional.pad(tensor, (0, 0, 0, pad_size), value=-1e9)
2090:            else:
2091:                # draft_token_ids: shape [seq_len], Padding at the end
2092:                tensor = nn.functional.pad(tensor, (0, pad_size))
2093:            token_indices_to_sample = ori_token_indices_to_sample
2094:
2095:        return tensor, token_indices_to_sample

21:from vllm_ascend.spec_decode.dflash_proposer import AscendDflashProposer
22:from vllm_ascend.spec_decode.draft_proposer import AscendDraftModelProposer
23:from vllm_ascend.spec_decode.eagle_proposer import AscendEagleProposer
24:from vllm_ascend.spec_decode.extract_hidden_states_proposer import (
25:    AscendExtractHiddenStatesProposer,

27:from vllm_ascend.spec_decode.medusa_proposer import AscendMedusaProposer
28:from vllm_ascend.spec_decode.ngram_proposer import AscendNgramProposer
29:from vllm_ascend.spec_decode.ngram_proposer_npu import AscendNgramProposerNPU
30:from vllm_ascend.spec_decode.step3p5 import AscendStep3p5MTPProposer
31:from vllm_ascend.spec_decode.suffix_proposer import AscendSuffixDecodingProposer

33:
34:def get_spec_decode_method(method, vllm_config, device, runner):
35:    if method == "ngram":
36:        return AscendNgramProposer(vllm_config, runner)
37:    elif method == "ngram_gpu":
38:        return AscendNgramProposerNPU(vllm_config, device, runner)
39:    elif method == "suffix":
40:        return AscendSuffixDecodingProposer(vllm_config, runner)
41:    elif method == "medusa":
42:        return AscendMedusaProposer(vllm_config, device)
43:    elif method in ("eagle", "eagle3", "mtp"):
44:        speculative_config = vllm_config.speculative_config
45:        if speculative_config is not None and speculative_config.use_step3p5_mtp():
46:            return AscendStep3p5MTPProposer(vllm_config, device, runner)
47:        return AscendEagleProposer(vllm_config, device, runner)
48:    elif method == "dflash":
49:        return AscendDflashProposer(vllm_config, device, runner)
50:    elif method == "draft_model":
51:        return AscendDraftModelProposer(vllm_config, device, runner)
52:    elif method == "extract_hidden_states":
53:        return AscendExtractHiddenStatesProposer(vllm_config, device, runner)
54:    else:
55:        raise ValueError(f"Unknown speculative decoding method: {method}")
